# Targum Jonathan on Isaiah

תרגום יונתן על ישעיהו

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah)

תרגום מרכזי על ספרי הנביאים שנקרא בציבור בבית הכנסת בתקופת התלמוד, ועדיין נקרא היום בקהילות של יהודי תימן.

Primary Targum on the books of the Prophets, read publicly in synagogues in talmudic times and still today by Yemenite Jews.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Targum Jonathan/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Mikraot Gedolot (Segments: 1291, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): London "Chaldee Paraphrase," 1871 (Segments: 1291)


## Chapter 1

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:1)


נְבוּאַת יְשַׁעְיָה בַר אָמוֹץ דְאִתְנַבֵּי עַל אֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה וְיָתְבֵי יְרוּשְׁלֵם בִּימֵי עֻזִיָה יוֹתָם אָחָז יְחִזְקִיָהוּ מַלְכַיָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה:


The Prophecy Of Isaiah, The Son Of Amoz, Which Is Prophesied Concerning The Men Of Judah And The Inhabitants Of JeruSalem, In The Days Of Uzziah, Jotham, Ahaz, And Hezekiah, The Kings Of The House Of JUDAH


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:2)


שְׁמַעוּ שְׁמַיָא דְזָעוּ כַּד יְהָבִית אוֹרַיְתִי לְעַמִי וַאֲצִיתִי אַרְעָא דְאִתְרְגִישַׁת מִן קֳדָם פִּתְגָמַי אֲרֵי יְיָ מַלֵיל עִמִי בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל קְרִיתִי לְהוֹן בְּנִין חֲבַבְתִּינוּן וִיקַרְתִּנּוּן וְאִנוּן מְרַדוּ בְמֵימְרִי:


Hear, O heavens, which trembled when I gave my law to my people, and give ear, O earth, which was agitated on account of my words, for the Lord has spoken. My people, the house of Israel, whom I called sons, I loved them, I made them glorious; but they have rebelled against my Word.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:3)


יְדַע תּוֹרָא זָבְּנֵיהּ וַחֲמָרָא אוֹרְיָא דִמְרוֹהִי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָא אֲלִיף לְמִדַע דְחַלְתִּי עַמָא לָא אִסְתַּכֵּל לְמֵתָב לְאוֹרַיְתִי:


The ox knoweth his purchaser, and the ass his master's crib; but Israel has not learned to know my fear, my people doth not consider to return to my law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:4)


וַי עַל דְאִתְקְרִיאוּ עַם קַדִישׁ וַחֲטוֹ כְּנִישָׁא בְחִירָא וְאַסְגִיאוּ חוֹבִין אִתְכְּנִיאוּ בְזַרְעָא רְחִימָא וְאַבְאִישׁוּ אִתְאַמַר לְהוֹן בְּנִין חֲבִיבִין וְחַבִּילוּ אוֹרְחָתְהוֹן שְׁבַקוּ יַת פּוּלְחָנָא דַייָ קָצוּ בְּדַחֲלַת קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וּבְדִיל עוֹבְדֵיהוֹן בִּישַׁיָא אִסְתְּחֲרוּ וַהֲווֹ לְאַחוֹרָא:


Woe to those who are called a holy people, because they have sinned; a chosen congregation, but they have multiplied sins; they are surnamed a beloved seed, but they have done evil; they are called beloved sons, but they have corrupted their paths; they have forsaken the worship of the Lord; they abhorred the fear of the Holy One of Israel, and because their works are evil they are turned away, and they are turned back.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:5)


לָא מִסְתַּכְּלִין לְמֵימָר עַל מָה לָקַנָא עוֹד מוֹסְפִין לְמֶחְטֵי וְלָא אָמְרִין מָה דֵין כָּל רֵישׁ מְרַע וְכָל לֵב דַוָי:


They do not consider so as to say, Wherefore has He smitten us? They continue in sin;' they do not say, Why is all the head sick, and the whole heart faint?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:6)


מִשְׁאָר עַמָא וְעַד רֵישָׁא לֵית בְּהוֹן דִשְׁלִים בִּדְחַלְתִּי כּוּלְהוֹן סָרְבִין וּמָרְדִין אִתְגַעֲלוּ בְחוֹבִין כְּמָחָא מְרַסְסָא לָא שָׁבְקִין מִזְדוֹנֵיהוֹן וְלָא מְחַמְדִין לִתְיוּבְתָּא אַף לָא זַכְוָן לְהוֹן לְאַגָנָא עֲלֵיהוֹן:


From the lowest of my people even unto the chief of them, there is not one amongst them who is perfect in my fear: all of them are disobedient and rebellious; they are polluted with sins, they are like an ulcerous wound; they have not forsaken their pride, neither long for repentance; they have also no righteousness to protect them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:7)


אַרְעֲכוֹן צַדְיָא קִרְוֵיכוֹן יְקִידַת נוּר אַרְעֲכוֹן לְקִבְלֵיכוֹן עַמְמַיָא מַחְסְנִין יָתָהּ וּבְחוֹבֵיהוֹן צְדִיאַת מִנְכוֹן אִתְחַלָפַת וַהֲוָה לְנוּכְרָאִין:


Your land is desolate, your cities each burnt with fire; in your presence the nations possess it. On account of their sins it is laid desolate: from you it is transferred, and it belongs to aliens.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:8)


וְאִשְׁתְּאַרַת כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְצִיוֹן כְּמִטַלַלְתָּא בְּכַרְמָא בָּתַר דְקַטְפוּהִי כְּעַרְסַל מַבְּתּוּתָא בְּמִקְשַׁיָא בָּתַר דִאַבְעָיוּהִי כְּקַרְתָּא דְצָיְרִין עֲלָהּ:


The congregation of Zion is left as a booth in a vineyard after they have gathered the grapes; as a lodge for passing the night in, in a garden of cucumbers after they have gleaned it; as a city against which they lay siege.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:9)


אִלוּלָפוֹן מוֹתָר טוּבֵיהּ דַייָ צְבָאוֹת אַשְׁאַר לָנָא שֵׁיזְבָא בְּרַחֲמוֹהִי אִית עִמָנָא חוֹבִין דְכֶאֱנָשׁ סְדוֹם פּוֹן אֲבַדְנָא וּכְיָתְבֵי עֲמוֹרָה אִשְׁתֵּיצָנָא:


Except the exceeding goodness of the Lord of hosts had caused to remain unto us in His mercy a deliverance, we had sins, on account of which we certainly had perished as the men of Sodom, and as the inhabitants of Gomorrah we would have been consumed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:10)


קַבִּילוּ פִּתְגָמָא דַייָ שִׁלְטוֹנַיָא דְעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן בִּישִׁין כְּשִׁלְטוֹנֵי סְדוֹם אֲצִיתוּ לְאוֹרַיְתָא דֶאֱלָהָנָא עַמָא דְעָבְדֵיהוֹן דָמְיָן לְעַם עֲמוֹרָה:


Hear ye the word of the Lord, ye rulers, whose works are evil, as of the rulers of Sodom; give ear to the law of our God, O people, whose works are evil like those of the people of Gomorrah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:11)


לָא רַעֲוָא קֳדָמַי בִּסְגִיאוּת נִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיכוֹן אֲמַר יְיָ שְׂבַעֵית מִסַת עֲלָוָת דִכְרִין וּתְרַב פַּטִימִין וְדַם תּוֹרִין וְאִמְרִין וְגַדְיָן דִכְאִלֵין לָא רַעֲוָא קֳדָמַי בְּהוֹן:


I'll have no pleasure in the multitude of your holy sacrifices, saith the Lord: I am full with the abundance of the burnt offerings of rams, and the fat of fed beasts, and the blood of bullocks, and of lambs, and of he-goats; for in such I have no pleasure.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:12)


כַּד אַתּוּן אָתָן לְאִתְחֲזָאָה קֳדָמַי מַן תְּבַע דָא מִיֶדְכוֹן לְמֵיתֵי וּלְאִתְדָשָׁא עֲזָרָתִי:


When ye come to appear before me, who has required this at your hand, and that ye should come? Tread not my courts;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:13)


לָא תוֹסְפוּן לְאַיְתָאָה מִנְחַת אֲנִיסָא קוּרְבַּן מְרַחַק הוּא קֳדָמַי יַרְחִין וְשַׁבִּין כְּנִישָׁא אַתּוּן מִתְכַּנְשִׁין לָא שָׁבְקִין חוֹבֵיכוֹן לְקַבְּלָא צְלוֹתְכוֹן בְּעִדַן כְּנִישַׁתְכוֹן:


Bring no more the oblation obtained by oppression; sacrifice is an abomination before me; the new moons and Sabbaths, the assembly ye are congregating, your sins shall not be forgiven, nor shall I hear your prayer at the time of your congregating yourselves together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:14)


יַרְחֵיכוֹן וּמוֹעֲדֵיכוֹן רָחֵיק מֵימְרִי הֲווֹ קֳדָמַי לְרִחוּק אַסְגֵיתִי לְמִשְׁבָּק:


Your new moons and your appointed feasts my Word hates; they are an abomination before me. I have often forgiven you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:15)


וְכַד כַּהֲנַיָא פָּרְסִין יְדֵהוֹן לְצַלָאָה עֲלֵיכוֹן מְסַלֵיקְנָא אַפֵּי שְׁכִנְתִּי מִנְכוֹן וְאַף כַּד אַתּוּן מַסְגַן לְצַלָאָה לָא רַעֲוָא קֳדָמַי לְקַבָּלָא צְלוֹתְכוֹן מִדִי יֶדְכוֹן דַם זַכַּאי מַלְיָן:


When your priests are spreading out their hands to pray for you, I shall make to ascend the presence of my Shekinah from you; and when you are multiplying prayer, it is not my pleasure to accept your prayer at your hands, they being full of the blood of the innocent.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:16)


תּוּבוּ לְאוֹרַיְתָא וְאִדַכּוּ מֵחוֹבֵיכוֹן וְאַעְדוֹ בִישׁוּת עוֹבָדֵיכוֹן מִקֳבֵיל מֵימְרִי אִתְמְנִיעוּ מִלְאַבְאָשָׁא:


Return to the law; make you clean from your sins; put away the evil of your doings from before the presence of my Word; cease to do evil;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:17)


אַלִיפוּ לְאוֹטָבָא תְּבָעוּ דִינָא זַכּוּ דַאֲנִיס דוּנוּ דִין יַתְמָא עֲבִידוּ קַבִּילוּ קְבֵילַת אַרְמַלְתָּא:


Learn to do well; seek judgment; judge ye justice for him who is suffering violence; do justice to the orphan; hear ye the cry of the widow.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:18)


בְּכֵן כַּד תְּתוּבוּן לְאוֹרַיְתָא תִּבְעוּן מִן קֳדָמַי וְאַעְבֵּיד בָּעוּתְכוֹן אֲמַר יְיָ אִם יְהוֹן חוֹבֵיכוֹן כְּתִימִין כְּצִבְעוֹנִין כְּתַלְגָא יֶחֱוָרוּן אִם יִסְמְקוּן כְּזָהֳרֵיתָא כַּעֲמַר נְקֵי יְהוֹן:


Then, when ye return to the law, ye shall pray before me, and I will grant your petition, saith the Lord. Though your sins be stained as with dye, they shall be white as snow; though they be red like crimson, they shall be pure as wool.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:19)


אִם תֵּיבוּן וּתְקַבְּלוּ לְמֵימְרִי טוּבָא דְאַרְעָא תֵּיכְלוּן:


If ye be willing and obedient to my Word, ye shall eat the good of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:20)


וְאִם תְּסָרְבוּן וְלָא תְקַבְּלוּן לְמֵימְרִי בְּחֶרֶב סָגְאָה תִּתְקַטְלוּן אֲרֵי מֵימְרָא דַייָ גְזַר כֵּן:


But if ye refuse, and will not be obedient to my Word, ye shall be slain with the sword of the enemy; for the Word of the Lord has decreed it thus.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:21)


אֵיכְדֵין תָּבוּ עוֹבְדָהָא לְמֶהֱוֵי כְטָעִיתָא קַרְתָּא מְהֵימַנְתָּא דַהֲוַת מַלְיָא עָבְדֵי דִינָא קוּשְׁטָא הֲוָה מִתְעֲבֵיד בָּהּ וּכְעַן אִינוּן קְטוֹלֵי נַפְשָׁן:


How are the works of the faithful city become like those of an harlot! she was full of those who did judgment, truth was wrought in her; but now they are murderers!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:22)


כַּסְפֵּךְ הֲוָה לִפְסוּלָא וְחַמְרֵיךְ מְעָרָב בְּמַיָא:


Thy silver is become dross, thy wine mixed with water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:23)


רַבְרְבַיִךְ מָרְדִין וְשׁוּתָּפִין לְגַנָבִין כּוּלְהוֹן רְחִימִין לְקַבָּלָא שׁוֹחֲדָא אָמְרִין גְבַר לְחַבְרֵיהּ עֲבֵיד לִי טַב בְּדִינִי וַאֲשַׁלֵם לָךְ בְּדִינָךְ דִין יַתְמָא לָא דַיְנִין וּקְבֵילַת אַרְמַלְתָּא לָא עֲלַת לָקֳדָמֵיהוֹן:


Thy princes are rebellious, and companions of thieves: all of them love gifts. One saith to the other, Show me kindness in my cause, and I will repay thee in thy cause. The fatherless they judge not, and the cry of the widow does not come before them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:24)


בְּכֵן אֲמַר רִבּוֹן עַלְמָא יְיָ צְבָאוֹת תַּקִיפָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל קַרְתָּא יְרוּשְׁלֵם אֲנָא עֲתִיד לְנַחֲמוּתָהּ בְּרַם וַי לְרַשִׁיעַיָא כַּד אִתְגְלֵי לְמֶעְבַּד פּוּרְעֲנוּת דִין מְסַנְאֵי עַמִי וְאָתִיב נְקָמָא לִבְעֵיל דְבָבִי:


Therefore saith the Lord of the world, the Lord of hosts, the Mighty One of Israel, The city of Jerusalem, I will comfort her; but woe to the wicked, when I shall reveal myself, to render just recompense to the enemies of my people, and render vengeance to my enemies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:25)


וְאָתִיב מְחַת גְבוּרְתִּי עֲלָךְ וַאֲבָרֵר כְּמָא דִמְנַקַן בְּבוֹרִיתָא כָּל רַשִׁיעַיָא וְאַעְדִי כָּל חוֹבָךְ:


And I will turn the blow of my might upon thee; and I will scour away all the wicked, as he that is scouring with soap, and take away all thy sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:26)


וַאֲמַנֵי בִיךְ דַיָנֵי קוּשְׁטָא תַקְנִין כִּבְקַדְמֵיתָא וּמָלְכֵי מִלְכָךְ כַּד מִן אַוְלָא וּבָתַר כֵּן יִתְקְרֵי לָךְ קַרְתָּא דְקוּשְׁטָא קַרְתָּא מְהֵימַנְתָּא:


And I will appoint in thee judges of truth, upright ones, as at the first, and thy counsellors, as at the beginning: afterward thou shalt be called the holy city, the faithful city.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:27)


צִיוֹן כַּד יִתְעֲבֵד בָּהּ דִינָא תִתְפְּרוֹק וְדַעֲבָדוּ אוֹרַיְתָא יְתוּבוּן לָהּ בְּזָכוּ:


As for Zion, when justice shall be practised in her, she shall be redeemed; and those that do the law shall return to her in righteousness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:28)


וְיִתַּבְּרוּן מָרוֹדִין וְחַיָבִין כַּחֲדָא וְדִשְׁבָקוּ אוֹרַיְתָא דַייָ יִשְׁתֵּיצוּן:


The rebels and the sinners shall be broken together; and those that forsake the law of the Lord shall be destroyed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:29)


אֲרֵי תִּתְבַּהֲתוּן מֵאִילָנֵי טַעֲוָתָא דַחֲמַדְתּוּן וְתִתְכַּנְעוּן מִגִנֵי טַעֲוָתָא דְאַתּוּן מִסְתַּיְעִין בְּהוֹן:


For ye shall be confounded of the oaks of idolatry which ye have desired; and ye shall be ashamed of the gardens of idolatry in which ye were seeking help.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:30)


אֲרֵי תְהוֹן כְּבוּטְמָא דִי מְנַתַּר טַרְפוֹהִי וּכְגִנַת שַׁקְיָא דְמַיָן לֵית לָהּ:


For ye shall be as an oak whose leaf fadeth, and as a watered garden that hath no water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 1:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/1:31)


וִיהֵי תוּקְפֵיהוֹן דְרַשִׁיעַיָא כִּנְעוֹרֶת כִּתָּנָא וְעוֹבַד יֶדְהוֹן כְּנִיצוֹץ נוּרָא כְּמָא דִמְקָרְבִין דֵין לִסְטַר דֵין וְדָלְקִין תַּרְוֵיהוֹן כַּחֲדָא כֵּן יְסוּפוּן רַשִׁיעַיָא אִינוּן וְעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן בִּישַׁיָא וְלָא יְהֵא עֲלֵיהוֹן חַיָס:


The strength of the wicked shall be as tow of flax, and the work of their hand as a spark of fire, when one approaches to the side of the other, both are burning together: thus the wicked shall be consumed, they and their evil works, and none shall have pity upon them.



## Chapter 2

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:1)


פִּתְגָם נְבוּאָה דְאִתְנַבֵּי יְשַׁעְיָה בַּר אָמוֹץ עַל אֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה וְיַתְבֵי יְרוּשְׁלֵם:


The Word Of Prophecy, Which Isaiah The Son Of Amoz Prophesied Concerning The Men Of Judah And The Inhabitants Of Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:2)


וִיהֵי בְּסוֹף יוֹמַיָא מְתַקֵן יְהֵי טוּר בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא דַייָ בְּרֵישׁ טוּרַיָא וּמְנַטַל מֵרָמָתָא וְיִתְפְּנוּן לְמִפְלַח עֲלוֹהִי כָּל מַלְכוּתָא:


And it shall come to pass in the end of days, that the mountain of the house of the sanctuary of the Lord shall be established in the top of the mountains, and it shall be exalted above the hills; and all the kingdoms shall be turned to worship upon it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:3)


יִיהָבוּן עַמְמִין סַגִיאִין וְיֵמְרוּן אֱתוֹ וְנִסַק לְטוּר בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא דַייָ לְבֵית שִׁכְנַת אֱלָהֵיהּ דְיַעֲקֹב וְיַלְפִינָנָא מֵאוֹרְחָן דְתַקְנָן קֳדָמוֹהִי וּנְהַךְ בְּאוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתֵיהּ אֲרֵי מִצִיוֹן תִּפּוֹק אוֹרַיְתָא וְאוּלְפָן פִּתְגָמָא דַייָ מִירוּשְׁלֶם:


And many people shall go and say, Come ye, and let us go up to the mountain of the house of the sanctuary of the Lord, to the house of the Shekinah of the God of Jacob ; and He will teach us of His ways, which are right before Him, and we will walk in the instruction of His law; for out of Zion shall go forth the law, and the instruction of the word of the Lord from Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:4)


וְיָדִין בֵּין מִלְכְּוָתָא וְיוֹכַח לְעַמְמִין סַגִיאִין וִיעַפְפוּן סְיָפֵיהוֹן לְסִכִּין וּמוּרְנְיָתֵיהוֹן לְמַגָלִין לָא יִטוֹל עַם בְּאַפֵּי עַם זְיַן וְלָא יִתְאַלְפוּן עוֹד מִבְּכֵן קְרָבָא:


And He shall judge among the kingdoms, and rebuke many people: and they shall beat their swords into plowshares, and their spears into sickles: nation against nation shall not lift up any weapon, neither shall they learn war any more.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:5)


יֵימְרוּן דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב אֲתוֹ וּנְהַךְ בְּאוּלְפָן אוֹרַיְתָא דַיְיָ:


Those of the house of Jacob shall say, Come ye, and let us walk in the instruction of the law of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:6)


אֲרֵי שְׁבַקְתּוּן דַחֲלָא תַקִיפָא דַהֲוָה פָּרֵיק לְכוֹן דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב אֲרֵי אִתְמְלִיאַת אַרְעֲכוֹן טַעֲוָן כְּמִלְקַדְמִין וְעָנְנִין כִּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וּבְנִימוּסֵי עַמְמַיָא אָזְלִין:


Because ye have forsaken the terrible, the mighty One, who has redeemed you of the house of Jacob, therefore your land is full of idols, as from the beginning; and they have become prognosticated by the clouds, like the Philistines; and they are walking in the laws of the nations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:7)


וְאִתְמְלִיאַת אַרְעֲהוֹן כְּסַף וּדְהַב וְלֵית סוֹף לְגִנְזוֹהִי וְאִתְמְלִיאַת אַרְעֲהוֹן סוּסְוָן וְלֵית סוֹף לִרְתִיכוֹהִי:


Also their land is full of silver and gold, and there is no end of their treasures; their land is also full of horses, and there is no end of their chariots.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:8)


וְאִתְמְלִיאַת אַרְעֲהוֹן טַעֲוָן לְעוֹבָדֵי יְדֵיהוֹן סָגְדִין לִדְאַתְקִינָא אֶצְבְּעָתֵיהוֹן:


Their land is also full of idols; they prostrate themselves to the work of their own hands, to that which is shaped by their own fingers.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:9)


וְיִמְאַךְ אֱנָשָׁא וַיֶחֱלַשׁ תְּקוֹף גַבְרִין וְלָא תִשְׁבּוֹק לְהוֹן:


The common man shall be humbled, and the strength of the great men shall become weak; and Thou shalt not pardon them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:10)


וְיַעֲלוּן לְמֶעֱרַק בְּמַעֲרַק בְּטִנָרָא וּלְאִטַמָרָא בְּעַפְרָא מִן קֳדָם דְחִילָא דַייָ וּמִזִיו יְקָרֵיהּ:


They shall enter into the rock in order to flee with a flight, and to be hidden in the dust, for fear of the Lord, and for the brightness of His glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:11)


עֵינֵי רָמוּת אֱנָשָׁא יִמְאֲכָן וְיֶחֱלַשׁ תְּקוֹף גַבְרִין וּתְקֵיף יְיָ בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא:


The lofty looks of the common man shall be humbled, and the strength of the great men shall become weak, and the Lord alone shall be mighty in that time.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:12)


אֲרֵי יוֹמָא דַעֲתִיד לְמֵיתֵי מִן קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת עַל כָּל גֵיוָתָנַיָא וּרְמֵי לִבָּא וְעַל כָּל תַּקִיפַיָא וְיִמְאֲכוּן:


For the day shall come from the Lord of hosts upon all the proud, and upon those who are highminded, and upon all the mighty, yea, they shall be humbled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:13)


וְעַל כָּל רַבְרְבָנֵי עַמְמַיָא תַּקִיפַיָא וַחֲסִינַיָא וְעַל כָּל טוּרְנֵי מְדִינָתָא:


And upon all the kings of the nations, the strong and the mighty; and upon all the princes of the provinces;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:14)


וְעַל כָּל טוּרַיָא רָמַיָא וְעַל כָּל רָמְתָא מְנַטְלָתָא:


And upon all the high mountains, and upon all the hills that are lifted up;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:15)


וְעַל כָל דְיָתְבִין בְּמִגְדָל רָם וְעַל כָּל דְשָׁרָן בְּשׁוּר כָּרִיךְ:


And upon all that dwell in a high tower; and upon all who dwell within fortified walls;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:16)


וְעַל כָּל דְיָתְבִין בְּנִיסֵי יַמָא וְעַל כָּל דְשָׁרָן בְּבִירַנְיָתָא שַׁפִּירָן:


And upon all that dwell in the islands of the sea; and upon all that dwell in beautiful palaces.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:17)


וְיִמְאַךְ רָמוּת אֱנָשָׁא וְיֶחֱלַשׁ תְּקוֹף גַבְרִין וְתַקִיף יְיָ בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא:


The pride of the common man shall be humbled, and the strength of the great men shall become weak, and the Lord alone shall be mighty in that time.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:18)


וְטַעֲוָתָא גְמִירָא יְסוּפוּן:


And the idols shall utterly come to an end.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:19)


וְיַעֲלוּן בִּמְעָרַת טִנָרַיָא וּבִמְחִילֵי עַפְרָא מִן קֳדָם דְחַלְתָּא דַייָ וּמִזִיו יְקָרֵיהּ בְּאִתְגַלְיוּתֵי לְמִתְבַר רַשִׁיעֵי אַרְעָא:


And they shall go into the caves of the rocks, and into the caverns of the earth, for the fear of the Lord, and for the brightness of His glory, when He shall be revealed to destroy the wicked of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:20)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יְרַחֲקוּן בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא יַת טַעֲוַת כַּסְפֵּיהוֹן וְיַת טַעֲוַת דְהַבְהוֹן דַעֲבָדוּ לְהוֹן לְמִסְגֵיד לְטַעֲוָתָא וּלְצַלְמָנַיָא:


At that time the sons of men shall abominate the idols of their silver, and the idols of their gold, which they have made for themselves to prostrate themselves to idols and to images.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:21)


לְמֵיעַל בִמְעָרַת טִנָרַיָא וּבִשְׁקִיפֵי כֵיפָא מִן קֳדָם דַחַלְתָּא דַייָ וּמִזִיו יְקָרֵיהּ בְּאִתְגַלְיוּתֵיהּ לְמִתְבַּר רַשִׁיעֵי אַרְעָא:


To go into the caves of the rocks, and into the holes of the rocks, for the fear of the Lord and for the brightness of His glory, when He shall be revealed to destroy the wicked of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 2:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/2:22)


אִתְמְנָעוּ לְכוֹן מִלְאִשְׁתַּעְבָּדָא לְאֱנָשָׁא בְּמֶעְבְּדֵיהּ דַחֲלָא דְנִשְׁמַת רוּחַ חַיִין בְּאַפּוֹהִי אֲרֵי יוֹמָא דֵין הוּא קַיָם וּמְחַר לֵיתוֹהִי וְכִלְמָא חֲשִׁיב הוּא:


Cease ye to make yourselves servile to man, because he is working terror; the breath of the spirit of life is in his nostrils, for to-day he is alive, but to-morrow he is not, and he is counted as nothing.



## Chapter 3

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:1)


אֲרֵי הָא רִבּוֹן עַלְמָא יְיָ צְבָאוֹת מַעְדֵי מִירוּשְׁלֵם וּמִיהוּדָה סְמַךְ וּסְעֵיד כָּל סְמַךְ מֵיכַל וְכָל סְעֵיד מִשְׁתֵּי:


For, behold, the Lord of the world, the Lord of hosts, doth take away from Jerusalem and from Judah the stay and the staff, the whole stay of food, and the whole stay of water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:2)


גִבָּר וּגְבַר עֲבֵיד קְרָב דַיַין וְסָפַר וּמִשְׁתָּאֵל וְסָב:


The mighty man, and the man that maketh war, the judge, and the prophet, and the scribe, and the diviner, and the ancient,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:3)


רַב חַמְשִׁין וּנְסֵב אַפִּין וּמָלֵיךְ מֵילַךְ וַאֲמַר וּמִשְׁתְּמַע וְחַכִּים וְסוּכְלְתָן בְּעֵצָה:


The captain of fifty, and the honourable man, and the counsellor, and he that commands, and he that obeyeth, and the wise in counsel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:4)


וַאֲמַנֵי יַנְקַיָא פַּרְנָסֵיהוֹן וְחַלָשַׁיָא יִשְׁלְטוּן בְּהוֹן:


And I will appoint sucklings to be their governors, and the weak shall rule over them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:5)


וְיִתְגָרוּן עַמָא גְבַר בִּגְבַר וּגְבַר בְּחַבְרֵיהּ יִשְׁלְטוּן עוּלֵימַיָא בְּסָבָא וּדְשַׁיִט בִּדְיַקִיר:


The nations shall wage war, man against man, and a man against his neighbour; children shall rule over the ancient, and the base over the honourable.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:6)


אֲרֵי יֵחוֹד גְבַר בַּאֲחוֹהִי מִזַרְעֲיַת בֵּית אֲבוּהִי לְמֵימַר כְּסוּ אִית לָךְ רַב תְּהֵא עֲלָנָא וּמַגְבִּיתָא הָדָא תְּהֵא תְּחוֹת יְדָךְ:


For a man shall take hold of his brother, of the family of his father's house, saying, Thou hast clothing, be thou a ruler over us, and this ruin shall be under thy government.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:7)


יְתִיב בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא לְמֵימָר לֵית אֲנָא כָשַׁר לְמֶהֱוֶי רֵישׁ וּבְבֵיתִי לֵית מָה דְאֵיכוּל וְלֵית מָה דְאִתְכַּסֵי לָא תְמַנוּנִי רַב בְּרֵישׁ כָּל עַמָא:


He shall answer in that time, saying, I am not a proper person to become a chief, and in my house I have nothing that I could eat, and nothing wherewith I could clothe myself; appoint me not a chief over all this people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:8)


אֲרֵי אִתְקִילוּ יַתְבֵי יְרוּשְׁלֵם וֶאֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה גָלוּ אֲרֵי מַמְלֵל פּוּמְהוֹן וַאֲגַר עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן גְלַן קֳדָם יְיָ וּמַרְגְזִין קֳדָם יְקָרֵיהּ:


Because the inhabitants of Jerusalem have stumbled, therefore the men of Judah shall go into captivity; surely the speech of their mouth and the wages of their works are revealed before the Lord, and they have provoked to anger His glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:9)


אִשְׁתְּמוֹדָעוּת אַפֵּיהוֹן בְּדִינָא אַסְהֵיד בְּהוֹן וַחֲטָאֵיהוֹן כִּסְדוֹמָאֵי קָבְלִין וְלָא מָנְעִין וַי לְנַפְשֵׁיהוֹן אֲרֵי גְרַמוּ דְתֵיתֵי לְהוֹן בִּישָׁא:


The shew of their faces in judgment bears testimony against them, and they declare their sins as the Sodomites, and they restrain not. Woe unto them! for they have caused that evil should come upon them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:10)


אֱמָרוּ לְצַדִיקָא טוֹבֵיכוֹן אֲרֵי פֵרַי עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן יִשְׁתַּלְמוּן:


Say ye to the righteous, that it shall be well with you, for the fruits of their work shall be recompensed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:11)


וַי לְרַשִׁיעַיָא דְעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן בִּישִׁין אֲרֵי גֻמְלָא דִידֵיהוֹן יִתְּתֵב לְהוֹן:


Woe to the wicked, whose works are evil; because the reward of their hands shall be returned to them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:12)


עַמִי דְסִרְכוֹהִי בַזְזְהוֹי כִמְעַלְלֵי לְכַרְמָא וּכְמָרֵי חוֹבָא שַׁלִיטוּ בֵּיהּ עַמִי מְשַׁבְּחָךְ אַטְעֲיוּךְ וְאוֹרַח שְׁבִילָךְ קִלְקִילוּ:


As for my people, their oppressors plunder them, as those who glean the vineyard; and as usurers they rule over them. 0 my people, those that call thee blessed cause thee to err; and the way of thy path they have perverted.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:13)


עֲתִיד לְמֵדָן יְיָ וּמִתְגְלֵי לְמֶעֱבַּד פּוּרְעֲנוּת דִין מִן עַמְמַיָא:


The Lord is ready to judge, and to reveal Himself to take vengeance of judgment of the nations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:14)


יְיָ בְּדִינָא יֵעוֹל יַת סָבֵי עַמָא וְסַרְכוֹהִי וְאַתּוּן אֲנִסְתּוּן יַת עַמִי חֲטוֹף מַסְכְּנָא בְּבָתֵּיכוֹן:


The Lord will enter into judgment with the elders of His people, and with their oppressors; because ye have robbed my people, the spoil of the poor is in your houses.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:15)


מַה לְכוֹן דְאַתּוּן מְמַסְכְּנִין יַת עַמִי וְאַפֵּי חֲשִׁיכַיָא מְבַלִין אַתּוּן וּמַיְתִין בְּדִינֵיהוֹן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת:


What mean ye, that ye impoverish my people and grind the faces of the poor? They are perishing in their contentions, saith the Lord, the God of hosts.'


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:16)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ חֲלַף דְאִתְרַבְרְבָא בְנָת צִיוֹן וְאָזְלָן מְרִימָן צַוַאר וּמְסַרְבְּקָן עַיְנִין אָזְלָן וּבְפָּתְהָן מַקְפָן וּבְרַגְלֵהֶן מַרְגְזָן:


And the Lord said, Because the daughters of Zion are haughty, and they are walking with stretched-forth necks, and they walk with their eyes painted, with their hair rolled up, and with their feet they excite lust.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:17)


וִישַׁעְבֵּיד יְיָ יִקְרַת בְּנָת צִיוֹן וַיְיָ יְקָרְהָן יַעֲדִי:


And the Lord shall make servile the glory of the daughters of Zion, and the Lord shall remove their dignity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:18)


בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא יַעֲדִי יְיָ יַת תּוּשְׁבְּחַת דִמְסָנַיָא וּשְׁבִיסַיָא וְסִבְכַיָא:


In that day the Lord will take away the ornament of their sandals and their cauls and head-nets;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:19)


עַנְקַיָא וְשֵׁירֵי יְדַיָא וַחֲנִיסְנְסַיָא:


The necklaces, and the bracelets for the hands, and the long robes;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:20)


כְּלִילַיָא וְשֵׁירֵי רְגִלַיָא וְקִלְמִסְמְסַיָא וְקָדָשַׁיָא וַחֲלִיטָתָא:


The crowns, the bracelets for the feet, the crisping-pin, and the ear-rings, and the ornament;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:21)


עִזְקָתָא וְטִלְטוּלַיָא:


And the finger-rings, and the jewels;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:22)


כִּתּוּנַיָא וְשׁוֹשִׁיפַיָא לִבּוּרְנְקִיָא וּמַחְכַּיָא:


The tunics, and the cloaks, and the wimples, and the stays;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:23)


וּמַחְזְיָתָא וְקַרְטְסַיָא וְכִתָּרַיָא וּכְבֵינָתָא:


The mirrors, and the fine linen, and the crowns, and the veils.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:24)


וִיהֵי אֲתַר דַהֲוָאָה יָהֲבַן בּוּסְמִין יִתְמְסֵי אֲתַר דַהֲוָאָה אָסְרָן פְּסִיקַיָא יְהֵי רוּשְׁמִין דִמְחָא וַחֲלַף אַקָפּוּת פָּתָא גִזוּז רֵישׁ וַחֲלַף דַהֲוָאָה מְהַלְכִין בְּגֵיוָה יַסְרָן סַקִין דָא פּוּרְעֲנוּתָא תִּתְעֲבֵיד מִנְהוֹן אֲרֵי טְעָאָה בְשׁוּפְרְהוֹן:


It shall come to pass, that in the place where they were giving forth sweet smells, there shall be putrefaction; and in the place where they were binding the girdles, shall be marks of smiting; and instead of plaited locks, baldness; and instead of their walking in pride, they shall bind on sackcloth; this vengeance shall be taken on them because they have committed fornication in their beauty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:25)


שְׁפַר גִבָּרַיִךְ בְּחַרְבָא יִתְקַטְלוּן וְעָבְדֵי נִצְחָנֵיךְ בִּקְרָבָא:


Thy beautiful men shall be killed by the sword, and thy heroes in the war.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 3:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/3:26)


וְיִצְדוּן וִיסוּפוּן תַּרְעֵי קִרְוָהָא וְתִּתְרוֹקִין אַרְעָא וְתֶחְרוֹב:


And the gates of her city shall be laid desolate, and shall come to an end; and the land shall be laid waste, and shall be destroyed.



## Chapter 4

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:1)


וְיִתְקְפָן שְׁבַע נְשִׁין בְּגַבְרָא חַד בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא לְמֵימָר מִדִילָנָא נֵיכוּל וּמִידִילָנָא נִתְכְּסֵי לְחוֹד יִתְקְרֵי שְׁמָךְ עֲלָנָא כְּנוֹשׁ חִסוּדָנָא:


And in that time seven women shall take hold of one man, saying, Of that which is ours we will eat, and of that which is ours we will cover ourselves: only let us be called by thy name, to take away our reproach.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:2)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהוּא יְהֵי מְשִׁיחָא דַייָ לְחֶדְוָה וְלִיקָר וְעָבְדֵי אוֹרַיְתָא לִרְבוּ וּלְתוּשְׁבְּחָא לְשֵׁיזְבַת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


At that time shall the Messiah of the Lord be for joy and for glory to those that are escaped, and those that keep the law shall be for greatness and for praise.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:3)


וִיהֵי דְאִשְׁתָּאַר יְתוּב לְצִיוֹן וּדְעָבַד אוֹרַיְתָא יִתְקַיֵם בִּירוּשְׁלֵם קַדִישׁ יִתְאֲמַר לֵיהּ כָּל דִכְתִיב לְחַיֵי עַלְמָא יֶחֱזֵי בִּנְחָמַת יְרוּשְׁלֵם:


And it shall come to pass, that he that shall return to Zion, and he that is doing the law, shall be established in Jerusalem, he shall be called holy; every one that is written for eternal life shall see the consolation of Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:4)


אִם יַעֲדֵי יְיָ יַת תֶּגְאָלַת בְּנַת צִיוֹן וְיַת אַשְׁדֵי דַם זַכֵי דְבִירוּשְׁלֵם יִגְלֵי מִגַוָהּ בְּמֵימַר דִין וּבְמֵימַר גְמִירָא:


When the Lord shall have put away the filth of the daughters of Zion, and when He shall have removed from her midst those that are shedding the innocent blood, which is in Jerusalem, by the word of judgment, and by the word of consummation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:5)


וְיִבְרִי יְיָ עַל כָּל מִקְדַשׁ טוּרָא דְצִיוֹן וְעַל אֲתַר בֵּית שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ עֲנַן יְקַר יְהֵא מַטִיל עֲלוֹהִי בִימָמָא וְאַמִטְתָא וְזֵיהוֹר כְּאֵשָׁא שַׁלְהוֹבֵי בְּלֵילְיָא אֲרֵי יַתִּיר מִן יְקָרָא דַאֲמִיר לְאַיְתָאָה עֲלוֹהִי שְׁכִנְתָּא תְהֵי מְנַנָא עֲלוֹהִי בְּגִנוּן:


And the Lord will create upon every holy place of the mountain of Zion, and upon the place of the house of His Shekinah a cloud of glory; which shall be shadowing over it by day, and a thick cloud and a brightness as of flaming fire by night; because of the excellency of the glory which He has promised to bring upon it, the Shekinah shall be protecting it with a protection.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/4:6)


וְעַל יְרוּשָׁלֵם תְּהֵי מַטְלַת עֲנָנֵי לְאַטְלָלָה עֲלָהּ בִימָם מִשְׁרָב וּלְבֵית סֵתֶר וּלְאַגָנָא מִזַרְמִית וּמִמִטְרָא:


And over Jerusalem shall be a tabernacle of clouds, to overshadow it by day from the scorching heat, and for a place of refuge from storm and from rain.



## Chapter 5

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:1)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא אַשְׁבְחֵיהּ כְּעַן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל דִמְתִיל לְכַרְמָא זַרְעֵיהּ דְאַבְרָהָם רַחֲמֵי תּוּשְׁבְּחַת רַחֲמִי לְכַרְמֵי עַמִי חֲבִיבַי יִשְׂרָאֵל יְהָבִית לְהוֹן אַחֲסָנָא בְּטוּר רָם בְּאַרְעָא שְׁמֵינָא:


The prophet said, Now I will sing unto Israel, who is like unto a vineyard, the seed of Abraham, my beloved, a song of my beloved, concerning his vineyard. My people, my beloved Israel, I gave to them an inheritance in a high mountain, in a fat land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:2)


וְקַדֵשְׁתִּינוּן וְיַקַרְתִּינוּן וְקַיַמְתִּינוּן כְּמַצַב גֶפֶן בְּחִירָא וּבְנִיתָא מַקְדְשִׁי בֵינֵיהוֹן וְאַף מַדְבְּחִי יְהָבִית לְכַפָּרָא עַל חֲטָאֵיהוֹן וַאֲמַרִית דְיַעְבְּדוּן עוֹבָדִין טָבִין קֳדָמַי וְאִינוּן אַבְאִישׁוּ עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן:


I sanctified them, and I made them glorious, I propped them up as a precious vine; and I built my sanctuary in the midst of them; and I gave also mine altar to make an atonement for their sins; and I thought that they should do good works before me, but they did evil works.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:3)


נְבִיָא אֲמַר לְהוֹן הָא בֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל מְרָדוּ מִן אוֹרַיְתָא וְלָא צָבָן לְמֵיתַב כְּעַן יַתְבֵי יְרוּשְׁלֵם וֶאֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה דִינוּ כְעַן דִינָא קֳדָמַי מִן עַמִי:


The prophet said unto them: They have revolted from the law, and are not willing to return. Now, 0 inhabitants of Jerusalem, and men of Judah, judge now a judgment between me and my people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:4)


מָה טָבָא אֲמָרִית לְמֶעְבַּד עוֹד לְעַמִי וְלָא עֲבָדִית לְהוֹן מַה דֵין אֲמָרִית דְיַעְבְּדוּן עוֹבָדִין טָבִין וְאִינוּן אַבְאִישׁוּ עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן:


What more good could I have thought to do to them, which I have not done to them? And what is this? I have thought that they should do good; but they have done evil.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:5)


וּכְעַן אֲחַוֵי כְעַן לְכוֹן יַת דַאֲנָא עֲתִיד לְמֶעְבַּד לְעַמִי אֲסַלֵק שְׁכִנְתִּי מִנְהוֹן וִיהוֹן לְמִבַּז אֶתְרַע בֵּית מַקְדַשְׁהוֹן וִיהוֹן לְדָיִשׁ:


And now, now I will tell you what I will do to my people; I will cause my Shekinah to remove from them, and they shall be for a spoil; I will break down the house of their sanctuary, and they shall be for a treading down.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:6)


וַאֲשַׁוִינוּן רְטִישִׁין לָא אִסְתַּעֲרוּן וְלָא יִסְתַּמָכוּן וִיהוֹן מְטַלְטְלִין וּשְׁבִיקִין וְעַל נְבִיַיָא אֲפַקֵיד דְלָא יִתְנַבּוּן עֲלֵיהוֹן נְבוּאָה:


I will make them abandoned ones; they shall not be visited, neither shall they be supported; and they shall be cast out and forsaken. I will command the prophets that they do not prophesy to them prophecy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:7)


אֲרֵי עַמָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל וֶאֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה נִצְבָא דְחֶדְוָתֵיהּ וַאֲמָרֵית דְיַעְבְּדוּן דִינָא וְהָא אִינוּן אֲנוּסִין אֲמָרִית דְיַעְבְּדוּן זָכוּ וְהָא אִינוּן מַסְגַן חוֹבִין:


For the people of the Lord of hosts is the house of Israel, and the men of Judah his pleasant plant. And I thought that they should do judgment, but, behold, they are oppressors; I thought that they should do righteousness, but, behold, they are multiplying sins.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:8)


וַי דְמַקְפִין בֵּיתָא עַל בֵּיתָא חֲקַל אוּנְסָא בַּחֲקַלְתְּהוֹן מַקְרְבִין אָמְרִין עַד דְנַחְסִין כָּל אֲתַר וּמְדַמָן דְיָתְבִין בִּלְחוֹדֵיהוֹן בְּגַוָה דְאַרְעָא:


Woe unto them that join house to house; the field of violence they bring nigh to their fields, saying, Until we possess every place; and they think they shall dwell alone in the midst of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:9)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא בְּאוּדְנַי הֲוֵיתִי שָׁמַע כַּד אִתְגְזָרֵית דָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת אִם לָא בָּתִּין סַגִיאִין לְצָדוּ יְהוֹן רַבְרְבִין וְטָבִין מִבְּלִי יָתֵיב:


The prophet said, With mine ears I have heard when this was decreed from before the Lord of hosts, Of a truth many houses shall be desolate, even the great and the fair, without inhabitant.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:10)


אֲרֵי בְחוֹבָא דְלָא יְהָבוּ מַעֲשְׂרַיָא בֵּית עֲסַר אַשְׁכְּרִין דִכְרַם יַעְבְּדוּן בֵּיתָא חֲדָא וּבֵית כּוֹר זֶרַע יַעְבֵּיד תְּלַת סָאִין:


Because of the sin of not giving tithes, the place of ten acres of vineyard shall produce one bath; and the place where a cor of seed was sown shall produce three measures.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:11)


וַי דִמְקַדְמִין בְּצַפְרָא לְמִשְׁתֵּי חֲמַר עַתִּיק רָדְפִין מְאַחְרִין לְמִפְטַר מְרַמְשִׁין עַל שִׁוְיֵהוֹן חֲמַר אוּנְסִין מַדְלֵיק לְהוֹן:


Woe unto them that rise up early in the morning, chasing after drink of old wine, delaying to separate; tarrying upon their couches till the dawn of the morning. The wine of oppression inflames them!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:12)


וַהֲוֵי עַל יְדֵי כִנוֹר וָנֵבֶל קַתְרוֹס אַבּוּבָא וְחַמְרָא מִשְׁתֵּיהוֹן וּבְאוֹרַיְתָא דַייָ לָא אִסְתַּכָּלוּ וְעוֹבֵד גְבוּרְתֵּיהּ לָא חֲזוֹ:


And in their feasts are the harp, and the viol, the cithara, the organ, and the wine: and the law of the Lord they regard not, neither consider the work of His power.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:13)


בְּכֵן גָלוּ עַמִי מִבְּלִי דַעְתָּא וְיַקִירֵהוֹן מִיתוּ בְכַפְנָא וְסִגְוְהוֹן בִּבְצוֹרָתָא בְצָחוּתָא:


Therefore my people are gone into captivity unawares, and their honourable men have died through famine, and their multitude through dryness and thirst.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:14)


בְּכֵן אַפְתִיאַת שְׁאוֹל נַפְשָׁהּ וּפְתָחַת פּוּמָהּ וְלֵית סוֹף וּנְחָתוּ יְקָרְהוֹן וְסִגְוְהוֹן וְאִתְרְגוּשְׁתְּהוֹן וְדִתְקוֹף בְּהוֹן:


Therefore hell hath enlarged herself, and opened her mouth without measure, and their nobles, and their multitudes, and their tumultuous assemblies, and he that is strong among them, shall descend into it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:15)


וְיִמְאַךְ אֱנָשָׁא וְיֶחְלַשׁ תְּקוֹף גַבְרִין וְעֵינֵי רְמַיָא יִמְאֲכוּן:


And the mean man shall be humbled, and the strength of the strong shall be weakened, and the eyes of the lofty shall be humbled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:16)


וּתְקֵיף יְיָ צְבָאוֹת בְּדִינָא וֵאלָהָא קַדִישָׁא קַדִישׁ בִּזְכוּתָא:


And the Lord of hosts shall be mighty in judgment, and the holy God shall be sanctified in righteousness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:17)


וְיִתְפַּרְנְסוּן צַדִיקַיָא כְּמָא דַאֲמִיר עֲלֵיהוֹן יִסְגוֹן וְנִכְסֵי רַשִׁיעַיָא צַדִיקַיָא יַחְסְנוּן:


And the righteous shall be fed, as it is promised concerning them, and they shall multiply; and the substance of the ungodly shall the righteous possess.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:18)


וַי דִמְשָׁרָן לְמֶחֱטֵי צַבְחַר נַגְדִין חוֹבִין בְּחַבְלֵי לְמָא אָזְלִין סָגָן עַד דְתַקִיפִין וְכִגְדִילַת עֶגְלְתָא חוֹבִין:


Woe to them that begin to sin by little, drawing sins with the ropes of vanity, and they go on and increase until that they are strong, and their sins are as a cart-rope.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:19)


דְאָמְרִין אֵימָתַי יוֹחֵי וִיגַלֵי פְּרִשְׁתֵּיהּ בְּדִיל דְנֶחֱזֵי וְיִקְרַב וְיֵיתֵי מַלְכֵי דְקַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְנֵדְעִינֵיהּ:


That say, When will He hasten and reveal His miracle, that we may see it: and let the counsel of the Holy One of Israel draw nigh and come, that we may know it!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:20)


וַי דְאָמְרִין לְרַשִׁיעַיָא דְמַצְלְחִין בְּעַלְמָא הָדֵין אַתּוּן טָבַיָא וּלְעִנְוְתָנַיָא אָמְרִין אַתּוּן רַשִׁיעַיָא הֲלָא בְּמֵיתֵי נְהוֹרָא לְצַדִיקַיָא יַחֲשׁוֹךְ לְרַשִׁיעַיָא וִיבַסְמוּן פִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא לְעָבְדֵיהוֹן וְיֵיתֵי מֵרְדָא לְרַשִׁיעַיָא וְיֵדְעוּן דִבְסוֹפָא מְרִיר חֶטְאָה לֶעָבְדֵיהּ:


Woe to them that say to the wicked who prosper in this world, Ye are good; and say to the meek, Ye are wicked. What, is it not so? When light cometh to the righteous, shall it not be dark with the wicked? And the words of the law shall be sweet to them that do them; but bitterness shall come to the wicked, and they shall know that in the end sin is bitter to them that commit it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:21)


וַי דְחַכִּימִין בְּעֵינֵי נַפְשְׁהוֹן וְקָבֵיל אַפֵּיהוֹן סוּכְלְתָנִין:


Woe unto them that are wise in their own eyes, and prudent in their own sight!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:22)


וַי דְגִבָּרִין לְמִשְׁתֵּי חֲמַר וְגִבָּרִין מָרֵי נִכְסִין לְאִתְרַוָאָה מִן עַתִּיק:


Woe unto them that are mighty to drink wine, and mighty lords of riches, to make themselves drunk with old wine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:23)


מְזַכָּן לֵיהּ לְחַיָבָא חֲלַף דִמְקַבְּלִין מִנֵיהּ מָמוֹן דִשְׁקַר וּזְכוּת זַכַּיָא בִרְשַׁע מַעֲדָן מִנְהוֹן:


Who justify the guilty in order to receive from him the mammon of falsehood, and wickedly take away the righteousness of the righteous from him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:24)


בְּכֵן יִתְאַכְּלוּן כְּקַשָׁא בְּאֶשְׁתָּא וְכַעֲמִירָא בְּשַׁלְהוֹבִיתָא מַסְגַן תּוּקְפְהוֹן כִּשְׁבַז יְהֵי וּמָמוֹן אוּנְסֵיהוֹן כְּאַבְקָא דְפָרַח אֲרֵי קָצוּ בְּאוֹרַיְתָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת וְיַת מֵימַר קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל רְחִיקוּ:


Therefore they shall be devoured as stubble in the fire, and like dry hay in the flame; they are multiplying their strength, it shall be like an ulcer, and the money of their oppression, as the dust that flieth away: because they despised the law of the Lord of hosts, and rejected the Word, the Holy One of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:25)


עַל כֵּן תְּקֵיף רוּגְזָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת בְּעַמֵיהּ וַאֲרִים מְחַת גְבוּרְתֵּיהּ עֲלֵיהוֹן כַּד מְחָנוּן וְזָעוּ טוּרַיָא וַהֲוָאָה נְבֶלְתְּהוֹן מְשַׁגְרָן כְּסָחִיתָא בְּגוֹ שׁוּקַיָא בְכָל דָא לָא תָבוּ מֵחוֹבֵיהוֹן דִיתוּב רוּגְזֵיהּ מִנְהוֹן וְעַד כְּעַן מַתְקְפִין מַרְדֵיהוֹן וְעוֹד מַחֲתֵיהּ עֲתִידָא לְאִתְפְּרַע מִנְהוֹן:


Therefore is the anger of the Lord of hosts mighty against His people, and He has lifted the stroke of His power upon them. When He smote them, then the mountains moved, and their carcasses were cast out as dung into the midst of the streets. By all this they turn not away from their sins, that His fury may turn away from them; but until now their rebellion groweth stronger, and His stroke is again to take vengeance on them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:26)


וְיִזְקוֹף אָת לְעַמְמַיָא מֵרָחִיק וְיַכְלֵי לֵיהּ מִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא וְהָא בִפְרִיעַ מְלַךְ בְּמַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ כַּעֲנָנִין קַלִילִין יֵיתֵי:


And He will lift up an ensign to the nations from far, and he will call him from the end of the earth: and, behold, a king with his army shall come swiftly, as light clouds.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:27)


לֵית דִמְשַׁלְהֵי וְלֵית דְמִתְקֵיל בֵּיהּ לָא יְנוּם וְלָא יִדְמוּךְ וְלָא יִשְׁתְּרֵי זְרִיז חַרְצֵיהּ וְלָא תִתְפְּסֵיק עַרְקַת מְסָנֵיהּ:


None shall be weary nor stumble among them; none shall slumber nor sleep; neither shall the girdle of their loins be loosed, nor the latchet of their sandals be broken.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:28)


דִי גִירוֹהִי מְחַרְפִין וְכָל קַשְׁתָּתֵיהּ מְתִיחָן פַּרְסַת סוּסְוָתֵיהּ כְּטָנָרָא תַקִיפִין וְגַלְגְלוֹהִי קַלִילִין הָא כְעַלְעוֹלָא:


Whose arrows are sharp, and their bows bent, their horses' hoofs strong like flint, and their wheels swift, behold, like a whirlwind.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:29)


נִיהוֹמֵי לֵיהּ כַּאַרְיָא וְיִנְהוֹם כְּבַר אַרְיְוָן וְיַכְלֵי וְיֵחוֹד בִּזָא וִישֵׁיזֵיב וְלֵית דְמַצִיל:


His roaring shall be like a lion, and he shall roar like a young lion, yea, he shall roar, and lay hold of the prey, and shall carry it away safe, and none shall deliver it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 5:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/5:30)


וְיַכְלֵי עֲלוֹהִי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא כְּאִתְרְגוּשַׁת יַמָא דְאִם יִבְעוֹן רַשִׁיעַיָא סָעֵיד מִיַתְבֵי אַרְעָא עָקָא וּתְבַר אַיְתֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן בְּרַם צַדִיקַיָא דִיהוֹן בְּשַׁעְתָּא הַהִיא יִתְכְּסוּן מִן קֳדָם בִּישְׁתָּא:


And in that time he shall roar against them like the roaring of the sea: so that if the wicked should seek support from the inhabitants of the earth, he will bring upon them oppression and destruction; but the righteous which shall be in that hour shall be protected on account of the evil.



## Chapter 6

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:1)


בְּשַׁתָּא דְאִתְנַגַע בָּהּ מַלְכָּא עֻזִיָה אֲמַר נְבִיָא חֲזֵיתִי יַת יְקָרָא דַייָ שָׁרֵי עַל כּוּרְסֵיהּ רָם וּמִתְנַטֵל בִּשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא וּמִזִיו יְקָרֵיהּ אִתְמְלֵי הֵיכְלָא:


In the year in which King Uzziah was smitten with the leprosy the prophet said, I saw the glory of the Lord sitting upon His throne, high, and lifted up unto the highest heavens, and the temple was filled with the brightness of His glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:2)


שַׁמָשִׁין קַדִישִׁין בְּרוּמָא קֳדָמוֹהִי שִׁתָּא גַפִּין לְחַד שִׁתָּא גַפִּין לְחַד בִּתְרִין מְכַסֵי אַפּוֹהִי דְלָא חָזֵי וּבִתְרֵין מְכַסֵי גְוִיָתֵיהּ דְלָא מִתְחֲזֵי וּבִתְרֵין מְשַׁמֵשׁ:


Holy ministers on high stood before him: each one had six wings; with twain he covered his face, that it should not see; and with twain he covered his body, that it should not be seen; and with twain he was ministering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:3)


וּמְקַבְּלִין דֵין מִן דֵין וְאָמְרִין קַדִישׁ בִּשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא עִלָאָה בֵית שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ קַדִישׁ עַל אַרְעָא עוֹבַד גְבוּרְתֵּיהּ קַדִישׁ לְעָלַם וּלְעָלְמֵי עָלְמַיָא יְיָ צְבָאוֹת מַלְיָא כָל אַרְעָא זִיו יְקָרֵיהּ:


And one cried unto another, and they were saying, Holy in the highest and exalted heavens is the house of His Shekinah, holy upon the earth is the work of His might, holy for ever, world without end, is the Lord of hosts, the whole earth is full of the brightness of His glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:4)


וְזָעוּ אֵילְוַת סִפֵּי הֵיכְלָא מִקָל מִלוּלָא וּבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא אִתְמְלֵי אֲמִטְתָא:


And the posts of the threshold of the temple moved at the voice of him that cried, and the house of the sanctuary was filled with cloudy darkness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:5)


וַאֲמָרֵית וַי לִי אֲרֵי חָבֵית אֲרֵי גְבַר חַיָב לְאוֹכָחָא אֲנָא וּבְגוֹ עַמָא דְמִגְעַל בְּחוֹבִין אֲנָא יָתִיב אֲרֵי יַת יְקַר שְׁכִינַת מְלַךְ עַלְמַיָא יְיָ צְבָאוֹת חֲזָאָה עֵינָי:


Then said I, Woe is me, for I have sinned, for I am a guilty man to reprove, and I dwell in the midst of a people polluted with sin: for mine eyes have seen the glory of the Shekinah of the King of the worlds, the Lord of hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:6)


וְאִשְׁתְּדִי לְוָתִי חַד מִן שַׁמָשַׁיָא וּבְפוּמֵיהּ מַמְלֵל דְקַבֵּיל מִן קֳדָם דִשְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ עַל כּוּרְסֵי יְקָרָא בִּשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא עֵיל מִן מַדְבְּחָא:


Then flew one of the ministers unto me, and in his mouth was a word, which he received from the Shekinah of Him who sat upon the throne of glory in the highest heavens, above the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:7)


וְסַדַר בְּפוּמִי וַאֲמַר הָא שַׁוִיתִי פִּתְגַמֵי נְבוּאָתִי בְּפוּמָךְ וְיַעְדוּן חוֹבָךְ וְחֶטְאָךְ יִתְכַּפְּרוּן:


And he placed it in my mouth, and said, Behold, I have put the words of my prophecies in thy mouth, and thy iniquities are put away, and thy sins are expiated.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:8)


וּשְׁמָעִית יַת קַל מֵימְרָא דַייָ דַאֲמַר יַת מַן אֲשַׁלַח לְאִתְנַבָּאָה וּמַן יֵזֵיל לְאַלָפָא וַאֲמָרִית הָא אֲנָא שְׁלַח יָתִי:


And I heard the voice of the Word of the Lord, which said, Whom shall I send to prophesy? and who will go to teach? Then said I, Here am I, send me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:9)


וַאֲמַר אֱזֵיל וְתֵימָר לְעַמָא הָדֵין דְשָׁמְעִין מִשְׁמַע וְלָא מִסְתַּכְּלִין וַחֲזוֹ מֶחְזָא וְלָא יָדְעִין:


And He said, Go, and tell this people, who are diligently hearing, but understand not, and see diligently, but know not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:10)


טַפֵּישׁ לִבֵּיהּ דְעַמָא הָדֵין וְאוּדְנוֹהִי יַקֵר וְעֵינוֹהִי עַמְעָם דִלְמָא יֶחֱזוּן בְּעֵינֵיהוֹן וּבְאוּדְנֵיהוֹן יִשְׁמְעוּן וּבְלִבְּהוֹן יִסְתַּכְּלוּן וִיתוּבוּן וְיִשְׁתְּבֵק לְהוֹן:


Make the heart of this people fat, and make their ears heavy, and darken their eyes; lest they see with their eyes, and hear with their ears, and understand with their heart, and repent, and it shall be forgiven them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:11)


וַאֲמָרִית עַד אֵימָתַי יְיָ וַאֲמַר עַד דְיִצְדוּן קִרְוַיָא מִבְּלִי יָתֵיב וּבָתַּיָא מִבְּלִי אֱנָשָׁא וְאַרְעָא תֶּחֱרוּב וְתִצְדֵי:


Then said I, Lord, how long? And He answered, Until the cities be wasted without an inhabitant, and the houses without man, and the land be laid desolate and wasted.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:12)


וְיַרְחַק יְיָ יַת בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא וְתִסְגֵי צַדְיוּתָא בְּגַוָהּ דְאַרְעָא:


And the Lord have removed the children of men far away, and the desolation be great in the midst of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/6:13)


וְיִשְׁתַּאֲרוּן בָּהּ חַד מִן עַסְרָא צַדִיקַיָא וִיתוּבוּן וִיהוֹן לִצְרָבָא כְּבוּטְמָא וְכִבְלוֹטָא דִבְמִתַר טַרְפּוֹהִי דָמִין לְיַבְשִׁין וְעַד כְּעַן אִינוּן רַטִיבָן לְקַיָמָא מִנְהוֹן זַרְעָא כֵּן גָלוּתָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יִתְכַּנְשׁוּן וִיתוּבוּן לְאַרְעֲהוֹן אֲרֵי זַרְעָא דְקוּדְשָׁא נִצְבַתְהוֹן:


And there shall be left in it righteous men, one out of ten: they shall return, and they shall be for poverty, as the terebynth and the oak when their leaves fall, they are like to dry trees, nevertheless, they are moist to raise up seed from them; thus the captivity of Israel shall be gathered, and shall return to their land, for the seed which is holy is their plantation.



## Chapter 7

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:1)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמֵי אָחָז בַּר יוֹתָם בַּר עֻזִיָה מְלַךְ שִׁבְטָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה סְלִיק רְצִין מַלְכָּא דַאֲרָם וּפֶקַח בַּר רְמַלְיָהוּ דְיִשְׂרָאֵל לִירוּשְׁלֵם לְאַגָחָא קְרָבָא עֲלָהּ וְלָא יָכִיל לְאַגָחָא עֲלָהּ:


And it came to pass in the days of Ahaz the son of Jotham, the son of Uzziah, the king of the tribe of the house of Judah, that Rezin the king of Syria, and Pekah the son of Remaliah, king of Israel, went up toward Jerusalem to wage war against it, but he was not able to fight against it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:2)


וְאִתְחַוָא לְבֵית דָוִד לְמֵימָר אִתְחַבַּר מַלְכָּא דַאֲרָם עִם מַלְכָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵיתֵי עֲלוֹהִי וְזָע לִבֵּיהּ וְלִבָּא דְעַמֵיהּ כְּאִשְׁתְּדָיוּת אִילָנֵי חוּרְשָׁא מִן קֳדָם רוּחָא:


And it was told the house of David, saying, Syria is joined with the king of Israel to come upon him. And his heart and the heart of his people moved as the moving of the trees of the wood with the wind.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:3)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִישַׁעְיָה פּוּק כְּעַן לִקֳדָמוּת אָחָז אַתְּ וּשְׁאָר תַּלְמִידָךְ דְלָא חֲטוֹ וּדְתָבוּ מֵחֶטְאָה לִסְיָפֵי מְזִיקַת בְּרֵיכָתָא עִלֵיתָא דִי בְּכֵבַשׁ חֲקַל מַשְׁטַח קְצִירַיָא:


Then said the Lord unto Isaiah, Go forth now to meet Ahaz, thou and the rest of thy disciples, who have not sinned, and who are turned away from sin, at the end of the conduit of the upper pool, which is by the way of the field of the spreading of the fullers,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:4)


וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ אִסְתַּמַר וְנוּחַ לָא תִדְחַל וְלִבָּךְ לָא יָזוּעַ מִן קֳדָם תְּרֵין רַבְרְבַיָא דְאִינוּן כְּאוּדַיָא מַתְנְנַיָא הָאִלֵין בִּתְקוֹף רְגַז רְצִין וַאֲרָם וּבַר רְמַלְיָהוּ:


And say unto him, Take heed, and be quiet; fear not, and let not thy heart be moved for the two kings, which are as smoking firebrands, for the strength of the anger of Rezin with Syria, and of the son of Remaliah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:5)


חֲלַף אֲרֵי מְלַךְ עֲלָךְ אֲרָם בִּישָׁא אֶפְרַיִם וּבַר רְמַלְיָהוּ לְמֵימָר:


Because Syria, Ephraim, and the son of Remaliah, have taken evil counsel against thee, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:6)


נִסַק בְּאַרְעָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה וּנְחַבְּרִינוּן וּנְשַׁוִינוּן עִמָנָא וְנַמְלִיךְ מַלְכָּא בְּגַוָהּ יַת מַן דְכָשַׁר לָנָא:


We will go up into the land of the house of Judah, and let us be united together, and make them profitable unto us. We will make a king in it whom we shall think proper.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:7)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים לָא תְקוּם וְלָא תֶהֱוֵי:


Thus saith the Lord God, It shall not stand, neither shall it come to pass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:8)


אֲרֵי רֵישׁ אֲרָם דַמֶשֶׂק וְרֵישׁ דַמֶשֶׂק רְצִין וּבְסוֹף שִׁתִּין וַחֲמֵשׁ שְׁנִין יִבְטְלוּן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמָלְכוּ:


For the head of Syria is Damascus, and the head of Damascus is Rezin, and at the end of threescore and five years the house of Israel shall cease from being a kingdom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:9)


וְרֵישׁ אֶפְרַיִם שֹׁמְרוֹן וְרֵישׁ שֹׁמְרוֹן בַּר רְמַלְיָה אִם לָא תְהֵימְנוּן בְּמִלֵי נְבִיָא אֲרֵי לָא תִתְקַיְמוּן:


And the head of Ephraim is Samaria, and the head of Samaria is the son of Remaliah. If ye will not believe the words of the prophet, surely ye shall not be established,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:10)


וְאוֹסִיף נְבִיָא דַייָ לְמַלָלָא עִם אָחָז לְמֵימָר:


Moreover the prophet of the Lord spake again unto Ahaz, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:11)


שְׁאַל לָךְ אָתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ שְׁאַל דִי יִתְעֲבֵיד לָךְ נֵס עַל אַרְעָא אוֹ תִתְחֲזֵי לָךְ אָת בִּשְׁמַיָא:


Ask thee a sign from before the presence of the Lord, that a miracle may be done for thee upon the earth, or that a sign may be shewn thee in heaven.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:12)


וַאֲמַר אָחָז לָא אֶשְׁאַל וְלָא אֲנַסֵי קֳדָם יְיָ:


But Ahaz said, I will not ask, neither will I tempt the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:13)


וַאֲמַר שְׁמָעוּ כְעַן בֵּית דָוִד הַזְעֵר לְכוֹן דְאַתּוּן מְהַלָן יַת נְבִייָא אֲרֵי תְהַלוּן אַף יַת פִּתְגָמֵי אֱלָהָי:


And he said, Hear ye now, O house of David; is it a small thing to you that ye are troublesome to the prophets, that ye refuse also the words of my God?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:14)


בְּכֵן יִתֵּן יְיָ הוּא לְכוֹן אָתָא הָא עוּלֶמְתָּא מְעַדְיָא וּתְלִיד בַּר וְתִקְרֵי שְׁמֵיהּ עִמָנוּאֵל:


Therefore the Lord Himself shall give you a sign; Behold, a young woman shall conceive, and bear a son, and she shall call His name Immanuel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:15)


שְׁמַן וּדְבַשׁ יֵכוּל עַד לָא יְדַע עוּלֵימָא לְרָחְקָא בִישָׁא וּלְקָרָבָא טָבָא:


Butter and honey a child eats before he knows to reject the evil and to choose the good;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:16)


אֲרֵי עַד לָא יְדַע עוּלֵימָא לְרָחָקָא בִישָׁא וּלְקָרָבָא טָבָא תִּתְרְטִישׁ אַרְעָא דִי אַתְּ מְעִיק מִן קֳדָם תְּרֵין מַלְכָּהָא:


But before a child shall know to reject the evil and to choose the good, the land shall be laid desolate, on account of which thou art in a strait, because of these two kings.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:17)


יֵיתֵי יְיָ עֲלָךְ וְעַל עַמָךְ וְעַל בֵּית אֲבוּךְ יוֹמִין דְלָא אֲתוֹ לְמִיוֹמָא דְאִתְפְּלִיגוּ בֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל דְבֵית יְהוּדָה יַת מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר:


The Lord shall bring upon thee, and upon thy people, and upon thy father's house, days that have not come from the day of the separation of the house of Ephraim from the house of Judah; even the king of Assyria.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:18)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יַכְלֵי יְיָ לְעַם קִטְרֵי מַשִׁרְיַת גִבָּרַיָא דְסַגִיאִין כִּדְבוּבַיָא וְיַיְתִינוּן מִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וְלַעֲזִיזֵי מַשִׁרְיָתָא דְאִינוּן תַּקִיפִין כְּדַבַּרְיָתָא וְיַיְתִינוּן מִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא דְאַתּוּר:


And it shall come to pass at that time that the Lord shall call to a people, to bands of armies, mighty men, who are numerous as flies, and shall bring them from the ends of the land of Egypt; and to mighty armies, who are powerful as bees, and shall bring them from the uttermost parts of the land of Assyria.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:19)


וְיֵיתוּן וְיִשְׁרוּן כּוּלְהוֹן בִּרְחוֹבֵי קִרְוַיָא וּבִשְׁקִיפֵי כֵיפַיָא וּבְכֹל מַדְבְּרֵי נַעֲצוּצַיָא וּבְכֹל בָּתֵּי תוּשְׁבְּחָתָא:


And they shall all of them come and dwell in the streets of the cities, and in the clefts of the rocks, and in all deserts full of thorn-hedges, and in all houses of praise.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:20)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יִקְטוֹל יְיָ בְּהוֹן כְּמָא דְמִתְקְטֵיל בְּחַרְבָּא חֲרִיפָא בְּסִבְכַיָא וּבְמִגְרַיָא בְּעֶבְרֵי נַהֲרָא בְּמַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר וְיַת מַלְכָּא וְיַת מַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ וְאַף יַת שִׁלְטוֹנֵי כַּחֲדָא יְשֵׁיצֵי:


In that time the Lord shall slay them as one is slain by a sharp sword, by clubs, and by saws, by those beyond the river, and by the king of Assyria; the king and his army, and even also his rulers, together shall he destroy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:21)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יְקַיֵם גְבַר עִגְלַת תּוֹרִין וּתְרֵין עָן:


And it shall come to pass in that time, that a man shall nourish a young cow and two sheep.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:22)


וִיהֵי מִסְגֵי טוּב מְשַׁח יֵכוֹל שְׁמַן אֲרֵי שְׁמַן וּדְבַשׁ יִתְפַּרְנְסוּן כָּל צַדִיקַיָא דְאִשְׁתָּאֲרוּן בְּגוֹ אַרְעָא:


And it shall come to pass on account of the abundance of the goodness of fatness, one shall eat oil; because with oil and honey shall all the righteous be fed, who are left in the midst of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:23)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יְהֵי כָל אֲתַר דִיהוֹן תַּמָן אֶלֶף גוּפְנִין שַׁוְיָן בְּאֶלֶף מָנִין כְּסַף לְהוֹבָאֵי וּבוּר יְהֵי:


And it shall come to pass in that time, that every place shall be, where there were a thousand vines for a thousand silverlings, it shall even be for briers and thorns.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:24)


בְּגִירִין וּבְקַשְׁתָּן יַהֲכוּן לְתַמָן הֲרֵי הוֹבָאִי וּבוּר תְּהֵי כָל אַרְעָא:


With arrows and with bows shall men come thither; because all the land shall become briers and thorns.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/7:25)


וְּכָל טוּרַיָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה דִי בְמַעְדֵר יִתְפַּלְחוּן לָא תֵיעוּל לְתַמָן דַחֲלַת הוֹבָאִי וּבוּר וִיהֵי לְבֵית אַרְבָּעָה בַקְרָן דְתוֹרִין וּלְבֵית מַשְׁרֵי עֶדְרִין דְעָן:


And all the hills of the house of Judah, which shall be digged with the mattock, there shall not come thither the fear of briers and thorns: but it shall be for a place of crouching down of oxen, and for a place of dwelling of flocks and of sheep.



## Chapter 8

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִי סַב לָךְ לוּחַ רַב וּכְתוֹב עֲלוֹהִי כְּתַב מְפָרַשׁ מוֹחִי לְמִבַּז בִּזָא וּלְמֶעְדֵי עֲדָאָה:


And the Lord said unto me, Take thee a great tablet, and write upon it a clear writing, Hasten to seize the prey, and to take away the spoil.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:2)


וְאַסְהֵיד קֳדָמַי סְהִידִין מְהֵמְנָן יַת לְוָטַיָא דַאֲמָרִית לְאַיְתָאָה בִּנְבוּאַת אוּרִיָה כַהֲנָא וְהָא אֲתוֹ אַף כֵּן כָּל נְחָמָתָא דַאֲמָרֵית לְאַיְתָאָה בִּנְבוּאַת זְכַרְיָה בַּר יְבָרֶכְיָה אֲנָא עֲתִיד לְאַיְתָאָה:
