And call to witness before me as faithful witnesses, the curses, which I have threatened to bring by the prophecy of Uriah the priest, and, behold, they have come. And also the consolations, which I promised to bring by the prophecy of Zechariah the son of Jeberechiah, I am about to bring.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:3)


וַעֲלֵית לְוַת נְבִיאֲתָא וְעַדִיאַת וִילֵדַת בַּר וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִי קְרִי שְׁמֵיהּ מוֹחִי לְמִבַּז בִּזָא וּלְמֶעְדֵי עֲדָאָה:


And I went to the prophetess; and she conceived, and bare a son. Then said the Lord unto me, Call his name 'Hasten to seize the prey, and to take away the spoil.'


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:4)


אֲרֵי עַד לָא יְדַע עוּלֵימָא מִקְרֵי אַבָּא וְאִמָא יִשְׁבֵי יַת נִכְסֵי דַמֶשֶׂק וְיַת עֲדָאֵי שֹׁמְרוֹן קֳדָם מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר:


For before the child shall have knowledge to cry, My father, and my mother, the riches of Damascus and the spoil of Samaria shall be led into captivity before the king of Assyria.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:5)


וְאוֹסִיף מֵימְרָא דַייָ לְמַלָלָא עִמִי עוֹד לְמֵימָר:


And the Word of the Lord spake again to me, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:6)


חֲלַף דְקָץ עַמָא הָדֵין בְּמַלְכוּתָא דְבֵית דָוִד דִמְדַבֵּר לְהוֹן בְּנִיחַ כְּמֵי שִׁלוּחָא דְנַגְדִין בְּנִיחַ וְאִתְרְעִיאוּ בִרְצִין וּבַר רְמַלְיָה:


Forasmuch as this people refuseth the kingdom of the house of David, which guides them in quietness, like the waters of Shiloah, which flow softly, and delight themselves in Rezin and Remaliah's son;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:7)


בְּכֵן הָא יְיָ מַיְתֵי וּמַסִיק עֲלֵיהוֹן יַת מַשִׁרְיַת עַמְמַיָא דְסַגִיאִין כְּמֵי נַהֲרָא תַּקִיפַיָא וַחֲסִינַיָא יַת מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר וְיַת כָּל מַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ וְיִסַק עַל כָּל פַּצִידוֹהִי וִיהַךְ עַל כָּל כֵּיפוֹהִי:


Therefore, behold, the Lord is bringing, and is causing to ascend upon them, the army of the people, who are many, as the waters of a river, strong and mighty, the king of Assyria, and his army; and he shall come up over all his channels, and go over all his banks.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:8)


וְיַעְדֵי בְאַרְעָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה כְּנַחַל מְגַבֵּר עַד יְרוּשְׁלֵם יִמְטֵי וִיהֵי עַם מַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ מָלֵי פְתָאֵי אַרְעָךְ עִמָנוּ אֵל:


And he shall pass through the land of the house of Judah as an overflowing torrent, unto Jerusalem shall he come; and the people of his army shall fill the breadth of thy land, O Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:9)


אִתְחַבָּרוּ עַמְמַיָא וְאִתְבָּרוּ וְאָצִיתוּ כֹּל דִי בִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא אַתְקִיפוּ וְאִתְבָּרוּ אַתְקִיפוּ וְאִתְבָּרוּ:


Associate yourselves, O ye people, yet ye shall be broken; and give ear all who are at the ends of the earth, strengthen yourselves, yet ye shall be broken, strengthen yourselves, yet ye shall be broken.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:10)


אִתְמְלִיכוּ מְלַךְ וְיַעֲדֵי מַלִלוּ פִּתְגָמָא וְלָא יִתְקַיֵם אֲרֵי בְסַעֲדָנָא אֱלָהָא:


Take counsel together, yet it shall pass away; speak the word, and it shall not be established, because God is our help.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:11)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ לִי בְּמִתְקַף נְבוּאָתָא וְאֶלְפֵנִי מִלְמֵהַךְ בְּאוֹרַח עַמָא הָדֵין לְמֵימָר:


For the Lord spake thus to me by the power of prophecy, and instructed me that I should not walk in the way of this people, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:12)


לָא תֵימְרוּן מְרוֹד לְכָל דְיֵמַר עַמָא הָדֵין מְרוֹד וְלִדְחַלְתֵּיהּ לָא תִדְחֲלוּן וְעַל תּוּקְפֵיהּ לָא תֵמְרוּן תְּקוֹף:


Say ye not a rebellion to all that this people shall say a rebellion; neither fear ye their fear; and concerning their strength say ye not strength.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:13)


יַת יְיָ צְבָאוֹת יָתֵיהּ תֵּימְרוּן קַדִישׁ וְהוּא דְחַלְתְּכוֹן וְהוּא תוּקְפֵכוֹן:


The Lord of hosts Him shall ye call holy; and let Him be your fear, and let Him be your strength.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:14)


וְאִם לָא תְקַבְּלוּן וִיהֵי מֵימְרֵיהּ בְכוֹן לְפוּרְעָן וּלְאֶבֶן מָחֵי וּלְכֵיף מְתַקֵל לִתְרֵין בָּתֵּי רַבְרְבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לִתְבַר וּלְתַקָלָא עַל דְאִתְפְּלִיגוּ עַל דְבֵית יְהוּדָה דְיָתְבִין בִּירוּשְׁלֵם:


And if ye will not obey, His Word shall be amongst you for revenge, and for a stone of stumbling, and for a rock of offence to both of the houses of the princes of Israel, for destruction and for a snare, because they contend against those of the house of Judah, who are dwelling in Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:15)


וְיִתְקְלוּן בְּהוֹן סַגִיאִין וְיִפְּלוּן וְיִתַּבְּרוּן וְיִתַּצְדוּן וְיִתְאַחֲדוּן:


And many among them shall stumble, and fall, and be broken, and be snared, and be taken.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:16)


נְבִיָא טַר סַהֲדוּתָא לָא תַסְהִיד בְּהוֹן אֲרֵי לָא מְקַבְּלִין חֲתוֹם וְטַמַר אוֹרַיְתָא לָא צָבָן דְיֵלְפוּן בָהּ:


O prophet, keep the testimony, testify not the testimony among them, because they do not obey; seal and hide the law, they are not willing to learn it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:17)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא עַל דָא צַלֵיתִי קֳדָם יְיָ דַאֲמַר לְסַלְקָא שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ מִדְבֵית יַעֲקֹב וּבְעֵיתִי מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי:


The prophet said, On account of this I prayed before the Lord, who has said that he would take away His Shekinah from the house of Jacob, and I intreated before Him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:18)


הָא עַד דַאֲנָא קַיֵם וְעוּלֵימַיָא דִיהַב לִי יְיָ יִתְקַיְמוּן בָּנָא אָתִין וּמוֹפְתִין דַאֲמִירָן לְמֵיתֵי עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל דְאִם יֶחֱזוּן וִיתוּבוּן תִּבָטֵיל גְזֵירְתָּא דִי גְזָרָא עֲלֵיהוֹן דְיִגְלוּן מִלְאִתְחֲזָאָה קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת דִי שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ בְּטוּרָא דְצִיוֹן:


Behold, whilst I live with the children, which the Lord has given me, the signs and the wonders, which are promised to come upon Israel shall be established among us. But if they would see and repent, the decree should be annulled, which He decreed against them, that they should go into, captivity, and that they should not appear before the Lord of hosts, whose Shekinah is in the mountain of Zion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:19)


וַאֲרֵי יֵימְרוּן לְכוֹן עַמְמַיָא דְאַתּוּן בֵּינֵיהוֹן תְּבָעוּ מִן בִּדִין וּמִן זְכוּרוּ דִמְנַצְפִין וְדִמְנַהֲמִין הֲלָא כֵן אוֹרַח עַמְמַיָא פַלְחֵי טַעֲוָתָא עַמָא מִן טַעֲוָתֵיהּ תַּבְעִין חַיָא מִן מֵיתַיָא:


And when the nations amongst whom you shall be shall say to you, Seek of necromancers, and of the wizards, that peep, and that mutter. Is not this the way of nations, the worshippers of idols? A people seek from their idol, the living from the dead.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:20)


כְּדֵין תֵּימְרוּן לְהוֹן לְאוֹרַיְתָא דְאִתְיְהֵיבַת לָנָא וּלְסַהֲדוּ אֲנַחְנָא שָׁמְעִין אֶלָא תִגְלוּן לְבֵינֵי עַמְמַיָא וְיֵימְרוּן לְכוֹן כְּפִתְגָמָא הָדֵין מִכְּעַן לֵית לֵיהּ מַן דִשְׁחַר וְיַבְעִינֵיהּ:


Thus ye shall say unto them: We will listen to the law which has been given to us, and to the testimony: yet ye shall go into captivity amongst the nations, and they shall speak to you according to this word. Now there is none amongst them, who would search and seek after it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:21)


וְיַעֲדֵי תַקְלָא בְּאַרְעָא וִיהֵי עָקָא וּכְפַן וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יֶחֱזֵי כְפַן וְסִיגוּף וְיִלוּט וְיִבְזֵי שׁוּם פְּתַכְרֵיהּ וְטַעֲוָתֵיהּ וְיִתְפְּנֵי לְעֵילָא לְמִיבְעֵי פּוּרְקַן בָּתַר דְתִתְחַתֵּם גְזֵירָתָא וְלָא יִכּוֹל:


And one shall pass through the land, stumbling along, and there shall be oppression and famine. And it shall come to pass, when he shall see famine and affliction, he shall curse and despise the name of his Moloch and his idolatry: and he shall look upwards to seek deliverance after the decree has been sealed, and he shall not be able to obtain it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:22)


וְסָעִיד מִיַתְבֵי אַרְעָא יִבְעֵי אֲרֵי יַיְתֵי עֲלוֹהִי עָקָא כְּפָן וְשַׁלְהֲבֵי עָקָא קְבֵל וּבַדוּר:


And he shall seek help from the inhabitants of the earth, because there shall come upon him oppression, famine, weariness of oppression, darkness, and dispersion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/8:23)


אֲרֵי לָא יִשְׁתַּלְהֵי כָּל דְיֵיתֵי לַאֲעָקָא לְהוֹן כְּעִידַן קֳדָמַי גְלוֹ עַם אֲרַע זְבוּלֻן וְעַם אֲרַע נַפְתָּלִי וּשְׁאַרְהוֹן מְלַךְ תַּקִיף יַגְלֵי עַל דְלָא אַדְכַּרוּ גְבוּרַת יַמָא וְנִסֵי יַרְדְנָא קְרַב כְּרַכֵּי עַמְמַיָא:


For none shall be weary who shall come to oppress them, as at the former time, when the people of the land of Zebulun, and the people of the land of Naphtali went into captivity: and those that were left, a mighty king led into captivity, because they did not remember the power of the Red Sea, neither the wonders of the Jordan, the war of the fortifications of the nations.



## Chapter 9

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:1)


עַמָא בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל דַהֲווֹ מְהַלְכִין בְּמִצְרַיִם כִּדְבְּקִבְלָא נְפָּקוּ לְמֶחֱזֵי נְהוֹר סַגִי יַתְבִין בְּאַרְעָא טוּלֵימוֹתָא נְהוֹרָא אַזְהַר עֲלֵיהוֹן:


The people, the house of Israel, which walked in Egypt as in the darkness, went forth to see a great light. They that dwelt in the land of the shadow of death, upon them hath the light shined.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:2)


אַסְגֵיתָא עַמָא בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל לְהוֹן אַסְגֵיתָא חֶדְוָא חֲדִיאוּ קֳדָמָךְ כְּחֶדְוַת נִצְחֵי קְרָב כְּמָא דַהֲווֹ חָדָן בְּפַלְגוּתְהוֹן בִּזְתָא:


Thou hast multiplied the people, the house of Israel, unto them Thou hast multiplied the joy: they joy before Thee as the joy of the conquerors of battle, as men rejoice when they divide the spoil.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:3)


אֲרֵי אַעְדֵיתָא יַת נִיר מָרְוָתִי וְיַת שׁוּלְטַן עַקְתֵיהּ שִׁלְטוֹן דַהֲוָת מְפַלַח בֵּיהּ אִיתְּבַר כְּיוֹם מִדְיָן:


For Thou hast taken away the yoke of his dominion, and the government of oppression, the government wherewith he enslaved him is broken as in the day of Midian.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:4)


אֲרֵי כָל מְסַבְהוֹן וּמִתַּנְהוֹן בִּרְשַׁע אִתְגְעָלוּ בְּחוֹבִין הָא כִכְסוּ דִמְלוּשָׁא בְדַם דְלָא דָכָן רִשְׁמֵי כִתְמָא מִנָהּ כֵּן כְּמָא דְלֵית בֵּיהּ צְרוֹךְ אֱלָהֵין לְאִתּוֹקָדָא בְנוּרָא כֵּן יִתֵּן עֲלֵיהוֹן עַמְמַיָא דְתַקִיפִין כְּאֶשְׁתָּא וְיִקְטְלוּנוּן:


All their transactions are in wickedness; they are polluted with sins, as a garment dipped in blood, the marks of its filth cannot be purified; thus it is like something, in which there is no use, but to be burned in fire: thus He shall bring forth against them nations, who are mighty as fire, and they shall slay them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:5)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא לְבֵית דָוִד אֲרֵי רָבֵי אִיתְיְלִיד לָנָא בַּר אִתְיְהַב לָנָא וְקַבֵל אוֹרַיְתָא עֲלוֹהִי לְמַטְרָהּ וְאִתְקְרֵי שְׁמֵיהּ מִן קֳדָם מַפְלִיא עֵצָה אֱלָהָא גִבָּרָא קַיָם לְעַלְמַיָא מְשִׁיחָא דִשְׁלָמָא יַסְגֵי עֲלָנָא בְּיוֹמוֹהִי:


The prophet said to the house of David, For unto us a child is born, unto us a son is given, and he has taken the law upon himself to keep it. His name is called from before Him who is wonderful in counsel, the mighty God who liveth to eternity — the Messiah whose peace shall be great upon us in his days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:6)


סַגִי רְבוּ לְעָבְדֵי אוֹרַיְתָא וּלְנַטְרֵי שְׁלָמָא לֵית סוֹף עַל כּוּרְסֵיהּ דָוִד וְעַל מַלְכוּתֵיהּ לְאַתְקָנָא יָתָהּ וּלְמִבְנָא בְדִינָא וּבִזְכוּתָא מִכְּעַן וְעַד עָלְמָא בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת תִּתְעֲבֵיד דָא:


The greatness of those who do the law shall be magnified, and to those, that preserve peace. There shall be no end to the throne of David, and of his kingdom, to establish it and to build it in judgment and in righteousness from henceforth, even for ever. By the Word of the Lord of hosts this shall be done.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:7)


פִּתְגָמָא שְׁלַח יְיָ בִּדְבֵית יַעֲקֹב וְאִשְׁתְּמַע בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


The Lord sent a word into the house of Jacob, and it was heard in Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:8)


וְאִתְרַבְרְבוּ עַמָא כוּלְהוֹן אֶפְרַיִם וְיָתֵיב שֹׁמְרוֹן בִּרְבוּ וּבִתְקוֹף לֵבָב לְמֵימָר:


The people, all of them have become great; Ephraim and the inhabitants of Samaria, that say in greatness and stoutness of heart,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:9)


רֵישַׁיָא גְלוּ וּלְטָבִין מִנְהוֹן נְמַנֵי נִכְסַיָא אִתְבְּזָזוּ וּדְשַׁפִּירִין מִנְהוֹן נִקְנֵי:


The princes are carried captive; but we will appoint better ones than they. Treasures are plundered, but we will purchase more beautiful ones than they.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:10)


וְתַקֵיף יְיָ סַנָאָה רְצִין עֲלוֹהִי וְיַת בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹהִי יְעָרֵר:


Therefore the Lord shall make strong the hatred of Rezin against him, and he will stir up his adversaries.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:11)


אֲרַם מִמַדִינְחָא וּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי מִמַעֲרָבָא וּבְזוֹ יַת נִכְסֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכָל אֲתָר בְּכָל דָא לָא תָבוּ מֵחוֹבֵיהוֹן דִתוּב רוּגְזֵיהּ מִנְהוֹן וְעַד כְּעַן מַתְקְפִין מַרְדֵיהוֹן וְעוֹד מָחָתֵיהּ עֲתִידָא לְאִתְפְּרַע מִנְהוֹן:


The Syrians from the east, and the Philistines from the west; and they shall spoil the treasures of Israel in every place. For all this they do not turn away from their sins, that His anger might turn away from them; but still they hold fast their rebellion, and yet His stroke will be to take vengeance on them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:12)


וְעַמָא לָא תָבוּ לְפוּלְחַן מִן דְאַיְיתֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן מָחָא וְאוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת לָא תְבָעוּ:


And the people have not returned to the worship of God, from the time that He brought upon them the stroke; and they do not seek the instruction of the Lord of hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:13)


וְשֵׁיצֵי יְיָ מִיִשְׂרָאֵל רֵישׁ וְהֶגְמוֹן שִׁלְטוֹן וְאִטְרוֹן יוֹמָא חָד:


And the Lord will destroy, from Israel the prince, the captain, the ruler, and the governor, in one day.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:14)


סָב וּנְסִיב אַפִּין הוּא רֵישָׁא וְסָפַר מַלִיף שְׁקַר הוּא חֲלָשָׁא:


The ancient and honourable one, he is the head, and the scribe that teaches lies, he is the feeble one.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:15)


וַהֲווֹ מְשַׁבְּחֵי עַמָא הָדֵין מַטְעָן וְרַבְרְבָנוֹהִי מְסַלְעֲמִין:


And those that praise this people cause them to err, and their princes swallow them up.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:16)


עַל כֵּן עַל עוּלֵימוֹהִי לָא יֶחְדֵי יְיָ וְעַל יַתְמוֹהִי וְעַל אַרְמְלָתוֹהִי לָא יְרַחֵם אֲרֵי כוּלְהוֹן חֲנִיפִין וּבִישִׁין וְכָל פּוּמְהוֹן מְמַלְלִין שְׁקַר בְּכָל דָא לָא תָבוּ מֵחוֹבֵיהוֹן דִיתוּב רוּגְזֵיהּ מִנְהוֹן וְעַד כְּעַן מַתְקְפִין מִרְדֵיהוֹן וְעוֹד מֶחֶתֵיהּ עֲתִידָא לְאִתְפְּרָעָא מִנְהוֹן:


Therefore the Lord shall have no joy in their young men, neither shall have mercy on their fatherless and widows: for all of them are hypocrites and evildoers, and all their mouths speak lies. For all this they do not turn away from their sins, that His anger might turn away from them; but still they hold fast their rebellion, and yet His stroke is ready to take vengeance on them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:17)


אֲרֵי תַדְלִיק כְּאֶשְׁתָּא פּוּרְעֲנוּת חוֹבֵיהוֹן חַטָאַיָא וְחַיָבַיָא תְּשֵׁיצֵי וְתִשְׁלוֹט בִּשְׁאַר עַמָא וּתְשֵׁיצֵי סַגִי מַשִׁרְיָתָא:


The punishment of their sins shall burn like fire; it shall consume the sinners and the wicked, and shall rule over the rest of the people, and shall make an end of a great host.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:18)


בְּתַבְעוֹר מִן קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת חֲרוֹכַת אַרְעָא וַהֲוָה עַמָא כִּיקֵידַת נוּר גְבַר עַל אֲחוּהִי לָא חָיְסִין:


Through the wrath from before the presence of the Lord of hosts the earth shall be burnt up, and the people shall be like the burning fuel of fire: no man shall spare his brother.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:19)


וּבַז מִן דָרוֹמָא וּכְפֵין וְשֵׁיצֵי מִן צִפּוּנָא וְלָא שְׂבַע גְבַר נִכְסֵיהּ קָרִיבֵיהּ יִבְזוּן:


And he shall spoil on the south, and be hungry; and he shall destroy on the north, and not be satisfied: they shall spoil the substance, every man the substance of his neighbour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/9:20)


דְבֵית מְנַשֶׁה עִם דְבֵית אֶפְרַיִם וּדְבֵית אֶפְרַיִם עִם דְבֵית מְנַשֶׁה כַּחֲדָא יִתְחַבְּרוּן לְמֵיתֵי עַל דְבֵית יְהוּדָה בְּכָל דָא לָא תָבוּ מֵחוֹבֵיהוֹן דִיתוּב רוּגְזֵיהּ מִנְהוֹן וְעַד כְּעַן מַתְקְפִין מַרְדֵיהוֹן וְעוֹד מְחָתֵיהּ עֲתִידָא לְאִתְפְּרַע מִנְהוֹן:


Those of the house of Manasseh, with those of the house of Ephraim, and they of the house of Ephraim, with those of the house of Manasseh, shall be joined together as one, to come against them of the house of Judah. For all this they do not turn away from their sins, that His anger might turn away from them; but still they hold fast their rebellion, and yet His stroke is ready to take vengeance on them.



## Chapter 10

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:1)


וַוי דְרַשְׁמִין רִשְׁמִין דְמֵינַס וּכְתַב דְלֵיאוּ כָּתְבִין:


Woe unto them that sign oppressive decrees, and to those who write a grievous enactment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:2)


לְאַסְטָאָה מִדִין מִסְכְּנִין וּלְמֵינַס מַה דִרָעָן מֵחֲשִׁיכֵי עַמָא בְּדִינָא לְמֶהֱוֵי אַרְמְלָן עֲדֵיהוֹן וְיַת נִכְסֵי יַתְמַיָא יִבְזוּן:


To turn aside the needy from judgment, and to rob whatever the poor of my people desire in judgment, that widows may be their prey, and that they may rob the wealth of the orphans.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:3)


וּמַה תַּעְבְּדוּן לְיוֹמָא דְיִסְעֲרוּן עֲלֵיכוֹן חוֹבֵיכוֹן וּלְרִיגוּשׁ עָקָא מֵרָחִיק יֵיתֵי לְאָן תְּעַרְקוּן לְסָעִיד וְהֵיכָא תִשְׁבְּקוּן יְקָרְכוֹן:


What will ye do in the day that your sins shall be visited upon you? when the tumult of oppression shall come from far? whither will ye flee for help? and where will ye leave your glory?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:4)


בְּכֵן בַּר מִן אַרְעֲכוֹן אֲסִירִין תִּתְאַסְרוּן וּבַר מִקִרְוֵיכוֹן קְטִילִין תִּתְרְמוּן בְּכָל דָא לָא תָבוּ מֵחוֹבֵיהוֹן דִיתוּב רוּגְזֵיהּ מִנְהוֹן וְעַד כְּעַן מַתְקְפִין מָרְדֵיהוֹן וְעוֹד מָחָתֵיהּ עֲתִידָא לְאִתְפְּרָע מִנְהוֹן:


Therefore out of your land ye shall be fettered amongst the captives; and out of your cities ye shall be cast out slain. For all this they do not turn away from their sins, that His anger might turn away from them; but still they hold fast their rebellion, and yet His stroke will be to take vengeance on them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:5)


וַוי אַתּוּרָאָה שׁוּלְטַן רוּגְזִי וּמַלְאָךְ שָׁלִיחַ מִן קֳדָמַי עֲלֵיהוֹן בִּלְוָט:


Woe to the Assyrian, the government of my fury, an angel is sent from before me against them for a curse!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:6)


בִּכְנֵישָׁא חֲנִיפָא אֲשַׁלְחִינֵיהּ וְעַל עַמָא דַעֲבָרוּ עַל אוֹרַיְתָא אֲפַקְדִינֵיהּ לְמִבַּז בִּזָא וּלְמֶעְדֵי עֲדָאָה וּלְשַׁוָיוּתֵיהּ לְדָיִישׁ כְּסִין שׁוּקִין:


I will send him against an hypocritical assembly, and against a people that have transgressed my law will I give him a charge, to take the spoil, and to take the prey, and to place him for a treading under foot like the mire of the streets.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:7)


וְהוּא לָא כֵן מְדַמֵי וְלִבֵּיהּ לָא כֵן מַחְשֵׁיב אֲרֵי לְשֵׁיצָאָה אֲמַר בְּלִבֵּיהּ וְלַאֲסָפָא עַמְמִין לָא בַּחֲיָס:


Howbeit he did not purpose so, neither doth his heart think so; but to destroy he hath said in his heart, and to make an end of nations without compassion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:8)


אֲרֵי יֵימַר הֲלָא כָל שִׁלְטוֹנָי כַּחֲדָא כְּמַלְכִין חֲשִׁיבִין קֳדָמַי:


For he saith, Are not all my rulers altogether as kings considered before me?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:9)


הֲלָא כְּמָא דְכַרְכְּמִישׁ כְּבִישָׁא קֳדָמַי כֵּן כַּלְנוֹ אִם לָא כְּמָא דְאַרְפַּד מְסִירָא בִידִי כֵּן חֲמָת וּכְמָא דַעֲבָדִית לְדַמֶשֶׂק כֵּן אַעְבֵּיד לְשֹׁמְרוֹן:


Is it not so? As Carchemish is subdued before me, shall not Calno be so? Surely, as Arphad is delivered into my hands, shall not Hamath be so? As I have done to Damascus, so will I do to Samaria.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:10)


כְּמָא דְאַשְׁכְּחַת יְדֵי מַלְכְוָן דְפַלְחָן לְטַעֲוָתָא וְצַלְמָנֵיהוֹן מִירוּשְׁלֵם וּמִיקָמָתָא דְשֹׁמְרוֹן:


As my hand hath found the kingdoms which worship idols, although their images were more than those of Jerusalem, and their statues more than those of Samaria;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:11)


הֲלָא כְּמָא דַעֲבָדִית לְשֹׁמְרוֹן וּלְטַעֲוָתָהָא כֵּן אַעְבֵיד לִירוּשְׁלֵם וּלְצַלְמָנַיָא דִי בָהּ:


Shall I not, as I have done to Samaria and to her idols, so do to Jerusalem and to her idols?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:12)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יְשֵׁיצֵי יְיָ לְמֶעְבַּד יַת כָּל דַאֲמַר בְּטוּרָא דְצִיוֹן וּבִירוּשְׁלֵם אַסְעַר עַל עוֹבָדֵי רָם לִבָּא דְמַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר וְעַל תּוּשְׁבְּחַת רְמוּת עֵינוֹהִי:


And it shall come to pass, when the Lord hath finished to do all he hath said in Mount Zion, and in Jerusalem, I will visit upon the works of the loftiness of the heart of the king of Assyria, and the glory of his high looks.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:13)


אֲרֵי אֲמַר בִּתְקוֹף יְדִי עֲבָדִית וּבְחוּכְמְתִי אֲרֵי סוּכְלְתָן אֲנָא וְאַגְלֵיתִי עַמְמַיָא מִמְדִינָא לִמְדִינָא וְקִרְוֵי תּוּשְׁבְּחַתְהוֹן בְּזֵיתִי וַאֲחָתֵית בִּתְקוֹף יַת יַתְבֵי כְּרַכִּין תַּקִיפִין:


For he saith, By the strength of my hand I have done it, and by my wisdom; for I am prudent: and I have led nations into captivity from province to province; and the cities of their glory I have plundered; and I have brought down with strength those that dwell in fortified places.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:14)


וְאַשְׁכָּחֵית כְּקִנָה יְדֵי נִכְסֵי עַמְמַיָא וּכְמַכְנַשׁ בֵּיעִין שְׁבִיקִין כָּל דַיְרֵי אַרְעָא אֲנָא כְנֵישִׁית וְלָא הֲוָה נָטֵיל מִכָּא וְשָׁדֵי כָּא וּפְתַח פּוּמָא וּמְמַלֵיל מִלָא:


And my hand hath found as a nest the riches of the people: and as one gathereth eggs that are left, I have gathered all the inhabitants of the earth; and there was none that moved from thence and flew thither, that opened his mouth and spoke a word.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:15)


הֲאֶפְשַׁר דְיִשְׁתַּבַּח חוֹלִילָא עַל דְחָלִיל בֵּיהּ לְמֵימָר הָא אֲנָא חֲלִילַת אִם יִתְרַבְרַב מַסְרָא עַל דְנָגִיר בָהּ לְמֵימָר הֲלָא אֲנָא נַגָרִית כַּאֲרָמָא חוּטְרָא לְמִמְחֵי לָא חוּטְרָא מָחֵי אֱלָהֵן מַן דְמָחֵי בֵיהּ:


What, is it possible that the axe should boast itself against him that heweth therewith, saying, Behold, I have cut! Or shall the saw magnify itself against him that draweth it, saying, Do I not draw? When a rod is lifted up to smite, it is not the rod that smites, but he that smites with it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:16)


חֲלַף דְאִתְרַבְרַב מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר בְּכֵן יְשַׁלַח רִבּוֹן עַלְמָא יְיָ צְבָאוֹת בְּרַבְרְבָנוֹהִי מָחָא וּתְחוֹת מָנַי יְקָרְהוֹן מֵיקַד יֵיקְדוּן כִּיקֵידַת אֶשָׁא:


Because the king of Assyria magnifies himself, therefore the Lord of the world, the Lord of hosts, shall send among his princes a blow; and beneath the vessels of their glory they are utterly burned up like the burning of a fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:17)


וִיהֵי מָרֵי נְהוֹרֵיהּ דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְקַדִישֵׁיהּ מֵימְרֵיהּ תַּקִיף כְּאֶשְׁתָּא וּפִתְגָמוֹהִי כְשַׁלְהוֹבִיתָא וְיִקְטוֹל וִישֵׁיצֵי שִׁלְטוֹנוֹהִי וְטוּרְנוֹהִי בְּיוֹמָא חָד:


And there shall be the Lord, the Light of Israel, and His holy one, His word, strong as fire, and His word as a flame; and it shall slay, and make an end of his rulers and of his governors in one day.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:18)


וִיקַר סַגִי מַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ נַפְשֵׁיהוֹן עִם פִּגְרֵיהוֹן יִשֵׁיצֵי וִיהֵי תְּבִיר וַעֲרִיק:


And it shall consume the glory of the multitud of his army, and their souls with their bodies, and he shall be broken, and be a fugitive.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:19)


וּשְׁאַר עָבְדֵי קְרָבֵיהּ יְסוּפוּן לְמֶהֱוֵי עַם דְמִנְיָן וּמַלְכוּ חֲלָשָׁא יִתְחַשְׁבוּן:


And the rest of his warriors shall come to an end, that the people shall be a small number, and they shall be reckoned a weak kingdom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:20)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא לָא יוֹסְפוּן עוֹד שְׁאָרָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְשֵׁיזְבַת בֵּית יַעֲקֹב לְאִיסְתַּמָכָא עַל עַמְמַיָא דַהֲווֹ מְפַלְחִין בְּהוֹן וְיִסְתַּמְכוּן עַל מֵימְרָא דַייָ קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל בִּקְשׁוֹט:


And it shall come to pass in that time, that the remnant of Israel, and such as are escaped of the house of Jacob, shall no more again lean on the people whom they served; but they shall lean upon the word of the Lord, the Holy One of Israel,' in truth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:21)


שְׁאָר דְלָא חֲטוּ וּדְתָבוּ מֵחֲטָאָה שְׁאָרָא דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב יְתוּבוּן לְמִפְלַח קֳדָם אֱלָהָא גִבָּרָא:


The remnant which have not sinned, and have turned away from sin, the remnant of the house of Jacob, shall return to worship before the mighty God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:22)


אֲרֵי אִם יְהֵי עַמָךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל סַגִי כְּחָלָא דְיַמָא שְׁאָר דְלָא חֲטוֹ וּדְתָבוּ מֵחֲטָאָה יִתְעַבְדוּן לְהוֹן גְבוּרָן דְמִתְגַבְּרִין וּמְדַבְּרָן בְּזָכוּ:


Though thy people Israel be as the sand of the sea, a remnant which hath not sinned, but hath turned away from sin, for them mighty things shall be done, which shall prevail, even those which speak in righteousness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:23)


אֲרֵי גְמִירָא וְשֵׁיצָאָה יְיָ אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת עָבַד עִם כָּל רַשִׁיעֵי בְּגוֹ אַרְעָא:


For a completion, and an end, the Lord, the God of hosts is making with all the wicked in the midst of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:24)


בְּכֵן כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת לָא תִדְחַל עַמִי יָתִיב צִיוֹן מֵאַתּוּרָא בְּשׁוּלְטָנֵיהּ יַמְחִינָךְ וּמַרְוָתֵיהּ יִרְמֵי עֲלָךְ כִּדְבְּאוֹרַח מִצְרָיִם:


Therefore, thus saith the Lord, the God of hosts, O my people, that dwellest in Zion, be not afraid of the Assyrian: he shall smite thee with his government, and his dominion he shall lift up upon thee, after the manner of Egypt.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:25)


אֲרֵי עוֹד צִבְחַר כִּזְעֵיר וִיסוּפוּן לְוָטַיָא מִנְכוֹן דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב וְרוּגְזֵי עַל עַמְמַיָא עָבְדֵי תִבְלָא לְשֵׁיצָיוּתְהוֹן:


For yet a little while, and the curses shall cease from you of the house of Jacob; and mine anger shall be upon the people that work iniquity, to destroy them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:26)


וְיַיתֵי עֲלוֹהִי יְיָ צְבָאוֹת מְחָא כְּמָחַת מִדְיָן בִּשְׁקִיף עוֹרֵב וּמַרְוָתֵיהּ תֶּעְדֵי מִנְכוֹן כְּמָא דַעֲדַת מַרְוַת פַּרְעֹה עַל יַמָא וְיִתְעַבְדָן לְכוֹן גְבוּרָן כִּדְבְּאוֹרַח מִצְרָיִם:


And the Lord of hosts shall bring upon him a stroke, as the stroke of Midian at the rock of Oreb: and his dominion shall depart from you, as the dominion of Pharaoh departed by the sea, and mighty works shall be done for you after the manner of Egypt.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:27)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא תֶּעְדֵי מַרְוָתֵיהּ מִנָךְ וְנִירֵיהּ מֵעַל צַוְרָךְ וְיִתְּבְרוּן עַמְמַיָא מִן קֳדָם מְשִׁיחָא:


And it shall come to pass at that time, that his dominion shall depart from you, and his yoke from off thy neck, and the nations shall be broken from before the Messiah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:28)


אֲתָא עַל עַיָת עָבַר בְּמִגְרוֹן לְמִכְמַשׁ יְמַנֵי רַבָּנֵי מַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ:


He is come to Aiath, he has passed through Migron; at Micmash he shall appoint the princes of his army.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:29)


גָזוּ עֲבָרוּ יַרְדְנָא בְּגֶבַע בָּתוּ לְהוֹן אִתְבָּרוּ יָתְבֵי רָמָתָא אֱנַשׁ גִבְעַת שָׁאוּל עֲרָקוּ:


They waded, they passed through the Jordan: at Geba they passed the night; the inhabitants of Ramah are broken; the men of Gibeah of Saul have fled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:30)


אָרִימוּ קָלֵיכוֹן אֱנַשׁ בַּת גַלִים אֲצִיתוּ דְיָתְבִין בְּלַיִשׁ דְדַיְרִין בַּעֲנִיָה עֲנָתוֹת:


Lift up your voice, men of the daughter of Gallim: give ear, ye who are dwelling in Laish, who are dwelling in poor Anathoth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:31)


אִתְבָּרוּ אֱנַשׁ מַדְמֵנָה יַתְבֵי גֵיבִים גְלוֹ:


The men of Madmenah are broken; the inhabitants of Gebim are fled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:32)


עַד כְעַן יוֹמָא רַב וְסַגִי עִדַן לֵיהּ לְמֵיעַל הָא סַנְחֶרֶב (מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר נְטַל וַעֲבַר תְּלַת אַרְנִין וּדְבַר עִמֵיהּ אַרְבְּעִין אַלְפִין גוּסְפְּנִין דִדְהַב דִבְנֵי מַלְכִין קְטִירֵי תָגָא יַתְבִין בְּהוֹן וּדְבַר עִמֵיהּ מָאתָן אַלְפִין אֲחִידֵי סַיְפִין וְרוּמְחִין וּדְבַר עִמֵיהּ מָאתָן וְשִׁיתִּין אַלְפִין מְחַצְצֵי גִירִין גוּבְרִין דְרָהֲטִין קֳדָמוֹהִי מְאָה אַלְפִין אוּרְכָּא דְמַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ אַרְבַּע מְאָה פַּרְסִין צַוָאר סוּסְוָתֵיהּ אַרְבְּעִין פַּרְסִין מִנְיָן מַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ מָאתָן וְשִׁתִּין אַלְפִין רִיבּוֹא חָסֵר חַד וְכֵן אֲתוֹ עַל אַבְרָהָם כַּד רְמוֹ יָתֵיהּ לְגוֹ נוּרָא יְקֵדְתָּא וְכֵן עַתִידִין לְמֵיתֵי עִם גוֹג וּמָגוֹג כַּד יִשְׁלֶה עַלְמָא קִצֵי לְמִתְפַרְקָא מַשִׁרְיָתָא קַדְמֵיתָא כַּד עֲבַרוּ בְיַרְדְנָא שָׁתוּ מַיָא דַהֲווֹ בְיַרְדְנָא מַשִׁירְיָתָא תִנְיֵתָא כַּד עֲבָרוּ בְיַרְדְנָא חָפָרוּ בִירִין וְשָׁתוּ מַיָא) אֲתָא וְקָם בְּנוֹב קִרְיַת כַּהֲנַיָא לִקֳבֵיל שׁוּרָא דִירוּשְׁלֵם וְעָנֵי וַאֲמַר לְחַיָלוּתֵיהּ הָלָא דָא הִיא קַרְתָּה יְרוּשְׁלֵם דַעֲלָא אַרְגֵשִׁית כָּל מַשִׁרְיָתִי וַעֲלָהּ כְּבֵשִׁית כָּל מְדִינָתִי הָא הִיא זְעֵירָא וַחֲלָשָׁא מִכָּל כְּרַכֵּי עַמְמַיָא דִכְבֵישִׁית בִּתְקוֹף יְדִי עֲלָהּ קָם מָנִיד בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ מוֹבִּיל וּמַיְתֵי בִּידֵיהּ עַל טוּר בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא דִי בְצִיוֹן וְעַל עֲזָרָתָא דְבִירוּשְׁלֵם:


As yet the day is still high, and he has much time to come. Behold, Sennacherib the king of Assyria. He journeyed and passed three stations, and he brought with him forty thousand seats of gold, in which the sons of the kings encircled with crowns were sitting; likewise, led with him two hundred thousand holding swords and spears; he also brought with him two hundred and sixty thousand archers, and a hundred thousand men ran before him. The length of his camp was four hundred parasangs; the neck of his horses forty parasangs; the number of his army, was two hundred and sixty thousand myriads lacking one. And thus they came upon Abraham, when they had cast him into the midst of the burning fire, and thus they shall come with Gog and Magog, when the world shall have completed its end to be redeemed. When the first host passed through the Jordan, they drank up the waters which were in the Jordan. When the second host passed through the Jordan, the hoofs of the horses absorbed, and drank the waters. When the third host passed through the Jordan, they dug wells, and drank the waters. He came and stood in Nob, the city of the priests, before the wall of Jerusalem; he answered and said to his army, Is not this the city of Jerusalem, against which I have tumultuously brought together all my camps, and on account of which I have oppressed all my provinces? Behold, she is smaller and weaker than all the fortified cities of the nations which I have subdued by the strength of my hand. He came up, he stood and shook his head; he stretched out and moved his hand against the mount of the house of the sanctuary which is in Zion, and against the court which is in Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:33)


הָא רִבּוֹן עַלְמָא יְיָ צְבָאוֹת רָמֵי קְטוֹל בְּמַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ כִּבְעוֹט דְמִתְבָּעֵיט בְּמַעֲצַרְתָּא וְרָמֵי קוֹמָא יִתְקַטְפוּן וְתַקִיפַיָא יִמְאֲכוּן:


Behold, the Lord of the world, the Lord of hosts, shall cast forth slaughter into his camp, as grapes that are trod in a wine-press: and the high ones of stature shall be hewn down, and the mighty shall be humbled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 10:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/10:34)


וְיִקְטֵיל גִבָּרֵי מַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ דְמִתְגַבְּרִין כְּבַרְזְלָא וְעָבְדֵי קְרָבָא עַל אַרְעָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יִתְרְמוּן:


And He shall slay the mighty men of his camp, who are strong as iron. And those that make war upon the earth shall be cast down.



## Chapter 11

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:1)


וְיִפּוֹק מַלְכָּא מִבְּנוֹהִי דְיִשַׁי וּמְשִׁיחָא מִבְּנֵי בְנוֹהִי יִתְרַבֵּי:


And a king shall come forth from the sons of Jesse, and from his children's children the Messiah shall be anointed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:2)


וְתִשְׁרֵי עֲלוֹהִי רוּחַ נְבוּאָה מִן קֳדָם יְיָ רוּחַ חוּכְמָא וְסוּכְלְתָנוּ רוּחַ מְלַךְ וּגְבוּרָא רוּח מַדַע וּדְחַלְתָּא דַיָי:


And there shall dwell upon him the spirit of prophecy from before the Lord: the spirit of wisdom and understanding, the spirit of counsel and might, the spirit of knowledge and of the fear of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:3)


וִיקָרְבִינֵיהּ לִדְחַלְתֵּיהּ יְיָ לָא לְחֵיזוּ עֵינוֹהִי יְהֵי דָאַן וְלָא לְמִשְׁמָע אוּדְנוֹהִי יְהִי מוֹכַח:


He shall bring him to the fear of the Lord: and he shall not judge according to the sight of His eyes, neither reprove after the hearing of his ears.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:4)


וְיָדִין בְּקוּשְׁטָא מִסְכֵּינִין וְיוֹכַח בְּהֵימְנוּתָא מַחֲשִׁיכֵי עַמָא דְאַרְעָא וְיַמְחֵי חַיָבֵי אַרְעָא בְּמֵימַר פּוּמֵיהּ וּבְמַמְלֵיל סִפְוָתֵיהּ יְהֵי מֵמִית אַרְמִילוֹס רַשִׁיעָא:


But with righteousness shall he judge the poor, and reprove with faithfulness the needy of the earth; and he shall smite the sinners of the earth with the word of his mouth, and with the speech of His lips he shall slay Armillus the wicked.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:5)


וִיהוֹן צַדִיקַיָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר לֵיהּ וְעָבְדֵי הַמְנוּתָא מְקָרְבִין לֵיהּ:


And the righteous shall be round about him, and the workers of faith shall draw nigh unto him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:6)


בְּיוֹמוֹהִי דִמְשִׁיחָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יִסְגֵי שְׁלָמָא בְּאַרְעָא וְיָדוּר דֵיבָא עִם אִמְרָא וְנִמְרָא עִם גַדְיָא יִשְׁרֵי וְעֶגְלָא וְאַרְיָא וּפַטִים כַּחֲדָא וְיָנִיק זְעֵיר יְהֵי מְדַבֵּר לְהוֹן:


In the days of the Messiah of Israel peace shall be multiplied in the earth. The wolf shall dwell with the lamb, and the leopard shall dwell with the kid; and the calf, and the lion, and the fatling together; and a little sucking child shall be leading them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:7)


וְתוֹרָא וְדִיבָא יִרְעֲיָן כַּחֲדָא יִשְׁרוֹן בְּנֵיהוֹן וְאַרְיָא כְּתוֹרָא יֵכוּל תִּבְנָא:


And the cow and the bear shall feed together, their young ones shall lie down together; and the lion shall eat straw like the ox.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:8)


וִיחַיִךְ יַנְקָא עַל חוֹר חִוֵי פֶתֶן וְעַל חֵיזוּ גִלְגְלֵי עֵינַי חִוֵי חוּרְמָן חֲסִילָא יְדוֹהִי יוֹשִׁיט:


And the sucking child shall play on the hole of the asp, and the weaned child shall put forth his hand on the sight of the pupil of the eyes of the cockatrice.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:9)


לָא יִבְאֲשׁוּן וְלָא יְחַבְּלוּן בְּכָל טוּרָא דְקוּדְשִׁי אֲרֵי תִתְמְלֵי אַרְעָא לְמִידַע יַת דְחַלְתָּא דַייָ כְּמַיָא דִלְיַמָא חָפָן:


They shall not hurt nor destroy in all my holy mountain, for the earth shall be full of the knowledge of the fear of the Lord, as the waters cover the sea.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:10)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא בַּר בְּרֵיהּ דְיִשָׁי עֲתִיד דִיקוּם אָת לְעַמְמַיָא לֵיהּ מַלְכְּוָן יִשְׁתַּמְעוּן וִיהֵי אֲתַר בֵּית מֵישְׁרוֹהִי בִיקָר:


And there shall be at that time a son of the son of Jesse, who shall stand for an ensign of the people; kings shall obey him, and the place of his dwelling shall be in glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:11)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יוֹסִיף יְיָ תִּנְיָנוּת גְבוּרְתֵּיהּ לְמִפְרוֹק יַת שְׁאָרָא דְעַמֵיהּ דְאִשְׁתָּאַר מֵאַתּוּר וּמִמִצְרַיִם וּמִפַּתְרוֹס מֵהוֹדוּ וּמֵעֵילָם וּמִבָּבֶל וּמֵחֲמָת וּמִנַגְוַת יַמָא:


And it shall come to pass at that time, that the Lord shall add a second time His might to redeem the remnant of His people, which shall be left, from Assyria, and from Egypt, and from Pattros, and from India, and from Elam, and from Babylon, and from Hamath, and from the islands of the sea.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:12)


וְיִזְקוֹף אָת לְעַמְמַיָא וְיַכְנֵישׁ מְבַדְרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגָלוּת יְהוּדָה יְקָרֵב מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחֵי אַרְעָא:


And He shall lift up an ensign for the nations, and shall assemble the scattered of Israel, and the captivity of Judah He shall gather together from the four winds of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:13)


וְתֶעְדֵי קִנְאֲתָא דְבֵית אֶפְרַיִם וְדִמְעִיקִין לִדְבֵית יְהוּדָה יִשְׁתֵּיצוּן דְבֵית אֶפְרַיִם לָא יְקַנוּן בִּדְבֵית יְהוּדָה וּדְבֵית יְהוּדָה לָא יְעִיקוּן לִדְבֵית אֶפְרָיִם:


The envy of the house of Ephraim shall depart, and those that oppress the house of Judah shall come to an end: those of the house of Ephraim shall not envy the house of Judah; and those of the house of Judah shall not oppress the house of Ephraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:14)


וְיִתְחַבְּרוּן כְּתָף חָד לְמִמְחֵי פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי דִי בְּמַעֲרָבָא כַּחֲדָא יְבוֹזוּן יַת בְּנֵי מַדִינְחָא בֶּאֱדוֹם וּמוֹאָב יוֹשִׁיטוּן יְדֵיהוֹן וּבְנֵי עַמוֹן יִשְׁתְּמָעוּן לְהוֹן:


They shall be united in one mind to smite the Philistines towards the west: they shall spoil the children of the east. Against Edom and Moab they shall stretch out their hands, and the children of Ammon shall obey them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:15)


וְיַבֵּשׁ יְיָ יַת לִישָׁן יַמָא דְמִצְרַיִם וִירֵים מְחַת גְבוּרְתֵּיהּ עַל פְּרָת בְּמֵימַר נְבִיוֹהִי וְיַמְחִינֵיהּ לְשַׁבְעָא נַחְלִין וִיהָכוּן בֵּיהּ בְּסַנְדָלִין:


And the Lord shall dry up the tongue of the Egyptian sea, and shall lift the stroke of His strength upon Euphrates by the word of His prophets, and shall smite it in seven streams, and men shall walk through it dryshod.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/11:16)


וּתְהֵי אוֹרַח כְּבִישָׁא לִשְׁאָרָא דְעַמֵיהּ דְיִשְׁתָּאַר מֵאַתּוּרָאָה כְּמָא דַהֲוַת לְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּיוֹם מַסְקֵיהוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


And there shall be a paved way for the remnant of His people, which shall be left, from Assyria, like as it was to Israel in the day of their coming up from the land of Egypt.



## Chapter 12

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:1)


וְתֵימַר בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא אוֹדִי קֳדָמָךְ יְיָ אֲרֵי עַל דְחָבֵית קֳדָמָךְ הֲוָה רוּגְזָךְ עֲלָי וּכְדוּ דְתָבֵית לְאוֹרַיְתָא יְתוּב רוּגְזָךְ מִנִי וּתְרַחֵם עֲלָי:


And thou shalt say at that time, I will confess before Thee, O Lord, for that I have sinned before Thee, Thine anger is upon me; but when I return to the law, Thine anger will turn from me, and Thou wilt have mercy on me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:2)


הָא עַל מֵימָר אֱלָהָא פּוּרְקָנִי אֲנָא רָחִיץ וְלָא אִזְדַעְזָא אֲרֵי תוּקְפִי וְתוּשְׁבַּחְתִּי דְחִילָא יְיָ אֲמַר בְּמֵימְרֵיהּ וְהַוָה לִי לְפָרִיק:


Behold, in the word of God is my salvation; I am trusting, and shall not be moved, because my strength and my praise is the fear of the Lord: He hath spoken by His word, and He is become my salvation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:3)


וּתְקַבְּלוּן אוּלְפַן חֲדַת בְּחֶדְוָא מִבְּחִירֵי צַדִיקַיָא:


And ye shall receive a new doctrine with joy from the chosen of the righteous.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:4)


וְתֵימְרוּן בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא אוֹדוּ קֳדָם יְיָ צַלוּ בִּשְׁמֵיהּ חֲווּ בְּעַמְמַיָא עוֹבָדוֹהִי אִדְכַּרוּ אֲרֵי תַקִיף שְׁמֵיהּ:


And at that time shall ye say, Praise before the Lord, pray in His name, declare His doings among the people, remember ye that powerful is His name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:5)


שַׁבְּחוּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֲרֵי גְבוּרָן עֲבַד גַלְיָא דָא בְּכָל אַרְעָא:


Praise the Lord, for He has done mighty works; this is revealed in all the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/12:6)


בּוּעִי וְשַׁבְּחִי כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְצִיוֹן אֲרֵי רַבָּא אֲמַר לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ בְּגַוָךְ קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Cry out and shout, O congregation of Zion, because He is great that promised to cause His Shekinah to dwell in the midst of thee, the Holy One of Israel.



## Chapter 13

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:1)


מַטֵל כָּס דִי לְוָט לְאַשְׁקָאָה יַת בָּבֶל דְאִתְנַבֵּי יְשַׁעְיָה בַּר אָמוֹץ:


The Burden Of The Cup Of Cursing To Give To Babylon To Drink, Which Isaiah The Son Of Amoz Did Prophesy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:2)


עַל כְּרַכָּא דְיַתְבָא שְׁלֵיוָא זְקוּפוּ אָתָא אֲרִימוּ קָלָא לְהוֹן אֲנִיפוּ יַד וְיֵיעֲלוּן בְּתַרְעָהָא מִתְנַדְבִין:


Against the fortified city that dwells securely lift up the ensign, exalt the voice unto them, shake the hand, that they may go into the gates of the nobles.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:3)


אֲנָא פַקְּדִית לְמִזְמַנֵי אַף עַרְעֵית גִבָּרַי וְיִתְפָּרְעוּן מִנְהוֹן רוּגְזִי תּוּקְפִי וְתוּשְׁבַּחְתִּי:


I have commanded those that are prepared by me, I have also called together my mighty ones, that they may avenge my wrath upon them, my strength, and my praise.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:4)


קַל הֲמוֹן בְּטוּרַיָא דְמוּת עַם סַגִי קַל אִתְרְגוּשַׁת מַלְכְּוָן עַמְמַיָא דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין יְיָ צְבָאוֹת מְמַנֵי מַשִׁרְיָן בִּקְרָבָא:


There is a voice of a multitude in the mountains, like as of a great people: the voice of the tumultuous assembly of kings, of nations being assembled, the Lord of hosts is mustering the armies for battle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:5)


אָתָן מֵאַרְעָא רְחִיקָא מִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא יְיָ וּמָנֵי כָס דִלְוָט קֳדָמוֹהִי לְחַבָּלָא כָּל רִשְׁעֵי אַרְעָא:


They are coming from a far country, from the ends of the earth: the Lord and the vessels of the cup of cursing before Him, to destroy all the wicked of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:6)


אַיְלִילוּ אֲרֵי קָרִיב יוֹמָא דַעֲתִיד לְמֵיתֵי מִן קֳדָם יְיָ כְּבִיזָא מִן קֳדָם שַׁדַי יֵיתִי:


Howl ye, for nigh is the day, ready to come from before the Lord; like spoil from before the Almighty it shall come.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:7)


עַל כֵּן כָּל יְדַיָא יִתְרַשְׁלוּן וְכָל לִבָּא דֶאֱנָשָׁא יִתְמְסֵי:


Therefore shall all hands be slackened, and every man's heart shall melt.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:8)


וְיִתְבַּהֲלוּן עָקָא וְחֶבְלִין יֶחְדוּנוּנוּן זִיעַ כְּלֵידָתָא יְזוּעוּן גְבַר לְחַבְרֵיהּ יִתְמְהוּן אַפֵּי שַׁלְהוֹבִין אַפֵּיהוֹן:


They shall be struck with consternation; distress and pangs shall take hold of them; they shall tremble as with the trembling of a woman that travaileth: they shall be amazed one at another; their countenances shall be like flames of fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:9)


הָא יוֹמָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ עֲתִיד לְמֵיתֵי אַכְזָרִי וְתַעֲבוֹר וּתְקוֹף רְגַז לְשַׁוָאָה אַרְעָא לְצָדוּ וְחַיָבָהָא יְשֵׁיצֵי מִנָהּ:


Behold, the day from before the presence of the Lord cometh, cruel both with wrath and fierce anger, to lay the land desolate: and He shall destroy the sinners out of it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:10)


אֲרֵי כוֹכְבַי שְׁמַיָא וּכְסִלֵיהוֹן לָא יִזְהֲרוּן נְהוֹרֵיהוֹן וּקְבֵל שִׁמְשָׁא בְּמִפְקֵיהּ וְסִיהֲרָא לָא מִזְהַר נְהוֹרֵיהּ:


For the stars of heaven and their constellations shall not give their light: the sun shall be darkened in his going forth; and the moon shall not cause her light to shine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:11)


וְאַסְעַר עַל זְדַיְרִין בְּתֵבֵל בִּישְׁתָא וְעַל רַשִׁיעַיָא חוֹבֵיהוֹן וַאֲבַטֵיל רְבוּת רַשִׁיעִין וּתְקוֹף תַּקִיפִין אַמְאִיךְ:


And I will visit the inhabitants of the world with the punishment for evil, and the wicked for their iniquity: and I will put an end to the greatness of the wicked, and the strength of the mighty I will humble.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:12)


אֲחַבֵּב דַחֲלַי מִדַהֲבָא דְמִתְיַקְרִין מִנֵיהּ בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא וְעָבְדֵי אוֹרַיְתָא מִמַסְנָנָא דְאוֹפִיר:


And I will love them that fear me more than gold, in which the sons of men glory; and those that keep the law more than the fine gold of Ophir.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:13)


עַל כֵּן שְׁמַיָא אַזִיעַ וּתְזוּעַ אַרְעָא מֵאַתְרָהָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת וּבְיוֹם תְּקוֹף רוּגְזֵיהּ:


Therefore I will shake the heavens, and the earth shall remove out of her place, from before the presence of the Lord of hosts, and in the day of the fierceness of His anger.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:14)


וִיהֵי כְּטַבְיָא מְפָרֵד וּכְעָאן וְלֵית דְמַכְנֵישׁ גְבַר לְעַמֵיהּ יִתְפְּנוּן וּגְבַר לְאַרְעֵיהּ יְעַרְקוּן:


And he shall be as a roe chased, and as sheep when there is none to gather them together: they shall every man turn to his own people, and flee every one into his own land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:15)


כָּל דְיִשְׁתְּכַח בָּהּ יִתְקְטֵל וְכָל דְיֵעוּל לִכְרַכֵּי צִיָרָא יִתְקְטֵל בְּחַרְבָּא:


Every one that shall be found in her shall be slain, and every one that shall enter into the fortified cities shall be slain by the sword.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:16)


וְעוּלֵימֵיהוֹן יִטְרְפוּן לְעֵינֵיהוֹן יִתְבְּזִיזוּן בָּתֵּיהוֹן וּנְשֵׁיהוֹן יִשְׁתַּכְבָן:


And their children shall be dashed before their eyes; their houses shall be plundered, and their wives ravished.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:17)


הָא אֲנָא מַיְתֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן יַת מָדַי דִי בְּכַסְפָּא לָא מְטַקְסִין וְדַהֲבָא לָא מִתְרְעִין בֵּיהּ:


Behold, I am bringing upon them the Medes, who are not impelled by silver; and as for gold, they delight not in it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:18)


וְקַשְׁתָּתְהוֹן עוּלֵימִין יְבַזְעָן וְעַל וְלַד מָעִין לָא יְרַחֲמוּן עַל בְּנַיָא לָא תָחוֹס עֵינֵיהוֹן:


And their bows shall strike through the young men; and they shall not compassionate the child of the womb; their eye shall not spare the children.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:19)


וּתְהֵי בָּבֶל דַהֲוַת מִלְקַדְמִין חֶדְוַת מַלְכְּוַן תּוּשְׁבָּחַת רְבוּת כַּסְדָאֵי כַּהֲפֵיכָתָא דַהֲפַךְ יְיָ יַת סְדוֹם וְיַת עֲמוֹרָה:


And Babylon shall become, she that was aforetimes the joy of kings, the beauty of the Chaldeens' excellency, as the overthrow wherewith the Lord overthrew Sodom and Gomorrah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:20)


לָא תִתַּב לַעֲלַם וְלָא תִשְׁרֵי עַד דָר וְדָר לָא יִפְרוֹס תַּמָן עֲרָבִי מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ וְרַעֲיָן לָא יִשְׁרוּן תַּמָן:


It shall not be inhabited for ever; nor shall it be dwelt in from generation to generation: neither shall the Arabian pitch his tent there, neither shall the shepherds dwell there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:21)


וְיִשְׁרוּן תַּמָן תַּמְוַן וְיִתְמְלוּן בָתֵּיהוֹן אוֹחִין וְיִשְׁרְיָן תַּמָן בְּנַת נַעֲמִין וְשֵׁידִין יְחַיְכוּן תַּמָן:


But the martens shall lodge there; and their houses shall be filled with owls; and there shall the ostriches dwell; and devils shall delight themselves there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/13:22)


וִינַפְצוּן חֲתוּלִין בְּבִרְנְיָתְהוֹן וִירוּדוּן בְּבֵית מֵישְׁרֵי תַּפְנוּקֵיהוֹן וְקָרֵיב לְמֵיתֵי עִדָן תַּבְרָהּ דְבָבֶל וְיוֹמָהָא לָא יִתְרַחֲקּוּן:


The wild cats shall shriek in their palaces; and dragons in the place of the abodes of their luxuries. And nigh to come is the time of the destruction of Babylon, and her days shall not be prolonged.



## Chapter 14

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:1)


אֲרֵי יְרַחֵם יְיָ עַל דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב וְיִתְרְעֵי עוֹד בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִשְׁרִינוּן עַל אַרְעֲהוֹן וְיִתּוֹסְפוּן גִיוֹרִין עֲלֵיהוֹן וְיִסְתַּמְכוּן עַל בֵּית יַעֲקֹב:


For the Lord will have compassion upon the house of Jacob, and will delight again in Israel, and shall make them to dwell in their land; and proselytes shall be joined unto them, and they shall be united unto the house of Jacob.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:2)


וְיַדְבְּרִינוּן עַמְמִין וְיוֹבְלוּנוּן לְאַרְעֲהוֹן וְיַחְסְנוּן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל אֲרַע שְׁכִנְתָּא דַייָ לְעַבְדִין וּלְאַמְהָן וִיהוֹן שָׁבִין לְשׁוֹבֵיהוֹן וְיִפְלְחוּן בִּמְשַׁעְבְּדֵיהוֹן:


And the nations shall take them, and shall bring them to their land; and the house of Israel shall possess them in the land of the Shekinah of the Lord for servants and handmaids: and they shall take them, captives, whose captives they were; those who had made them their servants shall now serve them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:3)


וִיהֵי בְּיוֹמָא דִינִיחַ יְיָ לָךְ מִצַעֲרָךְ וּמִמַרְוָתָךְ וּמִן פוּלְחָנָא קַשְׁיָא דְאִתְפְּלַח בָּךְ:


And it shall come to pass in that day, that the Lord shall give thee rest from thy distress, from thy bondage, and from the hard servitude which was served upon thee,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:4)


וְתִטוֹל מַתְלָא הָדֵין עַל מַלְכָּא דְבָבֶל וְתֵימַר אֵיכְדֵין בְּטֵילַת מַרְוַת מְשַׁעְבְּדָנָא סָף תְּקוֹף חַיָבָא:


That thou shalt take up this proverb against the king of Babylon, and say, How hath the dominion that made us servile ceased! How is come to an end the strength of the sinner!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:5)


תְּבַר יְיָ תְּקוֹף רַשִׁיעִין שׁוּלְטַן חַיָבִין:


The Lord hath broken the strength of the wicked, the government of sinners,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:6)


דַהֲוָה מָחֵי עַמְמִין בִּתְקוֹף מְחָא דִי לָא פְּסִיק מַפְלַח בִּתְקוֹף עַמְמִין מַפְלַח וְלָא מְנָע:


Of him who was smiting the people in fury, with a perpetual stroke, of him who was ruling the nations in anger, ruling and none hindered.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:7)


נָחַת שְׁדוֹכַת כָּל אַרְעָא יַבְעוּ תּוּשְׁבְּחָא:


The whole earth is at rest, is quiet, they rejoice with a song.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:8)


אַף שִׁלְטוֹנִין חֲדִיאוּ עֲלָךְ עַתִּירֵי נִכְסַיָא אָמְרִין מֵעִידַן דִשְׁכֵיבְתָּא לָא סְלִיק מְחַבְּלָא עֲלָנָא:


Rulers also rejoiced over thee; the rich in treasures said, Since thou art laid down, the destroyer is not come up upon us.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:9)


שְׁאוֹל מִלְרַע זָעַת לָךְ לִקֳדָמוּת מֵיתָךְ אֲעִירַת לָךְ גִבָּרִין כָּל עַתִּירֵי נִכְסַיָא אָקִימוּ מִכּוּרְסְוַתְהוֹן כָּל מַלְכֵי עַמְמַיָא:


Hell from beneath is moved for thee, to meet thee at thy coming; she rouseth for thee the mighty: all the rich in treasures, all the kings of the nations rise from their thrones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:10)


כּוּלְהוֹן יְתִיבוּן וְיֵימְרוּן לָךְ אַף אַתְּ מְרַעְתָּא כְוָתָנָא לָנָא דְמִיתָא:


All of them shall answer and say unto thee, Art thou become sick as we? Unto us, thou hast become like.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:11)


אִתְּחַת לִשְׁאוֹל יְקָרָךְ אִתְרְגוּשַׁת תּוּשְׁבְּחַת זְמָרָךְ תְּחוֹתָךְ יְשַׁווּן רִמָה וְעֵיל מִנָךְ רִיחֲשָׁא:


Thy glory is brought down to the grave, the rattling noise of the songs of the musical instruments: the worm is spread under thee, and upon thee vermin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:12)


אֵיכְדֵין אִתְרְכִינְתָּא מִן רוּמָא דַהֲוֵיתָא זִיוְתָן בְּגוֹ בְְנֵי אֱנָשָׁא כְּכוֹכָב נוֹגְהָא בֵּין כּוֹכְבַיָא אִתְרַטֵישְׁתָּא לְאַרְעָא דַהֲוֵיתָא קְטוֹל בְּעַמְמַיָא:


How art thou cast down from on high, who wast shining among the sons of men as the star Venus among the stars: thou art dashed down to the earth, who wast a slaughterer among the nations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:13)


וְאַתְּ אֲמַרְתְּ בְּלִבָּךְ לְרוּמָא אֶסַק עֵיל מִן עַמֵיהּ דֵאלָהָא אֲשַׁוֵי כוּרְסֵי מַלְכוּתִי וְאֶתֵּיב בְטוּר זְמַן בִּסְיָפֵי צִפּוּנָא:


And thou, thou hast said in thy heart, I will climb up the heavens; I will place the throne of my kingdom above the people of God, and I will sit upon the mountain of the solemn assembly upon the ends of the north;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:14)


אֶסַק עִלַוֵי כָל עַמָא אֶהִי עִילָאִי מִכּוּלְהוֹן:


I will ascend over all people; I will be higher than all of them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:15)


בְּרַם לִשְׁאוֹל תִּתְחַת לִסְיָפֵי גוֹב בֵּית אַבְדָנָא:


But to the grave thou shalt be brought down, to the ends of the pit of the place of perdition.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:16)


חָזָךְ עֲלָךְ יְדוּקוּן בָּךְ יִסְתַּכְּלוּן וְיֵימְרוּן הָדֵין גַבְרָא אֲזִיעַ אַרְעָא אַצְדֵי מַלְכְּוָתָא:


Those that see thee shall look attentively at thee, they shall consider thee, and say, "Is this the man that made the earth to tremble, that laid waste kingdoms?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:17)


שַׁוֵי תֵּבֵל כְּמַדְבְּרָא וְקִרְווֹהִי פִּגֵר לַאֲסִירוֹהִי לָא פְּתַח תַּרְעָא:


That made the world as a wilderness, that laid waste its cities, and did not open the gate to his prisoners?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:18)


כָּל מַלְכֵי עַמְמַיָא כּוּלְהוֹן שָׁכְבִין בִּיקָר גְבַר בְּבֵית עַלְמֵיהּ:


All the kings of the nations, all of them, lie down in glory, each in his eternal abode."


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:19)


וְאַתְּ אִתְרְכִיתְּנָא מִקִבְרָךְ כְּחָט טְמִיר חֲפֵי קְטִילִין מְטַעֲנֵי חֶרֶב נַחֲתִין לְגוֹב אַבְנֵי בֵּית אַבְדָנָא כִּפְגַר מְדַשְׁדָשׁ:


But thou art cast out of thy grave as a hidden branch, as those hidden among the slain, as those pierced by the sword, as those that are descending the lowest pit of the place of destruction, as a carcass trodden under foot.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:20)


לָא תְהֵי כְּחַד מִנְהוֹן בִּקְבוּרָה אֲרֵי אַרְעָךְ חַבֵּילְתָּא וְעַמָךְ קָטֵילְתָּא לָא יִתְקַיֵים לַעֲלָם זֶרַע מַבְאָשִׁין:


Thou shalt not be like one of them in burial; because thou hast destroyed thy country, thou hast slain thy people: the seed of evildoers shall not be established for ever.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:21)


אַתְקִינוּ לִבְנֵיהוֹן קִטוֹל בְּחוֹבַי אֲבָהַתְהוֹן דִלְמָא יְקוּמוּן וְיַחְסְנוּן אַרְעָא וְיִתְמְלוּן אַפֵּי תֵבֵל בְּעֵיל דְבָבוּ:


Prepare ye slaughter for his children, for the iniquity of their fathers; lest they rise and possess the earth, and the faces of the world be filled with enemies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:22)


וְאִתְגְלֵי לְאִתְפְּרָעָא מִנְהוֹן אֲמַר יְיָ צְבָאוֹת וֶאֱשֵׁיצֵי לְבָבֶל שׁוּם וּשְׁאָר בַּר וּבַר בַּר אֲמַר יְיָ:


And I will reveal myself to take vengeance of them, saith the Lord of hosts; and I will destroy of Babylon the name, and the remnant, and the son, and the son's son, saith the Lord of hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:23)


וַאֲשַׁוִינָהּ לִירוּתַת קוּפְדִין בֵּית צָדוּ בִּיצִין דְמַיָן וֶאֱהַמְמִינָהּ כְּמָא דִמְהַמְמִין בִּמְבִינָא וַאֲתַבְּבִינָהּ לְשֵׁיצָאָה אֲמַר יְיָ צְבָאוֹת:


And I will make it an inheritance for the hedgehog, a place of desolation, pools of water: and I will sweep her as they are sweeping with a besom: and I will throw her down to destroy her, saith the Lord of hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:24)


קַיָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת לְמֵימָר אִם לָא כְּמָא דַחֲשֵׁיבִית כֵּן תְּהֵא וּכְמָא דְיַעֲצִית הִיא תָקוּם:


The Lord of hosts hath sworn, saying, Surely as I have thought, so shall it be; and as I have purposed, so shall it stand.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:25)


לְמִתְבַּר אַתּוּרָאָה בְּאַרְעִי וְעַל טוּרֵי עַמִי אַדוֹשְׁשִׁינֵיהּ וְתֶעְדֵי מִנְהוֹן מַרְוָתֵיהּ וְנִירֵיהּ מֵעַל צַוְרֵיהוֹן יִתְפְּסַק:


To break the Assyrian in my land, and upon the mountains of my people I will tread him under foot; then there shall depart from them his dominion, and his yoke off from their necks shall cease.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:26)


דָא מַלְכָּא דִי מְלִיךְ עַל כָּל יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא וְדָא גְבוּרְתָּא דִי מְרַמְמָא דְשַׁלִיט עַל כָּל מַלְכְּוָתָא:


This is the purpose that is purposed upon all the inhabitants of the earth; and this is the might of the ruler which is lifted up over all the kingdoms.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:27)


אֲרֵי יְיָ צְבָאוֹת מְלַךְ וּמַן יַעְדֵי וּגְבוּרְתֵּיהּ מְרַמְמָא וְלֵית דִיתִיבִינֵיהּ:


For the Lord of hosts has purposed, and who can remove it? yea, His power is exalted, and who can withstand it?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:28)


בְּשַׁתָּא דְמִית מַלְכָּא אָחָז הֲוָה מַטַל נְבוּאָתָא הָדָא:


In the year that king Ahaz died was the burden of this prophecy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:29)


לָא תֵיחְדוּן פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי כּוּלְכוֹן אֲרֵי אִתְּבַּר שׁוּלְטַן דַהֲוָה מַפְלַח בְּכוֹן אֲרֵי מִבְּנֵי בְנוֹהִי דְיִשָׁי יִפּוֹק מְשִׁיחָא וִיהוֹן עוֹבָדוֹהִי בְּכוֹן כְּחֵיוֵי מַפְרִיחַ:


Rejoice not, 0 Philistia, all of ye, because the government that hath made you servile is broken; because from the children of Jesse shall come forth the Messiah, and his works among you shall be as a flying serpent.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:30)


וְיִתְפַּרְנְסוּן חֲשֵׁיכֵי עַמָא וְעִנְוְתָנַיָא בְּיוֹמוֹהִי לְרוֹחֲצָן יִשְׁרוֹן וְיֵמִית בְּכַפְנָא בְּנֵךְ וּשְׁאַר עַמִיךְ יִקְטוֹל:


And the poor of my people shall be fed, and the meek in his days shall dwell in safety; and he shall slay with famine thy children, and the remnant of thy people he shall kill.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:31)


אֱלִילִי עַל תַּרְעָךְ צַוְחִי עַל קִרְוָךְ אִתְבַּרְתּוּן פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי כּוּלְכוֹן אֲרֵי מִצִפּוּנָא פּוּרְעֲנוּתָא אָתֵי וְלֵית דִמְאַחַר בְּזִמְנוֹהִי:


Howl, on account of thy gate, cry on account of thy city, ye, O Philistines, all of you; ye shall be broken: because from the north vengeance is coming, and is not delaying its appointed time.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 14:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/14:32)


וּמַה יְבַסְרוּן אִזְגַדֵי עַמְמַיָא אֲרֵי יְיָ שַׁכְלֵיל צִיוֹן וּבָהּ יִתְרַחֲצוּן חֲשִׁיכֵי עַמָא:


And what good news will they tell the messengers of the nations? That the Lord hath founded Zion, and the poor of His people shall trust in it.



## Chapter 15

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:1)


מַטַל כָּס דִלְוָט לְאַשְׁקָאָה יַת מוֹאָב אֲרֵי בְּלֵילְיָא אִתְבְּזִיזַת לְחַיַת מוֹאָב וְאִינוּן דְמִיכִין וּבְלֵילְיָא אִתְכְּבֵישׁ כְּרַכָּא דְמוֹאָב וְאִינוּן רְדִימִין:


The Burden Of The Cup Of Cursing To Give Moab To Drink. Because in the night Leheyath Moab is destroyed, and they were asleep. Yea, in the night the fortified city of Moab was subdued, and they were in deep slumber.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:2)


סְלִיקוּ לְבָתַּיָא דְדִיבוֹן לְבָמָתָא לְמִבְכֵּי עַל נְבוֹ וְעַל מֵידְבָא מוֹאֲבָאֵי מְיַלְלִין בְּכָל רֵישֵׁיהוֹן מְרָטָה וּבְכָל דְקַן גִלוּחַ:
