They went up to the houses in Dibon, to the high places, to weep. Over Nebo, and over Medeba the Moabites are howling: on every head is baldness; every beard is shorn.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:3)


בְּשׁוֹקֵיהוֹן יִסְרוּן סַקִין עַל אִיגָרֵיהוֹן וּבִרְחוֹבֵיהוֹן כּוּלְהוֹן מְיַלְלִין מְצַוְחִין וּבָכָן:


In their streets they shall gird themselves with sackcloth: on their house-tops, and in their broad-places all of them shall howl, crying and weeping.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:4)


וּצְוָחוּ יַתְבֵי חֶשְׁבּוֹן וְאֶלְעָלֵה עַד יַהַץ אִשְׁתְּמַע קַלְהוֹן עַל כֵּן מְזַרְזֵי מוֹאָב מְיַלְלִין וְעַל נַפְשֵׁיהוֹן מְצַוְחִין:


And the inhabitants of Heshbon and Elealeh shall cry out aloud; unto Jahats is their voice heard: therefore the armed of Moab are howling, and crying out aloud for their souls.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:5)


בְּלִבְּהוֹן מוֹאֲבָאֵי יֵימְרוּן לְמַעֲרַק עַד צוֹעַר עֶגְלַת תִּלְתּוּת רַבְּתָא אֲרֵי בְמַסוּקִית לוּחִית בִּדְבָכָן יִסְקוּן בֵּיהּ אֲרֵי בְמָחוֹתִית חוֹרְנַיִם צִוְחַת תְּבִירֵי קְרָב יְבַסְרוּן:


The Moabites shall think to flee to Zoar, unto Eglath Tiltum' Rabb'tha; for the ascent of Luhith they shall ascend it as with weeping; because in the descent of Horonaim they shall declare the cry of the broken in battle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:6)


אֲרֵי מֵי נִמְרִים לְצָדוּ יְהוֹן אֲרֵי יָבֵשׁ עִסְבָּא סַף דְתָאָה יְרוֹק לָא הֲוָה:


For the waters of Nimrim shall become desolate; for the herbage is withered away; the grass faileth, there is no green herb.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:7)


עַל כֵּן שְׁאָר נִכְסֵיהוֹן יִתְבַּזְזוּן וּתְחוּמְהוּן דְעַל יַמָא מַעַרְבָא יִתְנְסֵיב מִנְהוֹן:


Therefore the remnant of their riches, shall be plundered; and their border, which is by the western sea, shall be taken from them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:8)


אֲרֵי אַקְפַת צִוְחָתָא יַת תְּחוּם מוֹאָב עַד אֶגְלַיִם מְיַלְלִין וּבְאֵר אֵלִים מְצַוְחִין:


For the cry encompasses the border of Moab; unto Eglaim are they howling, and unto Beer Elim they are crying aloud.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/15:9)


אֲרֵי מֵי דִימוֹן אִתְמְלִיאוּ דַם קְטוֹלִין אֲרֵי אֱמַנֵי עַל דִימוֹן כְּנִישַׁת מַשִׁירְיָן לְשֵׁיזָבַת מוֹאָב מֶלֶךְ בְּמַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ יִסַק וּלְמִבָּז שְׁאָרָא דְאַרְעֲהוֹן:


For the waters of Dimon shall be full of blood: for I will appoint upon Dimon an assembly of armies. Upon the escaped of Moab, a king with his army shall ascend; yea, to spoil the remainder of their land.



## Chapter 16

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:1)


יְהוֹן מַסְקֵי מִסִין לִמְשִׁיחָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְיִתְקוֹף עַל דַהֲווֹ בְּמַדְבְּרָא טוּר כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְצִיוֹן:


They shall bring tributes to the Messiah of Israel, who shall prevail over those who are in the wilderness, unto the mountain of the congregation of Zion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:2)


וִיהֵי כְעוֹפָא דְאַפְרָחוֹהִי מִקִנֵיהּ וִיטַלְטֵיל יִתַּפְקָן וִיהֶוְיָן בְּנַת מוֹאָב מְגִיזַן לְהוֹן לְאַרְנוֹן:


Otherwise it shall come to pass, as a bird which men have driven away, being cast out of its nest, the daughters of Moab shall be led round about, made to Wadi Arnon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:3)


אִתְמְלִיכוּ מְלַךְ עֱבִידוּ עֵצָה שַׁוִיאוּ כְלֵילְיָא טוּלֵיךְ בִּימָמָא בְּגוֹ טִיהֲרָא טַמְרֵי מְטַלְטְלַיָא וּמְבַדְרַיָא לָא תִקְרְבוּן:


Take counsel, execute counsel. Make thy shadow as the night in the day, in the midst of the noonday: hide the outcasts; touch not the dispersed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:4)


יִתְיַתְבוּן בִּיךְ מְטַלְטְלֵי מַלְכוּת מוֹאָב הֲוֵי סִתְרָא לְהוֹן מִן קֳדָם בְּזוֹזִין אֲרֵי סַף מֵעַיָקָא אִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בְזָזָא סָפוּ כָּל דַהֲווֹ דָיְשִׁין מִן אַרְעָא:


Let my outcasts dwell with thee, O kingdom of Moab! be thou a covert to them on account of the spoilers: for the extortioner is at an end, the spoiler ceaseth, all that trampled under foot have come to au end.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:5)


בְּכֵן מְשִׁיחָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יְתַקֵן בְּטוּב כּוּרְסוֹהִי וִיתֵיב עֲלוֹהִי בִקְשׁוֹט בְּקַרְתָּא דְדָוִד דָאִין וְתָבַע דִין וַעֲבַד קְשׁוֹט:


Then the throne of the Messiah of Israel shall be established in goodness, and he shall sit upon it in truth in the city of David, judging and seeking justice, and executing truth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:6)


שְׁמַעְנָא רַבְרְבֵי מוֹאָב דְמִתְגָאָן לַחֲדָא יַקִירֵיהוֹן וּמְפַנְקֵיהוֹן וְגַוְתָנוֹתְהוֹן לָקֳבֵל פּוּרְעָנוּתְהוֹן לָא שָׁוֵי לְהוֹן:


We have heard of the princes of Moab; who are very proud; their nobles, and their delicate ones, and their pride shall not profit them, when their punishment comes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:7)


בְּכֵן מְיַלְלִין מוֹאֲבָאֵי עַל מוֹאֲבָאֵי כּוּלְהוֹן מְצַוְחִין וּמְיַלְלִין עַל אֱנַשׁ כְּרַךְ תּוּקְפֵהוֹן דָוָן יֵמְרוּן בְּרַם כְּבִישִׁין:


Therefore the Moabites shall howl: over the Moabites all of them shall cry aloud; and they shall howl over the men of the city of their strength; they, mourning, shall say, surely they are conquered.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:8)


אֲרֵי אִתְבְּזִיזָא מַשִׁרְיַת חֶשְׁבּוֹן אִתְקַטְלָא סִיעַת שִׂבְמָה מַלְכֵי עַמְמַיָא קְטִילוּ שִׁלְטוֹנֵיהוֹן עַד יַעְזֵר מְטוֹ טָעוֹ לְמַדְבְּרָא מְטַלְטִלֵיהוֹן גָזוּ עֲבַרוּ יַמָא:


Because the armies of Heshbon are spoiled, the multitude of Sebama are killed, the kings of the people have killed their rulers; they reached unto Jazer; they strayed to the desert; their outcasts passed by, they crossed the sea.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:9)


עַל כֵּן כְּמָא דְאַיְתֵיתִי מַשִׁרְיָן עַל יַעְזֵר כֵּן אַיְתֵי קְטוֹלִין עַל שִׂבְמָא אֱרַוִינָךְ דִמְעָתָא חֶשְׁבּוֹן וְאֶלְעָלֵה אֲרֵי עַל חַצְדִיךְ וְעַל קִיטְפִיךְ בְּזוֹזִין נְפָלוּ:


Therefore, as I have brought armies against Jazer, so will I bring slayers against Sibmah; I will water thee with my tears, O Heshbon and Elealeh! for upon thy harvest, and upon thy vintage the robbers have fallen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:10)


וְתִתְכְּנֵישׁ בִיעַ וְחֶדְוָה מִן כַּרְמְלָא וּבְכַרְמַיָא לָא יְדוּצוּן וְלָא יְחַיְכוּן וַחֲמַר בְּמַעְצְרַיָא לָא יַעְצְרוּן עָצוֹרִין עָצוֹר אַבְטֵלִית:


And joy is taken away and gladness out of the fruitful field, and in the vineyards they shall not rejoice, neither shall they play sport; in the vats the treaders shall not tread out the wine; the treading of grapes I have made to cease.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:11)


עַל כֵּן מְעֵיהוֹן דְמוֹאֲבָאֵי כְּכִּנָרַיָא הָמָן וְלִבְּהוֹן עַל אֱנַשׁ כְּרַךְ תוּקְפֵיהוֹן דָוָן:


Wherefore the bowels of the Moabites shall sound as a harp, and their heart shall mourn over the men of the city of their strength.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:12)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יֶחֱזֵי אֲרֵי יִלְאָה מוֹאָב עַל בָּמָתָא וְיֵעוּל לְבֵית טַעֲוָתֵיהּ לְמִבְעֵי וְלָא יִכּוּל:


And it shall come to pass, that one shall see, that when Moab is wearied out upon his high place, he shall enter his idol-temple to pray, yet he shall not prevail.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:13)


דֵין פִּתְגָמָא דִי מַלֵיל יְיָ עַל מוֹאֲבָאֵי מִבְּכֵן:


This is the word which the Lord spake concerning the Moabites long ago:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/16:14)


וּכְעַן מַלֵיל יְיָ לְמֵימָר בִּתְלַת שְׁנִין כִּשְׁנֵי אֲגִירָא וְיֵסוּף יְקָרְהוֹן דְמוֹאֲבָאֵי בְּכֹל הֲמוֹן סַגְיָא וּשְׁאָר צִבְחַר כִּזְעֵיר יְסוּף כָּל יְקָרֵיהוֹן:


But now the Lord hath spoken, saying: Within three years, as the years of an hireling, the glory of the Moabites shall come to an end, with all that great multitude; and the remnant shall be small, a few; all their glory shall come to an end.



## Chapter 17

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:1)


מַטֵל כַּס דִלְוָט לְאַשְׁקָאָה יַת דַמֶשֶׂק הָא דַמֶשֶׂק מִתְעַדְיָא מִמַלְכוּ וּתְהֵי לִכְרַךְ מְחַמְרָא:


The Burden Of The Cup Of Cursing To Give Damascus To Drink. Behold, Damascus is removed, so as to be no more a kingdom: and it shall be as a city laid waste.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:2)


שְׁבִיקַן קִרְוֵיהוֹן חֲרָבוּ לְבֵית מִשְׁרֵי עֶדְרִין דְעָן יְהוֹן וְיִשְׁרוֹן וְלֵית דְמָנִיד:


Their cities are deserted, they are laid waste; they shall be for dwelling-places for flocks of sheep; they shall lie down, and none shall make them to move away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:3)


וְיַבְטֵיל שׁוּלְטָן מֵאֶפְרַיִם וּמַלְכוּ מִדַמֶשֶׂק וּשְׁאָר יְחִידָאִין מֵאֲרַם וִיקָרְהוֹן כִּיקָר בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יְהוֹן אֲמַר יְיָ צְבָאוֹת:


The government shall cease from Ephraim, and the kingdom from Damascus, and the remnant of the solitary ones from Syria, and their glory shall be as the glory of the children of Israel, saith the Lord of hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:4)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יִמְאַךְ יְקָר יַעֲקֹב וְעוֹתֵר יְקָרֵיהּ יִגְלֵי:


And it shall come to pass at that time, that the glory of Jacob shall be humbled, and the riches of his glory shall be carried away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:5)


וִיהֵי כְּמִכְנַשׁ חָצֵד קָמָה וְדַרְעֵיהּ שׁוּבְּלִין יַחֲצוֹד וִיהֵי כִמְלַקֵט שׁוּבְּלִין בְּמֵישַׁר גִבָּרַיָא:


And it shall be, as when one gathereth the standing harvest, and reapeth the ears with his arm; and it shall be as one that is gathering ears in the valley of giants.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:6)


וְיִשְׁתַּאֲרוּן בֵּיהּ עוֹלְלִין כְּבִיעוּר זֵיתָא תְּרֵין תְּלָתָא גַרְגְרִין בְּרֵישׁ עַנְפָא אַרְבְּעָא חַמְשָׁא בְסוֹכָא מֵרְדָא כֵּן יִשְׁתַּאֲרוּן יְחִידָאִין צַדִיקַיָא בְּגוֹ עַלְמָא בְּנֵי מַלְכְּוָתָא אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Yet a gleaning shall be left in it, as in the shaking of the olive tree, two or three berries on the top of the uppermost branch; so shall the righteous be left lonely in the midst of the world, among the kingdoms, saith the Lord, the God of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:7)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יִסְתְּמֵיךְ אֱנָשָׁא לְפוּלְחַן עָבְדֵיהּ וְעֵינוֹהִי לְמֵימַר קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יְסַבְּרוּן:


At that time a man shall stay himself upon the service of his Maker, and his eyes shall hopefully look for the WORD, the Holy One of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:8)


וְלָא יִסְתְּמֵיךְ עַל אֱגוֹרַיָא עוֹבַד יְדוֹהִי וּדְאַתְקְנָא אֶצְבְּעְתֵיהּ לָא יִתְקַיֵים וַאֲשֵׁרַיָא וַחֲנִיסְנְסַיָא:


He shall not rest upon the altars, the work of his hands, or stay himself upon that which his fingers have prepared, neither upon the groves nor upon the sun-images.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:9)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יֶהֱוְיָן קִרְוֵי תוּקְפֵיהוֹן כִּכְרַךְ דַחֲרִיב וְאִתְחַמַר דִי אִשְׁתְּבֵיק מִן קֳדָם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּתְהֵי לְצָדוּ:


At that time the cities of their strength shall be as a fortification that was laid desolate and waste from before the children of Israel, and each shall be a desolation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:10)


אֲרֵי שְׁבַקְתְּ אֱלָהָא דְפוּרְקָנִיךְ וְדַחְלַת תַּקִיפָא דִי מֵימְרֵיהּ בְּסַעֲדָךְ לָא אִדְכַּרְתְּ בְּדִיל דְאַתְּ נְצִיבָא נִצְבָא בְּחִירָא וְעוֹבְדֵין מְקַלְקְלִין אַסְגֵית:


Because thou hast forsaken the God of thy salvation, and thou hast not remembered the fear of the Mighty One, whose Word was thy support; therefore thou hast planted a choice plant, and hast multiplied despicable works.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:11)


בַּאֲתַר דְאִתְקַדַשְׁתּוּן לְמֶהֱוֵי עַם תַּמָן קִלְקַלְתּוּן עוֹבָדֵיכוֹן וְאַף כַּד עַלְתּוּן לַאֲרַע בֵּית שְׁכִנְתִּי תַּמָן הֲוָה חֲזֵי לְכוֹן לְמִפְלַח שְׁבַקְתּוּן פּוּלְחָנִי וּפְלַחְתּוּן לְטַעֲוָתָא אַרְחֵיקְתּוּן תְּיוּבְתָּא עַד דִמְטָא יוֹם תְּבַרְכוֹן בְּכֵן הֲוָה כֵּיבְכוֹן לְמַפְחַת נְפָשׁ:


After that ye were sanctified to be a people, there ye made your works despicable; and also when ye came to the land, the place of my Shekinah, there it was your duty' to worship me; ye forsook my worship, and ye served idols: ye abhorred repentance till the day of your destruction came, then your sorrow was a breathing out of soul.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:12)


וַי הֲמוֹן עַמְמִין סַגִיאִין דִי כְמַהֲמֵי יַמָא הָמָן וְאִתְרְגוּשַׁת מַלְכְּוָן דִכְאִתְרְגוּשַׁת מַיִין תַּקִיפִין מִתְרַגְשִׁין:


Woe to the multitude of many people, which make a noise like the noise of the sea, and to the tumultuous assembly of kings, which roar like the roaring of mighty waters.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:13)


מַלְכְּוָן דִכְאִתְרְגוּשַׁת מַיִין סַגִיאִין מִתְרַגְשִׁין וִיזוּף בֵּיהּ וְיֶעְרוּק מֵרָחִיק וִיתְרַדֵף כְּמוֹץ טוּרַיָא קֳדָם רוּחָא וּכְגַלְגְלָא קֳדָם עַלְעוּלָא:


The kings roar like the roaring of many waters, and he shall rebuke him, and he shall flee far off, and shall be driven as the chaff of the hills before the wind, and like a rolling thing before the whirlwind.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/17:14)


לְעִדַן רַמְשָׁא וְהָא כִדְלָא הֲוָה עַד דְלָא צַפְרָא וְהָא כִדְלֵיתוֹהִי דֵין חוּלַק לְאָסְנָנָא וַעֲדַב לְבָזְזָנָא:


And at eveningtide behold, as if he had not been; and before the morning behold, as if he had not been. This is the portion of those that hate us, and the lot of those who spoil us.



## Chapter 18

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:1)


וַי אַרְעָא דְאָתָן לָהּ בִּסְפִינָן מֵאַרְעָא רְחִיקָא וְקִלְעֵיהוֹן פְּרִיסָן כְּנִשְׁרָא דְטָאִיס בְּכַנְפוֹהִי דְמֵעִבַּר לְנַהֲרֵי הוֹדוּ:


Ho! the land which is beyond the rivers of India, to which they come in ships from a far country, whose sails are spread as an eagle flieth with its wings.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:2)


דִמְשַׁלַח בְּיַמָא אִזְגַדִין וּבִדְגוּגִין עַל אַפֵּי מַיָא אֱזִילוּ אִזְגַדִין קַלִילִין לְוַת עַמָא אֲנִיסָא וּבְזִיזָא לְוַת עַמָא דִתְקַיף מִבְּכֵין וּלְהַלָא עַמָא אֲנִיסָא וּבְזִיזָא דְבָזוּ עַמְמַיָא אַרְעֵיהּ:


That sendeth ambassadors by the sea, even in ships on the face of the waters, saying, Go, ye swift messengers, unto a people oppressed and plundered, unto a people that was mighty in times past, and shall be so in time to come: a people oppressed and robbed, whose land the nations have spoiled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:3)


כָּל דְיָתְבִין בְּתֵבֵל וּדְשָׁרָן בְּאַרְעָא כְּמִזְקַף אָתָא עַל טוּרַיָא תֶחְזוּן אָתָא וּכְמִתְקַע שׁוֹפָרָא תִּשְׁמְעוּן פוּרְקַן:


All ye inhabitants of the world, and ye dwellers on the earth, when the standard is lifted up on the mountains, ye shall see the standard, and the trumpet shall sound, ye shall hear of redemption.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:4)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ לִי אַנִיחַ לְעַמִי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאַשְׁקֵיט לְהוֹן וְאֶתְרְעִי מִמְדוֹר קוּדְשִׁי לְאוֹטָבָא לְהוֹן בִּרְכָן וְנַחֲמָן אַיְתִי לְהוֹן בִּפְרִיעַ כִּשְׁחִין פָּצִיחַ עַל שֶׁמֶשׁ כַּעֲנָן טַל בִּשְׁחִין חֲצַד:


For thus hath the Lord said unto me: I will give rest to my people, yea, I will make them to rest, and I will delight in my holy habitation to do them good: blessings and consolations I will bring upon them quickly, when the heat is intense through the heat of the sun, then I will be to them a cloud of dew as in the heat of harvest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:5)


אֲרֵי עַד לָא יִמְטֵי זְמַן חַצְדָא אִלָנָא לְאַפְרָחָא וּבִסְרָא מִנֵהּ סְמָדָר וְיִקְטוֹל שִׁלְטוֹנֵי עַמְמַיָא בְּחַרְבָּא וְיַת תַּקִיפֵיהוֹן יַעְדֵי וְיֶעְבַּר:


Before the time of harvest cometh the tree is ready to bud, and the unripe grape bursts forth into a flower: He shall slay the rulers of the nations with the sword, and their mighty ones He shall remove, and cause to pass away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:6)


יִשְׁתַּבְּקוּן כַּחֲדָא לְעוֹפָא דְטוּרַיָא וְלִבְעִירָא דְאַרְעָא בְּקַיְטָא יְהֵי שָׁרֵי עֲלוֹהִי כָּל עוֹפָא דִשְׁמַיָא וְכָל בְּעִירָא דְאַרְעָא עֲלוֹהִי יַעַבְּרוּן סִתְוָא:


They shall be left together unto the fowl of the mountains, and to the beasts of the earth: all the fowls of the heavens shall dwell upon them in the summer, and all the beasts of the earth shall winter upon them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/18:7)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יַיְתִינֵיהּ בְּתוּקְרַבְתָּא לִקֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת לְעַמָא אֲנֵיסָא וּבְזִיזָאוּ לְוַת עַמָא דְיִתְקֵף מִבְּכֵן וּלְהַלָא עַמָא אֲנִיסָא וּבָזִיזָא דְבַזוּ עַמְמַיָא אַרְעֵהּ לְאַתְרָא דְאִתְקְרֵי שְׁמָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת דִשְׁכִנְתֵּהּ בְּטוּרָא דְצִיוֹן:


At that time shall one bring an offering unto the Lord of hosts, to a people oppressed and spoiled, to a people that was mighty in times past, and shall be so in time to come, a people oppressed and robbed, whose land the nations have spoiled, unto the place which is called by the name of the Lord of hosts, whose Shekinah is in the mountain of Zion.



## Chapter 19

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:1)


מַטַל כַּס דִלְוָט לְאַשְׁקָאָה יַת מִצְרָאֵי הָא יְיָ מִתְגְלֵי בַּעֲנָן יְקָרֵהּ לְאִתְפְּרָעָא מִמִצְרַיִם וְיִתַּבְּרוּ טַעֲוַת מִצְרָאֵי מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי וְלִבָא דְמִצְרָאֵי יִתְמְסֵי בִּמְעֵיהוֹן:


The Burden Of The Cup Of Cursing To Make The Egyptians To Drink. Behold, the Lord is revealed in the cloud of His glory, to take vengeance of the Egyptians; and the idols of the Egyptians shall be broken before His face, and the heart of the Egyptians shall be melted within them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:2)


וֶאֱגָרֵי מִצְרָאֵי בְּמִצְרָאֵי וִיגִיחוּן קְרָב גְבַר בַּאֲחוּהִי וּגְבַר בְּחַבְרֵהּ קְרִי בִּקְרִי וּמָלְכוּ בְמָלְכוּ:


And I will excite Egyptians against Egyptians, and they shall wage war, every man against his brother, and every man against his neighbour: city against city, kingdom against kingdom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:3)


וְתִתְמְסֵי רוּחָא דְמִצְרָאֵי בִּמְעֵיהוֹן וְחַכִּימֵיהוֹן אֱסַלְעֵם וְיִתְבְּעוּן מִן טַעֲוָן וּמִן חֲרָשִׁין וּמִן בִּידִין וּמִן זְכוּרוּ:


And the spirit of the Egyptians shall melt within them, and their wise men I will destroy: and they shall seek to the idols, and to the charmers, to the deceitful, and to the diviners.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:4)


וְאֶמְסַר יַת מִצְרָאֵי בְּיַד רִבּוֹן קְשֵׁי וּמְלַךְ תַּקִיף יִשְׁלוֹט בְּהוֹן אֲמַר רִבּוֹן עַלְמָא יְיָ צְבָאוֹת:


And I will deliver the Egyptians into the hand of a cruel lord; and a fierce king shall rule over them, saith the Lord of the world, the Lord of hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:5)


וְיִצְדוּן מַיָא מִיַמָא וּנְהַרְהוֹן יֶחְרוֹב וְיִיבַשׁ:


And the waters of the sea shall be laid desolate, and their river shall be wasted and dried up.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:6)


וְיִצְדוּן נַהַרְוָתְהוֹן וְיִבְשׁוּן וְיֶחְרְבוּן נַהֲרֵיהוֹן עַמִיקַיָא קָנֵי וְגוֹמָא לָא יִסְקוּן:


And their rivers shall be laid waste, and their deep streams shall be dried up and wasted: the reeds and flags shall not grow.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:7)


וְיִיבַשׁ רוּבֵהּ דְנַהֲרָא וִיהֵי בְכֵיפֵי וְכָל בֵּית מִזְרַע נְהַרְהוֹן יִיבַשׁ וְיֶחֱרַב וְלָא יִצְמַח:


And the greatest part of the river shall wither, and that which is on its bank, and every sown place of their rivers shall wither, and shall be dried up, and nothing shall grow.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:8)


וְיִצְדוּן צַיְדֵי נוּנַיָא וְיִתְאַבְּלוּן כָּל דַהֲווֹ רָמָן בְּנַהֲרָא חַכְּתָא וּפַרְסֵי מַצְדָן עַל אַפֵּי מַיָא יִצְדוּן:


The fishermen shall be laid desolate, and all they that cast angle into the brook, and they that spread nets upon the faces of the waters shall be destroyed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:9)


וְיִבְהֲתוּן פַּלְחֵי כִּתָּנָא דִסְרִיקִין וּמְחָן מִנֵהּ מַצְדָן:


They shall be confounded which work combed flax, and weave nets of it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:10)


וִיהֵי אֲתַר בֵּית שָׁתֵי מְחָא כְבִישִׁין אֲתַר דַהֲווֹ עָבְדִין סִכְרָא וְכָנְשִׁין מַיָא גְבַר לְנַפְשֵׁהּ:


And there shall be trodden under foot the well watered place, where they have been making pits, waterpools each for himself.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:11)


בְּרַם אִטַפָּשׁוּ רַבְרְבַי טָאנֵס חַכִּימַיָא דְמַלְכוּהִי לְפַרְעֹה מְלַךְ דְטָעוּ אֵיכְדֵין תַּמְרוּן לְפַרְעֹה בְּנֵי חַכִּימִין אֲנַחְנָא וְאַתְּ בַּר מַלְכִין דְמִלְקַדְמִין:


Surely the princes of Tanes have become foolish; the wise, the counsellors of Pharaoh have counselled an erroneous counsel; how will Pharaoh, We are the sons of the wise, and thou art the son of ancient kings.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:12)


אָן אִינוּן חַכִימָךְ וִיחַווּן כְּעַן לָךְ וְיֵדְעוּן מַה מְלַךְ יְיָ צְבָאוֹת עַל מִצְרָיִם:


Where are thy wise men? Let them tell thee now, and let them know what the Lord of hosts has counselled against Egypt.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:13)


אִטְפַּשׁוּ רַבְרְבֵי טָאנֵס טְעוֹ אֱנָשׁ מַפִיס אַטְעַיוּ יַת מִצְרָאֵי רַבָּנֵי פַלְכָהָא:


The princes of Tanes have become foolish, the men of Memphis have erred; they caused the Egyptians, the lords of the provinces, to err.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:14)


יְיָ רָמָא בֵינֵיהוֹן רוּחַ דְטָעוּ וְאַטְעִיאוּ יַת מִצְרַיִם בְּכָל עוֹבְדֵיהוֹן כְּמָא דְטָעֵי רַוְיָא וּמִדַשְׁדֵשׁ בְּתִיוּבֵהּ:


The Lord hath sent among them a spirit of error, and they have caused the Egyptians to err in all their works, as a drunkard staggereth, and tramples in his vomit.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:15)


וְלָא יְהֵי לְמִצְרַיִם מַלְכָא דִמְלַךְ רֵישׁ וְהֶגְמוֹן שִׁלְטוֹן וְאִטְרוֹן:


And the Egyptians shall have no king to reign, no prince, noble, governor, or ruler.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:16)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יְהוֹן מִצְרָאֵי חַלָשִׁין כְּנִשִׁין וִיזוּעוּן וְיִתַּבְּרוּן מִן קֳדָם אֱרָמוּת גְבוּרְתָּא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת דְהוּא מֵרִים עֲלֵיהוֹן:


At that time the Egyptians shall be weak, they shall be afraid, and fear on account of the exultation of the power of the Lord of hosts, which He is lifting up against them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:17)


וּתְהֵי אַרְעָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה לְמִצְרַיִם לְדַחֲלָא כָּל דְיִדְכַּר יָתָהּ לֵיהּ יְזוּעַ מִן קֳדָם מַלְכָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת דְהוּא מְלִיךְ עֲלֵיהוֹן:


And the land of the house of Judah shall be a terror unto Egypt; if anyone mention it unto them, they shall tremble because of the counsel of the Lord of hosts, which he hath counselled against them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:18)


בְעִדָנָא הַהִיא יֶהֶוְיָן חֲמֵשׁ קִרְוִין בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מְמַלְלָן מַמְלַל כְּנַעֲנָאָה וְקַיְמָן בִּשְׁמָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת קַרְתָּא בֵית שֶׁמֶשׁ דַעֲתִידָא לְמֶחְרַב יִתְאַמַר הִיא חֲדָא מִנְהוֹן:


At that time, there shall be five cities in the land of Egypt, speaking the language of Canaan, and swearing by the name of the Lord of hosts. The city of Beth-Shemesh, which is to be destroyed, shall be called one of them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:19)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יְהֵי מַדְבְּחָא מְתַקֵן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּגוֹ אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וְקָמְתָא בִּסְטַר תְּחוּמָהּ קֳדָם יְיָ:


At that time there shall be prepared an altar before the Lord in the midst of the land of Egypt; and a pillar by the border thereof before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:20)


וִיהֵי לְאָת וּלְסָהִיד קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם אֲרֵי יְצַלוּן קֳדָם יְיָ מִן קֳדָם דָחֲקֵיהוֹן וִישַׁלַח לְהוֹן פָּרִיק וְדַיָן וִישֵׁיזְבִינוּן:


And it shall be for a sign, and for a witness before the Lord of hosts in the land of Egypt, when they shall pray before the Lord because of their oppressors, and He shall send unto them a Saviour, and a Judge, and He shall deliver them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:21)


וְתִתְגְלֵי גְבוּרְתָּא דַייָ לְאוֹטָבָא לְמִצְרָאֵי וְיֵדְעוּן מִצְרָאֵי לְמִדְחַל מִן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא וְיִפְלְחוּן בְּנִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין וּבְקוּרְבָּנִין וִינַדְרוּן נִדְרִין קֳדָם יְיָ וִישַׁלְמוּן:


And the power of the Lord shall be revealed to do good to the Egyptians, and the Egyptians shall know the fear of the Lord at that time, and they shall worship with holy sacrifices and offerings; yea, they shall vow vows before the Lord, and shall perform them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:22)


וְיִמְחֵי יְיָ יַת מִצְרַיִם מְחָא וְיַסִנוּן וִיתוּבוּן לְפוּלְחָנָא דַייָ וִיקַבֵּל צְלוֹתֵיהוֹן וְיַסֵי יַתְהוֹן:


And the Lord shall smite Egypt with a stroke, and shall heal them, and they shall return to the worship of the Lord, and He shall hear their prayers, and shall heal them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:23)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא תְּהֵי אוֹרַח כְבִישָׁא מִמִצְרַיִם לְאַתּוּר וִינוּחוּן אֲתּוּרָאֵי לְמִצְרָאֵי וּמִצְרָאֵי בְּאַתּוּרָאֵי וְיִפְלְחוּן מִצְרָאֵי יַת אַתּוּרָאֵי:


And at that time there shall be a highway from Egypt to Assyria; and the Assyrians shall fight against the Egyptians, and the Egyptians against the Assyrians, and the Egyptians shall serve the Assyrians.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:24)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יְהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל תְּלִיתָאָה לְמִצְרָאֵי וּלְאַתּוּרָאֵי בִּרְכָא בְּגוֹ דְאַרְעָא:


And at that time Israel shall be a third party to the Egyptians and to the Assyrians, a blessing in the midst of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 19:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/19:25)


דְבָרְכֵהּ יְיָ צְבָאוֹת לְמֵימָר בְּרִיךְ עַמִי דְאַפְקֵית מִמִצְרַיִם דְעַל דְחָבוּ קֳדָמַי אַגְלֵיתִי יַתְהוֹן לְאַתּוּר וּכְדוּ דְתָבוּ מַתְקְרָן עַמִי וְאַחְסַנְתִּי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Whom the Lord of hosts hath blessed, saying, Blessed be my people, whom I have brought out of Egypt; and because they sinned before me, I carried them captive into Assyria, but when they repent, they are called my people, and Israel mine inheritance.



## Chapter 20

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:1)


בְּשַׁתָּא דַאֲתָא תַרְתָּן לְאַשְׁדוֹד כַּד שְׁלַח יָתֵהּ סַרְגוֹן מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר וְאַגִיחַ קְרָבָא בְאַשְׁדוֹד וְכַבְּשָׁהּ:


In the year that Tharthan marched to Ashdod, (when Sargon, the king of Assyria, sent him,) and he waged battle against Ashdod, and subdued it;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:2)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא גְזַר יְיָ בְּיַד יְשַׁעְיָה בַּר אָמוֹץ לְמֵימָר אֱזֵיל וְתֵיסַר סַקָא בְחַרְצָךְ וּמְסָנָךְ תִּשְׁלוֹף מֵעַל רִגְלָךְ וַעֲבַד כֵּן אָזַל פָחֵיחַ וְיָחֵף:


At that time the Lord decreed by Isaiah, the son of Amoz, saying, Go and remove the sackcloth which is on thy loins, and put off thy sandals from off thy feet: and he did so, walking naked and barefoot.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:3)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ כְּמָא דְהַלֵיךְ עַבְדִי יְשַׁעְיָהוּ פְחֵיחַ וְיָחֵיף תְּלַת שְׁנִין אָת וּמוֹפֵת עַל מִצְרָאֵי וְעַל כּוּשָׁאֵי:


And the Lord said, Like as my servant Isaiah hath walked naked and barefoot three years for a sign and wonder upon Egypt and Cush:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:4)


כֵּן יְדַבֵּר מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר יַת שְׁבִי מִצְרַיִם וְיַת גָלוּת כּוּשׁ עוּלֵימִין וְסָבִין פְּחִיחִין וִיחֵיפִין וּגְסָן עֶרְיָה קְלָן מִצְרָיִם:


So shall the king of Assyria lead the captives, and the captivity of Cush, the young and old, naked and barefoot, even with their shame uncovered, to the disgrace of Egypt.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:5)


וִיתַּבְּרוּן וִיבְהֲתוּן מִכּוּשׁ בֵּית רוֹחְצָנֵיהוֹן וּמִן מִצְרַיִם תּוּשְׁבַּחְתְּהוֹן:


And they shall be afraid and confounded because of Cush, the place of their confidence, and because of Egypt, their boasting.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/20:6)


וְיֵמַר יָתֵיב נִסָא הָדֵין בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא הַכְדֵין הֲוָה בֵית רוּחֲצָנָא דַהֲוֵינָא סְבִירִין לְמֶעְרוֹק לְתַמָן לְסָעִיד לְאִשְׁתֵּיזְבָא מִן קֳדָם מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר עַד כְּדוּן נַפְשֵׁהוֹן לָא שֵׁזִיבוּ וְאֵיכְדֵין נִשְׁתֵּזִיב אֲנָחְנָא:


At that time the inhabitant of this island shall say: Such is become the place of our trust, to which we were hoping to flee for help to be delivered from before the king of Assyria, if hitherto they could not deliver themselves, how shall we be delivered?



## Chapter 21

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:1)


מַטַל מַשִׁרְיָן דְאָתְיָן מִמַדְבְּרָא כְּמֵי יַמָא דְנָגְדִין כְּעַלְעוֹלִין דְאָתָן בְּאוֹרַח דָרוֹמָא מַדְבְּרָא וְאַתְיָן מִמַדְבְּרָא מֵאַרְעָא דְאִתְעֲבִידָא בָּהּ חֲסִינִין:


The burden of the armies which are coming from the wilderness, as the waters of the sea, rushing along as the tempests: yea, they are coming by the way of the south, hurrying along; yea, they are coming from the wilderness, from the land, in which terrible things are done.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:2)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא נְבוּאָה קָשְׁיָא אִתְחַוָאָה לִי אֲנִיסַיָא מִתְאַנְסִין וּבָזְזַיָא מִתְבַּזְזִין סַקוּ עֵילָמָאֵי תְקִיפוּ מְדָאֵי כָּל דַהֲווֹ מִתְאַנְחִין מִן קֳדָם מַלְכָּא דְבָבֶל אַנְחֵית לְהוֹן:


The prophet said: a dreadful vision has been declared unto me. The violent deal violently, and the spoilers spoil. Come up, ye Elamites; encompass, ye Media! I will give rest to all who sigh on account of the king of Babylon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:3)


עַל כֵּן אִתְמְלִיאוּ חַרְצֵיהוֹן זִיעַיָא דַחֲלָא אֲחַדְתִּינוּן הָא כְּחֶבְלִין לְיָלֵידְתָּא אִטַפָּשׁוּ מִלְמִשְׁמַע טָעוּ מִלְמֶחֱזֵי:


Therefore their loins shall be full of terror: behold, fear shall seize them, like the pangs of a woman that travaileth. They are become foolish, so that they cannot hear; they have erred, so that they cannot see.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:4)


טְעָה לִבְּהוֹן עָקָא וּבִיעוּתִין אַחֲדוּנוּן אֲתַר בֵּית רוֹחְצָנֵיהוֹן הֲוָה לְהוֹן לִתְבַר:


Their heart is bewildered; distress and terrors have seized them, because the place of their confidence hath become destruction unto them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:5)


סַדַרוּ פָּתוֹרִין אֲקִימוּ סַכְוָאִין אֲכוֹלוּ שְׁתוּ קוּמוּ רַבְרְבַיָא מְרִיקוּ וְצַחְצַחוּ זְיָנָא:


Prepare the table, place the watchmen, eat ye, drink ye; rise, ye princes, polish, and make bright the arms!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:6)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר לִי יְיָ אֱזֵיל אֲקֵים סַכְוָאָה דְיֶחֱזֵי חַוֵי:


For thus hath the Lord said unto me: Go, set a watchman, let him report what he seeth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:7)


וְיֶחֱזֵי רְתַךְ אֱנַשׁ וְעִמֵהּ זוֹג פָּרָשִׁים רָכֵב עַל חֲמַר רָכִב עַל גַמְלָא אֲמַר נְבִיָא אֲצֵיצִית אֲצָתָא וַחֲזֵית וְהָא מַשִׁרְיָן לַחֲדָא:


And he saw a chariot, a man, and with him a couple of horsemen: a rider on an ass, a rider on a a camel. The prophet said, I listened diligently, and, behold, I saw mighty armies!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:8)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא קַל מַשִׁרְיָן דְאַתְיָן בְּסִיטוּנֵיהוֹן כְּאַרְיָא וְעַל סָכוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֲנָא קָאִים תְּדִירָא בִּימָמָא וְעַל מַטַרְתִּי אֲנָא מְעַתַּד כָּל לֵילְיָא:


The prophet said, the voice of armies, coming with coats of mail, is as a lion; and I stand continually upon the watchtower before the Lord in the day, and as a guard I stand all the night.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:9)


וְהָא דֵין אָתָא רְתַךְ אֱנַשׁ וְעִמֵיהּ זוֹג פָּרָשִׁין וְאָתִיב וַאֲמַר נְפַלַת וְאַף עֲתִידָא לְמִפַּל בָּבֶל וְכָל צַלְמֵי טַעֲוָתָהָא יְדַקְדְקוּן לְאַרְעָא:


And, behold, this was coming: A chariot! a man! and with him a couple of horsemen. He answered, and said, She is fallen! Yea, it shall come to pass, that Babylon shall fall, and all the images of her idols shall be dashed to pieces to the ground.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:10)


מַלְכִין דְאוּמָנִין לְאַגָחָא קְרָבָא יֵיתוּן עֲלָהּ לְמִבְזָהָא כְּאִכָּרָא דְאוּמָן לְמֵידַשׁ יַת אִדְרָא אֲמַר נְבִיָא דִשְׁמַעֵית יַת קַל מֵימְרָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל חַוֵיתִי לְכוֹן:


Kings who are skillful to wage war, shall come against her to plunder her, like a husbandman who is skillful to thrash the floor. The prophet said, The voice of the WORD of the Lord of Hosts, the God of Israel, which I have heard, I have declared unto you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:11)


מַטַל כָּס דִלְוָט לְאַשְׁקָאָה יַת דוּמָה עֲלֵי אַכְלֵי מִן שְׁמַיָא נְבִיָא פָּרֵישׁ לְהוֹן יַת נְבוּאֲתָא נְבִיָא פָּרֵישׁ לְהוֹן מָה דַעֲתִיד לְמֵיתֵי:


The Burden Of The Cup Of Cursing To Give Duma To Drink. He thundered unto me from the heavens, Prophet! explain unto them the prophecy; prophet! explain unto them what shall hereafter come to pass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:12)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא אִית אֲגַר לְצַדִיקַיָא וְאִית פּוּרְעֲנוּת לְרַשִׁיעַיָא אִם תַּיְבִין אַתּוּן תּוּבוּ עַד דְאַתּוּן יָכְלִין לְמֵגָתָב:


The prophet said, there is a reward to the righteous, and there is punishment to the wicked: if ye will repent, repent ye, whilst ye are able to repent.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:13)


מַטַל כָּס דִלְוָט לְאַשְׁקָאָה יַת עֲרָבָאֵי בְּחוּרְשָׁא בְרַמְשָׁא יְבִיתִין שְׁיָרַת בְּנֵי דְדָן:


The Burden Of Give The Arabians To Drink. In the forest, at even, the caravan of the sons of Dedan shall remain for the night.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:14)


לְאַפֵּי צַחְיָא פּוּקוּ נַגְדִין דְמַיָא דְיָתְבֵי בְּאַרְעָא דְרוֹמָא לַחְמָא דְאַתּוּן אָכְלִין זַמִינוּ לִמְעַרְקַיָא:


Bring bottles of water before the thirsty, who dwell in land of the south; prepare for the fugitives the daily food which ye eat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:15)


אֲרֵי מִן קֳדָם קְטוֹל עֲרָקוּ מִן קֳדָם חַרְבָּא שְׁלִיפָא וּמִן קֳדָם קֶשֶׁת מְתִיחָא וּמִן קֳדָם תְּקוֹף קְרָבָא:


Because, on account of the slaughter they have fled: from the face of the drawn sword; and from the face of the bent bow; and from the face of the strength of the battle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:16)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ לִי בְּסוֹף שְׁנַיָא כִשְׁנֵי אֲגִירָא וְיָסוּף כָּל יְקָרְהוֹן דַעֲרָבָאֵי:


For thus hath the Lord said unto me: at the end of the years, as the years of an hireling, all the glory of the Arabians shall come to an end.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/21:17)


וּתְקוֹף עָבְדֵי קְרָב גִבָּרֵי בְּנֵי עֲרָבָאֵי יִזְעֲרוּן אֲרֵי בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל גְזִיר כֵּן:


And the strength of the warriors, the mighty, the sons of the Arabians, shall be lessened, because by the WORD of the Lord, the God of Israel, it is thus decreed.



## Chapter 22

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:1)


מַטַל נְבוּאֲתָא עַל קַרְתָּא דְיַתְבָא בְחֵילְתָא דְאִתְנַבִּיאוּ עֲלָהּ נְבִיַיָא מָה לְכוֹן כְּדֵין אֲרֵי סְלֵקְתּוּן כּוּלְכוֹן לְאִגָרַיָא:


The Burden Of The Prophecy Concerning The City That Dwelleth In The Valley, Of Which The Prophets Prophesied. What aileth you here, that all of you are gone up to the house tops?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:2)


מִן קֳדָם אִתְרְגוּשָׁא דְאִתְמְלִיאַת קַרְתָּא מְשַׁבַּחְתָּא כְּרַכָּא חֲדָאָה קְטִילַיִךְ לָא אִתְקְטִילוּ בְחַרְבָּא וְאַף בִּקְרָבָא לָא מִיתוּ:


On account of the tumult wherewith the praiseworthy, the fortified, the joyous city is filled. Thy slain were not slain by the sword, neither did they die in battle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:3)


כָּל שִׁלְטוֹנַיִךְ אִטַלְטַלוּ כַחֲדָא מִן קֳדָם מְתַח קַשְׁתָּא גְלוֹ כָּל דְאִשְׁתַּכַּח בִּיךְ אִיתְקְטְלוּ כַחֲדָא מֵרָחִיק עֲרַקוּ:


All thy rulers have been led forth; from before the bent bow, they are gone into captivity together: all that were found in thee have been slain, together they have fled afar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:4)


עַל כֵּן אֲמַרֵית שְׁבוּקוּ מִנִי אֶבְכֵּי בִמְרָר לָא תִתְבְּעִיתוּ לְנַחֲמוּתִי עַל תְּבַר כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְעַמִי:


Wherefore I said: Leave off from me, I will weep bitterly, seek ye not to comfort me for the desolation of the congregation of my people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:5)


אֲרֵי יוֹם רְגוּשׁ וְאִתְּדָשָׁא וּקְטוֹל קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהֵי צְבָאוֹת עַל קַרְתָּא דְיַתְבָא בְחֵילְתָא דְאִתְנַבִּיאוּ עֲלָהּ נְבִיַיָא מַבְלְשִׁין בָּתַּיָא מַקְפִין מִגְדְלַיָא בְּרֵישֵׁי טוּרַיָא:


For it is a day of tumult, and of treading under foot, and of slaughter before the Lord, the God of hosts, against the city that dwelleth in the valley, against which the prophets prophesied. Thy search the houses, they encompass the towers which are on the tops of the mountains.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:6)


וְעֵילֵמָאֵי נְטָלוּ זְיָן בִּרְתָךְ אֱנָשׁ וְעִמֵהּ זוֹג פָּרָשִׁין וְעַל שׁוּר דְבַקוּ תְרֵיסִין:


And the Elamites have taken arms in the chariot of a man, and with him are a couple of horsemen, and on the wall they hang the shields.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:7)


וַהֲוָה שְׁפַר מִשְׁרָךְ אִתְמְלִיאוּ רְתִיכִין וּפָרָשִׁין מְמַנָן עַל תַּרְעִין:


And it shall come to pass that the most beautiful of thy valleys shall be filled with chariots; and the horsemen shall be set against the gates.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:8)


וְגַלֵי יַת מַטְמוֹרִית בֵּית יְהוּדָה וְאִסְתְּכֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא עַל זְיַן בֵּית גִנְזֵי מַקְדְשָׁא:


And he shall discover the covering of Judah, and he shall look at that time upon the arms of the house of the treasury of the sanctuary.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:9)


וְיַת פְּלוּגַת קַרְתָּא דְדָוִד חֲזֵיתוּן אֲרֵי סַגִיאַת וְכַנֵישְׁתּוּן יַת עַמָא לְמֵי בְרִיכְתָּא אַרְעִיתָא:


And the breaches of the city of David ye shall see, because they are many; and ye shall gather together the people to the lower pool.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:10)


וְיַת בָּתֵּי יְרוּשְׁלֵם מְנִיתוּן וְתָרַעֲתּוּן בָּתַּיָא לְתַקָפָא שׁוּרָא:


And ye shall number the houses of Jerusalem, and ye shall break down the houses to fortify the wall.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:11)


וּמִקְוַיָא עֲבַדְתּוּן בֵּין שׁוּרַיָא לְמֵי בְּרֵכְתָּא עַתִּיקְתָּא וְלָא אִסְתַּכַּלְתּוּן בִּדְעָבְדָהּ וְדֵי בְרָאָהּ מִלְקָדְמִין לָא חֲזֵיתוּן:


And ye shall make a lake between the walls of the water of the old pool: but ye have not looked unto the maker thereof, neither had respect unto him that created it of old.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:12)


וְאַכְלֵי נְבִיָא דַייָ אֱלֹהִי צְבָאוֹת בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא לְמִבְכֵּי וּלְמִסְפֵּד וּלְגִזוּז רֵישׁ וּלְמֵיסַר סַקִין:


And the prophet of the Lord, the God, the God of hosts, called in that day to weeping, and to mourning, and to baldness, and to girding with sackcloth:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:13)


וְהָא בִּיַע וְחֶדְוָה אָמְרִין נִקְטוֹל תוֹרִין וְנִנְכַס עָאן נֵיכוּל בְּסַר וְנִשְׁתֵּי חֲמַר נֵיכוּל וְנִשְׁתֵּי מִדִנְמוּת לָא נְחֵי:


But, behold, joy and gladness; they say, Let us slay oxen, and kill sheep, we will eat flesh, we will drink wine; let us eat, and drink, since we shall die, and not live.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:14)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא בְאוּדְנִי הֱוֵיתִי שְׁמַע כַּד אִתְגְזַר דָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת אִם יִשְׁתְּבַק חוֹבָא הָדֵין לְכוֹן עַד דִי תְמוּתוּן מוֹתָא תִנְיָנָא אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת:


The prophet said, with mine ears I was hearing when this was decreed from before the Lord of hosts, namely, that this your iniquity shall not be forgiven you till you die the second death, said the Lord, the God, the God of hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:15)


כִּדְנֵן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת אֱתָא אֱזֵיל לְוַת פַרְנָסָא הָדֵין לְוַת שֶׁבְנָא דִי מְמַנָא עַל בֵּיתָא:


Thus said the Lord, the God, the God of hosts, Come, go to this ruler, even to Shebna, who is appointed over the house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:16)


וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ מַה לָךְ כָּא מַה לָךְ כְּדֵין אֲרֵי אַתְקְנִית לָךְ כָּא אֲתַר אַתְקִין בְּרוּמָא אַתְרֵהּ שַׁוֵי בְּכֵיפָּא בֵּית מְדוֹרֵהּ:


And thou shalt say to him, What hast thou here? and what hast thou here, that thou hast prepared for thyself here a place? He hath prepared on high his place! he hath put in the rock the place of his habitation!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:17)


הָא יְיָ מְטַלְטְלָךְ טִלְטוּל דְגַבְרָא וְתַחֲפִינָךְ בַּהֲתָא:


Behold, the Lord will cast thee out with a mighty casting out, and confusion shall cover thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:18)


יַעְדֵי מִנָךְ יַת מִצְנֶפְתָּא וְיַקִיפוּנָךְ בַּעֲלֵי דְבַב כְּשׁוּר מַקַף וְיַגְלוּנָךְ לְאַרְעָא פְתַיוּת יְדַיִין תַּמָן תְּמוּת וּלְתַמָן רְתִיכֵי יְקָרָךְ יְתוּבוּן בְּקָלָן עַל דְלָא נְטַרְתָּא יְקָר בֵּית רִבּוֹנָךְ:


He shall take away from thee thy tiara, and the enemies shall enclose thee, like a surrounding wall, and they shall lead thee into captivity into a spacious country, there thou shalt die, and thither the chariots of thy glory shall return in disgrace, because thou hast not preserved the honours of thy master's house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:19)


וְאַדְחִינָךְ מִמְקוֹמָךְ וּמִשַׁמוּשָׁךְ יְמַגְרִינָךְ:


And I will thrust thee down from thy station, and I will divest thee of thy office.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:20)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא וְאַרְבֵּי לְעַבְדֵי לְאֶלְיָקִים בַּר חִלְקִיָהוּ:


And it shall come to pass at that time, that I will make my servant Eliakim great, the son of Hilkiah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:21)


וְאַלְבְּשִׁנֵיהּ כִּתּוּנָךְ וְהֶמְיָנָךְ אֲזַרְזִנֵהּ וְשׁוּלְטָנָךְ אֶתֵּן בִּידֵהּ וִיהֵי לְאַב לְיַתְבֵי יְרוּשְׁלֵם וּלְבֵית יְהוּדָה:


And I will clothe him with thy robe, and with thy girdle I will gird him, and I will commit thy government into his hand, and he shall be a prince to the inhabitants of Jerusalem, and to the house of Judah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:22)


וְאֶתֵּן מַפְתֵּחַ בֵּית מִקְדְשָׁא וְשׁוּלְטַן בֵּית דָוִד וְיִפְתַּח וְלֵית דְאָחִיד וְיֵיחוֹד וְלֵית דְפָתַח:


And I will place the key of the house of the sanctuary, and the government of the house of David, in his hand; and he shall open, and none shall shut; and he shall shut, and none shall open.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:23)


וְאֶמַנִנֵיהּ אַמַרְכֶּל מְהֵימָן מְשַׁמֵשׁ בַּאֲתַר קַיָם וִיהֵי לְכוּרְסֵי יְקָר לְבֵית אֲבוּהִי:


And I will appoint him a faithful chief-governor, an officer in a firm place, and he shall be for a glorious throne to his father's house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:24)


וְיִסְתַּמְכוּן עֲלוֹהִי כָּל יַקִירֵי בֵית אָבוּהִי בְּנַיָא וּבְנֵי בְנַיָא מֵרַבְרְבַיָא וְעַד דְעַדְקַיָא מִכַּהֲנַיָא לָבְשֵׁי אֵפוֹדָא וְעַד בְּנֵי לֵוִי אֶחֲדֵי נִבְלַיָא:


All the nobles of his father's house shall rest themselves upon him, children, and children's children, from young men to little ones, from the priests clothed with the ephod to the Levites that are holding the lyres.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 22:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/22:25)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא אֲמַר יְיָ צְבָאוֹת יַעְדֵי אַמַרְכֶּל מְהֵימָן דִי מְשַׁמֵשׁ בַּאֲתַר קַיָם וְיִתְקַטֵף וְיִפוֹל וְתִבְטַל מְטַל נְבוּאֲתָא דִי עֲלוֹהִי אֲרֵי בְמֵימְרָא דַייָ גְזַר כֵּן:


At that time, saith the Lord of hosts, there shall be removed the faithful chief governor, who ministered in a firm place, and he shall be cut off, and he shall fall: and there shall be accomplished the burden of prophecy, which was concerning him; because the WORD of the Lord hath so decreed it.



## Chapter 23

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:1)


מַטָל כָּס דִלְוָט לְאַשְׁקָאָה יַת צוֹר אֵילִילוּ נָחֲתֵי סְפִינֵי יַמָא אֲרֵי אִתְבְּזִיזוּ מְחוֹזֵיהוֹן מִלְמֵיתֵי מֵאַרְעָא כִסְאֵי אָתֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן:


The Burden Of The Cup Of Cursing, To Give Tyre To Drink. Howl, O ye that are embarking! in ships of the sea, because their havens are spoiled, so that none can enter in. From the land of Chittim it is coming upon them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:2)


אִתְּבַּרוּ יָתְבֵי נִיסָא תַּגְרֵי צִידוֹן דַהֲווֹ עָדָן בְּיַמָא מַלוּךְ:


The inhabitants of the island are destroyed, the merchants that pass over the sea they used to replenish thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:3)


דַהֲוַת מְסַפְּקָא סְחוֹרָא לְעַמְמִין סַגִיאִין דְבַחֲצַד כְּנִישַׁת נַהֲרָא עֲלַלְתָּהּ נַהֲרָא סְחוֹרָא לְעַמְמַיָא:


She was the plentiful mart for many nations; the harvest of the increase of the river was her revenue, and she became the mart of the nations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:4)


בְּהִיתוּ צִידוֹנָאֵי אֲרֵי אֲמַר מְעַרְבָאָה דִי יָתֵיב בִּתְקוֹף יַמָא לְמֵימָר לְוַי לָא מַרְעֵית לָא אַעְדֵיתִי וְלָא רַבֵּיתִי עוּלֵמִין פַּנְקֵית בְּתוּלָן:


The Sidonians are confounded, because the west hath spoken, which dwells in the strength of the sea, saying, O that I had never travailed, never conceived, and never nourished up young men, never brought up virgins delicately!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:5)


כְּמָא דִי שְׁמָעוּ לְמַחֲתָא דִלְקוֹ מִצְרָאֵי זָעוּ כַּד שְׁמַעוּ צוֹרָאֵי:


When they heard of the stroke wherewith the Egyptians were smitten, the Tyrians quaked when they heard it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:6)


גְלוֹ לִמְדִינַת יַמָא אֵילִילוּ יָתְבֵי גִיסָא:


They have gone to a province of the sea. Howl, O inhabitants of the island!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:7)


הֲהָדָא לְכוֹן תַּקִיפְתָּא צוֹר מִיוֹמֵי קֶדֶם מִלְקָדְמִין הֲווֹ מַיְתָן לָהּ תַּקְרוּבְתָּא מֵאֲרַע רְחִיקָא כְדוֹ לְאִתּוֹתָבָא הָא גַלְיָא:


Is this your strong city, Tyre, of ancient days? In olden time they were bringing her gifts from a distant land; behold, now she is removed to sojourn afar off!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:8)


מַן מְלִיךְ דָא עַל צוֹר דַהֲיַת מְמַלְכָּא מַלְכִין דִי תַגָרָהָא רַבְרְבִין שִׁלְטוֹנָאָה יְקִירֵי אַרְעָא:


Who hath taken this counsel against Tyre, against her that was giving advice? whose merchants are princes, and whose rulers the nobles of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:9)


יְיָ צְבָאוֹת מִלְכָהּ לְאַפָסָא יְקָר כָּל חֶדְוָתָא לְאַקָלָא כָּל יַקִירֵי אַרְעָא:


The Lord of hosts is the counsellor against her, to defile the glory of all the objects of rejoicing, and make contemptible all the nobles of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:10)


גְלָא מֵאַרְעִיךְ כְּמֵי נַהֲרָא עֲרוֹקִי לִמְדִינַת יַמָא לֵית תְּקוֹף עוֹד:


Remove from thy land, as the waters of a river; flee to a province of the sea: there is no more strength.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:11)


גְבוּרְתֵּהּ מְרַמְמָא עַל יַמָא לַאֲזָעָא מַלְכְּוָתָא יְיָ פַּקֵיד עַל כְּנַעֲנָאָה לְשֵׁיצָאָה תּוּקְפָאָה:


His power is lifted up against the sea, to make the kingdom to tremble; the Lord has given the command concerning the merchant city, to make an end of her strength.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:12)


וַאֲמַר לָא תוֹסְפוּן עוֹד לְמִתְקַף דַהֲוֵיתוּן אָנְסִין לְעַמָא דִי בְצִידוֹן לְכִתִּים קוּמוּ גְלוֹ אַף לְתַמָן לָא יְהֵי לְכוֹן נְיָח:


And he said, ye shall no more be strong, ye that did violence to the people which are in Zidon. Arise, pass over to Chittim; even there ye shall have no rest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:13)


הָא אַרְעָא כַּסְדָאֵי דֵין עַמָא כַּד לָא הֲוָה אַתּוּרָאָה שַׁכְלְלָהּ לְנַגְוַן אֲקִימוּ חֶזְוָתָהָא פַּגְרוּ בִרְנְיָתָהָא שַׁוְיוּהָא לִדְמְחַמְרָא:


Behold the land of the Chaldeans, this is the people that formerly did not exist; the Assyrians founded it in the islands; they raised up her beacons, they destroyed her palaces, they have made her a ruinous heap.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:14)


אֵילִילוּ נָחֲתֵי סְפִינֵי יַמָא אֲרֵי אִתְבְּזִיזוּ מְחוֹזֵי תוּקְפֵכוֹן:


Howl, ye that are embarking in ships of the sea, for the seacoast of your strength is spoiled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:15)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא וְתִתְרְחֵיק צוֹר שַׁבְעִין שְׁנִין כְּיוֹמֵי מַלְכָּא חַד מִסוֹף שַׁבְעִין שְׁנִין יְהֵי לְצוֹר כְּתוּשְׁבְּחַת נַפְקַת בָּרָא:


And it shall come to pass at that time, that Tyre shall be put by for seventy years, according to the days of one king: at the end of seventy years, it shall be unto Tyre as the song of an harlot


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:16)


אִתְחַלֵיף יְקָרִיךְ אִטַלְטֵל לִמְדִינָא קַרְתָּא דִי הֲוֵת כְנַפְקַת בְּרָא אִתְרַחֲקַת הֲפוּכִי כִנָרַיִךְ לְאֶבְלָא וּזְמָרַיִךְ לְאִלְיָא מָאִים יֵעוּל דָכְרָנֵיךְ:


Thy glory is changed: wander to another country, O city, who hast been as an harlot, thou art doomed to oblivion: thy harp is turned into mourning, and thy song to lamentation; perhaps, thou mayest be remembered.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:17)


וִיהֵי מִסוֹף שַׁבְעִין שְׁנִין יֵעוּל דָכְרָנָהּ דְצוֹר קֳדָם יְיָ וּתְתוּב לְאַתְרָהּ וּתְהֵי מְסַפְּקָא סְחוֹרָא לְכָל מַלְכְּוַת עַמְמַיָא דִי עַל אַפֵּי אַרְעָא:


And it shall come to pass after the end of seventy years that Tyre shall be remembered before the Lord; and she shall return to her place, and her mart shall be plentiful for all the kingdoms of the nations, which are upon the face of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 23:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/23:18)


וִיהֵי סְחוֹרְתָּהּ וְאַגְרָהּ קוּדְשָׁא קֳדָם יְיָ לָא יִתְאַצֵר וְלָא יִתְגְנַז אֲרֵי לְדִמְשַׁמְשִׁין קֳדָם יְיָ יְהֵי אַגְרָה לְמֵיכַל לְמִסְבַּע וְלִכְסוּ דִיקָר:


And her merchandise and her gain shall be holy before the Lord, it shall not be treasured nor laid up; for it shall be for them that are serving before the Lord; her gain shall be for food sufficient, and for vestments of glory.



## Chapter 24

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:1)


הָא יְיָ מַבֵּיז אַרְעָא וּמְסַר לָהּ לְסָנְאָה וְתַחְפֵּי בַהֲתָא אַפֵּי רַבְרְבָהָא עַל דַעֲבַרוּ עַל אוֹרַיְתָא וִיבַדַר יַת דְיָתְבִין בָּהּ:


Behold, the Lord shall spoil the land and deliver it over to the enemy; and confusion shall cover the faces of the princes, because they have transgressed the law, and scattered its inhabitants.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:2)


וִיהֵי חִילוֹנָאָה כְּכַהֲנָא עַבְדָא כְּרִיבּוֹנֵהּ אַמְתָא כְּמֶרָתָהּ זָבְנָא כִּמְזַבְּנָא יָזְפָא כְּמוֹזְפָא רָשָׁא כְּמַן דְרָשֵׁי בֵיהּ:


And it shall be, as with the laity, so with the priest; as with the servant, so with his master; as with the handmaid, so with her mistress; as with the buyer, so with the seller; as with the borrower, so with the lender: as with the giver of usury, so with the usurer.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:3)


אִתְבְּזָזָא תִּתְבְּזֵז אַרְעָא וְאִתְדָשָׁא תִּתְדָשׁ אֲרֵי יְיָ מַלֵל יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין:


The land shall be utterly destroyed, it shall be utterly trampled under foot; for the Lord hath spoken this word.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:4)


אִתְאַבְּדַת חֲרוֹבַת אַרְעָא צְדִיאַת חֲרוֹבַת תֵּבֵל סְפוּ תְקוֹף עַמָא דְאַרְעָא:


The land mourneth, it is laid desolate; the world is laid waste, it is laid desolate: the strength of the people of the land hath come to an end.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:5)


וְאַרְעָא חָבַת תְּחוֹת יַתְבָהָא אֲרֵי עֲבַרוּ עַל אוֹרַיְתָא אַבְטִילוּ מוֹעֲדַיָא אַשְׁנִיאוּ קְיָמָא דִי מִן עַלְמָא:


The land hath become guilty beneath her inhabitants, because they have transgressed the law, and they have made the festivals to cease, they have changed the everlasting covenant.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:6)


עַל כֵּן מִן קֳדָם מוֹמֵי דִשְׁקַר חֲרוֹבַת אַרְעָא וּצְדִיאוּ דְיָתְבִין בָּהּ עַל כֵּן סָפוּ יָתְבֵי אַרְעָא וְאִשְׁתְּאַר אֱנָשָׁא כִּזְעֵיר:


Because of perjury the land hath become a desert, and all the inhabitants in it are laid desolate: therefore are the inhabitants of the land destroyed, and the men that are left are but few.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:7)


אִתְאַבַּלוּ כָל שָׁתֵי חֲמַר אֲרֵי אִתְפְּרִיכוּ גוּפְנַיָא אִתְאֲנַחוּ כָּל חָדֵי לִבָּא:


All that drink wine mourn, because the vines are broken down, all the merry-hearted sigh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:8)


בְּטֵלַת חֶדְוַת תּוּפִּין אִתְמְנַעַת אִתְרְגוּשַׁת תַּקִיפִין פִּסְקַת חֶדְוַת כִּנָרָא:


The mirth of tabrets ceaseth; the tumultuous assembly of the mighty is restrained, the joy of the harp ceaseth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:9)


עַל יְדֵי זְמָר לָא יִשְׁתּוּן חֲמַר יֵימַר עֲתִּיקָא לְשָׁתוֹהִי:


They shall no more drink wine with songs; strong drink shall be bitter to them that drink it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:10)


אִתְּבַרַת קַרְתְּהוֹן צְדִיאַת אֲחִידוּ כָל בָּתַּיָא מִלְמֵעַל:


Their city is broken down, and laid desolate: all their houses are shut up, so that no one can enter.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:11)


מְצַוְחִין עַל חַמְרָא בְּשׁוּקַיָא שְׁלֵימַת כָּל חֶדְוָתָא גְלָא בִּיעַ מִן אַרְעָא:


They cry for wine in the streets; all joy is ended: all gladness is removed from the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:12)


אִשְׁתְּאַר בְּקַרְתָּא צְדוֹ וְאִתְרְגוּשְׁתָּא בְפִגוּר תַּרְעִין:


Desolation is left in the city, and there is a tumult in the destruction of the gates.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:13)


אֲרֵי כְדֵין יִשְׁתְּאֲרוּן יְחִידָאִין צַדִיקַיָא בְגוֹ אַרְעָא בֵּינֵי מַלְכְּוָתָא כְּבִיעוּר זֵיתָא כְּעוֹלְלָן בָּתַר קָטַף:


When thus the righteous shall be left alone in the midst of the earth, among the kingdoms, as the shaking of olives, as the gleaning of grapes after the vintage.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:14)


אִנוּן יִרְמוֹן קָלֵיהוֹן יְשַׁבְּחוּן בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ יִבְעוֹן כְּמָא דִבְעוֹ עַל גְבוּרָן דְאִתְעֲבִידָא לְהוֹן עַל יַמָא:


They shall lift up their voice, they shall praise on account of the WORD of the Lord: they shall rejoice as they rejoiced on account of the mighty works which were done for them by the sea.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:15)


עַל כֵּן בְּמֵיתֵי נְהוֹרָא לְצַדִיקַיָא יִקְרוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּנִיסֵי יַמָא יְהוֹן מוֹדָן וּמְבָרְכִין שְׁמָא דַייָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


When light shall come to the righteous, they shall glorify the Lord: in the islands of the sea they shall laud and bless the name of the Lord, the God of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:16)


מִבֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא דְמִתַּמָן עֲתִיד לְמִפַּק חֶדְוָא לְכָל יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא תּוּשְׁבְּחָא שְׁמַעְנָא לְצַדִיקַיָא אָמַר נְבִיָא רָב אֲגַר לְצַדִיקַיָא לְרַשִׁיעַיָא אִתְגְלֵי לִי וַי לַאֲנוּסַיָא דְמִתְאַנְסִין וּלְבִזוּז בָּזְזִין דְהָא מִתְבָּזִין:


From the house of the sanctuary, yea, from thence joy shall go forth to all the inhabitants of the earth, for we have heard the praise of the righteous! The prophet said, a secret! A reward to the righteous is shown to me, a secret! A punishment for the wicked is revealed to me. Woe to the oppressors, for they shall be oppressed; and to those who spoil, for, behold, they shall be spoiled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:17)


דַחֲלָא וְכוּמְצָא וּמְצָדָא עֲלָךְ יָתֵיב אַרְעָא:


The terror, the pit, and the snare, are upon thee, O inhabitant of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:18)


וִיהֵי דְיֶעֱרוֹק מִן קֳדָם דַחֲלָא יִפּוֹל לְגוֹ כּוּמְצָא וְדִיַסֵק מִגוֹ כּוּמְצָא יִתְאַחַד בִּסְרִיגְתָּא אֲרֵי גְבוּרָן בִּשְׁמַיָא אִתְעֲבִידָא וְזָעוּ יְסוֹדֵי אַרְעָא:


And it shall come to pass, that he who fleeth from before the terror, he shall fall into the midst of the pit; and he that cometh up out of the midst of the pit shall be taken in the snare: for mighty works are done in the heavens, therefore the foundations of the earth quake.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:19)


מַזָע תְּזוּעַ אַרְעָא מֵנָד תָּנוּד אַרְעָא פְּרוֹק תִּתְפְּרַק אַרְעָא:


The land is terribly shaken, the land terribly reeleth to and fro, the land is utterly broken.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:20)


אִשְׁתְּדָאָה תִּשְׁתְּדֵי אַרְעָא כְּרַוְיָא וּתְהֵי אַזְלָא וְאַתְיָא כְּעַרְסְלָא וְיִתְקְפוּן עֲלָהּ חוֹבָהָא וְתִפּוֹל וְלָא תוֹסִיף לְמֵקָם:


The land is utterly cast down like a drunkard, she is totterings like a couch: and her sins are heavy upon her: she shall fall, and rise no more.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:21)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יַסְעַר יְיָ עַל חַלְוָתָא תוּקְפָא דְיָתְבִין בְּתוּקְפָא וְעַל מַלְכֵי בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא דְדָיְרִין עַל אַרְעָא:


And it shall come to pass at that time, that the Lord shall punish the mighty host that is dwelling in power, and the kings, the sons of men, who are dwelling upon the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:22)


וְיִכְנְשׁוּנוּן מִכְנַשׁ לְבֵית אַסִירֵי וִיעַנְגוּנוּן לְבֵית עַגְנָה וּמַסְגֵי יוֹמִין יֵעוּל דָכְרָנֵיהוֹן:


And they shall be utterly gathered for the prison, and they shall be shut up in the dungeon, and after many days they shall be remembered.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 24:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/24:23)


וְיִבְהֲתוּן דְפָלְחִין לְסִיהֲרָא וְיִתְכַּנְעוּן דְסָגְדִין לְשִׁמְשָׁא אֲרֵי תִתְגְלֵי גְבוּרְתָּא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת בְּטוּרָא דְצִיוֹן וּבִירוּשְׁלֶם וּקְדָם סָבֵי עַמֵהּ בִּיקָר:


And they shall be confounded that worship the moon, and they shall be ashamed that worship the sun; because the power of the Lord of hosts shall be revealed in mount Sion, and before the elders of his people in glory.



## Chapter 25

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:1)


יְיָ אֱלָהַי אַתְּ אֲרוֹמְמִינָךְ אוֹדֵי שְׁמָךְ אֲרֵי עָבַדְתָּא פְרִישָׁן מִלְכִין דַאֲמַרְתָּא לְאַיְתָאָה מִלְקָדְמִין בְּכֵן אַיְתֵיתָא וְקַיַמְתָּא:


O Lord, thou art my God: I will exalt thee; I will praise thy name; for thou hast done wonderful things; surely thou hast brought to pass and hast established the counsels which thou hadst promised of old to bring to pass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:2)


אֲרֵי שַׁוִיתָא קִרְוֵי פַצְחִין לְגַלִין כְּרַךְ תַּקִיף לְדִמְחַמְרָא בֵּית דַחֲלַת עַמְמַיָא בְּקַרְתָּא יְרוּשְׁלֵם לַעֲלָם לָא יִתְבְּנֵי:


For thou hast made the unfortified cities heaps; the fortified city is a ruin; the idolatrous house of the nations in the city of Jerusalem, shall never be built up again.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:3)


עַל כֵּן יִקְרוּן קֳדָמָךְ עַם תַּקִיף קִרְיַת עַמְמִין חֲסִינִין יִדַחֲלוּן מִן קֳדָמָךְ:
