Therefore shall the strong people glorify thee, the city of terrible nations shall fear thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:4)


אֲרֵי הֲוֵיתָא תְקוֹף לְמִסְכַּן סְעֵיד לַחֲשִׁיךְ בְּעִדַן עָקָא כְּמָא דְמִטַמְרָן מִן קֳדָם זַרְמִית כִּטְלַל מִשְׁרָב כֵּן מִלֵי רַשִׁיעַיָא לְצַדִיקַיָא כְּזַרְמִית בְּשָׁקְפָא בְּכוֹתֵל:


For thou hast been a strength to the poor, a help to the needy in the time of distress; as those who are protected from before a storm, or who are protected as in a shade from the scorching heat; thus the words of the wicked against the righteous are as a storm that beats against the wall.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:5)


כִּשְׁרָב בְּאַרְעָא צַחְיָא אִתְרְגוּשַׁת תַּקִיפִין תְּמָאֵיךְ בִּטְלַל כֵּיף מַקַר בְּאַרְעָא מְשַׁלְחַיָא כֵן נְיַח רְוָחָא לְצַדִיקַיָא כַּד יִמְאֲכוּן רַשִׁיעַיָא:


As the scorching heat in a dry land thou shalt bring low the tumult of the mighty: as the shade of a cooling rock in a parched land, thus peace of mind shall be to the righteous, when the wicked shall be humbled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:6)


וְיַעְבֵּד יְיָ צְבָאוֹת לְכָל עַמְמַיָא בְּטוּרָא הָדֵין שֵׁירוּ וְזִמְנָא מְדַמָן דְהִיא דִיקָר וּתְהֵי לְהוֹן לִקְלָן מִחָן חֵילָא מָחָן דְלָא יִשְׁתֵּיזְבוּן מִנְהוֹן מָחָן דִיסוּפוּן בְּהוֹן:


And in this mountain shall make a feast and a banquet; they think that it shall be for their glory; but it shall be to them for disgrace and for mighty afflictions, from which they shall not deliver themselves, afflictions through which they shall come to an end.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:7)


וְיִסְתְּלַעֲמוּן בְּטוּרָא הָדֵין אַפֵּי רַבָּא דְרַב עַל כָּל עַמְמַיָא וְאַפֵּי מַלְכָּא דְשַׁלִיט עַל כָּל מַלְכְּוָתָא:


The face of the prince, of the prince of all the people shall be destroyed; and the face of the king, of the ruler of all the kingdoms.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:8)


יִתְנְשׁוּן מוֹתָא לְעַלְמִין וְיִמְחֵי יְיָ אֱלֹהִים דִמְעָתָא מֵעַל כָּל אַפַּיָא וַחֲסוּדֵי עַמֵיהּ יַעְדֵי מֵעַל כָּל אַרְעָא אֲרֵי בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ גְזִיר כֵּן:


Death shall be forgotten for ever; and the Lord God shall wipe away tears from off all the faces, and the reproach of his people he shall remove from off all the earth, because it hath been decreed thus by the WORD of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:9)


וְיֵימַר בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא הָא אֱלָהָנָא דֵין דִי סְבַרְנָא לֵיהּ וְיִפְרְקִינָנָא דֵין יְיָ דַהֲוֵינָא מְסַבְּרִין לְמֵימְרֵהּ נְבוֹעַ וְנֶחֱדֵי בְּפוּרְקָנַהּ:


And one shall say at that time: Behold, this is our God; this is he for whom we have hoped, and he shall “save us: this is the Lord, for His WORD we have hoped; we will rejoice, and be glad in his salvation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:10)


אֲרֵי תִתְגְלֵי גְבוּרְתָּא דַייָ בְּטוּרָא הָדֵין וְיִתְדָשׁוּן מוֹאֲבָאֵי בְּאַתְרֵיהוֹן כְּמָא דְמִתְּדָשׁ תִּבְנָא בְּטִינָא:


For the power of the Lord of hosts shall be revealed in this mountain; and the Moabites shall be trodden under foot even as straw is in the clay.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:11)


וְיַפְרֵיס מְחַת גְבוּרְתֵּהּ בֵּינֵיהוֹן כְּמָא דְמַפְרֵיס שַׁיְטָא לְמֵישַׁט וְיַמְאִיךְ יְקָרֵהּ עִם אַשְׁוַת יְדוֹהִי:


And he shall extend the stroke of his power among them, as the swimmer extends himself to swim; and he shall humble his glory, together with the wiles of his hands.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/25:12)


וּכְרַךְ תְּקוֹף כְּרַכֵּי עַמְמַיָא יְמַגֵר יִרְמֵי יִמְטֵי לְאַרְעָא עַד עַפְרָא:


He shall cast down the fortified city, the great city; he shall thrust it down, it shall come to the earth, even to the dust.



## Chapter 26

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:1)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יְשַׁבְּחוּן תוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא חַדְתָּא בְּאַרְעָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה קִרְיַת תּוּקְפָא לָנָא פוּרְקָן יִתְשָׂם עַל שׁוּרָהָא וְרַחֲמִין:


At That Time They Shall Sing A New Song In The Land Of The House Of Judah: We have a strong city, salvation and mercy shall be established upon her walls.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:2)


פְּתַחוּ תַּרְעַיָא וְיֵעוּל עַמָא זַכָּאָה דִי נְטָרוּ אוֹרַיְתָא בְּלֵבָב שָׁלֵם:


Open ye the gates, and let the righteous nation enter, which have kept the law with a perfect heart.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:3)


בְּלֵבָב שָׁלֵם נְטָרוּ שְׁלָמָא שְׁלָמָא יִתְעֲבֵיד לְהוֹן אֲרֵי בְמֵימְרָךְ אִתְרְחִיצוּ:


In a perfect heart they shall preserve peace: peace shall be wrought for them, because they have trusted in thy WORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:4)


אִתְרְחִיצוּ עַל מֵימְרָא דַייָ לְעָלַם וּלְעָלְמֵי עָלְמַיָא בְּכֵן תִּתְפַרְקוּן בְּמֵימַר דְחִילָא דַייָ תַּקִיף עָלְמַיָא:


Trust ye in the Word of the Lord for ever and ever: thus ye shall be saved by the WORD, who is the fear of the Lord, the mighty One to eternity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:5)


אֲרֵי אַמְאִיךְ יַתְבֵי רוּמָא קִרְיָא תַקִיפָא יַמְאֲכִינָא וְיַרְמִינָהּ עַד אַרְעָא יִמְטִינָהּ עַד עַפְרָא:


For he will humble the inhabitants of the high and strong city; he will humble her, he will cast her unto the ground, he will bring her even to the dust.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:6)


יְדוּשׁוּנָה רִגְלִין רִגְלֵי צַדִיקַיָא פַּרְסַת עִנְוְתָנַיָא חֲשִׁיכֵי עַמָא:


The feet shall tread her down, the feet of the just, the sole of the foot of the poor, of the needy of the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:7)


אוֹרְחַת צַדִיקַיָא כַּוָנָן אַף עֲבַדוּ אוֹרְחַת צַדִיקַיָא תְּתַקֵן:


The paths of the righteous are right: thou wilt establish the works of the ways of the just.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:8)


אַף לְאוֹרַח דִינָךְ יְיָ סְבַרְנָא לִשְׁמָךְ וּלְדָכְרָנָךְ חֲמֵדַת נַפְשָׁנָא:


Yea, for the way of thy judgment, O Lord, have we hoped; to thy name and to the remembrance of thee is the desire of our soul.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:9)


נַפְשִׁי מַחְמְדָא לְצַלָאָה קֳדָמָךְ בְּלֵילְיָא אַף רוּחִי בִמְעַי מְבָרְכָא לָךְ אֲרֵי כְּמָא דְדִינָךְ תַּקִין לְאַרְעָא קוּשְׁטָא יִתְאַלְפוּן לְמֶעְבַד דְדָיְרִין בְּתֵבֵל:


My soul desireth to pray before thee in the night; yea, my spirit within me is blessing thee: when thy judgments are prepared for the earth, those that dwell in the world shall be taught to practise truth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:10)


יְהַבְתָּא לְהוֹן אַרְכָּא לְרַשִׁיעַיָא אִם יְתוּבוּן לְאוֹרַיְתָךְ וְלָא תָבוּ כָּל יוֹמִין דַהֲווֹ קַיָמִין יַעְבְּדוּן קְשׁוֹט עַל אַרְעָא חֲווֹ שַׁקָרִין אַף אִינוּן לָא יֶחֱזוּן בְּתוּשְׁבְּחַת יְקָרָךְ יְיָ:


Thou hast granted unto the wicked continuance, that verily they should return to the law; but they did not return, all the days that they lived ;—that they should practise truth in the earth, but they dealt falsely: yea, they will not regard the praise of thy glory, O Lord!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:11)


יְיָ כַּד תִּתְגְלֵי בִּגְבוּרְתָּךְ לְאוֹטָבָא לִדְחַלְתָּךְ לָא יִנְהַר לְסָנְאֵי עַמְךְ יֶחֱזוּן רַשִׁיעַיָא וְיִבְהֲתוּן פּוּרְעֲנוּת עַמָא תַחְפִּינוּן אַף אֶשְׁתָּא לְבַעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ תְּשֵׁיצֵי:


O Lord, when thou shalt be revealed in thy power to do good to them that fear thee, there will be no light to the enemies of thy people: the wicked shall see it, and shall be confounded. The vengeance of thy people shall cover them; yea, fire shall consume thy enemies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:12)


יְיָ תְּתַקֵין שְׁלָמָא לָנָא אֲרֵי בְּכָל עִדָן חֲסֵיכְנָא מֵחוֹבָנָא אַתְּ עֲבֵיד עִמָנָא:


O Lord, thou wilt ordain peace for us: for at all times, when we restrained ourselves from our sins, thou wast working for us.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:13)


יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא שַׁלִיטוּ בָנָא עַמְמַיָא לְמֶהֱוֵי עֲלָנָא רִבּוֹנִין בַּר מִנָךְ בְּרַם בְּמֵימְרָךְ אֲנַחְנָא רְחִיצִין בִּשְׁמָךְ אֲנַחְנָא מוֹדָן:


O Lord our God, the nations have had dominion over us, being lords over us beside thee; but we trust in thy WORD; we praise thy name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:14)


אִינוּן פַּלְחִין לְמֵתִין דְלָא יֵחוּן לִגְבָּרֵהוֹן דְלָא יְקוּמוּן בְּכֵן כַּד תַּסְעַר עֲלֵיהוֹן חוֹבֵיהוֹן וּתְשֵׁיצִינוּן וְתוֹבִיד כָּל דָכְרָנְהוֹן:


They worship idols, who do not live, their heroes, who shall not rise; therefore, when thou “shalt visit their sins, thou wilt make an end of them, and make all memorial of them to perish.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:15)


אִתְגְלֵיתָא לְכַנָשָׁא מְבַדְרֵי עַמָךְ יְיָ אַף עָתִיד אַתְּ לְקָרָבָא גָלוּתְהוֹן לְאִתְגְלָאָה בִגְבוּרְתֵּךְ לְרַחָקָא כָּל רַשִׁיעַיָא לִגְהִנָם:


Thou shalt reveal thyself to gather the dispersed of thy people, thou wilt bring together their captivity. Thou wilt reveal thyself in thy power to cast all the wicked into hell.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:16)


יְיָ בְּעָקָא הֲווֹ דְכִירִין לִדְחַלְתָּךְ בְּעָקַתְהוֹן הֲווֹ מַלְפִין אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתָךְ בַּחֲשָׁי:


O Lord, in distress they remembered thy fear; in their trouble they were ardently learning the doctrine of thy law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:17)


כִּמְעַדְיָא דִמְטָא דִבְעִדָן מֵילְדָה זָיְעָא וּמְצַוְחָא בְּחֶבְלָהָא כֵּן הֲוֵינָא מִן קֳדָם דְחַבְנָא קֳדָמָךְ יְיָ:


Like as a woman with child, who draweth near the time of her delivery, trembleth, and crieth out in her pangs, so have we been, because we have sinned before thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:18)


אֲחַדְתָּנָא עָקָא כִּמְעַדְיָא דִמְטָת עִדָן מֵילְדָהּ קַלִיל כְּרוּחָא פּוּרְקַן לָא אַיְתִיאוּ אַרְעָא לָא עֲבַדוּ אַף פְּרִישָׁן לָא יָכְלִין לְמֶעְבַּד דְדָיְרִין בְּתֵבֵל:


Distress swift as the wind hath seized us, like a woman with child, who draweth near the time of her delivery; the inhabitants of the world have not brought deliverance: they have not tilled the earth, neither have they been able to perform any miracles.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:19)


אַתְּ הוּא מְחַיֵי מֵיתִין גַרְמֵי נְבֶלְתְּהוֹן אַתְּ מֵקִים יֵחוּן וִישַׁבְּחוּן קֳדָמָךְ כָּל דַהֲווֹ רְמָן בְּעַפְרָא אֲרֵי טַל נְהוֹר טַלָךְ לְעָבְדֵי אוֹרַיְתָךְ וְרַשִׁיעַיָא דִי יְהַבְתָּא לְהוֹן גְבוּרָא וְאִינוּן עֲבַרוּ עַל מֵימְרָךְ לִגְהִנָם תִּמְסַר:


Thou art he who dost quicken the dead, the bones of their dead bodies thou dost raise up. They shall live, and offer praise before thee, all that were cast into the dust, because thy dew is the dew of light to them who do thy law; but thou wilt deliver the wicked into hell, to whom thou hast given power, for they have transgressed against thy WORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:20)


אֱזֵיל עַמִי עֲבֵיד לָךְ עוֹבָדִין טָבִין דְיִגְנוּן עֲלָךְ בְּעִדַן עָקָא אִטַמַר כִּזְעֵיר זְמַן עַד דְיֶעְדֵי לְוָט:


Come, O my people; produce for thyself good works, which shall protect thee in the time of distress: hide thyself as it were for a little moment, until the curse shall have passed away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 26:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/26:21)


אֲרֵי הָא יְיָ מִתְגְלֵי מֵאֲתַר שְׁכִנְתֵּהּ לְאַסְעָרָא חוֹב יָתֵיב אַרְעָא עֲלוֹהִי וּתְגַלֵי אַרְעָא עַל דַם זַכַּאי דְאִתְשֵׁיד בָּהּ וְלָא תְכַסֵי עוֹד עַל קְטִילָהָא:


For, behold, the Lord is revealing himself from the place of his Shekinah, to punish the inhabitants of the earth for their sins; and the earth shall disclose the innocent blood that was shed in her, and shall no more cover her slain.



## Chapter 27

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:1)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יַסְעַר יְיָ בְּחַרְבֵּהּ רַבְּתָא תַקִפְתָּא וַחֲסַנְתָּא עַל מַלְכָּא דְאִתְרַבְרַב כְּפַרְעֹה מַלְכָּא קַדְמָאָה וְעַל מַלְכָּא דְאִתְגָאֵי כְּסַנְחֶרִיב מַלְכָּא תִנְיָנָא וְיִקְטוֹל יַת מַלְכָּא דְתַקִיף כְּתַנִינָא דִי בְיַמָא:


At that time the Lord shall punish with his great, mighty, and strong sword the king, who has magnified himself as Pharaoh the first, and the king who has exalted himself as Sennacherib the second; and he shall slay the king that is strong as the dragon that is in the sea.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:2)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְהִיא כְּכֶרֶם נְצִיב בְאַרְעָא טָבָא שַׁבַּחוּ לָהּ:


At that time the congregation of Israel, which is like a vineyard planted in a goodly land, shall sing concerning that vineyard:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:3)


אֲנָא יְיָ נְטַר לְהוֹן קְיָם אֲבָהָתְהוֹן וְלָא אֱשֵׁיצִינוּן אֶלָא בִזְמַן דְאִינוּן מַרְגְזִין קֳדָמַי אֲנָא מַשְׁקֵי לְהוֹן כָּס פּוּרְעֲנוּתְהוֹן אִלוּלֵי חוֹבֵיהוֹן גָרְמִין לְאִתְפְּרַע מִנְהוֹן כְּבַר לֵילֵי וֵימָם מֵימְרִי מֵגִין עֲלֵיהוֹן:


I, the Lord, keep the covenant of their fathers with them, that I may not destroy them; but at the time that they provoked me to anger, I gave them the cup of their punishment to drink; but their sins were the cause of their punishment; nevertheless, my WORD shall protect them by day and by night.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:4)


הָא גְבוּרָן סַגִי קֳדָמַי הֲלָא אִם יְשַׁווּן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲפֵּיהוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד אוֹרַיְתָא אֶשְׁלַח רוּגְזִי וַחֲמָתִי בְּעַמְמַיָא דְמִתְגְרוּן בְּהוֹן וָאֱשֵׁיצִינוּן כְּמָא דִמְשֵׁיצֵי אֶשְׁתָּא הוֹבַאִי וּבוּר כַּחֲדָא:


Behold, many mighty works are before me. Is it not so? If the house of Israel would set their face to do my law, I would send my anger and my fury among the nations, who are waging war against them, and I would destroy them, as the fire destroyeth the briers and the thorns together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:5)


אִם יִתְקְפוּן בְּפִתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְתִי אִתְעֲבֵיד שְׁלָמָא לְהוֹן מִכְּעַן שְׁלָמָא יִתְעֲבֵיד לְהוֹן:


If they would lay hold on the words of my law, peace would be made with them; henceforth peace would be made with them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:6)


יִתְכַּנְשׁוּן מִבֵּינֵי גַלְוַתְהוֹן וִיתוּבוּן לְאַרְעֲהוֹן תַּמָן יִתְיְלִידוּן דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב יִפְשׁוּן וְיִסְגוּן דְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִמְלוּן אַפֵי תֵבֵל בְּנֵי בְנִין:


They shall be gathered from the midst of their captivity, and return to their country; there children shall be born to the house of Jacob; they of the house of Israel shall be fruitful, and they shall multiply; their children's children shall fill the face of the world.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:7)


הַכְּמַחְתָּא דַהֲוָה מָחֵי מָחוֹהִי אִם כִּקְטוֹל קְטִילוֹהִי יִתְקְטֵיל:


What according to the smiting wherewith he was smiting them that smote him, or with the slaughter of his slain is he slain?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:8)


בְּסָאתָא דַהֲוֵיתָא כְאֵיל בָּהּ יְכִילוּן לָךְ דַהֲוֵיתָא שָׁלַח וּמֵעִיק לְהוֹן הֲגֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן בְמִלָא מַתְקֵיף עֲלֵיהוֹן בְּיוֹם לְוָט:


With the measure wherewith thou didst measure, they shall measure unto thee, thou didst send forth and oppress them. He meditated a word against them. He prevailed against them in the day of wrath.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:9)


בְּכֵן בְּדָא יִשְׁתְּבֵיקוּן חוֹבַי בֵּית יַעֲקֹב וְדֵין כָּל עוֹבָדֵי אַסְטִיוּת חוֹבוֹהִי בְּשַׁוְיוּתֵהּ כָּל אַבְנֵי אֱגוֹרָא כְּאַבְנָא גִיר מְנַפְצָן לָא יִתְקַיְמוּן אֲשֵׁרַיָא וְחַנִסְנְסַיָא:


By this, therefore, shall the sins of the house of Jacob be forgiven; and thus it shall be with all the works of the perverseness of their sins, namely, that they shall make all the stones of the (idolatrous) altar like chalk stones that are beaten asunder; the groves and the sun-images shall not; be raised up again.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:10)


אֲרֵי קַרְתָּא דַהֲוָת כְּרִיכָא בִּלְחוֹדָא תִּתַב תְּהֵא מְטַלְטְלָא וּשְׁבִיקָא כְּמַדְבְּרָא בָּהּ יְגִיחוּן צַדִיקַיָא וְיִבְזוּן יַת נִכְסָהָא וִיסוּפוּן מַשִׁרְיָתָהָא מִלְמִפָּק:


But the city that was fortified shall sit solitary: she shall totter, and be forsaken like a desert; the just shall wage war against her, and plunder her treasures, and make an end of her hosts, so that none shall go forth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:11)


יִתְקַצַר חֵילֵיהוֹן וְיִבְהֲתוּן בְּעוֹבְדֵיהוֹן יִתַּבְּרוּן נְשַׁיָא עָלָן לְבֵית דְחַלְתְּהוֹן וּמַלְפָן יַתְהוֹן אֲרֵי לָא עַם סוּכְלְתָן אִינוּן עַל כֵּן לָא יְרַחֵם עֲלֵיהוֹן דְעַבְדִינוּן וּדְבָרָנוּן לָא יָחוּס עֲלֵיהוֹן:


Their armies shall be cut off; and they shall be confounded on account of their works, which shall be broken: women shall enter the house of their idolatry, and teach them, because they are a people of no understanding: therefore He that made them shall not have mercy on them, and He that created them shall show them no favour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:12)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יִתְרְמוּן קְטִילִין קֳדָם יְיָ מִכֵּיף נַהֲרָא פְּרָת עַד נַחֲלָא דְמִצְרָיִם וְאַתּוּן תִּתְקָרְבוּן חַד לִסְטַר חַד בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


And it shall come to pass at that time, that the slain of the Lord shall be cast from the bank of the river Euphrates into the river of Egypt; and ye shall be brought near one to the other, o ye sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 27:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/27:13)


וִיהֵי בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יִתְקַע בְּשׁוֹפָרָא רַבָּא וְיֵיתוּן דִי גְלוֹ לְאַרְעָא דְאַתּוּר וּדְאִטַלְטָלוּ לְאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וְיִסְגְדוּן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּטוּרָא דְקוּדְשָׁא בִּירוּשְׁלֵם:


And it shall come to pass at that time, that the great trumpet shall be blown, and they shall come, who had gone into captivity into the land of Assyria, and those who were cast into the land of Egypt, and they shall worship before the Lord, in the holy mountain at Jerusalem.



## Chapter 28

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:1)


וַי דִי יָהֵיב כִּתְרָא לְגֵיוְתָנָא טִפְשָׁא רַבָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְיָהֵב מִצְנַפְתָּא לְרַשִׁיעַיָא דְבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא תּוּשְׁבַּחְתֵּהּ דִי עַל רֵישׁ חֵילָא שְׁמֵינָא כְּתִישֵׁי חֲמָר:


Woe that He gave a crown to the proud and foolish prince of Israel, and that He gave a diadem to the wicked of the house of the sanctuary of His praise; they who are at the head of the valley of fatness are smitten with wine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:2)


הָא מָחָן תַּקִיפָן וַחֲסִינָן אַתְיָן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ כְּזַרְמִית בָּרָד כְּעַלְעוּל רוּחַ כְּזַרְמִית מַיָין תַּקִיפִין שָׁטְפִין כֵּן יֵתוּן עֲלֵיהוֹן עַמְמַיָא וְיִגְלוּנוּן מֵאַרְעֲהוֹן לְאַרְעָא אוֹחֲרִי בְּחוֹבַיָא דִי בִידֵהוֹן:


Behold, plagues, strong and mighty, are coming from the Lord: like a storm of hail, like a whirlwind, like a storm of mighty waters, overflowing, thus the nations shall come upon them, and shall lead them into captivity to another land on account of the sins which are in their hands.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:3)


בְּרִגְלִין יִתְּדָשׁ כִּתְרָא דְגֵיוְתָנָא טִפְּשָׁא רַבָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


The crown of the pride of the foolish prince of Israel shall be trodden down with feet.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:4)


וִיהֵי דְיָהִיב מִצְנַפְתָּא לְרַשִׁיעַיָא דְבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא תּוּשְׁבַּחְתֵּהּ דִי עַל רֵישׁ חֵילָא שְׁמֵינָא כְּבִכּוּרָא עַד לָא קַיְטָא דְאִם יֶחֱזֵי דְחָזֵי יָתָהּ כְּאִלוּ הִיא בִּידֵהּ יְבַלְעִינָהּ:


And the diadem, which He gave to the wicked of the house of the sanctuary of His praise, which is on the head of the valley of fatness, shall be as the first ripe fig before the summer, which, when one sees it, no sooner is it in the hand, than one devours it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:5)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יְהֵי מְשִׁיחָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת לִכְלִילָא דְחֶדְוָא וּלְכֶתֶר דְתוּשְׁבָּחָא לִשְׁאָרָא דְעַמֵהּ:


At that time, the Messiah of the Lord of hosts shall be for a crown of rejoicing, and for a crown of praise to the remnant of His people:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:6)


לְמֵימָר קְשׁוֹט דְדִין לִדְיָתְבִין בְּבֵית דִינָא לְמִדָן דִין דִקְשׁוֹט וּלְמִתַּן נִצְחָנָא לִדְנָפְקִין בִּקְרָבָא לַאֲתָבוּתְהוֹן בִּשְׁלָם לְבָתֵּיהוֹן:


For a word of true judgments to them that sit in the house of justice, in order that they should judge according to truth; and to give victory to them that are going forth into battle, to bring them back in peace to their homes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:7)


וְאַף אִלֵין בְּחַמְרָא רְווֹ וּבְעַתִּיקָא אִסְתַּלְעֲמוּ כַהֲנָא וְסָפַּר רְווֹ מִן עַתִּיק אִסְתַּלְעֲמוּ מִן חֲמַר טְעוֹ מִן עַתִּיק אִתְפְּנִיאוּ בָּתַר מֵיכַל בְּסִיס טְעוֹ דַיָנְהָא:


But verily these are drunk with wine, they are swallowed up of old wine: the priest and the scribe are drunk, in old wine they are swallowed up: on account of wine they have erred; they are turned after sweet food; their judges have erred.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:8)


אֲרֵי כָל פָּתוֹרֵיהוֹן מְלָן מֵיכַל מִגְעַל וּמְשַׁקֵץ לֵית לְהוֹן אֲתַר דִי נָקִי מִן אוֹנָס:


Because all their tables are full of polluted and loathsome food: they have not a spot free from rapine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:9)


לְמַן אִתְיְהִיבַת אוֹרַיְתָא וּמַן אִתְפְּקַד לְמִסְבַּר חוּכְמָא הֲלָא לְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל דִרְחִימִין מִכָּל עַמְמַיָא וַחֲבִיבִין מִכָּל מַלְכְּוָתָא:


To whom was the law given? and to whom was the command given to understand wisdom? Was it not to the house of Israel, who were beloved above all nations, and beloved above all the kingdoms?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:10)


אֲרֵי אִתְפַּקַדוּ לְמֶעְבַּד אוֹרַיְתָא וּמָא דְאִתְפַּקַדוּ לָא צְבִיאוּ לְמֶעְבַּד וְאִתְנַבִּיאוּ עֲלֵיהוֹן נְבִיַיָא דְאִם יְתוּבוּן יִשְׁתְּבֵק לְהוֹן וּלְמִלֵי נְבִיַיָא לָא קַבִּילוּ הַלִיכוּ בַּרַעֲוַת נַפְשְׁהוֹן וְלָא חֲמִידוּ לְמֶעְבַּד אוֹרַיְתִי סְבָרוּ דְיִתְקַיֵים לְהוֹן פּוּלְחַן טַעֲוָתָא וְלָא סְבָרוּ לְפוּלְחַן בֵּית מַקְדְשִׁי כִּזְעֵר הֲוָה בְּעֵינֵיהוֹן בֵּית מַקְדָשִׁי לְמִפְלַח תַּמָן כִּזְעֵיר בְּעֵינֵיהוֹן שְׁכִנְתִּי תַּמָן:


Surely, they were commanded to do the law; but what they were commanded, they were not willing to do. The prophets prophesied unto them, that if they would repent, it should be forgiven them; but they did not obey the words of the prophets: they walked after the desire of their soul, neither did they desire to do my law. They hoped to have idolatrous worship established among them, and they did not consider the worship of the house of my sanctuary: to worship in the house of my sanctuary was as a little thing in their eyes, my Shekinah was as a little thing in their eyes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:11)


אֲרֵי בְּשִׁנוּי מַמְלֵל וּבְלִישָׁן תּוֹלְעֲבָא הֲווֹ מַלְעִגִין לְקָבֵיל נְבִיַיָא דְמִתְנַבָּן לְהוֹן עַמָא הָדֵין:


Because with feigned speech, and with mocking language, this people mocked the prophets, who prophesies unto them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:12)


דְאָמְרִין לְהוֹן נְבִיַיָא דֵין בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא פְּלָחוּ בֵיהּ וְדָא אַחְסָנַת בֵּית נְיָחָא וְלָא אָבוּ לְקַבָּלָא אוּלְפַן:


The prophets said unto them, This is the house of the sanctuary, worship ye in it: and this is the possession in which there is rest; but they would not accept instructions.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:13)


דֵין יְהֵי כָס פּוּרְעֲנוּתְהוֹן עַל דַעֲבָרוּ עַל פִּתְגָמָא דַייָ וְעַל דְאִתְפַּקָדוּ לְמֶעְבַּד אוֹרַיְתָא וּמָא דְאִתְפַּקַדוּ לָא צְבִיאוּ לְמֶעְבַּד בְּכֵן יִתְמַסְרוּן לְעַמְמַיָא דְלָא יְדָעוּ אוֹרַיְתָא עַל דְהַלִיכוּ בִרְעוּת נַפְשְׁהוֹן וְלָא חֲמִידוּ לְמֶעְבַּד רְעוּתִי בְּכֵן יְסַבְּרוּן לְסָעִיד בְּעִדָן דְאַיְתִי עֲלֵיהוֹן עָקָא וְלָא יְהֵי לְהוֹן סָעִיד וְסָמִיךְ עַל דַהֲוָה זְעֵיר בְּעֵינֵיהוֹן בֵּית מַקְדָשִׁי לְמִפְלָח תַּמָן בְכֵן יִשְׁתָּאֲרוּן כִּזְעֵיר בֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא דְיִגְלוֹן לְתַמָן בְּדִיל דִיהָכוּן וְיִתְקְלוּן לַאֲחוֹרָא וְיִתַּבְּרוּן וְיִתַּצְדוּן וְיִתְאַחֲדוּן:


This shall be the cup of their punishment, because they have transgressed the word of the Lord, and because they were commanded to do my law, but would not do what they were commanded. Therefore they shall be delivered unto the nations, who do not know the law; because they walked after the desire of their soul, and had no delight to do my will. Therefore they shall hope for help at the time that I shall bring distress upon them; but they shall have no help or support. Because the house of my sanctuary was too little in their eyes to worship there, therefore they shall be left as a little thing in the eyes of the nations, among whom they shall go in captivity, in order that they may walk, and stumble backward, and be broken, and snared, and taken.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:14)


בְּכֵן קַבִּילוּ פִּתְגָמָא דַייָ גַבְרִין רַשִׁיעִין שִׁלְטוֹנֵי עַמָא הָדֵין דִי בִירוּשְׁלֵם:


Therefore, hear ye the word of the Lord, O wicked men, rulers of this people that are in Jerusalem:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:15)


אֲרֵי אֲמַרְתּוּן גְזַרְנָא קְיָם עִם קְטוֹלָא וְעִם מַחְבְּלָא עֲבַדְנָא שְׁלָמָא מְחַת סַנְאָה אֲרֵי תֵיתֵי עֲלֵיכוֹן כְּנַחַל מַגְבַּר דְאַתּוּן אָמְרִין לָא תֵיתֵי עֲלָנָא אֲרֵי שַׁוֵינָא בִּכְדִיב רָחֲצָנָנָא וּבְגִין שִׁקְרִין אִטַמַרְנָא:


Because ye say, “We have made a covenant with death, and have made peace with the destroyer:” ye say, when the blow of the enemy shall come upon you like an overwhelming river, “It shall not come upon us, because we have placed our confidence in a lie, and have hidden ourselves under falsehood.”


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:16)


בְּכֵן כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים הָא אֲנָא מְמַנֵי בְצִיוֹן מֶלֶךְ מֶלֶךְ תַּקִיף גִיבַּר וְאֵימְתָן אַתְקְפִינֵהּ וְאַחְסְנִינֵהּ אֲמַר נְבִיָא וְצַדִיקַיָא דְהֵימָנוּ בְאִלֵין בְּמֵיתֵי עָקָא לָא יִזְדַעְזְעוּן:


Therefore, thus saith the Lord God, Behold, I appoint a king in Zion; a king mighty, powerful, and terrible: I will make him powerful, and I will strengthen Him, saith the prophet. But the righteous, who believe these things shall not be moved, when distress shall come.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:17)


וֶאֱשַׁוֵי דִנָא תַקִין דְאִטַמַרְתּוּן כְּחוּט בִּנְיָנָא וּזְכוּתָא כְּאֶבֶן מַשְׁקוּלְתָּא וְיִדְלַק רוּגְזִי בְּרוֹחֲצָן כִּדְבֵיכוֹן וְעַל דְאִטַמַרְתּוּן מִן קֳדָם מְעִיקָא יַתְכוֹן עַמְמַיָא יִגְלוֹן:


And I will make judgment straight as a line of a building, and justice as a plummet, because ye would hide yourselves. And my justice shall burn against the confidence of your lie; and because ye would hide yourselves from the coming distress, the nations shall lead you into captivity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:18)


וִיבַטֵל קִיָמְכוֹן דְעִם קָטוֹלָא וּשְׁלָמְכוֹן דְעִם מְחַבְּלָא לָא יִתְקַיָם מְחַת סָנְאָה אֲרֵי תֵיתֵי עֲלֵיכוֹן כְּנַחַל מַגְבַּר וּתְחוֹן לְהוֹן לְדָיִשׁ:


And your covenant with death shall be destroyed, and your peace with the destroyer shall not stand. When the stroke of the enemy shall come upon you, it shall be like an overwhelming river, and ye shall be unto them for a treading under foot.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:19)


בִּזְמַן מֶעֱדוֹהִי יְגַלֵי יַתְכוֹן אֲרֵי בִּצְפָר בִּצְפָר יֶעֱדִי בִּימָם וּבְלֵילִי וִיהֵי עַד לָא יִמְטֵי זְמַן לְוָטָא תִּסְתַּכְּלוּן בְּמִלֵי נְבִיַיָא:


At the time of its passing by, it shall lead you captive, because each morning it shall pass by, by day and by night: and it shall come to pass before the time of the curse shall have come, that ye shall consider the words of the prophets.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:20)


אֲרֵי יִתְקַצַר חֵילֵיהוֹן מִשִׁעְבּוּד תַּקִיף וְשִׁלְטוֹן בַּבְלָאֵי מְעִיק יִסְגֵי מֵרְוָא:


For their strength shall be diminished by reason of mighty slavery; and the government of the oppressor shall increase their subjection.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:21)


אֲרֵי כְּמָא דְזָעוּ טוּרַיָא כַּד אִיתְגְלֵי יְקָרָא דַייָ בְּיוֹמֵי עוּזִיָה מַלְכָּא וּכְנִיסִין דַעֲבַד לִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בְּמֵישַׁר גִבְעוֹן לְאִתְפְּרַע מֵרַשִׁיעַיָא דַעֲבַרוּ עַל מֵימְרֵהּ כֵּן יִתְגְלֵי לְאִתְפְּרָעָא מִדְעָבְדִין עוֹבָדִין נוּכְרָאִין עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן וּמִדְפָלְחִין פּוּלְחַן טַעֲוָתָא פּוּלְחַנְהוֹן:


For as the mountains trembled when the glory of the Lord was revealed in the days of king Uzziah, and in the wonders which He performed for Joshua in the valley of Gibeon, taking vengeance on the wicked, who had transgressed against His Word: so shall He be revealed to take vengeance on them, who work works, strange works, and on those who worship with idolatrous worship.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:22)


וּכְעַן לָא תִתְרַשְׁעוּן דִלְמָא יִתְקְפוּן יְסוּרֵיכוֹן אֲרֵי גְמִירָא וְשֵׁיצָאָה שְׁמָעִית מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת עַל כָּל יָתְבֵי אַרְעָא:


But now deal not wickedly, lest your bands be made strong: for I have heard from the Lord, the God of hosts, a consummation, and an end, upon all the inhabitants of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:23)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא אֲצִיתוּ וּשְׁמַעוּ קָלִי אִתְפְּנוּ וְקַבִּילוּ לְמֵימְרִי:


The prophet said, Listen to, and hear my voice, turn and hear my word.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:24)


בְּכָל עִדָן מִתְנַבָּן נְבִיַיָא לַאֲלָפָא מָאִים יִתְפַּתְּחָן אוּדְנֵי חַיָבַיָא וִיקַבְּלוּן אוּלְפַן:


The prophets prophesied at all times in order to teach, if peradventure the ears of sinners might be opened, and receive instruction.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:25)


הֲלָא אִם יְשַׁווּן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל אַפֵיהוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד אוֹרַיְתָא וִיתוּב וְיַכְנֵישׁ יַתְהוֹן מִבֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא דְאִינוּן מְבַדְרִין בֵּינֵיהוֹן הָא כְקִצְחָא וְכִכְמוֹנָא דְמִזְדְרִיק וְיַקְרֵיב יַתְהוֹן זַרְעֲיָן לְשִׁבְטֵיהוֹן הָא כְּזָרְעֵי חִטִין עַל בַּיִיר וּסְעוֹרִין עַל סוּמָנִין וְכוֹנְתִין עַל תְּחוּמִין:


Is it not so? If the house of Israel would set their faces to do the law, and repent, then behold, He would gather them from among the nations, amongst whom they were dispersed, like fitches and cumin that are scattered: and, behold, he would bring their offspring together according to their tribes, as seed of wheat in the uncultivated field, and barley in the appointed place, and spelt in the borders.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:26)


הָא כָּל אִלֵין אוּלְפַן דְדִין דְיֵדְעוּן דִי אֱלָהָא מְחַוֵי לְהוֹן אוֹרְחָא דְתַקְנָא דִיהֲכוּן בָּהּ:


All these things are instruction of judgment, that they may know, that our God shows them the right path, in which they ought to walk.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:27)


אֲרֵי לָא בְמוֹרִיגֵי בַּרְזְלָא מַדְרְכִין קִצְחָא וְגַלְגַלֵי עֶגְלָא עַל כַּמוֹנָא מְחַזְרִין אֲרֵי בְחוּטְרָא חָבְטִין קִצְחָא וְכַמוֹנָא בְמַחֲבוֹטָא:


For they do not tread out the fitches with an iron threshing instrument, nor do they turn the wheels of a cart upon the cumin; but they beat out the fitches with a staff, and the cumin with a rod.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:28)


יַת עִבּוּרָא מַדְרְכִין אַף יָתֵהּ אֲרֵי לָא לְעַלְמָא אַדְרָכָא יַדְרְכוּנֵהּ וּמְבַלְבֵל בְּגִלְגְלֵי עֶגְלָתֵהּ וּמַפְרֵישׁ יַת עִבּוּרָא וּמַפְרַח יַת דוּקָא:


Corn they tread out, yet they will not continue to tread it out for ever; but he will throw it into confusion with the wheel of his wain, and would separate the corn, and blow away the chaff.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 28:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/28:29)


אַף דָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת נַפְקָא דְאַתְקִין עַלְמָא בְּמַחְשָׁבַת דַעְתֵּהּ רַבְּתָא אַסְגֵי עוֹבָדֵי בְּרֵאשִׁית בְּסַגִי חוּכְמְתֵהּ:


This also cometh forth from the Lord of hosts, who in the vast knowledge of His mind hath established the world; He hath multiplied His works in the beginning by His great wisdom.



## Chapter 29

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:1)


וַי מַדְבְּחָא מַדְבְּחָא דִבְנֵי בְקַרְתָּא דִי שְׁרָא בָהּ דָוִד מִן קֳדָם כְּנִישַׁת מַשִׁרְיָן דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין עֲלָהּ שְׁנָא בִשְׁנָא בִּדְחַגִין יִבְטְלוּן:


Woe to the altar, the altar which is built in the city in which David dwelt; because of the assembly of the armies which are gathering themselves together against her year by year, because the festivals shall cease.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:2)


וְעָאִיק לְקַרְתָּא דְמַדְבְּחָא בָהּ וּתְהֵי צַדְיָא וְרֵקַנְיָא וּתְהֵי מַקְפָא קֳדָמַי דַם קְטִילִין כְּאַקָפוּת מַדְבְּחָא דַם נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא בְּיוֹמָא דְחַגָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


And I will distress the city, in which the altar is, and she shall be desolate and empty; and she shall be surrounded before me with the blood of the slain, as the altar is surrounded round about with the blood of the holy sacrifices in the day of the festival.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:3)


וְאַשְׁרֵי עֲלָיִךְ מַשִׁרְיָן וְאַבְנֵי עֲלָיִךְ כַּרְקוֹם וְאֶצְבּוֹר עֲלָיִךְ מְלֵיתָא:


And I will encamp against thee with armies; and I will build a fortified camp against thee, and I will cast up a mound against thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:4)


וְתִמְאֲכִין מֵאַרְעָא תְמַלְלִין וּמֵעַפְרָא יִנְצְפָן מִלַיִךְ וִיהֵי כְּאוֹב מֵאַרְעָא קָלַיִךְ וּמֵעַפְרָא מִלַיִךְ יִנְצְפָן:


And thou shalt be brought low, and shalt speak from beneath the earth, thy words shall gibber from the dust; and thy voice shall come like that of a necromancer out of the ground; yea, thy words shall gibber from the dust.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:5)


וִיהֵי כְּאָבָק דָקִיק הֲמוֹן מְבַדְרָיִךְ וּכְמוֹץ דְעָדִי אִתְרְגוּשָׁא תַּקִיפִין וִיהֵי בִּתְכֵּיף אִתְרְגוּשָׁא:


Moreover the multitude of those scattering thee shall be like small dust, and the tumultuous assembly of the mighty as chaff that passeth away; and there shall be a tumult suddenly.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:6)


מִן קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת תִּתְפַּקֵיד בְּרַעַם וּבְזִיעַ וְקַל סַגִי בְּעַלְעוֹל וּבְרוּחַ וְשַׁלְהוֹבֵי אֶשָׁא דִמְשֵׁיצַיָא:


Thou shalt be visited with thunder from the Lord of hosts, and with earthquake, and with a great noise; with storm, and tempest, and a flame of devouring fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:7)


וִיהֵי כְמֶחְלֵם הִרְהוּר לֵילֵי הֲמוֹן כָּל עַמְמַיָא דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין עַל קַרְתָּא דְמַדְבְּחָא בָהּ וכָל מַשִׁרְיָתְהוֹן וְחֵילֵיהוֹן וְדִמְעִיקִין לְהוֹן:


And the multitude of all the nations, that are gathered together against the city, and the altar which is in her, and all their camps and their armies, that are oppressing her, shall be like a phantom of the night.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:8)


וִיהֵי כְּמָא דִי יַחֲלוֹם כַּפְנָא וְהָא אָכֵיל וּמִתְעַר וַחֲסֵירָא נַפְשֵׁיהּ וּכְמָא דְיַחֲלוֹם צָחְיָא וְהָא שְׁתֵי וּמִתְעָר וְהָא מְשַׁלְחֵי וְנַפְשֵׁהּ מְשַׁלְהַיָא כֵּן יְהֵי הֲמוֹן כָּל עַמְמַיָא דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין עַל טוּרָא דְצִיוֹן:


And it shall be as when a hungry man dreameth, behold, he eateth; but he awakes, and het is in want: or as when a thirsty man dreameth, and behold, he drinketh, but he awaketh, and behold, he is faint, he is spent: so shall the multitude of all the nations be, that gather themselves together against the mountain of Zion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:9)


אִתְרְפוּ וּשְׁחוֹ אִשְׁתְּגִישׁוּ אִיזְדַמִימוּ וְאִשְׁתְּמָמוּ רְווֹ וְלָא מִן חֲמַר טְעוֹ וְלָא מִן עַתִּיק:


Be astounded, wonder, be ye terrified, and muse, and marvel. They are drunk, but not with wine; they stagger, but not with old wine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:10)


אֲרֵי רְמָא בֵינֵיכוֹן יְיָ רוּחַ דְטָעוּ וְאַטְמַר מִנְכוֹן יַת נְבִיַיָא וְיַת סַפְרַיָא וְיַת מַלְפַיָא דַהֲווֹ מַלְפִין לְכוֹן אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתָא טַמָר:


Because the Lord shall cast among you a spirit of error; and He shall hide the prophets from you, and He shall hide the scribes, and the teachers, who teach you the instruction of the law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:11)


וַהֲוַת לְכוֹן נְבוּאַת כּוֹלָא כְּפִתְגָמֵי סִפְרָא דַחֲתִים דְאִם יִתְּנוּן יָתֵהּ לִדְיָדַע סִפְרָא לְמֵימַר קְרֵי כְעַן דֵין וְיֵימַר לֵית אֲנָא יָכִיל אֲרֵי חֲתִים הוּא:


And all the prophecy shall be unto you as the words of a sealed book, which if one gives to a man that is learned, saying, “Read this now;” then he shall answer, “I am not able, because it is sealed.”


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:12)


וְיִתְיְהַב סַפְרָא לִדְלָא יָדַע סִפְרָא לְמֵימַר קְרֵי כְעַן דֵין וְיֵימַר לֵית אֲנָא יָדַע סִפְרָא:


Or should the book be given to one that is not learned, saying, “Read this now,” then he, shall answer, “I am not learned.”


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:13)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ חֲלַף דְאִתְרַבְרַב עַמָא הָדֵין בְּפוּמְהוֹן וּבְסִפְוָתְהוֹן מְיַקְרִין קֳדָמַי וְלִבְּהוֹן אִתְרַחֵק מִדַחַלְתִּי וַהֲוַת דְחַלְתְהוֹן קֳדָמַי כְּתַפְקִידַת גַבְרִין מַלְפִין:


Wherefore the Lord hath said: Because I am magnified by the mouth of this people, and with their lips they do honour me, but their heart is far from my fear, and their fear towards me is as the commandment of men teaching them:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:14)


בְּכֵן הָא אֲנָא מוֹסִיף לְמִמְחֵי יַת עַמָא הָדֵין מָחָן מַפְרְשָׁן וְתֵיבַד חוּכְמְתָא מֵחַכִּימֵיהוֹן וְסֻכְלְתָנֻתָא מִסֻכָלְתָנֵיהֹן תִּטַמָר:


Therefore, behold, I shall again strike this people with wonderful strokes; the wisdom of their wise men shall perish, and the understanding of their prudent men shall be hidden.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:15)


וַי דִמְדַמָן לְאִטַמָרָא עֵצָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וַהֲווֹ כִדְבְקִבְלָא עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן וְאָמְרִין לֵית דְחָזֵי לָנָא וְלֵית דְיָדַע בְּעוֹבָדָנָא:


Woe unto them that purpose to hide counsel from before the Lord, that their works may be in darkness, and they say, None sees us, and none takes cognizance of our works.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:16)


הַלְמֶהְפַּךְ עוֹבָדֵיכוֹן אַתּוּן בָּעָן הָא כְמָא דְטִינָא בְּיַד פָּחֲרָא כֵּן אַתּוּן חֲשִׁיבִין קֳדָמַי הַאֶפְשַׁר דְיֵימַר טִינָא לְעָבְדֵיהּ לָא עֲבַדְתָּנִי וּבִרְיְתָא דְתֵימַר לְבָרָהָא לָא חַכֵּימְתָּא לִי:


Why do ye seek to pervert your works? Behold, as the clay in the hand of the potter, thus are ye accounted before me. Is it possible that the clay should say to its maker, Thou hast not made me? or should the creature say to its creator, Thou dost not understand me?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:17)


הֲלָא עוֹד צִיבְחַר כִּזְעֵיר וִיתוּב לִבְנָן לְמֶהֱוֵי כְּכַרְמְלָא וְכַרְמְלָא קִרְוִין סַגִיאִין יְתִיב:


Is it not yet a very little while, and Lebanon shall turn into a fruitful field, and the fruitful field shall be inhabited by many cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:18)


וְיִשְׁמְעוּן בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא דִכְחַרְשִׁין מִלֵי סְפַר וּמִקְבֵיל וּמַחֲשׁוֹךְ עֵינֵי סָמָן יֶחְזְיָן:


And at that time those that are like the deaf shall hear the words of the book, and the eyes of the blind shall see out of obscurity, and out of darkness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:19)


וְיוֹסְפוּן מְקַבְּלֵי עוּלְבָן בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ חֶדְוָא וַחֲשִׁיכֵי בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא בְּמֵימַר קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יִבְעוּן:


And those that have suffered affliction on account of the WORD of the Lord shall increase their joy, and the poor of the sons of men shall rejoice in the Word of the holy One of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:20)


אֲרֵי סָף מְעִיקָא וְאִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בָזְזָא וְסָפוּ כָּל דַהֲווֹ מְשַׁחֲרִין לְמֵינָס:


For the oppressor is come to an end, and the plunderer is made to cease, and all that rose up early to act violently have come to an end;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:21)


מְחַיְבִין בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא בְּמִלֵיהוֹן וְלִדְמוֹכַח לְהוֹן בֵּית דִינָא בְתַרְעָא פִתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא בָּעָן לֵיהּ תַּקָלָא וְאַסְטִיאוּ בִשְׁקַר דִין זַכָּאִין:


All that declare the sons of men guilty on account of their words. The court of justice that is in the gate seeks to ensnare him, who reproves them with the words of the law. They turn aside the just by false judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:22)


בְּכֵן כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ עַל בֵּית יַעֲקֹב דִפְרִיק יַת אַבְרָהָם לָא מִכְּעַן יִבַּהֲתוּן דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב וְלָא מִכְּעַן אֲפֵּיהוֹן יִשְׁתַּנוֹן:


Therefore thus hath the Lord said, who hath redeemed Abraham, concerning the house of Jacob: They of the house of Jacob shall not henceforth be confounded, neither shall their faces henceforth look dismayed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:23)


אֲרֵי בְּמַחֲזוֹהִי גְבוּרָן דְאַעְבֵיד לִבְנוֹהִי טַבְוָת אַבְרָהָם לְזַרְעֵיהּ בַּתְרוֹהִי עַל אַרְעֲהוֹן בֵּינֵיהוֹן יְקַדְשׁוּן שְׁמִי וְיֵימְרוּן קַדִישׁ עַל קַדִישָׁא דְיַעֲקֹב וְעַל אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יֵימְרוּן תַּקִיף:


But when they of the house of Jacob shall see the mighty deeds which I shall do for their children, the kindness which I have promised Abraham, and his posterity after him in their own land, they shall sanctify my name among them; they shall say, Holy is the holy One of Jacob; and concerning the God of Israel they shall say, He is mighty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 29:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/29:24)


וְיֵדְעוּן דְלָא אֲלִיפוּ רוּחַ דְסוּכְלְתָנוּ וְדַהֲווֹ אָמְרִין לֵית כָּל אִלֵין יְקַבְּלוּן אוּלְפַן:


And they who were not taught by the spirit of understanding shall have knowledge; and they who said, All these things are nothing, shall receive instruction.



## Chapter 30

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:1)


וַי בְּנַיָא מְרוֹדַיָא אֲמַר יְיָ לְמֶעְבַּד עֵצָה וְלָא מִמֵימְרִי וּלְאִתְמַלָכָא מְלַךְ וְלָא שָׁאֲלִין בִּנְבִיֵי בְּדִיל לְאוֹסָפָא חוֹבִין עַל חוֹבֵי נַפְשְׁהוֹן:


Woe unto the rebellious children, saith the Lord, who take counsel, but not of my WORD, who consult a consultation, but do not ask my prophets, that they may add sin unto the sins of their soul.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:2)


דְאָזְלִין לְמֵיחַת לְמִצְרַיִם וּבְפִתְגָמֵי נְבִיֵי לָא שְׁאִילוּ לְאִתַּקָפָא בִּתְּקוֹף פַּרְעֹה וּלְאִתְרְחָצָא בִּטְלַל מִצְרָיִם:


Who go to descend into Egypt, but ask not the words of my prophets: to strengthen themselves with the strength of Pharaoh, and to trust in the shadow of Egypt.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:3)


וִיהֵי לְכוֹן תְּקוֹף פַּרְעֹה לְבַהֲתָא וְאִתְרָחֲצָנָא בִּטְלַל מִצְרַיִם לְאִתְכְּנִיעוּ:


Therefore the strength of Pharaoh shall be unto you for confusion; and confidence in the shadow of Egypt for disgrace.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:4)


אֲרֵי הֲווֹ בְטָאנֵיס רַבְרְבוֹהִי וְאִזְגַדוֹהִי עַד תַּחְפַּנְחֵס מְטוֹ:


For their princes were in Tanes, and their messengers have reached Taphnes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:5)


כּוּלְהוֹן אָזְלִין לְמִבְהַת לְוַת עַמָא דְלָא יַהֲנוּן לְהוֹן לָא לְסָעִיד וְלָא לַהֲנָאָה אֲרֵי לְבַהֲתָא וְאַף לְחִסוּדִין:


All of them go unto a people to be confounded, a people that shall not profit them, who shall be of no help, and of no profit, but for a confusion, and reproaches.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:6)


מַטְלִין עַל בְּעִירְהוֹן בְּאוֹרַח דָרוֹמָא בַּאֲרַע עָקָא וְעִיוּק אֲתַר דְאַרְיָא בַר אַוְיְוָן וְחֵיוָן הוּרְמָנִין מַפְרְחִין נָטְלִין עַל כְּתַף עִילִין נִכְסֵיהוֹן וְעַל חִטוּרִיאַת גַמְלִין מָא דִבְאָצְרֵיהוֹן מוֹבְלִין לְעַם לָא יַהֲנוֹן לְהוֹן:


They carry upon their beasts of burden on the way southward, into a land of oppression and distress, a place of the lion, and the whelps of the lionesses, and serpents, and of the flying basilisks—they carry upon the shoulders of their young cattle their treasures, and they bring upon the bunches of camels whatever is in their treasuries unto a people, that shall not profit them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:7)


וּמִצְרָאֵי לְמָא וְרֵיקָנוּ סְעַדְהוֹן בְּכֵן עֲרַעָיַת מִנְהוֹן קְטִילִין זְמִינִין אַיְתֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן:


And as for the Egyptians, their help is vanity and emptiness; therefore I shall meet many of them slain: I will bring armed men upon them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:8)


כְּעַן עוֹל כְּתַב עַל לוּחַ בֵינֵיהוֹן וְעַל שִׁיטִין דִסְפַר רָשׁוּם וּתְהִי לְיוֹם דִין לְסַהֲדוּ קֳדָמַי עַד עַלְמָא:


Now go, write it amongst them upon a tablet, and mark it upon lines of a book, that it may be for a witness before me in the day of judgment for ever:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:9)


אֲרֵי עַם סַרְבָן אִינוּן בְנַיָא כַּדִיבַיָא בְּנַיָא לָא אֲבוּ לְקַבָּלָא אוּלְפָן אוֹרַיְתָא דַיָי:


That this is a rebellious people, lying children, children that will not receive the instruction of the law of the Lord:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:10)


דְאָמְרִין לִנְבַיָיא לָא תִתְנַבּוּן וּלְמַלְפַיָא לָא תַלְפוּן לָנָא אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתָא בְּסִימָן מַלִילוּ עִמָנָא אִשְׁתַּעוּ לָנָא שְׁנִין:


Who say to the prophets, Prophesy ye not: and as for doctrines, teach us not the doctrine of the law: speak ye with us with signs, relate unto us various things:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:11)


אַסְטְיוּנָא מֵאוֹרְחָא דְתַקְנָא אַבְטְלוּנָא מִמְסַרְתָּא אַרְחִיקוּ מִן קֳדָמָנָא יַת מֵימַר קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Turn us from the right path, make us to cease from tradition; put at a distance from us the WORD of the Holy One of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:12)


בְּכֵן כִּדְנַן אָמַר קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל חֲלַף דְקַצְתּוּן בְּפִתְגָמָא הָדֵין וְאִתְרַחֵיצְתּוּן בְּשִׁקְרָא וּבְאוּנְסָא וְאִסְתְּמִכְתּוּן עֲלוֹהִי:


Wherefore thus saith the Holy One of Israel, Because ye have despised this word, and trust in lie and oppression, and stay thereon:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:13)


בְּכֵן יְהֵי לְכוֹן חוֹבָא הָדֵין כִּכְרַךְ דַחֲרוֹב וְאִתְחַמַר כְּשׁוּר מַתְקַף דְבִתְכֵּיף שָׁלוּ יֵיתֵי תַּבְרֵיהּ:


Therefore this sin shall be unto you as a city laid waste, and made a ruinous heap, as a bending: wall, whose breaking cometh very suddenly.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:14)


וְתַבְרָהּ כְּתִבוּר מַן דַחֲסַף דְפַחְרָא דְמִדַקַק לָא בַחֲיָס וְלָא מִשְׁתְּכַח בְּדִיקוּקֵיהּ חֲסַף לְמֶחְתֵי גוּר מִמַבְעוֹר וּלְמִדְלַח מַיִין מִפְצִיר:


And its breaking shall be as the breaking of a potter's vessel of clay, who is breaking it without compassion, and among its fragments there shall not be found a potsherd to take fire from the hearth, or to draw water from the cistern.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:15)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲמָרֵית דִי תְתוּבוּן לְאוֹרַיְתָא תְנוּחוּן לְאוֹרַיְתָא תְנוּחוּן וְתִתְפָּרְקוּן תִּשְׁקְטוּן וְתִשְׁרוּן לְרוֹחֲצָן תְּהוֹן גִבָּרִין וְלָא אֲבִיתוּן:


For thus saith the Lord God, the Holy One of Israel: I have said, If ye return to my law, ye shall have rest, and ye shall be redeemed, ye shall be quiet, and ye shall dwell in safety, and be mighty; but ye would not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:16)


וַאֲמַרְתּוּן לָא אֱלָהֵין עַל סוּסְוָתָא נֶעְרוֹק וְעַל כֵּן תַּעַרְקוּן וְעַל קַלִילִין נִרְכּוּב עַל כֵּן יְהוֹן קַלִילִין רָדְפֵיכוֹן:


And ye said: Nay, but we will flee on horses; therefore shall ye flee; and, We will ride upon the swift; therefore shall they be swift that pursue you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:17)


אַלְפָא חָד מִן קֳדָם מְזוֹפִית חַמְשָׁא תֶּעְרְקוּן עַד דְתִשְׁתַּאֲרוּן כְּכוֹעֲרָא עַל רֵישׁ טוּרָא וּבְאָתָא עַל רָמָתָא:


One thousand of you shall flee because of the rebuke of one, because of the rebuke of five: till ye be left as a signal fire on the top of a mountain; and as a beacon on a high hill.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:18)


וּבְכֵן עֲתִיד יְיָ לְמֵיחָס עֲלֵיכוֹן וְתַקֵיף הוּא דִירַחֵם עֲלֵיכוֹן אֲרֵי אֱלָהָא עֲבִיד דִינָא יְיָ טוּבֵי צַדִיקַיָא דְמִסַבְּרִין לְפוּרְקָנֵהּ:


And therefore the Lord will shew you pity, and He that is mighty will compassionate you; for the Lord is the God who doeth judgment: blessed are the righteous who wait for His salvation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:19)


אֲרֵי עַמָא בְּצִיוֹן יָתֵיב בִּירוּשְׁלֵם מִבְכָּא לָא תִבְכֵּי רַחֲמָא יְרַחֵם עֲלָךְ קָל צְלוֹתָךְ יְקַבֵּל וְיַעְבֵּיד בָעוּתָךְ:


For the people of Zion shall dwell in Jerusalem thou shalt weep no more. He will assuredly shew thee compassion: the voice of thy prayer He will hear, and He will answer thy supplication.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:20)


וִיהַב לְכוֹן יְיָ נִכְסֵי סַנְאָה וּבִזַת מְעִיקָא וְלָא יְסַלֵק עוֹד שְׁכִינְתֵּהּ מִבֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא וְיֶהְוְיָן עֵינָךְ חֶזְיָן יַת שְׁכִנְתִּי בְּבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא:


And the Lord shall give unto you the treasures of the enemy, and the spoil of the oppressor, and He will no more take away His Shekinah from the house of the sanctuary, and thine eyes shall behold my Shekinah in the house of the sanctuary.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:21)


וְאוֹדְנָךְ יִשְׁמְעוּן פִּתְגָמָא מִבִּתְרָךְ לְמֵימָר הֲדָא אוֹרְחָא דְתַקְנָא הֲלִיכוּ בָהּ לָא תִסְטוּן מִנֵיהּ לְיַמִינָא וְלִסְמָאלָא:


And thine ears shall hear a word behind thee, saying, This is the right way, walk ye in it, and turn ye not from it to the right, or to the left.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:22)


וּתְסַאֲבוּן יַת חִפּוּי צַלְמֵי כַּסְפֵּיכוֹן וְיַת תִּקוּן מַתְּכַת דַהַבְכוֹן תַּרְחִיקִינוּן כְּמָא דִמְרַחֲקִין יַת טוּמְאֲתָא כֵּן תַּרְחִיקִינוּן:


And ye shall defile the covering of your idols of silver, and the ornament of thy molten images of gold; ye shall abominate it, as they abominate the impurity of a menstruous woman, thus ye shall abominate it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:23)


וְיִתֵּן מְטַר זַרְעֵךְ דִי תִזְרַע יַת אַרְעָא וְעִבּוּרָא וַעֲלָלָא יִסְגֵי בְאַרְעָא וִיהֵי פַרְנוּס וְטָב וְיִתְפַּרְנְסוּן צַדִיקַיָא מִיגֵיתֵיהוֹן בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא שְׁמֵן רַכִּיכִין וּפַטִימִין:


Then shall He give rain for thy seed, with which thou shalt sow the ground, and corn and fruit shall increase in the land, and there shall be sustenance and goodness; and at that time, the just shall be nourished from their cattle, with the marrow of the tender and the fat ones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:24)


וְתוֹרַיָא וַחֲמָרַיָא דִי מְפַלְחִין בְּהוֹן יַת אַרְעָא בְּלִיל מְפַטֵם יֵכְלוּן דִי דְרָא בְרִיחֲתָא וּבִמְדֵירָא:


And the oxen and the asses, with which they plough the ground, shall eat fattening meslin, which has been winnowed with the shovel and with the fan.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:25)


וִיהֵי עַל כָּל טוּר רָם וְעַל כָּל רָמָא מְנַטְלָא פַצִידִין נָגְדִין מַיִין תַּקָלָא לְמַלְכִין וּלְמַשִׁרְיַתְהוֹן בְּיוֹם קְטוּל רַב בְּמַפַּל רַבְרְבִין:


And there shall be upon every high mountain, and upon every high and lofty hill, rivers flowing with water at the time of the ruin of the kings and their armies, in the day of the great slaughter, at the falling of the princes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:26)


וִיהֵי נֵיהוֹר סִיהֲרָא כְּנֵיהוֹר שִׁמְשָׁא וְנֵיהוֹר שִׁמְשָׁא יְהֵי עֲתִיד לְאַנְהָרָא עַל חָד תְּלַת מְאָה אַרְבְּעִין וּתְלָתָא כְּנֵיהוֹר שַׁבְעַת יוֹמַיָא בְּיוֹמָא דְיָתִיב יְיָ יַת גַלְוַת עַמֵיהּ וּמְרַע מְחָתֵיהּ יָסֵי:


Moreover the light of the moon shall be as the light of the sun, and the light of the sun shall in future shine three hundred and forty-three times more brightly, as the light of seven days, in the day that the Lord shall turn the captivity of His people, and heal the sickness caused by His blow.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:27)


הָא שְׁמָא דַייָ מִתְגְלֵי כְמָא דְאִתְנַבִּיאוּ עֲלוֹהִי נְבִיָיא מִלְקַדְמִין תַּקִיף רוּגְזֵהּ וּקְשֵׁי מִלְסוֹבְרָא מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי עַל רַשִׁיעַיָא נְפַק לְוָט וּמֵימְרֵהּ כְּאֶשָׁא אָכְלָא:


Behold, the Name of the Lord shall be revealed, as the prophets of old have prophesied concerning Him: His wrath is mighty, and too heavy to bear. His curse shall go forth from before Him upon the wicked, and His word as a consuming fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:28)


וּמֵימְרֵהּ כְּנַחַל מַגְבַּר עַד צַוָאר תַּקִיפִין יִקְטֵיל לַאֲרָמָא עַמְמַיָא בְּרָמוּת רֵיקָנוּ וּזְמָם דְטָעוּ בְּלִיסַת עַמְמַיָא:


And His word is as an overwhelming river reaching unto the neck: He shall slay the mighty; He will assuredly agitate the nations with the agitation of vanity: and there shall be a bridle of error in the jaws of the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:29)


תּוּשְׁבְּחָא תְּהֵי לְכוֹן כְּלִילְיָא דְאִתְקַדֵשׁ בֵּיהּ חַגָא וְחֶדְוַת לִבָּא כְּמָא דְנָגִין בְּתוֹדָתָא בְּאַבּוּבָא לְמֵיעַל בְּטוּר קוּדְשָׁא דַייָ לְאִתְחֲזָאָה קֳדָם תַּקִיפָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Ye shall have a song as in the night, as in the night when the festival is sanctified with joy of heart, as when they march with thanksgivings and the pipe, to enter the holy mountain of the Lord, to appear before the mighty One of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:30)


וְיַשְׁמַע יְיָ יַת זִיו קַל מֵימְרֵהּ וּתְקוֹף דְרַע גְבוּרְתֵּהּ יְגַלֵי בִּתְקוֹף רְגַז וְשַׁלְהוֹבֵי אֶשָׁא דִמְשֵׁיצָא פְּסִילָן בַּדוּר וְזַרְמִית וְאַבְנִין דְבָרָד:


And the Lord shall proclaim the brightness of the voice of His WORD, and He shall reveal the strength of His arm in the fury of His anger," and in flames of fire, destroying the graven images with scattering, and storm, and hailstones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:31)


אֲרֵי מִקָל מֵימְרָא דַייָ מִתְּבַר אַתּוּרָאָה דִי דִי בְשׁוּלְטָן מָחֵי:


For through the voice of the WORD of the Lord the Assyrian shall be broken, he that smote by his power.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:32)


וִיהֵי כָּל מַעֲבַר רַבְרְבֵיהוֹן וְתַקִיפֵיהוֹן דִי יַנִיחַ יְיָ פּוּרְעֲנוּת גְבוּרְתֵּהּ בֵּינֵיהוֹן בְּתוּפִּין וּבְכִינוֹרִין יְשַׁבְּחוּן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל וְעַל קְרָבָא תַקִיפָא דְיִתְעֲבֵּיד לְהוֹן בְּעַמְמַיָא:


And it shall come to pass, that the Lord shall cause to rest the vengeance of His might in every pass of their princes and mighty ones, even amongst them. The house of Israel shall praise with tabrets and harps, because of the mighty war which shall be waged for them against the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 30:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/30:33)


אֲרֵי מְסִדְרָא מִן עַלְמִין לָקֳבֵיל חוֹבֵיהוֹן גֵהִנָם אַף הִיא מְלַךְ עָלְמַיָא אַתְקָנָא לַעֲמִיקָא וּלְאַפְתָּאָה מְדוּרָא דְאֶשְׁתָּא דָלְקָא בָהּ כַּד בָּאָעִין סַגִיאִין מֵימְרָא דַייָ כְּנַחַל מַגְבַּר גָפְרִית דָלְקָא בָהּ:


For hell is made ready from eternity on account of their sins; yea, the eternal King hath prepared it deep and wide: a fiery pyre as of abundance of fuel burns in it: the WORD of the Lord, like an overwhelming torrent of brimstone, shall kindle it.



## Chapter 31

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:1)


וַי דְנַחֲתִין לְמִצְרַיִם לְסָעִיד וְעַל סוּסְוָתָא מִסְתַּמְכִין וּמִתְרַחֲצִין עַל רְתִיכַיָא אֲרֵי סַגִיאִין וְעַל פָּרָשַׁיָא אֲרֵי תַקִיפִין לַחֲדָא וְלָא אִסְתְּמִיכוּ עַל מֵימַר קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְאוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לָא תְבָעוּ:


Woe to them that go down to Egypt for help, who stay themselves upon horses, and trust in chariots, because they are many: and in horsemen, because they are very strong; but they stay not themselves upon the WORD of the Holy One of Israel, neither seek instruction from the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:2)


וְאַף הוּא בְּחוּכְמְתֵיהּ אַיְתֵי בִישְׁתָּא עַל דְעַבְדֵהּ וְיַת פִּתְגָמוֹהִי לָא בַטֵיל וַאֲקֵים עַל בֵית מַבְאֲשִׁין וְעַל דְסָעֲדִין לֵיאוּת שְׁקָר:


And also He in His wisdom will bring evil on account of what he is doing; and He will not make to fail His words, but He will arise against the house of evildoers; and against those who help the work of a lie.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:3)


וּמִצְרַיִם אֱנַשׁ וְלָא רָב וְסוּסְוָתְהוֹן בְּסַר וְלָא רוּחַ וַיָי יָרִים מְחַת גְבוּרְתֵּהּ וְיִתְקַל סָעִיד וְיִפּוֹל סָעִיד וְכַחֲדָא כּוּלְהוֹן יִשְׁתֵּיצוּן:


And the Egyptians are men, and none of them a mighty one, and their horses are flesh, and not spirit: and the Lord shall deal the stroke of His power, and he that helpeth shall stumble, and he that is holpen shall fall, and all of them together shall be destroyed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:4)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ לִי כְּמָא דְמַכְלֵי אַרְיָא בַּר אַרְיָוָן עַל צֵידֵיהּ דְמִזְדַמְנִין עֲלוֹהִי דִיר רַעֲיָן מִקָלְהוֹן לָא אִתְבַּר וּמֵאִתְרְגוּשְׁתְּהוֹן לָא מִזְדַעְזַע כֵּן תִּתְגְלֵי מַלְכוּתָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת לְמִשְׁרֵי עַל טוּרָא דְצִיוֹן וְעַל רָמָתָהּ:


For thus the Lord hath said unto me: Like as the lion and the lion's whelp roar over their prey, when a multitude of shepherds are come together, he will not be afraid of their voice, neither be terrified at their tumult; thus shall the kingdom of the Lord of hosts be revealed encamping? upon the mountain of Zion, and upon its hill.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:5)


כְּעוֹפָא דְטָאִים כֵּן תִּתְגְלֵי גְבוּרְתֵּהּ דַייָ צְבָאוֹת עַל יְרוּשְׁלֵם יָגֵין וִישֵׁיזֵיב יַצִיל וְיַעְדֵי:


As a bird by flying, so the power of the Lord of hosts shall be revealed; He shall protect, He shall deliver, He shall save, and He shall make to pass away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:6)


תּוּבוּ לְאוֹרַיְתָא אֲרֵי אַסְנֵיתוּן לְמֶחְטֵי בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Return to the law; for you have multiplied sin, O sons of Israel!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:7)


אֲרֵי בְעִדָנָא הַהִיא יְרַחֲקוּן גְבַר טַעֲוַת כַּסְפְּהוֹן וְטַעֲוַת דְהַבְהוֹן דַעֲבָדוּ לְכוֹן יְדֵיכוֹן חוֹבָא:


At that time each man shall abhor the idols of their silver and the idols of their gold, which your hands have made for you for a god.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:8)


וְיִפּוֹל אַתּוּרָאָה בְּחֶרֶב לָא אֱנַשׁ וְחֶרֶב לָא אֱנשׁ תְּשֵׁיצִינֵהּ וְיֶעְרוֹק לֵיהּ כְּמִן קֳדָם דְקַטְלִין בְּחַרְבָּא וְגִבְרוֹהִי לִתְּבַר יְהוֹן:


Then shall the Assyrian fall by a sword not of man; and the sword not of a man shall destroy him: and he shall betake himself to flight as from before those who slay with the sword, and his heroes shall be for destruction.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/31:9)


וְשִׁלְטוֹנוֹהִי מִן קֳדָם דַחֲלָא יֶעְדוּן וִיתַּבְרוּן מִן קֳדָם נִיסָא רַבְרְבוֹהִי אֲמַר יְיָ דְזֵיהוֹר לֵיהּ בְּצִיוֹן לְעָבְדֵי אוֹרַיְתָא וְתַנוּר בָּעֵיר לֵיהּ דְאֶשָׁא בִירוּשְׁלֵם לִדְעֲבָרוּ עַל מֵימְרֵהּ:


And his princes shall flee through fear, they shall move away; and his princes shall be broken on account of the miracle, saith the Lord, whose splendour is in Zion to them who obey the law, and whose burning furnace of fire is in Jerusalem for them who transgress His word.



## Chapter 32

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:1)


הָא לְקוּשְׁטָא יִמְלוֹךְ מַלְכָּא וְצַדִיקַיָא לְמֶעְבַּד פּוּרְעֲנוּת דֵין מִן עַמְמַיָא יִתְרְבוּן:


Behold, a king shall reign in truth, and the righteous shall be magnified to execute just vengeance on the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:2)


וִיהוֹן צַדִיקַיָא דְמִיטַמְרִין מִן קֳדָם רַשִׁיעַיָא כְּמָא דְמִיטַמְרִין מִן קֳדָם זַרְמִית יְתוּבוּן וְיִתְרַבּוּן וְיִתְקַבֵּיל אוּלְפַנְהוֹן בִּפְרִיעַ כְּפַצִידֵי מַיָא דְנַגְדִין בְּאַרְעָא מְשַׁלְהֲיָא כִּטְלַל כֵּיף מְיַקֵר בְּאַרְעָא מְשַׁלְהַיָא:


And the just that were hidden because of the wicked, as those who hide themselves on account of a storm, shall return, and shall be magnified, and their instruction shall be received quickly, like the waters that flow into a dry land, like the shadow of a great rock in a parched land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:3)


וְלָא יִטַמְטְמַן עֵינֵי צַדִיקַיָא וְאוּדְנֵי מְקַבְּלֵי אוּלְפַן יְצִיתָן:


And the eyes of the righteous shall not be shut, and the ears of those who receive instruction shall hear.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:4)


וּלְבַב מְהַרְהֲרִין יְסַבַּר לְמֵידַע וְלִישָׁנְהוֹן דַהֲוָה בְלִים יוֹחִי לְמַלָלָא בְּצַחְצְחָן:


Even the heart of the rash shall understand knowledge, and the tongue of those that was tied shall be ready to speak plainly.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:5)


לָא יִתְאֲמַר עוֹד לְרַשִׁיעַיָא צַדִיקַיָא וְלִדְעַבָרוּ עַל מֵימְרֵהּ לָא יִתְאַמַר תַּקִיפִין:


And the wicked man shall no more be called just, and he that transgresses against His WORD shall not be called mighty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:6)
