אֲרֵי רַשִׁיעַיָא רִשְׁעָה מְמַלְלִין וּבְלִיבְּהוֹן מִתְעַשְׁתִּין אוֹנֶס לְמֶעְבַּד שְׁקַר וּלְמַלָלָא קֳדָם יְיָ סַטְיָא לְשַׁלְהָאָה נֶפֶשׁ צַדִיקַיָא דִמְחַמְדִין לְאוּלְפָנָא הָא כְּכָפְנָא לְלַחְמָא וּפִתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא דְאִינוּן כְּמַיָא לְצַחָיָא מְדַמָן לְבַטָלָא:


For the wicked will talk wickedness, and in their heart they meditate violence, to practise falsehood, and to speak revolt against the Lord, to weary the soul of the righteous, who long after instruction, as the hungry after bread; and after the words of the law, which are like water to him that is athirst, they purpose to make to cease.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:7)


וְרַשִׁיעַיָא דְעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן בִּישִׁין וְאִינוּן עַל עֵיצַת חֶטְאִין מִתְמַלְכִין לְחַבָּלָא עִנְוְתָנַיָא בְּמִלֵי שְׁקַר וּפִּתְגָמֵי חֲשִׁיכַיָא בְּדִינָא:


And the wicked, whose works are evil, take counsel with sinners to destroy the poor with lying words, and the words of the needy in judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:8)


וְצַדִיקַיָא קוּשְׁטָא מִתְמַלְכִין וְאִינוּן עַל קוּשְׁטֵיהוֹן יִתְקַיְמוּן:


But the righteous counsel truth, and in their truth they shall be established.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:9)


מְדִינָן דְיַתְבִין שְׁלֵיוָן קוּמָא שְׁמַעֲנָא קָלִי כְּרַכִּין דִי שְׁרָן לְרוֹחֲצָן אַצִיתָא לְמֵימְרִי:


Ye provinces that dwell at ease: arise, hear my voice. Ye cities that dwell in safety, listen to my word.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:10)


יוֹמִין עִם שְׁנִין יְזוּעוּן דִי שָׁרָן לְרוֹחֲצָן אֲרֵי סַף עִבּוּרָא עֲלָלָא לֵית לְמִכְנַשׁ:


Those who dwell at ease shall be agitated for days and years, because the corn is come to an end, and there is no fruit to gather.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:11)


אִיתְּבַרוּ דְיַתְבִין שְׁלֵיוָן זָעוּ דִי שָׁרָן לְרוֹחֲצָן שְׁלָחוּ וְאִיתְעַרְטִלוּ וֶאֱסָרוּ עַל חַרְצִין:


They that dwell at ease shall be broken, they that dwell in safety shall tremble; strip ye, and make you bare, and gird sackcloth upon your loins.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:12)


עַל דַדִין סִפְּדִין עַל חַקְלֵי חֲמִידְתָּא עַל גוּפְנִיי טָעֲנִין:


They beat the breasts for the pleasant fields, for the fruit-laden vines.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:13)


עַל אַרְעָא דְעַמִי הוּבֵאִי וּבוּר תַּסִיק אֲרֵי עַל כָּל בָּתֵּי דַיִץ קִרְוָא תַּקִיפָא:


Upon the land of my people thorns and thistles shall come up, yea, in all the houses of joy in the fortified city.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:14)


אֲרֵי בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא חֲרוֹב הֲמוֹן קִרְוַיָא דַהֲוָאָה פַלְחִין בֵּיהּ צְרִיאָה בֵּית תָּקְפָנָא וּמַטְמוֹרָנָא אִתְבְּלֵשׁ וּכְרִי חֲרוֹב וּצְדִי עַד זְמַן אֲתַר דַהֲוָה בֵית חֶדְוָא וְחֵידוּ לְמַלְכַיָא כְדוֹ הֲוָה מִבַז לְמַשִׁרְיָן:


For the house of the sanctuary shall be laid waste; the noisy city shall be desolate, in which they worship: the house of our strength and hiding place shall be searched out, dug up, desolate and waste unto a time. The place which was a house of joy and gladness for kings is now become a spoil for armies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:15)


כָּל דְנָא עַד דְיֵיתֵי לָנָא רְוַח מִן קֳדָם דִשְׁכִנְתֵּהּ בִּשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא וִיהֵי מַדְבְּרָא לְכַרְמְלָא וְכַרְמְלָא קִירְוִין סַגִיאִין יְתִיב:


All these things shall come upon us until refreshing shall come to us from the face of Him, whose Shekinah is in the highest heavens. The wilderness shall become a fruitful field, and the fruitful field shall be inhabited by many cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:16)


וְיִשְׁרוּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא עָבְדֵי דִינָא וְעָבְדֵי צְדַקְתָּא יַת כַּרְמְלָא יֵיתְבוּן:


Then they that do justice shall dwell in the wilderness, and those that do righteousness shall inhabit the fruitful field.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:17)


וִיהוֹן עָבְדֵי צְדַקְתָּא שְׁלָם וּמְפַלְחֵי צְדַקְתָּא יִשְׁקְטוּן וְיִשְׁרוּן לְרוֹחֲצָן עַד עָלְמַיָא:


And they that do righteousness shall be at peace, and those that adorn righteousness shall be in quietness, and they shall be in safety for ever.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:18)


וְיֵתְבוּן עַמִי בִּמְדוֹרֵיהוֹן שְׁלָם וְעַל אַרְעֲהוֹן לְרוֹחֲצָן וּבְקִירְוֵיהוֹן שְׁלֵיוָן:


And my people shall dwell in their habitations in peace, and in their land in safety, and in their cities at ease.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:19)


וְיֵחוֹת בַּרְדָא וְיִקְטֵיל מַשִׁרְיַת עַמְמַיָא פַּלְחֵי טַעֲוָתָא וְיִצְדוּן וִיסוּפוּן דָיְרֵיהוֹן:


And hail shall descend and shall slay the armies of the nations, and their encampments shall be laid desolate, and come to an end.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 32:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/32:20)


טוּבֵיכוֹן צַדִיקַיָא עֲבַדְתּוּן לְכוֹן עוֹבָדִין טָבִין דְאַתּוּן דָמָן לִדְזַרְעִין עַל שַׁקְיָא מְשַׁלְחִין לְאַדְרָכָא בְּתוֹרַיָא וּלְמִכְנַשׁ בְּחַמְרַיָא:


Blessed are ye, O just, ye work out for yourselves good works: because ye are like those, who sow by watered places, sending the oxen to tread out the corn, and asses to gather it in.



## Chapter 33

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:1)


וַי דְאָתֵי לְמִבַז וְיָתָךְ לָא יִבְזוּן וּדְאָתֵי לְמֵינַס וְיָתָךְ לָא יֵינְסוּן כַּד תַּיְתֵי לְמִבַּז יִבְזוּנָךְ וְכַד תִּלְאֵי לְמֵינַס יַנְסוּנָךְ:


Woe to him who is coming to spoil thee, and shall they not spoil thee And woe to him that is coming to plunder, and shall they not plunder thee? When thou shalt come to spoil, they shall spoil thee, and when thou shalt be weary of plundering, they shall plunder thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:2)


יְיָ רְחִים עִלָנָא לְמֵימְרָךְ סַבַּרְנָא הֱוֵי תוּקְפָנָא בְּכָל יוֹם אַף פּוּרְקָנָנָא בְּעִידַן עָקָא:


O Lord, be merciful unto us; we have hoped for Thy WORD: be Thou our strength every day, our salvation also in the time of distress.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:3)


מִקַל הֲמוֹן אִתְבְּרוּ עַמְמַיָא מִסְגֵי גְבוּרָן אִתְבַּדָרוּ מַלְכְוָתָא:


At the voice of a tumultuous noise the people are afraid, on account of the multitude of mighty actions kingdoms are scattered.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:4)


וְיִכְנְשׁוּן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל נִכְסֵי עַמְמַיָא סַנְאֵיהוֹן כְּמָא דְכִינְשִׁין יַת זַחְלָא וְאָזַן בְּמָאנֵי זְיָנָא כְּמָא דְאָזָן בְּזִירְקְתָא:


And the house of Israel shall gather the treasures of the people, their enemies, as they gather the locusts. They shall be armed with the instruments of warfare, as they that are armed with a sling.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:5)


תַּקִיף יְיָ דְאַשְׁרֵי שְׁכִינְתֵּהּ בִּשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא דַאֲמַר לְמִמְלֵי צִיוֹן עָבְדֵי דִין דִקְשׁוֹט וּזְכוּ:


Mighty is the Lord who maketh His Shekinah to dwell in the highest heavens, who hath promised to fill Zion with them that do true justice and righteousness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:6)


וִיהֵי מָא דַאֲמַרְתָּ לְאוֹטָבָא לִדְחַלְתָּךְ אַיְתֵיתָא וְקַיֵמְתָּא בְּעִידָנֵהּ תְקוֹף וּפוּרְקַן חוּכְמָתָא וּמַדַע לְדָחְלַיָא דַייָ אוֹצַר טוּבֵיהּ עֲתִיד:


And it shall come to pass, whatever good Thou hast promised to them that fear Thee, Thou wilt bring and establish it in its time: strength and salvation, wisdom and knowledge. For them that fear the Lord, the treasure of His goodness is prepared.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:7)


הָא כַּד אִתְגְלֵי לְהוֹן יְצַוְחוּן בִּמְסַר אִיזְגַדֵי עַמְמַיָא דַאֲזָלוּ לְבַסָרָא שְׁלָם תָּבוּ לְמֶהֱוֵי בָכָן בִּמְרִיר נְפָשׁ:


When Thou shalt reveal Thyself to them, the messengers of the nations shall cry bitterly in the street; those who had gone forth to proclaim peace, shall return to weep in the bitterness of soul.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:8)


צְדִיאוּ כִבְשַׁיָא פְּסָקוּ עָדֵי אוֹרְחָא עַל דְאַשְׁנִיאוּ קְיָמָא אִיתְרְחִיקוּ מִקִרְוֵיהוֹן לָא חֲשִׁיבוּ בְנֵי אֱנָשָׁא דְבִישְׁתָּא אַתְיָא עֲלֵיהוֹן:


The highways lie desolate, the wayfaring man ceaseth: because they have forgotten the covenant: they shall be removed far from their cities; they have not considered the sons of men, evil shall come upon them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:9)


אִיתְאַבְּלַת חֲרוֹבַת אַרְעָא יָבֵישׁ לִבְנָן נְתַר הֲוָה שָׁרוֹנָא כְּמֵישְׁרָא צְדֵי מַתְנַן וְכַרְמְלָא:


The land mourneth, it is laid desolate; Lebanon is withered, it shakes off its leaves: Sharon has become like a desert; Bashan is laid desolate, and Carmel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:10)


כְּעַן אֶתְגְלֵי אֲמַר יְיָ כְּעַן אֶתְרוֹמֵם כְּעַן אֶתְנַטָל:


Now I will reveal myself, saith the Lord; now will I lift myself up on high; now will I be exalted.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:11)


אִתְעַשְׁתּוּן לְכוֹן עַמְמַיָא עַשְׁתּוֹנִין דִרְשַׁע עֲבַדְתּוּן לְכוֹן עוֹבָדִין בִּישִׁין בְּדִיל עוֹבָדֵיכוֹן בִּישַׁיָא מֵימְרִי כְּעַלְעוֹלָא לְקַשָׁא יְשֵׁיצֵי יַתְכוֹן:


Ye, O people! have purposed for yourselves purposes of iniquity; ye have worked for yourselves evil works, because your works are evil; my WORD shall consume you, as a whirlwind consumes chaff.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:12)


וִיהוֹן עַמְמַיָא יְקֵידַת נוּר כּוּבִין מְפַסְכִין בְּנוּרָא יִתּוֹקְדוּן:


And the people shall be as the burning of fire, as thorns are cut up and burnt in the fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:13)


שְׁמָעוּ צַדִיקַיָא דִנְטָרוּ אוֹרַיְתָא מִלְקָדְמִין מָא דַעֲבָדִית דְעוּ חַיָבַיָא דְתָבוּ לְאוֹרַיְתִי קָרִיב גְבוּרְתִּי:


Hear ye, O righteous, that keep the law from of old, what I have done; and know ye, ye sinners that have returned to my law, that my strength is nigh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:14)


אִתְבָּרוּ בְצִיוֹן חַיָבִין דַחֲלָא אֲחַדְתִּינוּן לְרַשִׁיעַיָא דַהֲווּ מְגַנְבִין אוֹרְחַתְהוֹן אָמְרִין מַן יָדוּר לָנָא בְּצִיוֹן דְבָהּ זֵיהוֹר שְׁכִינְתֵּה כְּאֶשָׁא אָכְלָא מַן יִתּוֹתָב לָנָא בִירוּשְׁלֵם עַתִידִין רַשִׁיעַיָא לְאִתְדָנָא לְאִתְמְסָרָא לִגְהִנָם יְקֵדוּת עֲלָם:


The sinners in Zion are afraid; terror hath seized the wicked, who, when they were committing theft in their ways, said: Who of us shall dwell in Zion, in which the brightness of His Shekinah is like devouring fire? Who of us shall sojourn in Jerusalem, where the wicked shall be judged, to be delivered into hell, into everlasting burning?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:15)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא יַתְבוּן בָהּ צַדִיקַיָא כָּל דִמְהַלֵךְ בִּזְכוּ וּמְמַלֵיל כֵּיוָנָן מִתְרַחֵק מִמָמוֹן דִשְׁקַר מַרְחֵיק נַפְשֵׁיהּ מִן אֲנוּסִין מְנַע יְדוֹהִי מִלְקַבָּלָא שׁוֹחְדָא מְטַמְטֵים אוּדְנֵהּ מִלְמִשְׁמַע בְּאַשְׁדֵי דַם זַכַּאי וְכָל עֵינוֹהִי מִלְמֶחְזֵי בְּעָבְדֵי בוּשׁ:


The prophet said, The just shall live in it, every one who walketh in righteousness, and speaketh honest things; removing himself to a distance from the mammon of iniquity; removing himself from oppressions, that restraineth his hands from holding a bribe, that stoppeth his ears from hearing of the shedding of innocent blood, and shutteth his eyes from looking at the workers of iniquity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:16)


הוּא בַּאֲתַר רָם וּמְנַטֵל יְהֵי בֵית מַשְׁרוֹהִי בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא תִּסְבַּע נַפְשֵׁיהּ מְזוֹנֵהּ מַסְפַּק מוֹהִי קַיָמִין כְּמַבּוּעַ דְמַיִין דְלָא פָּסְקִין מוֹהִי:


The house of his inhabitation shall be in a high and exalted place; in the house of the sanctuary shall his soul be satisfied ; his food shall be sufficient; his waters shall continue; like a fountain of water, whose waters fail not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:17)


יַת יְקָר שְׁכִינַת מֶלֶךְ עָלְמַיָא בְּתוּשְׁבַּחְתֵּהּ יֶחֶזְיָן עֵינָךְ תִּסְתַּכֵּל וְתֶחֱזֵי בְּנָחְתֵי אֲרַע גֵהִנָם:


Thine eyes shall see the Shekinah of the king of the worlds in his beauty; thou shalt behold and see those that descend down to hell.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:18)


לִבָּךְ יַחְשָׁב רַבְרְבָן אֵיכָא סַפְרַיָא אֵיכָא רַבְרְבַיָא אֵיכָא חֲשָׁבַיָא יֵתוּן אִם יֵכְלוּן לְחַשָׁבָא מִנְיָן קְטִילֵי רֵישֵׁי מַשִׁרְיַת גִבָּרַיָא:


Thy heart shalt meditate great things: where are the scribes? where are the rulers? where are the mathematicians? let them come, if they are able to count the number of the slain of the chief of the camp of the mighty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:19)


יַת מַרְוַת עַם תַּקִיף לָא תִכּוּל לְמֶחֱזֵי עַמָא דַהֲוָה עָמִיק מַמְלַלְהוֹן מִלְמִשְׁמַע מְלַעֲגִין בְּלִישָׁנְהוֹן מִדְלֵית בְּהוֹן סוּכְלְתָנוּ:


Thou shalt not be able to see the government of a mighty people, whose language is so unintelligible that thou canst not understand it, whose tongue stammereth, because there is no understanding in them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:20)


תֶּחֱזֵי בְּמַפַּלְתְּהוֹן צִיוֹן קִרְיַת זִמְנָנָא עֵינָךְ יֶחֱזְיָן בְּנֶחָמַת יְרוּשְׁלֶם בְּאַצְלָחוּתָא בִּשְׁלֵוָתָא בְּמַשְׁכְּנָא דְלָא מִתְפְּרַק וְלָא מִשְׁתַּלְפָן סִכּוֹהִי לַעֲלַם וְכָל אַטוֹנוֹהִי לָא יִתְפַסְקוּן:


O Zion, thou shalt see their fall; O city of our festivals, thine eyes shall see the consolation of Jerusalem in her prosperity and security, like a tabernacle that is not taken down, and whose pegs are not drawn out for ever, and whose cords shall not be broken.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:21)


אֲרֵי אֱלָהֵן מִתַּמָן תִּתְגְלֵי גְבוּרְתָּא דַייָ לְאוֹטָבָא לָנָא מֵאֲתַר נַפְקִין נַהֲרִין שָׁטְפִין פְּתָיוּת יְדִין דְלָא תֵיזֵיל בֵּיהּ סְפִינַת צַיָדִין וּבוּרְנִי רַבְּתָא לָא תְגוֹזִנֵהּ:


Yea, surely from thence the power of the Lord shall be revealed, to do good unto us from the place whence overflowing rivers of broad span shall flow, through which shall not pass the fishermen's boat, nor the large pinnace go through it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:22)


אֲרֵי יְיָ דַיָנָנָא דְאַפְקָנָא בִּגְבוּרְתֵּהּ מִמִצְרָיִם יְיָ מַלְפָנָא דִי הָב לָנָא אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתָא מִסִינַי יְיָ מַלְכָּנָא הוּא יִפְרְקִנָנָא וְיַעְבֵּיד לָנָא פּוּרְעֲנוּת דִין מִן מַשִׁירְיַת גוֹג:


For the Lord is our judge, who by His power brought us out of Egypt; the Lord, who is our teacher, who gave us the doctrine of the law from Sinai; the Lord is our king, He will redeem us, and execute for us vengeance of judgment on the host of Gog.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:23)


בְּעִדָנָא הַהִיא יִתַּבְּרוּן עַמְמַיָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר יְרוּשְׁלֶם מִתּוּקְפְהוֹן וִיהוֹן דָמָן לִסְפִינָא דְאִתְפַּסְקוּ חֲבָלָהָא וְלֵית לָהּ תְּקוֹף עַל תָּרְנְהוֹן דְאִתְפְּסִיק וְלָא אֶפְשַׁר לְמִפְרַס עֲלוֹהִי קְלַע בְּכֵן יְפַלְגוּן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל נִכְסֵי עַמְמַיָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר יְרוּשְׁלֶם סַגִיאוּת עָדֵי וּבוּז וַאֲפִילוּ יִשְׁתַּאֲרוּן בְּהוֹן עַוֵרִין וַחֲגִירִין אַף אִינוּן יְפַלְגוּן סַגִיאוּת עָדֵי וּבוּז:


At that time the people shall be bereft of their strength, and shall be like a ship, whose ropes are broken; neither shall there be strength in their mast, which is cut down, so that it is not possible to spread a sail upon it; then shall the house of Israel divide the treasures of the people, the abundance of spoil and prey; and although the blind and the lame are left among them, they also shall divide the abundance of spoil and prey.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 33:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/33:24)


וְלָא יֵימְרוּן מִכְּעַן לְעַמָא דְשָׁרָן סְחוֹר סְחוֹר אֲתִיב לִשְׁכִינְתָּא מִלְוַתְכוֹן אֲתָת עַלָנָא מְחַת מְרַע עַמָא בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל יִתְכַּנְשׁוּן וִיתוּבוּן לְאַרְעֲהוֹן שְׁבִיק לְחוֹבֵיהוֹן:


Henceforth they shall not say to the people which dwell round about them, I shall return to the Shekinah. (From you is come upon us the evil plague.) The people, the house of Israel, shall be gathered together, and they shall return to their land, their sins being forgiven.



## Chapter 34

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:1)


אִתְקַרְבוּ עַמְמַיָא לְמִשְׁמַע וּמַלְכְּוָתָא אֲצִיתוּ תִּשְׁמַע אַרְעָא וּמְלָהָא תֵּבֵל וְכָל דְדָיְרִין בָּהּ:


Come near, ye nations, to hear; and hearken, ye kingdoms: let the earth hear, and the world's fulness, and all that dwell in her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:2)


אֲרֵי רְגַז מִן קֳדָם יְיָ עַל כָּל עַמְמַיָא וְרוּגְזָא עַל כָּל מַשִׁירְיָתֵיהוֹן חַיְבִינוּן מְסַרְנוּן לְקַטָלָא:


For the wrath from before the presence of the Lord is upon all the nations, and slaughter upon all their hosts: He hath condemned them and delivered them over to the slaughter.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:3)


וּקְטִילֵיהוֹן יִתְרְמוּן וּפִגְרֵיהוֹן יִסַק תְּנָנְהוֹן וְיִתְיַמְסוּן טוּרַיָא מִדָמְהוֹן:


And their slain shall be cast out; and their stink shall ascend from their carcases; and the mountains shall be melted with their blood.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:4)


וְיִתְיַמְסוּן כָּל חֵילֵי שְׁמַיָא וְיִתְמְחוּן מִתְּחוֹת שְׁמַיָא כְּמָא דַאֲמִיר עֲלֵיהוֹן בְּסִפְרָא וְכָל מַשִׁירְיָתְהוֹן יְסוּפוּן כְמִיתַר טְרַף מִגֶפֶן וּכְנִבְלָא מִתְּאֵנָה:


And all the pleasant host of heaven shall be dissolved, and they shall be blotted out from beneath the heavens, as it is said concerning them in this book; and all their armies shall come to an end, as the fading leaf from the vine, and as the withering fig from the fig-tree.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:5)


אֲרֵי תִתְגְלֵי בִשְׁמַיָא חַרְבִּי הָא עַל אֱדוֹם תִּתְגְלֵי וְעַל עַמָא דְחַיָבִית לְדִינָא:


Because my sword is revealed in heaven; behold, it shall be uncovered upon Edom, and upon the people which I have condemned to judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:6)


חַרְבָּא קֳדָם יְיָ אִיתְמְלִיאַת דַם אִדַהֲנַת מִתְּרַב מִדַם מַלְכִין וְשִׁלְטוֹנִין מִתְּרַב כָּלְיָת רַבְרְבִין אֲרֵי קְטָלָא קֳדָם יְיָ בְּבָצְרָה וְנִכְסָא סַגִיאָה בְּאַרְעָא דֶאֱדוֹם:


The sword before the presence of the Lord is filled with blood, it is fattened, it is fattened with the blood of kings and rulers, it is fattened with the kidneys of princes; because there is a slaughter before the Lord in Bozrah, and a great sacrifice in the land of Edom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:7)


וְיִתְקַטְלוּן גִבָּרִין דְאַרְעָא חֲדָא עִמְהוֹן וְשִׁילְטוֹנִין עִם טוֹרְנִין וְתִרְוֵי אַרְעֲהוֹן מִדִמְהוֹן וְעַפְרְהוֹן מִתַּרְבְּהוֹן יִדְהָן:


And the mighty men shall be slain with them, and the rulers, and the princes; and their land shall be drunken with their own blood; and their dust made fat with their own fat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:8)


אֲרֵי יוֹם פּוּרְעֲנוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ שְׁנַת תוּשְׁלֵמָא לְמֶעְבַּד פּוּרְעֲנוּת דִין עוּלְבָּנָא דְצִיוֹן:


For it is the day of vengeance before the presence of the Lord; the year of recompense, to take vengeance of judgment on account of the ignominy of Zion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:9)


וְיִתְהַפְּכוּן נַחֲלָהָא לְזֵפָא וְעַפְרָא לְגוּפְרֵיתָא וּתְהֵי אַרְעָהּ לְזֵפָא בוֹעֵרָא:


And the rivers of Samaria shall be turned into pitch, and her dust into brimstone, and the land shall become burning pitch.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:10)


לֵילֵי וִימָם לָא תִטְפֵי לַעֲלָם יִסַק תְּנָנָהּ מִדָר לְדָר תֶּחֱרוֹב לַעֲלָם עַלְמַיָא לֵית דִי עָדֵי בָּהּ:


By night and by day it shall not be extinguished; her smoke shall ascend for ever: from generation to generation she shall lie waste; none shall pass through her to everlasting ages.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:11)


וְיַחְסְנוּנָהּ קָתִין וְקִפּוֹדִין וְקִפוֹפִין וְעוֹרְבִין יִשְׁרָן בָּה וְיִתְנְגִיד עֲלָהּ חוּט דְחוּרְבָּנָא וּמִשְׁקַלְתָּא דְצַדְיוּתָא:


The pelican and the porcupine shall inherit her, and the owls and the ravens shall dwell in her: and the line of devastation shall be extended over her, and the plummet of desolation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:12)


דַהֲווֹ אָמְרִין בְּנֵי חֵירִין אֲנַחְנָא וְלָא צְבָן לְקַבָּלָא אֲנַחְנָא וְלָא צְבָן לְקַבָּלָא עֲלֵיהוֹן מַלְכוּ וְכָל רַבְרָבָהָא יְהוֹן לִלְמָא:


They said, we are the sons of free men, and they were not willing to accept over them a kingly government; and all her princes shall come to nothing


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:13)


וְיִסְקוֹן בִּרְנְיָתָהָא סִירִין קַרְסוּלִין וְחוֹחִין בִּתְקוֹף כְּרַכָּהָא וּתְהֵי מְדוֹר יְרוֹדִין אַתְרָא לְבַת נַעֲמָא:


And in her palaces shall spring up thorns, and the nettles, and the brambles in their fortified city; and she shall become a habitation for dragons, a place for the daughter of the ostrich.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:14)


וִיעַרְעָן תַּמְוָן בַּחְתוּלִין וְשֵׁידִין חַד עִם חַבְרֵהּ יְחַיְכוּן בְּרַם תַּמָן יִשְׁרְיָן לִילִין וְיִשְׁכְּחָן לְהֶן נְיָה:


And there shall meet one another, martens and wild cats, and demons, each shall sport with its companion: verily dryads shall dwell there, and they shall find rest for themselves.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:15)


תַּמָן תְּקַנִין קוּפְדָא וּתְשֵׁיזִיב וְקִירֵיהוֹן יְנַצְפוּן בְּטוּרָא בְּרַם לְתַמָן יִתְכַּנְשׁוּן דַיָתָא אִתְּתָא לְוַת חֲבֶרְתָּהּ:


And the hedgehog shall make its nest there, and shall breed there; partridges shall chirp in the shady boughs: surely, the vultures shall be gathered thither, every one with her mate.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:16)


תְּבָעוּ מֵעַל סִפְרָא דַייָ וּבְעוֹ חֲדָא מִנְהוֹן לָא שְׁנָת וְאִתְּתָא לַחֲבֶרְתָּהּ לָא עַכִּיבַת אֲרֵי בְּמֵימְרֵהּ יִתְכַּנְשִׁן וּבִרְעוּתֵהּ יִתְקָרְבָן:


Search ye out of the book of the Lord, and seek: no one of them shall fail, none shall tarry for her mate, for by His WORD they shall be gathered together, and by His pleasure they shall be brought together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 34:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/34:17)


וְהוּא בְמֵימְרֵהּ רְמָא לְהוֹן עַדְבָא וּבִרְעוּתֵהּ פְּלִיג לְהוֹן בְּחוּטָא עַד עַלְמָא יַחְסְנוּנָהּ לְדוֹר וָדוֹר יִשְׁרוֹן בָּהּ:


And He by His WORD hath cast the lot for them; and by His will He hath divided it unto them by line; they shall possess it for ever; from generation to generation they shall dwell therein.



## Chapter 35

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:1)


יֶחְדוּן דְיַתְבִין בְּמַדְבְּרָא בְּאַרְעָא צִיחַיָא וְיִבְעוּן דְשָׁרָן בְּמֵישְׁרָא וְיִזְהֲרוּן כְּשׁוֹשַׁנְיָא:


They that dwell in the wilderness, in a thirsty land, shall rejoice; and those that inhabit the desert shall rejoice, and shall shine as the lilies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:2)


מֵידָץ יְדוּצוּן וְיֶחְדוּן אַף בִּיעַ וְחֶדְוָא יְקַר לִבְנָן יִתְיְהֵיב לְהוֹן זִיו כַּרְמְלָא וְשָׁרוֹנָא בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל דִלְהוֹן אָמְרִין אִלֵין אִינוּן יֶחֱזוּן יְקָרָא דַייָ זִיו תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא דֶאֱלָהָנָא:


They shall greatly rejoice and be glad, yea, with joy and gladness. The glory of Lebanon shall be given unto them; the splendour of Carmel and of Sharon. The house of Israel to whom these things are promised, they, they shall see the glory of the Lord, the beauty of our God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:3)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא תַּקִיפוּ יָדִין דִמְרַשְׁלָן וּרְכוּבִין דִרָעֲלָן חֲסִינוּ:


The prophet said: Strengthen ye the weak hands, and confirm ye the feeble knees.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:4)


אֲמַרוּ לְדָמוֹחָן בְּלִבְּהוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד אוֹרַיְתָא תְּקָפוּ וְלָא תִדַחֲלוּן הָא אֶלָהָכוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד פּוּרְעֲנוּת דִין אִתְגְלֵי מָרֵי גְמַלְיָא יְיָ הִיא יִתְגְלֵי וְיִפְרְקִינְכוֹן:


Say ye to the fearful of heart that they may keep the law, Be ye strong, and fear ye not: behold, your God shall be revealed to take vengeance of judgment: the Lord of retributions, the Lord shall be revealed, and He shall save you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:5)


בְּכֵן יִתְפַּתְּחָן עֵינֵי בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל דְאִינוּן כְּסָמָן מִן אוֹרַיְתָא וְאוּדְנֵיהוֹן דִכְּחַרְשִׁין לְקַבָּלָא לְמִלֵי נְבִיָא יְצִיתָן:


Then the eyes of Israel shall be opened, which were blind to the law, and their ears, which were as of the deaf, shall hear and receive the words of the prophets.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:6)


בְּכֵן בְּמֶחְזְהוֹן גַלְוָתָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין וְסַלְקָן לְאַרְעֲהוֹן הָא כְּאַיָלִין קַלִילִין וְלָא לְאִתְעַכָּבָא וִישַׁבַּח לִישָׁנְהוֹן דַהֲוָה בְּלִים אֲרֵי בְּכֵן אִתְבְּזָעוּ בְמַדְבְּרָא מַיָא וְנַחֲלַיָא בְּמֵישְׁרָא:


Then when they shall see the captives of Israel gathered to go up to their own land as the swift harts, and not tarry, they shall sing with their tongue, which has been tied, because then the waters shall gush forth in the wilderness, and rivers in the plain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:7)


וִיהֵי שְׁרוֹבָא לַאֲגַמִין דְמַיָן וּבֵית צָחְוָנָא לְמַבּוּעֵי מַיָא אֲתַר דַהֲוָה יְרוֹדִין שָׁרְיָן תַּמָן יִסְקוּן קָנֶה וְגוֹמָא:


Then the mirage shall become pools of water, and the thirsty place springs of water, in the place where the dragons dwell, reeds and rushes shall come up.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:8)


וִיהֵי תַמָן כֵּבַשׁ אוֹרַח וְתִקוּן אוֹרְחָא דְקוּדְשָׁא יִתְקְרֵי לָהּ לָא יֶעְדוּן בֵּיהּ מְסָאֲבִין וְלָא יִפְסְקוּן עָדֵי אוֹרְחָא וּדְלָא אֲלִיפוּ לָא יִטְעוּן:


And a trodden way shall be there, and a straight one; and it shall be called the way of holiness; the unclean shall not pass over it, and the wayfaring men shall not cease; the ignorant shall not err.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:9)


לָא יְהֵי תַמָן מֶלֶךְ מַבְאֵישׁ וְשִׁלְטוֹן מְעִיק לָא יְגוֹזִינֵהּ וְלָא יִשְׁתַּכְּחוּן תַּמָן וִיהָכוּן פְרִיקִין:


There shall not be there a king doing evil, and an oppressive governor shall not pass over it, yea, they shall not be found there; but the redeemed shall walk there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 35:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/35:10)


וּפְרִיקַיָא דַייָ יְתוּבוּן וְיַתְכַּנְשׁוּן מִבֵּינֵי גַלְוַתְהוֹן וְיָעַלוּן לְצִיוֹן בְּתוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא וְחֶדְוַת עֲלַם תְּהֵי לְהוֹן דִי לָא פַסְקָא וַעֲנַן יְקָר תְּהֵי מַטַל עַל רֵישֵׁיהוֹן בִּיעַ וְחֶדְוָא יִשְׁכְּחוּן וִיסוּף מִנְהוֹן מִבֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל דְוָנָא וְתֵינָחָתָא:


And the redeemed of the Lord shall return, because they shall be gathered from the midst of their captivity; and they shall come to Zion with a song, and they shall have everlasting joy, which shall not cease, and a cloud of glory shall overshadow their heads: joy and gladness shall be found, and sorrow and sighing shall cease from them, namely, from the house of Israel.



## Chapter 36

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:1)


וַהֲוָה בְּאַרְבַּע עַסְרֵי שְׁנִין לְמַלְכָּא חִזְקִיָה סְלִיק סַנְחֶרִיב מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר עַל כָּל קִרְוַיָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה כְּרִיכְתָּא וְאַחֲדִנוּן:


Now it came to pass in the fourteenth year of king Hezekiah, that Sennacherib king of Assyria came up against all the fenced cities of the house of Judah, and took them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:2)


וּשְׁלַח מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר יַת רַבְשָׁקֵה מִלָכִישׁ לִירוּשְׁלֵם לְוַת מַלְכָּא חִזְקִיָה בְּמַשִׁרְיָת סַגִיאָן וְקָם בִּמְזִיקַת בְרֵיכְתָּא עִלִיתָא דִכְבִישׁ חֲקַל מִשְׁטַח קַצָרַיָא:


And the king of Assyria sent Rabshakeh from Lachish to Jerusalem unto king Hezekiah with a great army. And he stood by the conduit of the upper pool in the highway of the field of the fullers.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:3)


וּנְפַק לְוָתֵהּ אֱלְיָקִים בַּר חִלְקִיָה דִי מְמַנָא עַל בֵּיתָא וְשֶׁבְנָא סַפְרָא וְיוֹאָח בַּר אָסָף דִי מְמַנָא עַל דָכְרָנַיָא:


Then came forth unto him Eliakim, Hilkiah's son, who was appointed over the household, and Shebna, the scribe, and Joah, Asaph's son, who was appointed over the records.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:4)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן רַבְשָׁקֵה אֲמָרוּ כְעַן לְחִזְקִיָה כִּדְנַן אֲמַר מַלְכָּא דְרַבְּתָנָא מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר מָה רוּחֲצָנָא הָדֵין דְאִיתְרַחֵיצְתָּא:


And Rabshakeh said unto them, say ye now to Hezekiah, Thus saith our great king, the king of Assyria, What confidence is this wherein thou trustest?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:5)


אֲמַרֵית בְּרַם בְּמַמְלֵל סִפְוָן בְּמֵלָךְ וּגְבוּרָא אַעְבֵּיד קְרָבָא כְּעַן עַל מַן אִיתְרַחֵיצְתָּא אֲרֵי מְרַדְתָּא בִּי:


I say (you speak but empty words?) I make war with counsel and might: now, in whom dost thou trust, that thou hast rebelled against me?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:6)


הָא אִיצְרַחֵיצְתָּא עַל סֶמֶךְ קַנְיָא רְעִיעָא הָדֵין עַל פַּרְעֹה מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם דְאִם יִסְתְּמֵיךְ גַבְרָא עֲלוֹהִי וְיֵעוֹל בִּידֵהּ וִיבַזְעִינֵהּ כֵּן פַּרְעֹה מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם לְכָל דְמִתְרַחֲצִין עַלוֹהִי:


Behold, thou trustest in the support of this broken reed, in Pharaoh, the king of Egypt; on which if a man lean, it will enter his hand and wound him: so is Pharaoh the king of Egypt to all that trust in him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:7)


וַאֲרֵי תֵימְרוּן לִי עַל מֵימְרָא דַייָ אֱלָהָנָא אִתְרַחֵיצְנָא הֲלָא הוּא דְאַעְדִי חִזְקִיָה יַת בָמָתוֹהִי וְיַת אֱגְרוֹהִי וַאֲמַר לֶאֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה וּלְיָתְבֵי יְרוּשְׁלֶם קֳדָם מַדְבְּחָא הָדֵין תִּסְגְדוּן:


But if ye say to me, We trust in the WORD of the Lord our God: is it not he, whose high places and whose altars Hezekiah hath taken away, and said to the men of Judah and to the inhabitants of Jerusalem, Ye shall worship before this altar?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:8)


וּכְעַן אִתְעָרֵב כְעַן עִם רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר וְאֶתֵּן לָךְ תְּרֵין אַלְפִין סוּסְוָן אִם תִּכּוּל לְמַנָאָה עֲלָךְ רָכְבִין עֲלֵיהוֹן:


And now, pledge thyself with my master the king of Assyria, and I will give thee two thousand horses, if thou be able on thy part to appoint riders upon them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:9)


וְאֵיכְדֵין אַתְּ מְדַמִי לַאֲתָבָא יַת אַפֵּי חַד מִשִׁלְטוֹנֵי עַבְדֵי רִיבּוֹנִי זְעֵרַיָא וְאִיתְרַחֵצְתָּא לָךְ עַל מִצְרָיִם לִרְתִיכִין וּלְפָרָשִׁין:


And how dost thou expect to turn back the face of one of the least princes, servants of my master? yet, thou trustest in Egypt for chariots and horsemen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:10)


וּכְעַן הֲבַּר מִמֵימְרָא דַייָ סַלְקֵית לְאַרְעָא הָדָא לְחַבְּלוּתָא יְיָ אֲמַר לִי סַק עַל אַרְעָא הָדָא וְחַבְּלָהּ:


Am I now come up without the WORD of the Lord against this land to destroy it? The Lord said unto me, Go up against this land, and destroy it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:11)


וַאֲמַר אֶלְיָקִים וְשֶׁבְנָא וְיוֹאָח לְרַבְשָׁקֵה מַלֵיל כְּעַן עִם עַבְדָךְ אֲרָמִית אֲרֵי שָׁמְעִין אֲנַחְנָא וְלָא תְמַלֵיל עִמָנָא יְהוּדִית קֳדָם עַמָא דִי עַל שׁוּרָא:


Then said Eliakim and Shebna and Joah unto Rabshakeh, Speak, I pray thee, unto thy servants in the Syrian language; for we understand it: and speak not to us in the Jews' language before the people that are upon the wall.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:12)


וַאֲמַר רַבְשָׁקֵה הַלְוַת רִבּוֹנָךְ וַעֲלָךְ שַׁלְחַנִי רִבּוֹנִי לְמַלָלָא יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִלֵין הֲלָא עַל גַבְרַיָא דְיַתְבִין עַל שׁוּרָא לְמֵיכַל יַת מַפְקַתְהוֹן וּלְמִשְׁתֵּי יַת מֵימֵי רִגְלֵיהוֹן בִּצְיָרָא עִמְכוֹן:


But Rabshakeh said, Hath my master sent me to thy master and to thee to speak these words? hath he not sent me to the men that sit upon the wall, that they may eat their own dung, and drink their own piss in the siege with you?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:13)


וְקָם רַבְשָׁקֵה וְאַכְלֵי בְקַל רַב יְהוּדִית וַאֲמַר שְׁמָעוּ יַת פִּתְגָמָא מַלְכָּא דְרַבְּתָנָא מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר:


Then Rabshakeh stood forth, and cried with a loud voice in the Jews' language, and said, Hear ye the words of our great king, the king of Assyria.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:14)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר מַלְכָּא לָא יִטְעֵי לְכוֹן חִזְקִיָה אֲרֵי לָא יִכוּל לְשֵׁיזָבָא יַתְכוֹן:


Thus saith the king, Let not Hezekiah cause you to err: for he shall not be able to deliver you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:15)


וְלָא יַרְחִיץ יַתְכוֹן חִזְקִיָה עַל מֵימְרָא דַייָ לְמֵימָר שֵׁיזָבָא יְשֵׁזְבָנָנָא יְיָ לָא תִּתְמְסַר קַרְתָּא הָדָא בְּיַד מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר:


Neither let Hezekiah make you trust in the WORD of the Lord, saying, The Lord will surely deliver us: this city shall not be delivered into the hand of the king of Assyria.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:16)


לָא תְקַבְּלוּן מִן חִזְקִיָה אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר עַבִידוּ עִמִי שְׁלָמָא וּפוּקוּ לְוָתִי וֶאֱכוּלוּ גְבַר פֵּרֵי גוּפְנוֹהִי וּגְבַר פֵּרֵי תֵינוֹהִי וּשְׁתוּ גְבַר מֵי גוּבֵהּ:


Hearken not to Hezekiah: for thus saith the king of Assyria, Make peace with me, and, come out to me: and eat ye every one of the fruit of his fig tree, and drink ye every one of the waters of his cistern;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:17)


עַד מֵיתָא וְאֶדְבַּר יַתְכוֹן לְאַרְעָא טָבָא כְּאַרְעָכוֹן לְאַרְעָא טָבָא כְּאַרְעָכוֹן אֲרַע עִבּוּר וַחֲמַר אֲרַע חֶקְלִין וְכַרְמִין:


Until I come and take you away to a good land like your own land, a land of corn and wine, a land of fields and vineyards.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:18)


דִלְמָא יַטְעֵי יַתְכוֹן חִזְקִיָה לְמֵימָר יְיָ יְשֵׁזְבָנָנָא הֲשֵׁיזְבָא דַחֲלַת עַמְמַיָא גְבַר יַת אַרְעֵהּ מִיַד מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר:


Beware lest Hezekiah cause you to err, saying, The Lord shall deliver us. Hath any of the gods of the nations delivered his land from the hand of the king of Assyria?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:19)


אָן דַחֲלַת חֲמָת וְאַרְפָּד אָן דַחֲלַת סְפַרְוָיִם וַאֲרֵי שֵׁיזָבוּ יַת שֹׁמְרוֹן מִן יְדִי:


Where are the gods of Hamath and Arphad? where are the gods of Sepharvaim? what have they delivered Samaria out of my hand?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:20)


מַן בְּכָל דַחֲלַת מְדִינָתָא הָאִלֵין דְשֵׁיזָבוּ יַת אַרְעֲהוֹן מִן יְדִי אֲרֵי יְשֵׁיזִיב יְיָ יַת יְרוּשְׁלֵם מִן יְדִי:


Who are they among all the gods of these lands, that have delivered their land from my hand, that the Lord shall deliver Jerusalem from my hand?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:21)


וּשְׁתִיקוּ וְלָא אָתִיבוּ יָתֵהּ פִּתְגָמָא אֲרֵי תַפְקִידַת מַלְכָּא הִיא לְמֵימָר לָא תֵתִיבוּנֵהּ:


But they held their peace, and answered him not a word: for the king's commandment was, saying, Answer him not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 36:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/36:22)


וַאֲתָא אֶלְיָקִים בַּר חִלְקִיָה דִי מְמַנָא עַל בֵיתָא וְשֶׁבְנָא סַפְרָא וְיוֹאָח בַּר אָסָף דִי מְמַנָא עַל דָכְרָנַיָא לְוַת חִזְקִיָה כַּד מְבַזְעִין לְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן וְחַוִיאוּ לֵיהּ יַת פִּתְגָמֵי רַבְשָׁקֵה:


Then came Eliakim, the son of Hilkiah, who was appointed over the household, and Shebna the scribe, and Joah, the son of Asaph, who was appointed over the records, to Hezekiah, now having their clothes rent, and told him the words of Rabshakeh.



## Chapter 37

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:1)


וַהֲוָה כַּד שְׁמַע מַלְכָּא חִזְקִיָהוּ בְזַע יַת לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְאִתְכַּסֵי בְּסַקָא וְעַל לְבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא דַיָי:


And it came to pass, when king Hezekiah heard it, he rent his clothes, and covered himself with sackcloth, and went into the house of the sanctuary of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:2)


וּשְׁלַח יַת אֶלְיָקִים דִי מְמַנָא עַל בֵּיתָא וְיַת שֶׁבְנָא סַפְרָא וְיַת סָבֵי כַּהֲנַיָא כַּד מְכַסָן סַקְיָא לְוַת יְשַׁעְיָהוּ בַּר אָמוֹץ נְבִיָא:


And he sent Eliakim, who was appointed over the household, and Shebna the scribe, and the elders of the priests, thus covered with sackcloth, unto Isaiah the prophet, the son of Amoz.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:3)


וְאָמְרוּ לֵיהּ כִּדְנַן אֲמַר חִזְקִיָה יוֹם עָקָא וְחִיסוּדִין וְנִיאוּצָא יוֹמָא הָדֵין אֲרֵי אֲקֵיפְתָּנָא עָקָא כְּאִתָּא דְיָתְבָא עַל מַתְבְּרָא וְחֵיל לֵית לָהּ לְמֵילַד:


And they said unto him, Thus saith Hezekiah: This day is a day of distress, of reproach, and of contumely: for distress hath laid hold on us, as a woman that sitteth upon the stool of a woman in labour, and hath no strength to bring forth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:4)


מָאִים שְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יַת פִּתְגָמֵי רַבְשָׁקֵה דְשַׁלְחֵהּ מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר רִבּוֹנֵהּ לְחַסָדָא עַמָא דַייָ קְיָמָא וְיַעְבֵּיד פּוּרְעֲנוּתָא עַל כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא דְשָׁמְעִין קֳדָם אֱלָהָךְ וְתִתְחַנֵן בִּצְלוֹ עַל שְׁאָרָא דְאִשְׁתְּאַר:


Perhaps the words of Rabshakeh may be heard by the Lord thy God, whom the king of Assyria, his master, hath sent to reproach the people of the living God, and he may take vengeance for the words which were heard before thy God, wherefore thou shalt supplicate in prayer for the remnant that is left.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:5)


וַאֲתוֹ עַבְדֵי מַלְכָּא חִזְקִיָה לְוַת יְשַׁעְיָה:


So the servants of king Hezekiah came to Isaiah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:6)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן יְשַׁעְיָה כְּדֵין תֵּימְרוּן לְרִיבּוֹנְכוֹן כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ לָא תִדְחַל מִן קֳדָם פִּתְגָמַיָא דִי שְׁמַעְתָּא דְחַסְדוּ עוּלֵימֵי מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר קֳדָמָי:


And Isaiah said unto them, Thus shall ye say unto your master, Thus saith the Lord, Be not afraid on account of the words that thou hast heard, wherewith the servants of the king of Assyria have blasphemed before me.

Behold, I will put a spirit within him, and he shall hear a rumour, and return to his own land: and I will cause him to fall by the sword in his own land.

And Sennacherib heard say concerning Tirhaka king of Ethiopia, he hath gone forth to wage war against thee. And when he heard it, he sent messengers to Hezekiah, saying,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:10)


כְּדֵין תֵּימְרוּן לְחִזְקִיָהוּ מְלַךְ שִׁבְטָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה לְמֵימָר לָא יַטְעִינָךְ אֱלָהָךְ דִי אַתְּ רָחֵיץ בֵּיהּ לְמֵימָר לָא תִתְמְסַר יְרוּשְׁלֵם בְּיַד מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר:


Thus shall ye speak to Hezekiah, king of the tribe of the house of Judah, saying, Let not thy God, in whom thou trustest, cause thee to err, saying, Jerusalem shall not be delivered into the hand of the king of Assyria.

Behold, thou hast heard what the kings of Assyria have done to all lands by destroying them utterly; and dost thou imagine that thou wilt be delivered?

Where is the king of Hamath, and the king of Arphad, and the king of the city of Sepharvaim? hath he not removed them, and carried them captive?

And Hezekiah received letters from the hands of the messengers, and read one of them: and Hezekiah went to the house of the sanctuary of the Lord, and spread it before the Lord.

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:16)


יְיָ אֱלָהָא צְבָאוֹת אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דִשְׁכִינְתֵּהּ שַׁרְיָא עֵיל מִן כְּרוּבַיָא אַתָּה הוּא יְיָ וְלֵית בַּר מִנָךְ לְכָל מַלְכְּוַת אַרְעָא אַתְּ עֲבַדְתָּ יַת שְׁמַיָא וְיַת אַרְעָא:


O Lord God of hosts, the God of Israel, whose Shekinah dwelleth above the Cherubim! Thou art the Lord, and there is none besides Thee in all the kingdoms of the earth; Thou hast made the heavens and the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:17

גְלֵי קֳדָמָךְ יְיָ וְדוּן וּשְׁמִיעַ קֳדָמָךְ יְיָ וְאִתְפְּרַע וַעֲבֵיד פּוּרְעַן עַל כָּל פִּתְגָמֵי סַנְחֵרִיב דִשְׁלַח לְחַסָדָא עַמָא דַייָ קְיָמָא:


It is manifest to Thee, O Lord, judge therefore; and it hath been heard by Thee, O Lord, avenge therefore! Take vengeance on account of all the words of Sennacherib, who hath sent to reproach the people of the living God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:18)

Of a truth, O Lord, the kings of Assyria have laid waste all the provinces and their lands,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:19)


וּמוֹקְדִין יַת טַעֲוַתְהוֹן בְּנוּרָא אֲרֵי לָא טַעֲוָן דְאִית בְּהוֹן צְרוֹךְ אִינוּן אֱלָהֵין עוֹבַד יְדֵי אֱנָשָׁא אָעָא וְאַבְנָא וְאַבְּדוּנוּן:


And have burnt their gods in the fire: for they were no gods, they are of no use, but they are the work of men's hands, wood and stone, therefore they have destroyed them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:20)

And now, O Lord our God, save us from his hand, that all the kingdoms of the earth may know that Thou art the Lord; there is none besides Thee.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:21)


וּשְׁלַח יְשַׁעְיָה בַּר אָמוֹץ לְוַת חִזְקִיָה לְמֵימָר כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דִבְעֵיתָא מִן קֳדָמַי עַל סַנְחֵרִיב מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר:


Then Isaiah the son of Amoz sent to Hezekiah, saying, Thus saith the Lord, the God of Israel, whereas thou hast prayed before me concerning Sennacherib, the king of Assyria:

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:23)


יַת מַן חַסַדְתָּא וְיַת מַן אִתְרָחַבְתָּא וְקָדָם מַן אֲרֵימָתָא קָלָא וּזְקֵפְתָּא לְרוּמָא עֵינָךְ וַאֲמַרְתָּא מִלִין דְלָא כַּשְׁרִין קֳדָם קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Whom hast thou reproached, and against whom hast thou exalted thyself? and before whom hast thou lifted up thy voice? Yea, thou hast lifted up thine eyes on high; because thou hast spoken words which are not right before the Holy One of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:24)


בְּיַד עַבְדָךְ חִסַדְתָּ עַמָא דַייָ וַאֲמַרְתָּ בִּסְגִיאוּת רְתִיכֵי אֲנָא סַלְקֵית לִתְקוֹף כְּרַכֵּיהוֹן וְאַף אֵיחוּד בֵּית מַקְדַשֵׁהוֹן וְאֶקְטוֹל שְׁפַר גִבָּרֵיהוֹן מִבְחַר שִׁלְטוֹנֵיהוֹן וְאֶכְבּוֹשׁ קִרְיַת תּוּקְפֵיהוֹן וֶאֱשֵׁיצֵי סַגִי מַשִׁרְיָתֵהוֹן:


By thy servants hast thou reproached the people of the Lord, and hast said, By the multitude of my chariots am I come up against their fortified cities; and moreover, I will seize the house of their sanctuary, and I will slay the most beautiful? of their mighty ones, and the choicest of their rulers, and I will subdue the city of their strength, and I will destroy the multitude of their army.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:25)


אֲנָא הֲוֵיתִי חָפֵיר גוּבִין וְשָׁתֵי מַיָן וּטְפָחֵית בְּפַרְסַת רִגְלֵי עַמָא דְעִמִי כָּל מֵי נַהֲרִין עֲמִיקִין:


I have digged cisterns, and I have drunk water, and I have dried up all the waters of the deep rivers with the sole of the people that were with me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:26)


הֲלָא שְׁמַעְתָּא מִלְקַדְמִין מָא עֲבָדִית לְפַרְעֹה מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם וְאַף עֲלָךְ אִתְנַבִּיאוּ נְבִיֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלָא תַבְתָּא וְדָא חַזְיָא קֳדָמַי מִיוֹמֵי קֳדָם לְמֶעְבַּד לָךְ וְאַף אַתְקֵנְתָּא כְּעַן אַיְתֵיתָה וְדָא הֲוָת לָךְ לְתַקָלָא עַל דַהֲוָאָה קֳדָמָךְ כְּאִתְרְגוּשַׁת גַלִין דִשְׁחוֹ קִרְוִין כְּרִיכָן:


Hast thou not heard long ago, what I have done to Pharao, king of Egypt? Yea, also, the prophets of Israel have prophesied concerning thee; but thou hast not repented. I contemplated doing this unto thee, since the days of old. I have prepared it, now I will bring it to pass, and it shall be ruin unto thee; because it shall be before thee as the raging of waves, which destroy fortified cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:27)


וְיָתְבֵיהוֹן אִתְקַצֵר חֵילֵיהוֹן אִתְבָּרוּ וּבְהִיתוּ הֲווֹ כְּעֵשֶׂב חַקְלַיָא וִירוֹק דִתְאָה כְּעֵשֶׂב אִגָרַיָא דִשְׁלוֹק עַד לָא מָטֵא לְמֶהֱוֵי שׁוּבְּלִין:


Therefore their inhabitants had their strength cut off, they were dismayed and confounded; they were as the grass of the fields, yea, as the green grass, as the herb on the housetops, as corn blasted before it becomes an ear of corn.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:28)


וּמַתְבָךְ בְעֵיצָה וּמַקְפָךְ לְבָרָא לְאַגָחָא קְרָבָא וּמֵיתְיָךְ לְאַרְעָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל גְלֵי קֳדָמַי וְיַת דְאַרְגֵיזְתָּא קֳדָמַי גְלֵי:


And thy sitting down in counsel, and thy going forth to wage war, and thy coming into the land of Israel is known to me, and that thou art enraged is known to me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:29)


חֲלַף דְאַרְגֵזְתָּא עַל מֵימְרִי וְאִתְרְגוּשְׁתָּךְ סְלִיקַת לִקֳדָמָי וַאֲשַׁוֵי שֵׁירִין בְּלִיסָתָךְ וּזְמַם בְּסִפְוָתָךְ וַאֲתֵיבִנָךְ בְּאוֹרְחָא דַאֲתֵיתָא בָהּ:


Because thou art enraged against my WORD, and thy tumultuous noise hath come up before me: therefore I will put a ring in thy nostril, and a bridle in thy lips, and I will turn thee back by the way by which thou camest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:30)


וְדֵין לָךְ אָתָא אֲכוֹל בְּשַׁתָּא הָדָא כַּתִּין וּבְשַׁתָּא תִּנְיֵתָא כַּתְכַּתִּין וּבְשַׁתָּא תְּלִיתָאָה זִרְעוּ וַחֲצוֹדוּ וְצוּבוּ כַרְמִין וֶאֱכוּלוּ אִבְּהֶן:


And this shall be a sign to thee: Eat this year such as groweth of itself; and in the second year, that which springeth of the same; and in the third year, sow ye, and reap, and plant vineyards, and eat the fruit thereof.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:31)


וְיָסְפוּן מְשֵׁזְבַיָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה דְאִשְׁתָּאֲרוּן כְּאִילָן דִמְשַׁלַח שׁוֹרְשׁוֹהִי מִלְרַע וּמֵרִים נוֹפֵהּ לְעֵילָא:


And again the escaped of the house of Judah that are left shall be like a tree which sends forth its roots downwards, and lifts its branches upwards.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:32)


אֲרֵי מִירוּשְׁלֵם יִפְקוּן שְׁאָר צַדִיקַיָא וְשֵׁיזְבַת מְקִימֵי אוֹרַיְתָא מִטוּרָא דְצִיוֹן בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת תִּתְעֲבֵיד דָא:


For the remnant of the righteous shall go forth from Jerusalem, and the escaped of them that establish the law from mount Zion: by the WORD of the Lord of hosts shall this be done.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:33)


בְּכֵן כִּדְנָן אֲמַר יְיָ עַל מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר לָא יֵעוּל לְקַרְתָּא הָדָא וְלָא יַקְשֵׁית תַּמָן נִיר וְלָא יְקַדְמִינֵהּ בִּתְרֵיסִין וְלָא יִצְבּוֹר עֲלָה מִלֵיתָא:


Therefore thus saith the Lord concerning the king of Assyria, He shall not come into this city, nor shoot an arrow there, nor come before it with shields, nor shall be cast up a mound against it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:34)


בְּאוֹרְחָא דַאֲתָא בָה יְתוּב וּלְקַרְתָּא הָדָא לָא יֵעוּל אֲמַר יְיָ:


By the way that he came, by the same he shall return, and he shall not come into this city, saith the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:35)


וְאַגֵין עַל קַרְתָּא הָדָא לְמִפְרְקָהּ בְּדִיל מֵימְרִי וּבְדִיל דָוִד עַבְדִי:


For I will defend this city to save it, for the sake of my WORD, and for the sake of David my servant.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:36)


וּנְפַק מַלְאָכָא דַייָ וּקְטַל בְּמַשְׁרִית אַתּוּרָאָה מְאָה וְתַמְנָן וְחַמְשָׁא אַלְפִין וְאַקְדִימוּ בְצַפְרָא וְהָא כוּלְהוֹן פִּגְרִין מֵתִין:


Then the angel of the Lord went forth, and slew in the camp of the Assyrians a hundred and four score and five thousand: and when they arose early in the morning, behold, they were all of them dead corpses.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:37)


וּנְטַל וַאֲזַל וְתַב סַנְחֵרִיב מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר וִיתֵיב בְּנִינְוֵה:


Then Sennacherib king of Assyria departed, and went away, and returned and dwelt at Nineveh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 37:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/37:38)


וַהֲוָה הוּא סָגִיד בֵּית נִסְרוֹךְ טַעֲוָתֵהּ וְאַדַרְמֶלֶךְ וְשַׁרְאֶצַר בְנוֹהִי קַטְלוֹהִי בְּחַרְבָּא וְאִינוּן אִשְׁתֵּזֵבוּ לְאַרְעָא קַרְדוּ וּמְלַךְ אַסַרְחַדוֹן בְּרֵיהּ תְּחוֹתוֹהִי:


And it came to pass, as he was worshipping in the temple of Nisroch his god, that Adramelech and Sharezer his sons slew him with the sword, and they escaped into the land of Kardu: and Eserhaddon his son reigned in his stead.



## Chapter 38

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:1)


בְּיוֹמַיָא הָאִנוּן מָרַע חִזְקִיָה לִמְמָת וַאֲתָא לְוָתֵהּ יְשַׁעְיָהוּ בַר אָמוֹץ נְבִיָא וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ פַּקֵד עַל אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתָךְ אֲרֵי מָאִת אַתְּ וְלָא תֵיהֵי מִמַרְעָךְ:


In those days was Hezekiah sick unto death. And Isaiah the prophet, the son of Amoz, came unto him, and said unto him, Thus saith the Lord, Order thy household, for thou art dying, and shalt not live.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:2)


וְאַסְחַר חִזְקִיָהוּ אַפּוֹהִי לְכוֹתֵל בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא וְצַלִי קֳדָם יְיָ:


Then Hezekiah turned his face towards the wall of the house of the sanctuary, and prayed before the Lord,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:3)


וַאֲמַר קַבֵּל בָּעוּתִי יְיָ אִדְכַּר כְּעַן יַת דִי פְלָחִית קֳדָמָךְ בִּקְשׁוֹט וּבְלֵב שָׁלֵם וּדְתַקִין בְּעֵינָךְ עַבְדִית וּבְכָא חִזְקִיָה בְּכִיא סַגִיאָה:


And said, Hear my prayer, O Lord, remember, I beseech thee, how I have served before Thee in truth, and with a perfect heart, and I have done that which is right in Thy eyes. And Hezekiah wept sore.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:4)


וַהֲוָה פִתְגָמֵי נְבוּאָה מִן קֳדָם יְיָ עִם יְשַׁעְיָה לְמֵימָר:


Then the words of prophecy came from before the Lord to Isaiah, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:5)


אֱזֵיל וְתֵימַר לְחִזְקִיָה כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלָהֵיהּ דְדָוִד אָבוּךְ שְׁמִיעַ קֳדָמַי צְלוֹתָךְ גַלְיָא קֳדָמַי דִמְעֲתָךְ הָא אֲנָא מוֹסִיף עַל יוֹמָךְ חֲמֵשׁ עַסְרֵי שְׁנִין:


Go, and say to Hezekiah, Thus saith the Lord, the God of David thy father, Thy prayer hath been heard by me, thy tears are observed by me: behold, I will add to thy days fifteen years;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:6)


וּמִיַד מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּר אֲשֵׁיזְבִינָךְ וְיַת קַרְתָּא הָדָא וְאַגִין עַל קַרְתָּא הָדָא:


And I will deliver thee and this city out of the hand of the king of Assyria: and I will defend this city.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:7)


וְדֵין לָךְ אָתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ דְיַעְבֵּיד יְיָ יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין דִי מַלֵיל:


And this shall be a sign unto thee from before the Lord, that the Lord will do this thing that He hath promised.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:8)


הָא אֲנָא מֵתִיב יַת טוּל אֶבֶן שְׁעַיָא דְנָחְתַת בְּמַסְקָנָא דְאָחָז שִׁמְשָׁא לַאֲחוֹרוֹהִי עֲסַר שְׁעָן וְתַב שִׁמְשָׁא עֲסַר שְׁעָן בְּצוּרַת אֶבֶן שְׁעַיָא דְנָחֲתַת:


Behold, I will turn back the shadow on the hour-lines by which the sun is gone down on the dial of Ahaz, ten hours backwards. And the sun returned ten hours on the figure of the hourlines, which it had gone down.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:9)


כְּתַב אוֹדָאָה עַל נִיסָא דְאִתְעַבֵיד לְחִזְקִיָה מְלַךְ שִׁבְטָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה כַּד מְרַע וְאִתְּסֵי מִמַרְעֵהּ:


The Writing Of The Thanksgiving For The Miracle, Which Had Been Done For Hezekiah, The King Of The Tribe Of The House Of Judah, When He Had Been Sick, And Was Healed Of His Sickness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:10)


אֲנָא אֲמָרֵית בִּדְוָן יוֹמֵי אֱהַךְ בְּתַרְעֵי שְׁאוֹל עַל דָכְרָנִי לְטַב אִתּוֹסַף עַל שְׁנָי:


I said in the sorrow of my days, I shall go into the gates of the grave; but because He remembered me for good, an addition hath been made to my years.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:11)


אֲמָרֵית לָא אִתְחַזֵי עוֹד קֳדָם דְחֵילָא דַייָ בְּאַרְעָא בֵית שְׁכִינְתֵּהּ דִי בָה אֲרִיכוּת חַיָא וְלָא אֶפְלַח קֳדָמוֹהִי עוֹד בְּבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא דְמִתַּמָן עֲתִיד לְמִפַּק חֶדְוָא לְכָל יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא מִלְמֵידַר בִּירוּשְׁלֵם קַרְתָּא דְקוּדְשָׁא מְדוֹרֵי פְּסָק:


I thought, I shall not appear again before the terrible God, in the land of the house of His Shekinah, in which is length of life: and I shall no more serve before Him in the house of the sanctuary, from whence shall go forth joy unto all the inhabitants of the earth; neither shall I dwell in Jerusalem the holy city; my habitation is cut down.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:12)


מִבְּנֵי דָרִי אִתְנְטִילוּ יוֹמֵי אִתְקַצִיצוּ וּגְלוּ מִנִי אִתְכְּפִילוּ כְּמַשְׁכְּנָא דְרָעֵי אִתְקְפָדוּ כְּנַחַל נַדְדָאִין חַיַי מִיקָר מַלְכוּתִי אֲנָא גָּלֵי יְמָמָא וְלֵילְוָתִי שְׁלִימוּ:


From the children of my generation my days are taken away, they are cut off, and gone away from me; they are folded up like a shepherd's tent; my life is cut off like a weaver's web; from the glory of my kingdom I am gone into captivity; my days and my nights are ended.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:13)


נַהֲמִית עַד צַפְרָא כְּאַרְיָא דְנָהֵים וְתָבַר גַרְמַי חֵיוָתָא כֵּן מִתַּבְּרִין מִן קֳדָם דְוָנָא כָּל גַרְמֵי יְמָמַי וְלֵילְוָתִי שְׁלִימוּ:


I roar until the morning like a lion that roareth when he breaketh the bones of a beast; thus on account of my sorrow all my bones are broken; my days and my nights are come to an end.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:14)


כְּסוּסְיָא דַאֲחִיד וּמְנַצֵיף כֵּן נְצֵיפַת וְנַהֲמֵית כְּיוֹנָה זְקָפִית עֵינַי דְיֵיתֵי לִי רְוַח מִן קֳדָם דִשְׁכִינְתֵּהּ בִשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא יְיָ קַבֵּיל צְלוֹתִי עֲבֵיד בָּעוּתִי:


As a swallow chirps which is caught, so I chirped; and I moan like a dove: I lifted up mine eyes, that refreshing to might come to me from (before) Him whose Shekinah is in the highest heavens: O Lord, hear my prayer; grant my petition.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:15)


מָה אֲמַלֵיל תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא וְאֵימַר קֳדָמוֹהִי וְהוּא אַסְגֵי טַבְוָן לְמֶעְבַּד עִמִי מָה אֶפְלַח וַאֲשַׁלֵים קֳדָמוֹהִי כָּל שְׁנַיָא דְאוֹסִיף עַל חַיַי וְשֵׁיזֵיב מִמְרִיר נַפְשִׁי:


What praise shall I utter and speak unto Him? For He hath multiplied His kindness towards me. With what shall I serve Him, and what shall I render unto Him for all the years He hath added to my life, and delivered my soul from bitterness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:16)


יְיָ עַל כָּל מֵיתַיָא אֲמַרְתָּא לְאַחָיָאָה וּקְדָם כּוּלְהוֹן אַחְיֵיתָא רוּחִי וְאַחְיֵיתָנִי וְקַיֵימְתָּנִי:


O Lord, Thou hast said concerning all the dead, that Thou wilt quicken them, and Thou hast quickened my spirit before any of them: Thou hast quickened, Thou hast made me to live.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:17)


הָא לְעָבְדֵי אוֹרַיְתָא סַגִי שְׁלָמָא קָדָמָךְ וְאַתְּ מַיְיתֵי מְרִירָא לְרַשִׁיעַיָא בְּכֵן כַּד יְדָעִית יוֹם מוֹתִי שְׁפָכִית דִמְעֲתִי בִּצְלוֹ קֳדָמָךְ מַר לִי סַגִי וְאַתְּ אִתְרְעֵיתָא בְּחַיֵי בְּדִיל דְלָא לְחַבָּלָא נַפְשִׁי אֲרֵי אַרְחֵיקְתָּא מִן קֳדָמָךְ כָּל חֲטָאָי:


Behold, to them that obey the law, peace shall be multiplied before Thee; but Thou wilt bring bitterness to the wicked: therefore, when I knew the day of my death, I poured out my tears in prayer before Thee; my bitterness was great, but Thou hast had pleasure in my life, not to destroy it; for Thou hast cast all my sins far away from Thy presence.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:18)


אֲרֵי לָא דְבִשְׁאוֹל מוֹדַן קֳדָמָךְ מֵיתַיָא לָא מְשַׁבְּחִין לָךְ לָא מְסַבְּרִין נַחְתֵי גוּב בֵּית אָבְדָנָא לְפוּרְקָנָךְ:


For they that are in the grave do not praise Thee; neither do the dead celebrate Thee, nor do those that descend into the pit of the place of Abaddon hope for Thy salvation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:19)


דְחַי חַי יוֹדֵי קֳדָמָךְ כְּוָתִי יוֹמָא הָדֵין אֲבָהָן לִבְנֵיהוֹן יְחַווּן גְבוּרְתֵּיךְ וְיוֹדוּן לְמֵימָר דְכָל אִילֵין קְשׁוֹט:


The living, the living shall praise Thee, as I do this day: the fathers unto their children shall make known Thy might, and They shall confess, saying, that all these things are truth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:20)


יְיָ לְמִפְרְקָנָא אֲמַר יְיָ וְנִגוּן תּוּשְׁבַּחְתֵּהּ נְנַגֵן כָּל יְמֵי חַיָנָא עַל בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא דַיָי:


The Lord has promised to save us: and we will sing the song of His praise all the days of our life in the house of the sanctuary of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:21)


וַאֲמַר יְשַׁעְיָהוּ יִסְבוּן דְבֵילַת תֵּינִין וִישׁוֹרְטוּן עַל שִׁיחֲנָא וְאִתַּסֵי:


For Isaiah had said, Let them take a lump of figs, and lay it on the boil, and he shall recover.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 38:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/38:22)


וַאֲמַר חִזְקִיָה מָה אָת אֲרֵי אֶסַק לְבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא דַיָי:


And Hezekiah said, What is the sign that I shall go up to the house of the sanctuary of the Lord?



## Chapter 39

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:1)


בְּעִידָנָא הַהִיא שְׁלַח מְרוֹדַךְ בַּלְאֲדָן בַּר בַּלְאֲדָן מַלְכָּא דְבָבֶל אִיגְרִין וְקוּרְבְּנִין לְוַת חִזְקִיָה כַּד שְׁמַע אֲרֵי מְרַע וְאִתַּסֵי:


At that time Merodach-Baladan, the son of Baladan, king of Babylon, sent letters and presents to Hezekiah, when he had heard that he had been sick, and was recovered.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:2)


וַחֲדֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן חִזְקִיָה וְאַחְזִנוּן יַת בֵּית גִנְזוֹהִי יַת כַּסְפָּא וְיַת דַהֲבָא וְיַת בּוּסְמָנָא וְיַת מִשְׁחָא טָבָא וְיַת כָּל בֵּית מְנוֹהִי וְיַת כָּל דְאִישְׁתַּכַח בְּגִנְזוֹהִי לָא הֲוָה מִדַעַם דְלָא אַחֲזִינוּן חִזְקִיָהוּ בְּבֵיתֵהּ וּבְכָל שׁוּלְטָנֵהּ:


And Hezekiah was glad of them, and showed them the house of his treasures, the silver, and the gold, and the spices, and the precious ointment, and all the house of his vessels, and everything that was found in his treasury: and there was not anything in his house, nor in all his dominion, that Hezekiah showed them not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:3)


וַאֲתָא יְשַׁעְיָה נְבִיָא לְוַת מַלְכָּא חִזְקִיָה וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ מָה אֲמָרוּ גַבְרַיָא הָאִלֵן וּמְנַן אֲתוֹ לְוָתָךְ וַאֲמַר חִזְקִיָה מֵאַרְעָא רְחִיקָא אֲתוֹ לְוָתִי מִבָּבֶל:


Then came Isaiah the prophet unto king Hezekiah, and said unto him, What said these men? and from whence came they unto thee? And Hezekiah said, They are come from a far country unto me, even from Babylon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:4)


וַאֲמַר מָה חֲזוֹ בְּבֵיתָךְ וַאֲמַר חִזְקִיָה יַת כָּל דִי בְּבֵיתִי חֲזוֹ לָא הֲוָה מִדָעַם דְלָא אַחְזִיתִינוּן בְּגִנְזָי:


Then said he, What have they seen in thine house? And Hezekiah answered, All that is in mine house have they seen: there is not anything in my treasures that I have not showed them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:5)


וַאֲמַר יְשַׁעְיָה לְחִזְקִיָה קַבֵּיל פִּתְגָמָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת:


Then said Isaiah to Hezekiah, Hear the word of the Lord of hosts:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 39:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:6)


הָא יוֹמַיָא אָתָן וְיִתְנַטֵיל כָּל דִי בְּבֵיתָךְ וְדִי גְנָזוּ אֲבָהָתָךְ עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין וְיִתּוֹבַל לְבָבֶל לָא יִשְׁתָּאַר מִדַעַם אֲמַר יְיָ:


Behold the days come, that all that is in thy house shall be taken away, and that which thy fathers have treasured up unto this day, yea, it shall be carried to Babylon, nothing shall be left, saith the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 39:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:7)


וּמִבְּנָךְ דְיִפְקוּן מִנָךְ דְתוֹלִיד יַדְבְּרוּן וִיהוֹן רַבְרְבִין בְּהֵיכָלָא מַלְכָּא דְבָבֶל:


And thy sons which shall come forth from thee shall be in the palace of the king of Babylon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 39:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/39:8)


וַאֲמַר חִזְקִיָה לִישַׁעְיָה תַּקִין פִּתְגָמָא דַייָ דְמַלֵילְתָּא וַאֲמַר אֲרֵי יְהֵי שְׁלַם וּקְשׁוֹט בְּיוֹמָי:


Then said Hezekiah to Isaiah, Right is the word of the Lord which thou hast spoken: and he said, for there shall be peace and truth in my days.



## Chapter 40

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:1)


נְבִיַיָא אִתְנַבִּיאוּ תַנְחוּמִין עַל עַמִי אֲמַר אֱלָהָכוֹן:


O ye prophets, prophesy comforts to my people, saith your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:2)


מַלִילוּ עַל לִבָּא דִירוּשְׁלֵם וְאִתְנַבּוּ עֲלָהּ אֲרֵי עֲתִידָא דְתִתְמְלֵי מֵעַם גַלְוָתָהָא אֲרֵי אִישְׁתְּבִיקוּ לָהּ חוֹבָהָא אֲרֵי קַבִּילַת כָּס תַּנְחוּמִין מִן קֳדָם יְיָ כְּאִלוּ לָקַת עַל חַד תְּרֵין בְּכָל חַטָאתָהָא:


Speak ye comfortably, to Jerusalem, and prophesy concerning her, that she shall be filled with the people of the captivity, because her sins are forgiven, for she hath received the cup of consolations from the Lord, as if she had been smitten twice for all her sins.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:3)


קַל דְמַכְלֵי בְּמַדְבְּרָא פַּנוּ אוֹרַח מִן קֳדָם עַמָא דַייָ כְּבִישָׁא בְּמֵישְׁרָא כִּבְשִׁין קֳדָם כְּנִשְׁתָּא דֶאֱלָהָנָא:


A voice of one crying in the wilderness prepare ye the way before the people of the Lord; cast up a highway in the plain before the congregation of our God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:4)
