כָּל חֵילַיָא יְרָאמוּן וְכָל טוּר וְרָמָא יִמְאֲכוּן וִיהֵי כִפְלָא לְמֵישְׁרָא וּבֵית גִדוּדִין לְבִקְעָה:


All the valleys shall be exalted, and every mountain and hill shall be made low, and the rugged way shall be a level, and the hilly ground a valley.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:5)


וְיִתְגְלֵי יְקָרָא דַייָ וְיֶחֱזוּן כָּל בְּנֵי בִשְׂרָא כַּחֲדָא אֲרֵי בְמֵימְרָא דַייָ גְזַר כֵּן:


And the glory of the Lord shall be revealed, and all the sons of flesh shall see together, that by the WORD of the Lord it has been decreed thus.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:6)


קַל דַאֲמַר אִתְנַבֵּי וּמְתֵיב וַאֲמַר מָה אִתְנַבֵּי כָּל רַשִׁיעַיָא כְּעִשְׂבָּא וְכָל תּוּקְפְהוֹן כְּמוֹצָא דְחַקְלָא:


The voice of one crying, Prophesy! He answered and said, What shall I prophesy? All the wicked are as grass, and all their strength as chaff of the field.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:7)


יָבֵשׁ עִשְׂבָּא נְתַר נִצֵיהּ אֲרֵי רוּחָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ נְשָׁבַת בֵּיהּ בְּכֵן כְּעִשְׂבָּא חֲשִׁיבִין רַשִׁיעַיָא בְּעַמָא:


The grass withereth, its flower fadeth: because the spirit of the Lord bloweth upon it: surely the wicked among a people are considered like grass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:8)


מִית רַשִׁיעַיָא אֲבָדוּ עֶשְׁתּוֹנוֹהִי וּפִּתְגָמָא דְאֶלָהָנָא קַיָם לְעָלְמִין:


The wicked dieth, and his thoughts perish; but the word of our God shall stand for ever.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:9)


עַל טוּר רָם סַקוּ לְכוֹן נְבִיַיָא דִי מְבַשְׂרִין לְצִיוֹן אֲרִימוּ בְחֵילָא קָלְכוֹן דִי מְבַשְׂרִין לִירוּשְׁלֵם אֲרִימוּ לָא תִדְחֲלוּן אֲמָרוּ לְקִרְוַיָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה אִתְגְלִיאַת מַלְכוּתָא דֶאֱלָהָכוֹן:


Upon a high mountain get up, ye prophets, that bring good tidings unto Zion; lift ye up your voice with strength that bring good tidings to Jerusalem; lift it up, be not afraid, say to the cities of the house of Judah, The kingdom of your God is revealed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:10)


הָא יְיָ אֱלֹהִים בִּתְקוֹף מִתְגְלֵי וּתְקוֹף דְרַע גְבוּרְתֵּהּ שַׁלְטָא קֳדָמוֹהִי הָא אֲגַר עָבְדֵי מֵימְרֵהּ עִמֵהּ דְכָל עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן גְלַן קֳדָמוֹהִי:


Behold, the Lord God shall be revealed, and the strength of the arm of His might shall rule before Him: behold, the reward of those who perform His word is with Him, because their works are manifest to Him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:11)


כְּרָעַיָא דְעֶדְרֵהּ רָעֵי בְּדַרְעֵהּ מַכְנַשׁ אִמְרִין וּבְחֵינֵהּ מְסוֹבַר רַכִּיכִין מֵינִיקָתָא בְּנִיחַ מְדַבָּר:


He shall feed those that are of His flock like a shepherd: He shall gather the lambs with His arm, and He shall carry the tender ones in His bosom; He shall lead gently those that give suck.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:12)


מַן אֲמַר אִלֵין קַיָם אֲמַר וַעֲבֵד דְכָל מֵי עַלְמָא חֲשִׁיבִין קֳדָמוֹהִי כְּטִפָּא בִשְׁעוֹלָא וּמִשְׁחַת שְׁמַיָא כְּאִלוּ בְּזֵירְתָא מְתַקְנִין וְעַפְרָא דְאַרְעָא כְּאִלוּ בִּמְכִילָא אִתְכַּל וְטוּרַיָא כְּאִלוּ מַתְקַל תַּקִילִין וְרָמָתָא כַּד בְּמוֹזְנַיָא:


He who hath promised these things, shall confirm them: He hath spoken it, and He will do it; He in whose sight all the waters of the world are accounted as a drop in the hollow of the hand, and the expansion of the heavens as if they had been prepared with the span; and the dust of the earth as if measured in a measure, and the mountains as if weighed in scales, and the hills in a balance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:13)


מַן תַּקִין יַת רוּחַ קוּדְשָׁא בְּפוּם כָּל נְבִיַיָא הֲלָא יְיָ וְצַדִיקַיָא עָבְדֵי מֵימְרֵהּ פִּתְגָמֵי רְעוּתֵהּ הוֹדְעִינוּן:


Who hath directed the Holy Spirit in the mouth of all the prophets? Is it not the Lord? He maketh known the words of His will to the righteous, the servants of His WORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:14)


לְהוֹן דִבְעוֹן מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי אֲסַבַּר חוּכְמָא וְאַלְפִינוּן אוֹרַח דְדִין וִיהַב לִבְנֵיהוֹן אוֹרַיְתָא וְאוֹרַח דְסוּכְלְתָנוּ לִבְנֵי בְנֵיהוֹן הוֹדְעִינוּן:


I will make known wisdom to them who pray for it from Him, and I will teach them the way of justice; yea, He will give the law to their sons, and He will make known to their sons' sons the path of understanding.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:15)


הָא עַמְמַיָא כְּטִפָּא מִדוּל וּכְעִיוּל מוֹזְנַיָא חֲשִׁיבִין הָא נַגְוָתָא כְּדוֹקָא דְפָרַח:


Behold, the nations are as a drop of a bucket, and are counted as the small dust of the balance: behold, the isles are as fine dust that flieth away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:16)


וְאִילָנֵי לִבְנָן לָא יְסַפְקוּן לְאַדְלָקָא וְחֵיוָתָא דְבָהּ לֵית הִיא כְּמִיסַת לַעֲלָתָא:


And the trees of Lebanon are not sufficient for fire, nor the beasts in it sufficient for a holocaust.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:17)


כָּל עַמְמַיָא כִּלְמָא עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן גְמִירָא וְשֵׁיצָאָה אִינוּן חֲשִׁיבִין קֳדָמוֹהִי:


All nations are as nothing, their works are accounted before Him as naught.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:18)


וּלְמַן אַתּוּן מְדַמִין קֳדָם אֵל וּמַה דְמוּת אַתּוּן מְסַדְרִין קֳדָמוֹהִי:


And whom do you think able to contend with God? and what is the likeness that ye can compare with Him?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:19)


הָא צִלְמָא נַגְרָא עֲבֵיד וְקֵינָאָה בִּדְהַב מְחַפֵּי לֵיהּ וְשַׁלְשְׁלַן דִכְסַף קֵינָאָה מְאָחֵיד לֵיהּ:


Behold, the artificer maketh an image, and the goldsmith overlayeth it with gold, and with silver chains the silversmith fasteneth it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:20)


אוֹרָן בָּרֵי אָעָא דְלָא אָחִיד בֵּיהּ רִקְבָא בָּחַר נַגַר אוּמַן בְּעֵי לֵיהּ לְאַתְקָנָא צְלַם דְלָא יִצְטְלֵי:


He cuts a wild ash of the forest, which rottenness will seize; he procures a skilful artist to fix the image, that it be not moved.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:21)


הֲלָא יְדַעְתּוּן הֲלָא שְׁמַעְתּוּן הֲלָא אִיתְחַוָאָה מִן אַוְלָא עוֹבַד סִדְרֵי בְרֵאשִׁית לְכוֹן הֲלָא תִּסְתַּכְּלוּן לְמִדְחַל קֳדָם מִן דִבְרָא יְסוֹדֵי אַרְעָא:


Have ye not known? have ye not heard? hath not the work in the creation been declared unto you in its order from the beginning? Have ye not understood that ye ought to fear Him, who hath created the foundations of the earth?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:22)


דְאַשְׁרֵי בִּתְקוֹף רוּמָא שְׁכִינַת יְקָרֵהּ וְכָל יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא חֲשִׁיבִין קֳדָמוֹהִי כְּקַמְצִין דְמָתַח כִּזְעֵיר שְׁמַיָא וּפַרְסִינוּן כְּמַשְׁכַּן יְקָרָא לְבֵית שְׁכִינְתָּא:


Who maketh the Shekinah of His glory to dwell in exalted strength, and all the inhabitants of the earth are in His estimation as locusts; He that stretched out the heavens as a little thing, and spreadeth them out as the tent of the glory of the house of the Shekinah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:23)


דִמְסַר שִׁלְטוֹנִין לְחוּלְשָׁא דַיָנֵי אַרְעָא כִּלְמָא עֲבֵיד:


Who gives princes over to weakness; the judges of the earth He bringeth to naught.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:24)


אֲפִילוּ יִפְשׁוּן אֲפִילוּ יִסְגוּן אֲפִילוּ יִתְרַבּוּן בְּאַרְעָא בְנֵיהוֹן וְאַף רוּגְזֵהּ יְשַׁלַח בְּהוֹן וְיִבְהֲתוּן וּמֵימְרֵהּ כְּעַלְעֵילָא לְקַשָׁא יְדַבֵּר יַתְהוֹן:


Although they multiply, although they increase, although their children become great in the earth, yet, nevertheless, He will send His wrath among them, and they shall be confounded: and His WORD shall scatter them, as the whirlwind the chaff.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:25)


וּלְמַן תְּדַמוּן קֳדָמַי וּתְשַׁווֹן יֵימַר קַדִישָׁא:


To whom, then, will ye liken me? and to whom will ye equal me? saith the Holy One.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:26)


זְקוּפוּ לְרוּמָא עֵינֵיכוֹן וַחֲזוּ לְמִדְחַל קֳדָם מַן דִבְרָא אִלֵין דְאַפֵּיק בְּמִנְיָן חֵילֵי שְׁמַיָא לְכוּלְהוֹן בְּשִׁמְהָן קָרֵי מַסְגֵי גְבוּרָן וּתְקוֹף חֵיל חַד מִסִדְרֵהּ לָא מִתְעַכָּב:


Lift up your eyes on high, and behold, that ye may fear Him, who hath created these things, who bringeth forth the host of the heavens by number: He calleth them all by their name: on account of the combination of forces and might of power, not one is hindered in its orb.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:27)


לְמָה תֵימַר יַעֲקֹב וּתְמַלֵיל יִשְׂרָאֵל מְטַמְרָא אוֹרְחִי מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּמִן קֳדָם אֱלָהִי דִינִי יְעָדֵי:


Why sayest thou, O Jacob, and speakest, O Israel, My way is hid from the Lord, and my cause is removed from my God?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:28)


הֲלָא יְדַעְתָּא אִם לָא שְׁמַעְתָּא אֱלָהָא עַלְמָא יְיָ דִי בְרָא יְסוֹדֵי אַרְעָא לָא בַעֲמַל וְלָא בְלֵיאוּ לֵית סוֹף לְסוּכְלְתָנוּתֵיהּ:


Hast thou not known? yea, hast thou not heard that the everlasting God, the Lord who hath created the foundations of the earth, is not fatigued, neither is weary? there is no end to His wisdom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:29)


דִיהַב לְצַדִיקַיָא דִמְשַׁלְהַן לְפִתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא חוּכְמָא וּדְלֵית לְהוֹן תְּקוֹף נִכְסִין מַסְגֵי:


Who giveth wisdom to the righteous that long for the words of the law; and to those who have no strength, he multiplieth strength.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:30)


וְיִשְׁתַּלְהוּן עוּלֵימִין חַיָבִין וְיִלְאוּן וּרְוָקֵי רַשִׁיעַיָא אִיתְּקָלָא יִתְקְלוּן:


And the wicked youths shall be fatigued and shall be weary; and the impious young men shall utterly fall.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 40:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/40:31)


וּדְסַבָּרוּ לְפוּרְקָנָא דַייָ יִתְכַּנְשׁוּן מִבֵּינֵי גָלוּתְהוֹן וְיוֹסְפוּן חֵיל וְיִתְחַדְתּוּן לְעוּלֵימַתְהוֹן כְּצִימוּחַ דְסָלֵיק עַל גַדְפֵי נִשְׁרִין יְרַהֲטוּן וְלָא יִלְאוּן יַהֲכוּן וְלָא וְלָא יִשְׁתַּלְהוּן:


But they that hope for the salvation of the Lord shall be gathered together from the midst of their captivity, and shall increase their strength, and their youth shall be renewed like the sprout that springeth up: they shall hasten upon the wings of eagles, and not be weary; they shall walk, and not faint.



## Chapter 41

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:1)


אֲצִיתוּ לְמֵימְרִי נַגְוָן וּמַלְכְּוָן יוֹסְפוּן חֵיל יִתְקָרְבוּן בְּכֵן יְמַלְלוּן כַּחֲדָא לְדִינָא נִתְקְרָב:


Listen unto my word, ye islands, and let the kingdoms increase their strength: let them come nigh, then let them speak; let us approach one another for judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:2)


מַן אַיְתֵי בִגְלַאי מִמַדִינְחָא אַבְרָהָם בְּחִיר צַדִיקַיָא בִּקְשׁוֹט קַרְבֵהּ לְאַתְרֵהּ מְסַר קֳדָמוֹהִי עַמְמִין וּמַלְכִין תַּקִיפִין תְּבַר רְמָא כְּעַפְרָא קָטוֹלִין קֳדָם חַרְבֵּהּ כְּקַשָׁא רְדוֹפִין קֳדָם קַשְׁתֵּהּ:


Who openly brought Abraham from the east? He brought the chosen of the righteous in truth to his place, He delivered up nations before him, and broke in pieces mighty kings, he cast the slain down like the dust before his sword, and he pursued them? like stubble before his bow.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:3)


רְדָפִינוּן עֲדָא שְׁלָם חַיָלַת אוֹרַח בְּרִגְלוֹהִי לָא עֲלָת:


He pursued them, and passed safely by; the roughness of the path shall not affect his feet.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:4)


מַן אֲמַר אִלֵין קַיָם אֲמַר וַעֲבִיד וְסִדַר דָרַיָא מִלְקָדְמִין אֲנָא יְיָ בָּרֵית עַלְמָא מִבְּרֵאשִׁית אַף עַלְמֵי עָלְמַיָא דִילִי אִינוּן בַּר מִנִי לֵית אֱלָהָא:


Who hath promised these things and established them? Who hath spoken, and it was done? And who hath ranged the generations in their order from the beginning? I, the Lord, I created the world from the beginning; yea, ages after ages are mine, and besides me there is no God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:5)


חֲזוֹ נַגְוָתָא וִידַחֲלוּן דְבִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא יְזוּעוּן יִתְקַרְבוּן וְיֵיתוּן:


The isles shall see and be afraid; they that are at the ends of the earth shall tremble; they shall draw nigh, they shall come.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:6)


גְבַר יַת חַבְרֵהּ יִסְעֲדוּן וְלַאֲחוֹהִי יֵימַר תַּקִיף:


Let every one help his neighbour, and every one say to his brother, Be of good courage.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:7)


הֲלָא יְבַהֲתוּן בְּעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן דְמַתְקֵיף נַגָרָא עִם קֵינָאָה מָחֵי בְּפַטִישָׁא עִם דִמְטַפַּח בְקוּרְנְסָא זִמְנָא אֲמַר עַל דִבְקָא תַּקִין הוּא וּמַתְקֵיף לֵיהּ בְמַסְמְרִין דְלָא יִצְטְלֵי:


Shall they not be confounded in their works? Because the artificer encourageth the silversmith, and he that smiteth with the great hammer him that smiteth with the small: It is time, one saith, for the soldering, it is ready; he fasteneth it with nails, that it may not be moved.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:8)


וְאַתְּ יִשְׂרָאֵל עַבְדִי יַעֲקֹב דְאִתְרְעֵיתִי בָּךְ זַרְעֵהּ דְאַבְרָהָם רְחִימִי:


But thou, Israel, my servant, Jacob, in whom I delight, the seed of Abraham my friend:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:9)


דְקָרֵיבְתָּךְ מִזַרְעִית אַרְעָא וּמִמַלְכְּוָתָא בְּחַרְתָּךְ וַאֲמָרֵית לָךְ עַבְדִי אַתְּ אִתְרְעֵיתִי בָּךְ וְלָא אַרְחֵקִינָךְ:


I have brought thee out of the families of the earth, I have chosen thee out of a kingdom: and I have said unto thee, Thou art my servant; I delight in thee, and I will not thrust thee away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:10)


לָא תִדְחַל אֲרֵי בְסַעֲדָךְ מֵימְרִי לָא תִתְּבַר אֲרֵי אֲנָא אֱלָהָךְ אִתְקְפִינָךְ אַף אֶסְעַדִינָךְ אַף אַחֲדִינָךְ בִּימִין קוּשְׁטִי:


Fear not, for my WORD shall be thy support; be not dismayed, for I am thy God: I will strengthen thee; yea, I will help thee; yea, I will uphold thee with the right hand of my truth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:11)


הָא יִבָּהֲתוּן וְיִתְכַּנְעוּן כָּל עַמְמַיָא דַהֲווֹ מִתְגָרִין בָּךְ יְהוֹן כִּלְמָא וְיֵבְדוּן אֱנָשֵׁי דִינָךְ:


Behold, all the nations that are incensed against thee shall be ashamed and confounded: they shall be as nothing; and they that strive with thee shall perish.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:12)


תַּבְעִינוּן וְלָא תַשְׁכְּחִינוּן לְאַנְשֵׁי מַצוּתָךְ יְהוֹן כִּלְמָא וּכְלָא מִדָעַם גַבְרִין דַהֲווֹ מִתְגָרִין לְמֶעְבַּד עִמָךְ קְרָב:


Thou shalt seek them, and shalt not find them, even them that contended with thee, they shall be as nothing; the men that stirred themselves up to make war with thee shall be less than nothing.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:13)


אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מַתְקֵיף יְמִינָךְ דַאֲמָרִית לָךְ לָא תִדְחַל מֵימְרִי בְסַעֲדָךְ:


For I am the Lord thy God, that will hold thee fast by thy right hand; who saith unto thee, Fear not, my WORD shall be thy help.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:14)


לָא תִדַחְלוּן שִׁבְטַיָא דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב זַרְעִיתָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל מֵימְרִי בְסַעֲדְכוֹן אֲמַר יְיָ וּפָרִיקְכוֹן קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Fear ye not, ye tribes of the house of Jacob, ye seed of Israel, my WORD shall be your help, saith the Lord, and your Redeemer, the Holy One of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:15)


הָא שַׁוִיתֵיכוֹן לְמוֹרַג תַּקִיף חֲדַת מָלֵי סִמְפּוֹרִין תְּקָטֵיל פַּלְחֵי כּוֹכְבַיָא וּתְשֵׁיצֵי וּמַלְכוּתָא כְּמוֹצָא תְשַׁוֵי:


Behold, I will make you a mighty threshing instrument full of sharp edges: thou shalt slay the nations, thou shalt consume the kingdoms, thou shalt make them like chaff.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:16)


תְּדִרִינוּן וְרוּחָא תִטְלוּנוּן וּמֵימְרֵהּ כְּעַלְעוֹלָא לְקַשָׁא יְבַדֵר יַתְהוֹן וְאַתְּ תְּבוּעַ בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ בְּקַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל תִּשְׁתַּבַּח:


Thou shalt fan them, and the wind shall carry them away, and my WORD shall scatter them as the whirlwind scattereth the chaff; but as for thee, thou shalt rejoice in the Word of the Lord, thou shalt glory in the Holy One of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:17)


עִנְוְתָנַיָא וַחֲשִׁיכַיָא דִמְחַמְדִין לְאוּלְפָנָא הָא כְּצַחְיָא לְמַיָא וְלָא מְשַׁכְּחִין רוּחֵיהוֹן בְּסִגוּפָא מְשַׁלְהַיָא אֲנָא יְיָ אֲקַבֵּיל צְלוֹתְהוֹן אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל לָא אֲרַחֵיקִנוּן:


The poor and the needy long after instruction, as the thirsty after water, but do not find it; their spirit faints in affliction. I, the Lord, will hear their prayer; I, the God of Israel, will not cast them off.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:18)


אֲקָרֵב גָלוּתְהוֹן מִבֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא וְאֶדְבְּרִינוּן בְּאוֹרַח תַקָנָא וְאֶפְתַּח לְהוֹן עַל נַגְדִין נַהֲרִין וּבְגוֹ מַשִׁרְיָן מַבּוּעִין אֲשַׁוֵי מַדְבְּרָא לַאֲגַמִין דְמַיָן וַאֲרַע בֵּית צַחְוָנָא לְמַבּוּעֵי מַיָא:


I will gather their captives from the midst of the nations, and I will lead them in the right path; and I will open for them rivers in torrentbeds, and fountains in the midst of the valleys: I will make the desert pools of water, and the land that is a thirsty place springs of water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:19)


אֶתֵּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא אַרְזִין שִׁיטִין וַהֲדַסִין וְאָעִין דִמְשַׁח אֱרַבֵּי בְמֵישְׁרָא בּוּרְוָן מוּרְנְיָן וְאֶשְׁכְּרוֹעִין כַּחֲדָא:


I will put cedars, acacias, and myrtles, and oil trees in the wilderness; I will make the fir tree, the elm, and the box tree to grow in the desert together:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:20)


בְּדִיל דְיֶחֱזוּן וְיֵדְעוּן וִישַׁווּן דְחַלְתִּי עַל לִבְּהוֹן וְיִסְתַּכְּלוּן כַּחֲדָא אֲרֵי גְבוּרְתָא דַייָ עֲבָדַת דָא וְקַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּרָא יָתָהּ:


In order that they may see, and know, and put my fear in their heart, and may consider together, that the might of the Lord hath done this, and the Holy One of Israel hath created it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:21)


קְרִיבוּ דִינְכוֹן אֲמַר יְיָ אָעִילוּ חֶזְוַתְכוֹן אֲמַר מַלְכֵּיהּ דְיַעֲקֹב:


Produce your cause, saith the Lord, bring forth your arguments, saith the King of Jacob.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:22)


יִתְקָרְבוּן וִיחַווּן לָנָא יַת דְעַרְעִינָנָא קַדְמֵיתָא מַה אִינוּן הֲווֹ וּנְשַׁוֵי לִבְּנָא וְנֵדָע סוֹפְהוֹן אוֹ דַעֲתִידִין לְמֵיתֵי בַּסְרוּנָא:


Let them approach, and show us what shall happen unto us; Let them declare the former things what they were, that we may consider and know their event: or declare unto us the things that shall come.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:23)


חֲווֹ דְאַתְיָן לְסוֹפָא וְנִדַע אִם לְטַעֲוָן דְאִית בְּהוֹן צְרוֹךְ אַתּוּן פָּלְחִין אִם יָכְלִין אִינוּן לְאֵיטָבָא וּלְאַבְאָשָׁא וְנִסְתַּכֵּיל וְנֵידוֹן כַּחֲדָא:


Declare the things that are to come hereafter, that we may know whether ye worship idols in which there is any profit, whether they are able to do good, or to do evil; that we may consider and argue together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:24)


הָא אַתּוּן לְמָא וְעוֹבָדֵיכוֹן לָא מִדָעַם תּוֹעֵיבָא דְאַתְרְעִיתוּן בֵּיהּ לְכוֹן:


Behold, ye are nothing, and your works are to no purpose; that in which ye delight is an abomination.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:25)


אַיְתֵיתֵי בִּגְלַאִי מְלַךְ דְתַקִיף מֵרוּחַ צִפּוּנָא וְיֵיתֵי כְּמִפַּק שִׁמְשָׁא בִּגְבוּרְתֵּהּ מִמַדִנְחָא אַגְבְּרִינֵהּ בִּשְׁמִי וְיֵיתִי וְיֵדוּשׁ שִׁלְטוֹנֵי עַמְמַיָא כְּמָא דְדָיְשִׁין יַת עַפְרָא וּכְפַחְרָא דְעָרִיךְ יַת טִינָא:


I will certainly bring a king who is mightier than the north wind, and he shall go forth as the going forth of the sun in might from the east: I will strengthen him by my name, and he shall come, and tread the rulers of the nations under foot, as those who tread the dust under foot, as the potter who prepares the clay.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:26)


מַן חַוֵי מִן אַוְלָא וְנֵדַע וּמִלְקָדְמִין וְנֵימַר קְשׁוֹט אַף לֵית דִי מְחַוֵי אַף לֵית דִמְבַסֵר אַף לֵית דְשָׁמַע לְמִילֵיכוֹן:


Who hath declared this from the beginning that we should know it? and beforetime, that we may say, It is true? Yea, there was none that foretold it; yea, none that declared it; yea, none that heard your words.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:27)


פִּתְגָמֵי נֶחְמָתָא דְאִתְנַבִּיאוּ נְבִיָיא מִלְקָדְמִין עַל צִיוֹן הָא אֲתוֹ וְלִירוּשְׁלֵם מְבַסֵר אֶתֵּן:


The words of consolation which the prophets prophesied from of old concerning Zion, behold, they shall come to pass! and unto Jerusalem I will give one that bringeth good tidings.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:28)


וּגְלֵי קֳדְמַי וְלֵית גְבַר דְלֵיהּ עוֹבָדִין טָבִין וּמֵאִלֵין לֵית דְמָלִיךְ מֵילַךְ וּשְׁאִילְתִּינוּן אִם יְתוּבוּן פִּתְגָם:


And it was made known to me, that there was no man whose works were good, and of these there was none who would take counsel, that I should ask them, and they would answer a word.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 41:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/41:29)


הָא כוּלְהוֹן לְמָא וְלָא מִדָעַם עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן בִּזָא וּתְבִרָא עֶשְׁתּוֹנֵיהוֹן:


Behold, all of them are nothing, and their work less than nothing: their thoughts are plunder and destruction.



## Chapter 42

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:1)


הָא עַבְדִי מְשִׁיחָא אֶקְרְבִינֵהּ בְּחִירִי דְאִתְרְעֵי בֵּיהּ מֵימְרִי אֶתֵּן רוּחָא דְקוּדְשִׁי עֲלוֹהִי דִינִין לְעַמְמִין יְגַלֵי:


Behold, my servant, the Messiah, whom I bring, my chosen in whom one delights: as for my Word, I will put my Holy Spirit upon him; he shall reveal my judgment unto the nations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:2)


לָא יִצְוַח וְלָא יַכְלֵי וְלָא יְרִים לְבָרָא קָלֵהּ:


He shall not cry aloud, nor raise a clamour, and he shall not lift up his voice in the street.”


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:3)


עִנְוְתָנַיָא דְאִינוּן דָמָן לְקַנְיָא רְעִיעַ לָא יִתַּבְּרוּן וַחֲשִׁיכַיָא דִכְבוּצִין עַמִי לָא יִטְפוּן לְקוּשְׁטָא יַפֵיק דִינָא:


The meek who are like a bruised reed He shall not break,; and the poor who are as a glimmering wick with him, he will not quench: he shall bring forth judgment unto truth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:4)


לָא יַלְהֵי וְלָא יִלְאֵי עַד דְיַתְקֵן בְּאַרְעָא דֵינָא וּלְאוֹרַיְתֵהּ נַגְוָן יְכַתְּרוּן:


He shall not faint nor be weary, till he have established judgment in the earth; and the isles shall wait for his law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:5)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלָהּ עַלְמָא יְיָ דִי בְרָא שְׁמַיָא וּתְלָנוּן שַׁכְלִיל אַרְעָא וְדַיְרָהָא יָהֵיב נִשְׁמְתָא לְעַמָא דִי עֲלָהּ וְרוּחַ לְדִמְהַלְכִין בָּהּ:


Thus saith the God of eternity, who hath created the heavens and suspended them, who hath founded the earth, and hath given breath to its inhabitants, even to the people upon it, and spirit to them that walk therein.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:6)


אֲנָא יְיָ רַבִּיתָךְ בִּקְשׁוֹט וְאַתְקְפֵית בִּידָךְ וְאַתְקְנִינָךְ וְאֶתְּנִינָךְ לִקְיַם עַם לְנֵיהוֹר עַמְמִין:


I, the Lord, I will make thee to grow in truth, and will hold thine hand, and I will direct thee, and give thee for a covenant of the people, for a light of the Gentiles;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:7)


לְפַתָּחָא עֵינֵי בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל דְאִינוּן כְּסָמָן מִן אוֹרַיְתָא לְאַפָקָא נַלְוַתְהוֹן מִבֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא דְאִינוּן דָמָן לַאֲסִירִין וּלְמִפְרַקְהוֹן מִשִׁעְבּוּד מַלְכְּוָתָא דְאִינוּן עֲנִינִין כַּאֲסִירֵי קְבָל:


To open the eyes of the house of Israel, who are blind to the law, to bring back their captivity from among the nations, where they are like prisoners, and to redeem them from the servitude of the kingdoms being shut up as those that are bound in darkness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:8)


אֲנָא יְיָ הוּא שְׁמִי וִיקָרִי דְאִתְגְלֵיתִי עֲלֵיכוֹן לְעַם אוֹחֲרָן לָא אֶתֵּן וְתוּשְׁבַּחְתִּי לְפַלְחֵי צַלְמַיָא:


I am the Lord: that is my name: and my glory in which I am revealed to you I will not give to another people; nor my praise to the worshippers of images.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:9)


קַדְמָיָתָא הָא אֲתָאָה וְחַדְתָּן אֲנָא מְחַוֵי עַד לָא אַתְיַן אֶבְסַר יַתְכוֹן:


Behold, the former things are come to pass, and new things do I declare: I apprize you of them before they come to pass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:10)


שַׁבָּחוּ קֳדָם יְיָ תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא חַדְתָּא אֱמָרוּ תּוּשְׁבַּחְתֵּהּ מִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא נַחֲתֵי יַמָא וּמְלָאָהּ נַגְוָן וְיָתְבֵיהוֹן:


Sing unto the Lord a new song, proclaim His praise from the ends of the earth, ye that go down to the sea, and its fulness; the isles, and the inhabitants thereof.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:11)


יְשַׁבַּח מַדְבְּרָא וְקִרְוִין דְיַתְבִין בֵּיהּ פְּצִיחִין יָתְבִין מִדְבַּר עַרְבָאִי יְשַׁבְּחוּן מֵיתַיָא כַּד יִפְקוּן מִבָּתֵּי עַלְמֵיהוֹן מְרֵישֵׁי טוּרַיָא יְרִימוּן קָלְהוֹן:


Let the wilderness praise Him, and the cities that are in it, the villages which inhabit the wilderness of the Arabians; let the dead praise Him, when they go forth from their long abodes; from the tops of the mountains let them lift up their voice.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:12)


יְשַׁווּן קֳדָם יְיָ יְקָרָא וְתוּשְׁבַּחְתֵּהּ בְּנַגְוָן יְחַווּן:


Let them ascribe glory unto the Lord, and declare His praise in the islands.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:13)


יְיָ לְמֶעְבַּד גְבוּרָן אִתְגְלֵי לְמֶעְבַּד גְבוּרָא מִתְגְלֵי בִּרְגַז בְּמִלוּל אַף בְּזִיעַ עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹהִי מִתְגְלֵי בִּגְבוּרְתֵּהּ:


The Lord shall be seen to do mighty things: He shall reveal Himself in anger to do a mighty work by the word of His wrath; He shall reveal Himself to His enemies by His might in an earthquake.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:14)


יְהָבִית לְהוֹן אַרְכָּא מֵעַלְמָא דְאִם יְתוּבוּן לְאוֹרַיְתָא וְלָא תָבוּ כְּחַבְלִין עַל יְלִידְתָּא יִתְגְלֵי דִינִי עֲלֵיהוֹן יִצְדוּן וִיסוּפוּן כַּחֲדָא:


I have given them prolongation for a long time, if they would but return to my law; but they did not return. My judgment shall be revealed upon them, as pains on a woman in travail; they shall be destroyed, and come to an end together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:15)


אַחֲרִיב טוּרִין וְרָמָן וְכָל עִסְבֵּיהוֹן אֲיַבֵּשׁ וַאֲשַׁוֵי נַהֲרִין לְנַגְוָן וַאֲגַמִין אֲיַבֵּשׁ:


I will make waste mountains and hills, and dry up all their herbs; and I will make the rivers islands, and I will dry up the pools.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:16)


וָאֱדַבֵּר לְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל דְדָמִין כְּסָמָן בְּאוֹרַח דְלָא יְדָעוּ בִּשְׁבִילִין דְלָא אֲלִיפוּ אַדַרְכִינוּן אֲשַׁוֵי קְבַל קֳדָמֵיהוֹן לִנְהוֹר וְכַפְלָא לְמֵישְׁרָא אִלֵין פִּתְגָמַיָא אַעְבְדִינוּן וְלָא אַרְחִיקִינוּן:


And I will lead the house of Israel, who are like the blind, in a way which they knew not, in paths they have not learned I will lead them; I will make darkness light before them, and the rugged place a plain. These things will I do for them, and will not forsake them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:17)


יִסְתַּחֲרוּן לַאֲחוֹרָא יִבַּהֲתוּן בַּהֲתָא פַלְחֵי צַלְמַיָא דְאָמְרִין לִצְלַם מַתְּכָא אַתּוּן טַעֲוָתָנָא:


They shall be turned back, they shall be ashamed, that worship images, that say to the molten images, Ye are our gods.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:18)


רַשִׁיעַיָא דְאִינוּן כְּחֵרְשִׁין הֲלָא אוּדְנִין לְכוֹן שְׁמָעוּ וְחַיָבַיָא דְאִינוּן כְּסָמָן הֲלָא עַיְנִין לְכוֹן אִסְתַּכָּלוּ וַחֲזוֹ:


Ye wicked, (who are like the deaf,) have ye no ears? hear ye! and ye sinners, (who are like the blind,) have ye no eyes?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:19)


הֲלָא אִם יְתוּבוּן רַשִׁיעַיָא יִתְקְרוֹן עַבְדֵי וְחַיָבַיָא דִנְבִיֵי שְׁלָחִית עֲלֵיהוֹן אֶלָא רַשִׁיעַיָא עֲתִידִין לְאִשְׁתַּלָמָא פּוּרְעֲנוּת חוֹבֵיהוֹן בְּרַם אִם יְתוּבוּן יִתְקְרוֹן עַבְדַיָא דַיָי:


Is it not so? if the wicked and the sinners, to whom I have sent my prophets, will repent, they shall be called my servants; but the wicked shall be paid with vengeance for their sins; but if they will repent, they shall be called the servants of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:20)


חֲזֵיתוּן סַגִיאָן וְלָא נְטַרְתּוּן אִתְפַּתָּחָא אוּדְנֵיכוּן וְלָא קַבֵּילְתּוּן אוּלְפַן:


Ye see many things, but ye observe not; having your ears opened, but ye receive not instruction.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:21)


יְיָ רָעֵי בְּדִיל לְזַכָּאוּתֵהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְרַבֵּי לְעָבְדֵי אוֹרַיְתֵהּ וְיַתְקֵיף יַתְהוֹן:


The Lord delights in justifying Israel ; He will magnify those that obey His law, yea, He will strengthen them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:22)


וְהוּא עַם בָּזִיז וַאֲנִיס אִתְחַפִיאוּ בַהֲתָא עוּלֵימִין כּוּלְהוֹן וּבְבָתֵּי יְסוּרִין עֲנִינִין הֲווֹ לַעֲדִי וְלֵית דִמְשֵׁיזֵיב לְבִזָא וְלֵית דַאֲמַר אֲתֵיב:


But this is a people robbed, and spoiled, and all their young men are covered with confusion, and shut up in prison-houses: they are for a prey, and there is none who delivereth; for a spoil, and none saith, Restore.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:23)


מַן בְּכוֹן יָצֵית דָא יְקַבֵּל וִיסַבַּר לְסוֹפָא:


Who is there among you that will listen to this? who will hearken, and consider for the time to come?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:24)


מַן מְסַר לַעֲדֵי יַעֲקֹב וְיִשְׂרָאֵל לִבְזוֹזִין הֲלָא יְיָ מִן קֳדָם דְחָבוּ קֳדָמוֹהִי וְלָא אֲבוּ לְמֵיהָךְ בְּאוֹרְחָן דְתַקְנָן קֳדָמוֹהִי וְלָא קַבִּילוּ אוּלְפָן אוֹרַיְתֵהּ:


Who delivered Jacob for a spoil, and Israel to the robbers? Was it not the Lord? Because they have sinned against Him; and they would not walk in His righteous paths before Him, neither would they receive the instruction of His law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 42:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/42:25)


וּשְׁפַךְ עֲלֵיהוֹן חֵימַת רוּגְזֵהּ וּתְקוֹף עָבְדֵי קְרָבֵהּ אַיְתִי עֲלֵיהוֹן וּקְטָלוּ בְהוֹן מִסְחוֹר סְחוֹר וְלָא יְדַעוּ וְשַׁלִיטוּ בְהוֹן וְלָא שַׁוִיאוּ דְחַלְתֵּהּ עַל לִבָּא:


Therefore He hath poured upon them the fury of His anger, and hath brought upon them the strength of His warriors, and hath slain them round about, and they knew it not; and they ruled over them, and they did not lay His fear to heart.



## Chapter 43

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:1)


וּכְעַן כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ דִבְרָאָךְ יַעֲקֹב וּדְאַתְקְנָךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לָא תִדְחַל אֲרֵי פְרִיקְתָּךְ רַבִּיתָךְ בִּשְׁמָךְ דִילִי אַתְּ:


But now thus saith the Lord that created thee, O Jacob, and He that formed thee, O Israel, Fear not, for I have redeemed thee, I have called thee who art mine by thy name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:2)


אֲרֵי בְקַדְמֵיתָא כַּד עֲבַרְתּוּן בְיַמָא דְסוּף מֵימְרִי הֲוָה בְּסַעְדְכוֹן פַּרְעֹה וּמִצְרָאֵי דְסַגִיאִין כְּמֵי נַהֲרָא לָא יְכִילוּ לְכוֹן וְאַף בְּתִנְיָנֵיתָא כַּד תַּהֲכוּן לְבֵינֵי עַמְמַיָא דְתַקִיפוּן כְּאֶשְׁתָּא לָא יִכְלוּן לְכוֹן וּמַלְכְּוָן דַחֲסִינָן כְּשַׁלְהוֹבִיתָא לָא יְשֵׁיצוּן יַתְכוֹן:


For at first when ye did pass the Red Sea my Word was your support. Pharaoh and the Egyptians, who were as many as the waters of the river, did not prevail against you. And again, when ye came amongst nations, who were as mighty as fire, they did not prevail against you; and kingdoms, which were strong as a flame, did not destroy you;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:3)


אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל פַּרְקָךְ יְהָבֵית חֲלִיפָךְ מִצְרָאֵי כּוּשׁ וּסְבָא תְּחוֹתָךְ:


Because I am the Lord thy God, the Holy One of Israel, thy Saviour: I gave Egypt in exchange for thee, Ethiopia, and Seba, in thy stead.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:4)


מִדְאַתְּ חֲבִיב קֳדָמַי אִתְיְקַרְתָּא וַאֲנָא רְחִימְתָּךְ וּמְסַרֵית עַמְמַיָא תְּחוֹתָךְ וּמַלְכְּוָתָא חֲלַף נַפְשָׁךְ:


Ever since I loved thee, thou wast precious; yea, I loved thee dearly and I gave nations instead of thee, and kingdoms for thy soul.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:5)


לָא תִדְחַל אֲרֵי בְסַעְדָךְ מֵימְרִי מִמַדִינְחָא אַיְתִי בְנָךְ וּמִמַעֲרְבָא אֱקָרֵיב גַלְוָתָךְ:


Fear not, for my WORD shall be thy support; I will bring thy children from the east, I will bring together thy captivity from the west.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:6)


אֲמַר לְצִפוּנָא אַיְתָא וּלְדָרוֹמָא לָא תְעַכֵּב אַיְתוּ בָנַי מֵרָחִיק וְגַלְוַת עַמִי מִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא:


I will say to the north, Bring; and to the south, Keep not back: bring my sons from afar, and the captivity of my people from the ends of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:7)


כָּל דָא בְּדִיל אֲבָהַתְכוֹן צַדִיקַיָא דְאִתְקְרֵי שְׁמִי עֲלֵיהוֹן וְלִיקָרִי בְרֵיתִינוּן אַתְקְנִית גַלְוַתְהוֹן אַף עֲבָדֵית לְהוֹן נִסִין:


All these things shall come to pass for the sake of your righteous fathers, upon whom my name was called, yea, for my glory I have created them. I will restore their captivity, yea, I will work wonders for them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:8)


דְאַפֵּיק עַמָא מִמִצְרַיִם דְאִינוּן כְּסָמִי וְעַיְנִין לְהוֹן וּכְחַרְשִׁין וְאוּדְנִין לְהוֹן:


When I brought forth my people from Egypt, they were like the blind, although they had eyes; and like the deaf, although they had ears.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:9)


כָּל עַמְמַיָא יִתְכַּנְשׁוּן כַּחֲדָא וְיִתְקַרְבוּן מַלְכְּוָן מַן בְּהוֹן יְחַוֵי דָא וְקַדְמָיָתָא יְבַסְרוּנָנָא יִתְּנוּן סַהֲדֵיהוֹן וְיִזְכּוּן וְיִשְׁמְעוּן וְיֵימְרוּן קְשׁוֹט:


Let all the nations be gathered together, let the kingdoms be brought together: who among them can declare this, and give us the joyful tidings of old? let them bring forth their witnesses, that they may be justified; let them hear, and let them speak the truth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:10)


אַתּוּן סַהֲדִין קֳדָמַי אֲמַר יְיָ וְעַבְדִי מְשִׁיחָא דְאִתְרְעֵתִי בֵיהּ בְּדִיל דְתִידְעוּן וּתְהֵימְנוּן קֳדָמָי וְתִסְתַּכְּלוּן אֲרֵי אֲנָא הוּא דְמִלְקַדְמִין אַף עַלְמֵי עַלְמַיָא דִילִי אִינוּן וּבַר מִנִי לֵית אֱלָהּ:


Ye are my witnesses, saith the Lord, and my servant, the Messiah, in whom is my delight, in order that ye may know, and that ye may believe in me, and understand that I am He who was from the beginning; yea, ages after ages are mine, and beside me there is no god.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:11)


אֲנָא אֲנָא יְיָ וְלֵית בַּר מִנִי פָּרִיק:


I, even I, am the Lord, and beside me there is no saviour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:12)


אֲנָא חֲוֵיתִי לְאַבְרָהָם אֲבוּכוֹן מָה דַעֲתִיד לְמֵיתָא אֲנָא פְּרָקִית יַתְכוֹן מִמִצְרָיִם כְּמָא דְקַיְמִית לֵיהּ בֵּין בִּתְרַיָא וַאֲנָא אַשְׁמְעֵית יַתְכוֹן אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתִי מִסִינַי וְעַד אַתּוּן קַיָמִין וְלֵית בְּכוֹן נוּכְרִי וְאַתּוּן סָהֲדִין קֳדָמַי אֲמַר יְיָ וַאֲנָא אֱלָהָא:


I, I have declared unto Abraham your father what would come to pass; I, I redeemed you out of Egypt, as I swore to him between the pieces; and I, I have made you to hear the doctrine of my law from Sinai, and ye are still alive, when there was no strange god among you; yea, ye are my witnesses, saith the Lord, that I am God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:13)


אַף מַעַלְמָא אֲנָא הוּא וְלֵית דְמִן יְדִי מְשֵׁיזֵיב אַעַבְדִינָהּ וְלָא אֲתֵיבִינָהּ:


Yea, from eternity I am He; and there is none that can deliver out of my hand: I will do it, and who shall turn it back?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:14)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ פָרִיקְכוֹן קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּדִיל חוֹבֵיכוֹן אַגְלֵיתִי יַתְכוֹן לְבָבֶל וְאַחֲתֵית בִּמְשׁוֹטִין כּוּלְהוֹן וְכַסְדָאֵי בִּסְפִינַי תּוּשְׁבַּחְתְּהוֹן:


Thus saith the Lord, your Redeemer, the Holy One of Israel, Because of your sins I led you captive to Babylon; but I will prostrate all of them with their oars, yea, the Chaldeans in the ships of which they boast.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:15)


אֲנָא יְיָ קַדִישְׁכוֹן דִבְרָאֵי לְיִשְׂרָאֵל מַלְכְּכוֹן:


I am the Lord, your Holy One, the Creator of Israel, your King.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:16)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ דְאַתְקֵין בְּיַמָא אוֹרְחָא וּבְמַיִין תַּקִיפִין שְׁבִילָא:


Thus saith the Lord, I am He who prepared a way in the sea, and a path in mighty waters.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:17)


דְאַפֵיק רְתִיכִין וְסוּסְוָן מַשִׁרְיָן וְעַם סַגִי כַּחֲדָא אִתְבְּלָעוּ וְלָא קָמוּ דְעִיכוּ כְּבוּצָא עַמִי טְפוֹ:


I who brought out the chariots, and horses, and a great host, yea, much people; they were swallowed up together, they did not rise, they were extinguished, they are extinct like the glimmering wick is extinguished.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:18)


לָא תִדְכְּרוּן קַדְמָיָתָא וּדְמִן אַוְלָא לָא תִסְתַּכְּלוּן:


Remember not the former things, neither consider the things of old.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:19)


הָא אֲנָא עָבִיד חַדְתָּא וּכְעַן תִּתְגְלֵי הֲלָא תֵידְעוּנָהּ אַף אַשַׁוֵי בְמַדְבְּרָא אוֹרַח בְּצַדְיוּתָא נַהַרְוָן:


Behold, I make a new thing, and now it shall be revealed: and will ye not know it? I will even make a way in the wilderness, and rivers in the desert.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:20)


יְיַקְרוּן קֳדָמַי כַּד אַיְתֵב מְדִינָן דְצַדְיָן אֲתַר דַהֲוָאָה שָׁרְיָן יְרוּדִין וּבְנָת נַעֲמִין אֲרֵי יְהָבִית בְּמַדְבְּרָא מַיָא נַהֲרִין בְּצַדְיוּתָא לְאַשְׁקָאָה גַלְוַת עַמִי דְאִתְרְעֵיתִי בָהּ:


They shall honour me, when I make habitable provinces at the desert, and the places where the dragons and the daughters of the ostrich dwell ; because I will give water in the wilderness, rivers in the desert, to give drink to the captives of my people, in whom I delight.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:21)


עַמָא דֵין אַתְקְנֵית לְפוּלְחָנִי בְתוּשְׁבַּחְתִּי יְהוֹן מִשְׁתָּעָן:


This people have I prepared for my service, they shall declare my praise.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:22)


אֱמִיר עַל יְדֵי נְבִיַיָא וְלָא בְּפּוּלְחָנִי עַרְעִיתוּן דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב אֲרֵי לָאֵיתוּן בְּאוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתִי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


It hath been said by the prophets, that ye of the house of Jacob have not met together for my worship; but that ye were weary of the doctrine of my law, O Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:23)


לָא אַיְתֵיתָא קֳדָמַי אִמְרִין לְעָלְתָא וְנִכְסַת קוּדְשָׁךְ לָא יַקַרְתָּא קֳדָמַי לָא אַסְגֵיתִי עֲלָךְ בְּקוּרְבָּנִין וְלָא אַתְקְפֵית עֲלָךְ בִּלְבוֹנְתָּא:


Thou hast not brought unto me the lambs of thy burnt offerings, and with thy holy sacrifices thou hast not honoured me. I have not caused thee to multiply offerings, neither did I burden thee with frankincense.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:24)


לָא זְבֵינְתָּא קֳדָמַי בִּכְסַף קָנִי בְּסָם וּתְרַב נִכְסַת קוּדְשָׁךְ לָא דְהֵינְתָּא עַל מַדְבְּחִי בְּרַם אַסְגֵיתָא קֳדָמַי בְּחוֹבָךְ אַתְקֵפְתָּא קֳדָמַי בַּעֲוָיָתָךְ:


Thou hast not bought for me the aromatic reed with silver; with the fat of thy holy sacrifices thou hast not anointed mine altar; but thou hast multiplied thy sins before me; thou hast burdened me with thine iniquities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:25)


אֲנָא הוּא שָׁבֵיק לְחוֹבָךְ בְּדִיל שְׁמִי וְחֶטְאָךְ לָא יִדְכְּרוּן:


I, even I, am He who forgiveth thy sins for the sake of my name, and thy sins shall not be remembered.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:26)


אֱמַר כְּעַן נְדוֹן כַּחֲדָא אִשְׁתָּעֵי אַתְּ בְדִיל אִם תִּכּוּל וְתִזְכֵּי:


Speak now, we will plead together, declare thou for the purpose, that thou mayest justify thyself, if thou art able to do so.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:27)


אֲבוּךְ קַדְמָאָה חָטָא וּמַלְפָךְ מְרַדוּ בְמֵימְרִי:


Thy first father hath sinned, and thy teachers have rebelled against my WORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 43:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/43:28)


וְאַפֵּיס רַבְרְבֵי קוּדְשָׁא וְאֶמְסַר לְקַטָלָא יַעֲקֹב וְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְחִסוּדִין:


Therefore I have profaned the princes of the sanctuary, and I have delivered Jacob to the slaughter, and Israel to reproaches.”



## Chapter 44

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:1)


וּכְעַן שְׁמַע יַעֲקֹב עַבְדִי וְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְאִתְרְעֵיתִי בֵיהּ:


Yet now hear, O Jacob, my servant; and Israel, whom I have chosen:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:2)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ דְעַבְדָךְ וּדְאַתְקְנָךְ מִמְעִין יְסַעְדִינָךְ לָא תִדְחַל עַבְדִי יַעֲקֹב וְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְאִתְרְעִיתִי בֵיהּ:


Thus saith the Lord, thy Maker; and He that formed thee from the womb, He will help thee; Fear not, O Jacob, my servant, and thou, O Israel, whom I have chosen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:3)


אֲרֵי כְמָא דְמִתְיַהֲבִין מַיָא עַל אֲרַע בֵּית צַחֲוָנָא וּמִתְנַגְדִין עַל יַבֶּשְׁתָּא כֵּן אֶתֵּן רוּחַ קוּדְשִׁי עַל בְּנָךְ וּבִרְכָתִי עַל בְּנֵי בְנָךְ:


For as waters are discharged upon the thirsty land, and are set flowing upon the dry land, so will I give my Holy Spirit unto thy children, and my blessing unto thy children's children.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:4)


וְיִתְרַבּוּן צַדִיקַיָא רַכִּיכִין וּמְפַנְקִין כְּלַבְלַבֵי עֲסַב כְּאִילָן דִמְשַׁלַח שָׁרְשׁוֹהִי עַל נַגְדִין דְמַיִין:


The righteous shall grow, and tender and delicate as the flowers of the grass, as a tree that sends forth its roots by the streams of waters.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:5)


דֵין יֵימַר מִדָחֲלַיָא דַייָ אֲנָא וְדֵין יְצַלֵי בְשׁוּם יַעֲקֹב וְדֵין יְקָרֵיב קוּרְבָּנֵהּ קֳדָם יְיָ וּבִשְׁמָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יִתְקְרֵי:


This one shall say, I am of them that fear the Lord, and another shall pray in the name of the God of Jacob; this one shall offer his oblation before the Lord, and draw near in the name of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:6)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ מַלְכָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וּפַרְקֵהּ יְיָ צְבָאוֹת אֲנָא הוּא דְמִלְקַדְמִין אַף עַלְמֵי עָלְמַיָא דִילִי אִינוּן וּבַר מִנִי לֵית אֱלֶהּ:


Thus saith the King of Israel, and His Redeemer, the Lord of hosts; I am He who was from the beginning, yea, eternities of eternities are mine; and beside me there is no God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:7)


וּמַן כְּוָתִי דִין עַרְעִינָהּ וִיחַוִינָהּ וִיסַדְרִינָהּ קֳדָמַי מִשַׁוָיוּתִי עַמָא דְמִן עַלְמָא וּדְאָתְיָן וְדַעֲתִידִין לְמֵיתֵי יְחַווּן לָנָא:


Who like me shall proclaim this, shall declare it, and set it in order before me, from the time that I appointed the ancient people? and let them declare unto us the things that are coming, and shall come.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:8)


לָא תִדַחֲלוּן וְלָא תִתַּבְּרוּן הֲלָא מִבְּכֵן בְסַרְתָּךְ וְחַוֵיתִי וְאַתּוּן סָהֲדִין קֳדָמַי הֲאִית אֱלָהָא בַּר מִנִי וְלֵית דְתַקִיף אֱלָהֵן דְמִן קֳדָמַי מִתְיְהַב לֵיהּ תְּקוֹף:


Fear ye not, neither be afraid: have not I told thee from that time, and have declared it? ye also are my witnesses, that there is no God beside me, and there is none strong, except he to whom strength is given from me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:9)


עַבְדֵי צַלְמַיָא כּוּלְהוֹן לְמָא וּפָלְחֵיהוֹן דְלָא יַהֲנוּן לְהוֹן וְסָהֲדִין אִינוּן בְּנַפְשָׁתֵיהוֹן דְלָא חָזָן וְלָא יָדְעִין בְּדִין דְיִבַּהֲתוּן:


They that make images are all of them vanity, and worship what does not profit them; and they are witnesses against themselves, that they do not see, nor know, that they may be ashamed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:10)


מַן עֲבַד דַחֲלָא וְצַלְמָא אַתִיךְ בְּדִיל דְלָא לַהֲנָאָה:


Whoever maketh a god or a molten image, it is for no purpose.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:11)


הָא כָּל פָּלְחֵיהוֹן יִבָּהֲתוּן וְאוּמָנִין עוּבְדָנִין מִבְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא יִתְכַּנְסוּן כּוּלְהוֹן יְקוּמוּן יִתַּבְּרוּן יִבַהֲתוּן כַּחֲדָא:


Behold, all their worshippers shall be ashamed, and the working artificers are workmen of the sons of men; let them all be gathered together, let them stand up; they shall fear and be confounded together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:12)


נַפְחָא מִבַּרְזְלָא חֲצִינָא עָבֵיד וּנְפַח נוּר בְּשִׁיחוּרִין וּבְמַקוּבִין מַתְקֵיף לֵיהּ וְעָבִיד לֵיהּ בִּתְקוֹף חֵילֵהּ דְאִם יִכְפַּן עַבְדֵהּ וְלָא יֵיכוּל לָא יְהֵי בֵיהּ חֵיל וְאִם יִצְחֵי וְלָא יִשְׁתֵּי מַיָא יְהֵי מְשַׁלְהֵי:


The smith maketh an axe out of iron, and bloweth the coals in the fire, and maketh it firm with the hammer, and worketh it with the power of his strength; but when he that worketh it is hungry, and does not eat bread, he hath no strength; and if he is thirsty, and drinketh no water, he fainteth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:13)


נַגָר אָעִין נָפֵיץ בְּחוּטָא מְסַר לֵיהּ בְּמַשְׁקוּלְתָּא מְגַזֵי לֵיהּ בְּאִזְמֵילַיָא וּבִנְצוּרִין מְאַחֵיד לֵיהּ וְעָבֵיד לֵיהּ כִּדְמוּת גְבַר כְּתוּשְׁבְּחַת אִתְּתָא לְמִיתַּב בְּבֵיתָא:


The carpenter stretcheth out the line, he applieth the plummet to it; he carveth it with a knife, and he dove-taileth it together, and he maketh it after the likeness of a man, according to the beauty of a woman, that it may remain in the house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:14)


לְמִקַץ לֵיהּ אַרְזִין וּנְסִיב תְּרֵז וּבְלוּט וּמַתְקֵיף לֵיהּ בַּאֲעֵי חוּרְשָׁא נְצַב אוּרְנָא וּמִטְרָא יְרַבֵּי:


He heweth for himself cedars and taketh the scarlet oak, and the chestnut, and seasons them. He plants the ash amongst the trees of the forest, and the rain nourisheth it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:15)


וֶהֱוֵי לֶאֱנָשָׁא לְאַדְלָקָא וּנְסֵיב מִנְהוֹן וּשְׁחֵין אַף אֲזָא וְאָפָא לְחֵם אַף עָבְדֵהּ דַחֲלָא וּסְגֵיד אִתְּכֵהּ צַלְמָא וּבְעָא מִנֵהּ:


That it may serve a man to burn: and he taketh thereof, and warmeth himself; yea, he kindleth it, and baketh bread; yea, he maketh it a god, and worshippeth it; he formeth it an image, and prayeth to it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:16)


פַּלְגֵהּ אוֹקִיד בְּנוּרָא עַל פַּלְגֵהּ בִּסְרָא אָכִיל טְוָא טָוִי וּסְבַע אַף שְׁחִין וַאֲמַר אָח שַׁחֵינִית חֲזֵיתִי נוּר:


He burneth half thereof in the fire; and by the other half he eateth flesh, he roasteth meat, and is satisfied; he also warmeth himself, and saith, Aha, I am warm, I have seen the fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:17)


וּשְׁאָרֵהּ לְדַחֲלָא עָבַד לְצַלְמָא סְגִיד לֵיהּ וְאֶשְׁתַּעְבֵּד לֵיהּ וּבְעָא מִנֵהּ וַאֲמַר שֵׁיזְבָנִי אֲרֵי דְחַלְתִּי אָתְּ:


And the remainder thereof he maketh a god; to the image he boweth down; he becometh servile to it, and prayeth to it, and saith, Deliver me; for thou art my god.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:18)


לָא יָדְעוּן וְלָא מִסְתַּכְּלִין אֲרֵי מְטַמְטְמָן מִלְמֶחֱזֵי עֵינֵיהוֹן מִלְאִסְתַּכָּלָא בְּלִבְּהוֹן:


They know not, neither do they understand; for their eyes are closely shut up, so that they cannot see, and their heart, that they cannot understand.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:19)


וְלָא מֵתִיב לְלִבֵּהּ וְלָא מַדַע וְלָא סוּכְלְתָנוּ לְמֵימַר פַּלְגֵהּ אוֹקֵידַת בְּנוּרָא וְאַף אֲפִיתִי עַל גוּמְרוֹהִי לְחֵם טְוֵית בִּסְרָא וַאֲכַלֵית וּשְׁאֵרֵהּ לְתוֹעֵבָא אַעְבֵּיד לְיִבְלֵי אָעָא אֶסְגוֹד:


And he does not consider in his heart, neither hath he knowledge or understanding to say, Half of it I have burned in the fire; yea, I have baked bread upon the coals thereof; I have roasted flesh and eaten it: and shall I make the remnant thereof a god? shall I bow myself down to the stock of a tree?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:20)


הָא דְחַלְתָּא פַלְגֵהּ קִטְמָא לִבֵּהּ שַׁטְיָא אַטְעֲיֵהּ וְלָא יְשֵׁיזֵיב יַת נַפְשֵׁהּ וְלָא יֵימַר הֲלָא שִׁקְרָא עֲבָדֵית בִּימִינִי:


Behold his god, part of it is ashes; his undiscerning heart has caused him to err, so that he cannot deliver his soul, nor say, Is it not a lie which I have made with my right hand?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:21)


אִדְכַּר אִלֵין יַעֲקֹב וְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי עַבְדִי אַתְּ אַתְקֵינְתָּךְ לְמֶהֱוֵי עֲבֵיד פְּלַח קֳדָמַי אַתְּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לָא תִתְנְשֵׁי דְחַלְתִּי:


Remember these things, O Jacob and Israel; for thou art my servant: I have formed thee that thou shouldest serve me, that thou, O Israel, shouldest not forget my fear.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:22)


מְחֵיתִי כְעֵיבָא חוֹבָךְ וְכַעֲנָן עֲדֵי כָּל חֶטְאָךְ תּוּב לְפוּלְחָנִי אֲרֵי פְרַקְתָּךְ:


I have blotted out, as a thick cloud, thy transgressions, and as a cloud vanisheth away, all thy sins : return to my worship, for I have redeemed thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:23)


שַׁבַּחוּ שְׁמַיָא אֲרֵי עֲבַד יְיָ פוּרְקַן לְעַמֵהּ יַבִּיבוּ יְסוֹדֵי אַרְעָא בּוּעוּ טוּרַיָא תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא חוּרְשָׁא וְכָל אִילָנַיָא דְבֵהּ אֲרֵי פְרַק יְיָ יַעֲקֹב וּבְיִשְׂרָאֵל יִשְׁתַּבַּח:


Sing, O ye heavens; for the Lord hath wrought redemption for His people: Let the foundations of the earth rejoice; rejoice, 0 mountains, in praise, O forest, and all the trees therein! for the Lord hath redeemed Jacob, and will glorify Himself in Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:24)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ דְפַרְקָךְ וּדְאַתְקְנָךְ מִמְעַיִין אֲנָא יְיָ עָבֵיד כּוֹלָא תְּלֵית שְׁמַיָא בְּמֵימְרִי שַׁכְלְלֵית אַרְעָא בִּגְבוּרְתִּי:


Thus saith the Lord, who hath redeemed thee, and who hath prepared thee from the womb, I am the Lord that maketh all things, I have suspended the heavens by my WORD, I have laid the foundations of the earth by my strength;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:25)


מְבַטֵל אֶתְוָת בַּדִין וְקִסוּמִין מְשַׁגֵשׁ מַתִיב חַכִּימָא לַאֲחוֹרָא וְדַעְתְּהוֹן מְקַלְקֵל:


That frustrateth the tokens of the liars, and maketh diviners mad; that turneth wise men backward, and bringeth their knowledge into contempt.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:26)


מְקַיֵם פִּתְגָמֵי עַבְדוֹהִי צַדִיקַיָא וּמְלַךְ שְׁלוּחוֹהִי מַשְׁלִים דַאֲמַר לִירוּשְׁלָם תִּתּוֹתַב וּלְקִרְוַיָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה יִתְבַּנְיָן וְחָרְבָתָהָא אֲקוֹמֵם:


That confirmeth the word of His righteous servants, and performeth the counsel of His messengers, that saith to Jerusalem, Thou shalt be inhabited; and concerning the cities of the house of Judah, They shall be built; and her desolated places I will raise up.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:27)


דַּאֲמַר עַל בָּבֶל תֶּחְרוֹב וְנַהֲרָתָא אֲיַבֵּשׁ:


That saith to Babylon, Be desolate, and I will dry up thy rivers.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 44:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/44:28)


דַאֲמַר עַל כּוֹרֶשׁ לְמִתַּן לֵיהּ מַלְכוּ וְכָל רְעוּתִי יְקַיֵם וְלִדְאֲמַר לִירוּשְׁלֶם תִּתְבְּנֵי וְהֵיכְלָא יִשְׁתַּכְלֵל:


That promises Cyrus that He will give him a kingdom; and that he shall establish all my pleasure, even to him that saith to Jerusalem, Thou shalt be built, and the temple shall have its foundation laid.



## Chapter 45

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:1)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ לִמְשִׁיחֵהּ לְכוֹרֶשׁ דְאַתְקְפֵית בִּימִינֵהּ לְמִמְסַר קֳדְמוֹהִי עַמְמִין וְחַרְצֵי מַלְכִין אַשְׁרֵי לְפַּתָּחָא קֳדָמוֹהִי דָשִׁין וְתַרְעִין לָא יִתְאַחָדוּן:


Thus saith the Lord to His anointed, to Cyrus, whom I hold firm by his right hand, to deliver the nations unto him; and I will loose the loins of the kings, to open the doors before him, and the gates shall not be shut.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:2)


מֵימְרִי קֳדָמָךְ יְהָךְ וְשׁוּרַיָא אֲכַבֵּשׁ דָשִׁין דִנְחָשׁ אֱתַּבֵר וְעַבְרִין דְבַרְזֶל אֲקַצֵץ:


My WORD shall go before thee, I will have a way in the plains ;I will break in pieces the doors of brass, and cut in sunder the bars of iron.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:3)


וְאֶתֵּן לָךְ אוֹצְרִין דַחֲשׁוֹךְ וְסִימָן דְמִטַמְרָן בְּדִיל דְתֵידַע אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ דְרַבֵּיתִי בִשְׁמָךְ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


And I will give thee the treasures of darkness, and hidden treasures, that thou mayest know, that I, the Lord, who called thee by thy name, am the God of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:4)


בְּדִיל עַבְדִי יַעֲקֹב וְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּחִירִי וּקְרֵית לָךְ בִּשְׁמָךְ אַתְקֵינְתָּךְ וְלָא יְדַעְתָּא לְמִדְחַל קֳדָמַי:


For Jacob my servant's sake, and Israel my elect, yea, I have called thee by thy name; I have guided thee, though thou hast not known that thou shouldest fear me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:5)


אֲנָא יְיָ וְלֵית עוֹד וּבַר מִנִי לֵית אֱלָהָא סְעַדְתִּיךְ וְלָא יָדַעְתָּא לְמִדְחַל קֳדָמַי:


I am the Lord, and none else; there is no God beside me: I have supported thee, though thou hast not known that thou shouldest fear me:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:6)


בְּדִיל דְיֵדְעוּן מִמַדְנַח שִׁמְשָׁא וּמִמַעַרְבָא אֲרֵי לֵית בַּר מִנִי אֲנָא יְיָ וְלֵית עוֹד:


That they may know from the rising of the sun, and from the west; I am the Lord, and there is none else:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:7)


דְאַתְקִין נְהוֹר וּבָרֵי חֲשׁוֹךְ עֲבֵיד שְׁלָם וּבָרֵי בִישׁ אֲנָא יְיָ עָבֵיד כֹּל אִלֵין:


Who prepares the light, and creates darkness, makes peace, and creates punishment for evil. I the Lord do all these things.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:8)


יְשַׁמְשׁוּן שְׁמַיָא מִלְעֵלָא וַעֲנָנַיָא יִגְדוּן טוּבָא תִּתְפְּתַח אַרְעָא וְיֵחוּן מֵתַיָא וּזְכוּתָא תִתְגְלֵי כַּחֲדָא אֲנָא יְיָ בְּרֵיתִינוּן:


Let the heavens drop down from above, and the clouds flow with good, let the earth open itself, and the dead live, and let righteousness be revealed together; I the Lord have created them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:9)


וַי דִי מִדַמֵי לְמֵידָן לִקֳבֵל פִּתְגָמֵי בָרְיֵהּ וְרָחַץ דְיוֹטְבִין לֵיהּ צַלְמֵי פַחֲרָא דְעָבְדִין מֵעֲפַר אַדְמָתָא הֲאֶפְשַׁר דְיֵימַר טִינָא לְעָבְדֵהּ לָא עֲבַדְתַּנִי וְעוֹבָדָךְ לֵית יְדִין לֵיהּ:


Woe to him who thinks of striving against the words of his Creator, and trusts that the images of a potter shall do him good, which are made out of the dust of the earth. Is it possible, that the clay could say to him that worketh it, Thou hast not made me? or thy work, He hath no hands?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:10)


וַי דַאֲמַר לְאָב מַה תּוֹלִיד וּלְאִתְּתָא מַה תֶּעְדִין:


Woe to him that saith to his father, What begettest thou? and to his mother, What hast thou brought forth?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:11)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וּדְאַתְקְנֵהּ אַתְיָא דַעֲתִידִין לְמֵיתֵי אַתּוּן שָׁאֲלִין מִן קֳדָמַי עַל עַמִי וְעַל עוֹבַד גְבוּרְתִּי תְּפַקְדוּנַנִי:


Thus saith the Lord, the Holy One of Israel, and He that formed him, Ye question me about things concerning my people, which shall come to pass; and will ye command me concerning the work of my power?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:12)


אֲנָא בְּמֵימְרִי עֲבַדֵת אַרְעָא וֶאֱנָשָׁא עֲלָהּ בְּרִיתִי אֲנָא בִּגְבוּרְתִּי תְּלֵית שְׁמַיָא וְכָל חֵילֵיהוֹן שֵׁכְלֵילִית:


It is I who have made the earth by my WORD, and I have created man upon it; it is I who have suspended the heavens by my power, and I have laid the foundation of all the hosts of them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:13)


אֲנָא אַיְתִינֵהּ בִּגְלֵי בִקְשׁוֹט וְכָל אוֹרְחָתֵהּ אֲתַקֵן הוּא יִבְנֵי קַרְתִּי וְגַלְוַת עַמִי יְשַׁלַח לָא בְדָמִין וְלָא בְּמָמוֹן אֲמַר יְיָ צְבָאוֹת:


It is I who will verily bring him forth publicly, and all his paths I will direct: he shall build my city, and he shall let the captives of my people go, not for a price nor for money, saith the Lord of hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:14)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ לֵיאוּת מִצְרַיִם וְתֵגָרֵי כוּשׁ וֶאֱנָשֵׁי סְבָא גַבְרִין דִסְחוֹרָא עֲלָךְ יֶעְדוּן וְדִילָךְ יְהוֹן בָּתַר פִּתְגָמָךְ יְהָכוּן בְּשַׁלְשְׁלָן יֶעְדוּן וְלִיךְ יִסְגְדוּן מִנִיךְ יִבְעוּן וְיוֹדוּן לְמֵימַר בְּקוּשְׁטָא בִיךְ אֱלָהָא וְלֵית עוֹד אֱלָהָא בַּר מִנָךְ:


Thus saith the Lord, the wealth of Egypt, and the merchandize of Ethiopia and of the men of of the Sabeans, the men of traffic, shall come unto thee, and thine they shall be: they shall walk according to thy command; they shall pass along in chains; and they shall bow down unto thee, and shall supplicate thee, saying, Verily God is in thee, and there is no God whatever besides Him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:15)


בְּקוּשְׁטָא אַתְּ אֱלָהָא אַשְׁרֵיתָא בִּתְקוֹף רוּמָא שְׁכִנְתָּךְ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל פָּרִיק:


Verily, thou art He, who dost make Thy Shekinah to dwell in the highest heaven, O God of Israel, the Saviour!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:16)


בְּהִיתוּ וְאַף אִתְכַּנִעוּ כּוּלְהוֹן כַּחֲדָא הֲלִיכוּ בְּאִתְכַּנְעוּ פָּלְחֵי צַלְמַיָא:


They shall be ashamed and confounded, all of them: the worshippers of images shall walk in confusion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:17)


יִשְׂרָאֵל יִתְפְּרִיק בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ פּוּרְקָן עָלְמַיָא לָא תִבְהֲתוּן וְלָא תִתְכַּנְעוּן לַעֲלַם וּלְעַלְמֵי עָלְמַיָא:


Israel shall be saved by the WORD of the Lord with an everlasting salvation: ye shall not be ashamed nor confounded for ever, yea, for ages after ages.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:18)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ דִי בְרָא שְׁמַיָא הוּא אֱלֹהִים דְשַׁכְלִיל אַרְעָא וְעָבְדָהּ הוּא אַתְקְנָהּ לָא לְרֵיקָנוּ בְרָהּ אֱלָהֵין לְאַסְגָאָה עֲלָהּ בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא אַתְקְנָא אֲנָא יְיָ וְלֵית עוֹד:


For thus saith the Lord that created the heavens; He who laid the foundation of the earth and made it, is God; He formed it, He created it not in vain; but He formed it, that the sons of man should multiply upon it. I am the Lord, and there is none else.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:19)


לָא בְסִתְרָא מַלְלֵית בַּאֲתַר אַרְעָא קָבֵל וְלָא אֲמָרֵית לְזַרְעָא דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב לְרֵיקָנוּ תְּבַעוּ דְחַלְתִּי אֲנָא יְיָ מְמַלֵל קְשׁוֹט מְחַוֵי כֵּיוְנָן:


I have not spoken in secret, in a dark place of the earth; I said not unto the seed of the house of Jacob, Seek me reverently in vain; I the Lord speak the truth, declaring upright things.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:20)


אִתְכְּנִישׁוּ וֶאֱתוּ אִתְקְרָבוּ כַחֲדָא מְשֵׁיזְבֵי עַמְמַיָא לָא יָדְעִין דְנָסְבִין יַת אָעֵי צַלְמֵיהוֹן וּבְעָן מִן דַחֲלָא דְלָא יִפְרוֹק:


Assemble yourselves and come; draw near together, ye that are escaped of the nations: they know nothing that carry about their wooden images, and who pray unto a god who shall not save.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:21)
