חַווֹ וְאִתְקְרָבוּ אַף אִתְמְלִיכוּ כַּחֲדָא מִן בְּסַר דָא מִלְקַדְמִין מִבְּכֵן חַוְיָהּ הֲלָא אֲנָא יְיָ וְלֵית עוֹד אֱלָהּ בַּר מִנִי אֱלָהּ דְזַכֵּי וּפָרִיק לֵית אֶלָא אֲנָא:


Tell ye, and draw ye near; yea, take counsel together: who hath declared this from ancient time? who hath told it from that time? have not I the Lord? and there is no God whatever besides me; a just God and a Saviour; there is none but I.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:22)


אִתְפְּנוּ לְמֵימְרִי וְאִתְפְּרִיקוּ כָּל דְבִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא אֲרֵי אֲנָא אֱלָהָא וְלֵית עוֹד:


Turn unto my WORD, and be ye saved, all that are at the ends of the earth: for I am the Lord, and there is none else.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:23)


בְּמֵימְרִי קַיְמֵת נְפַק מִן קֳדָמַי בִּזְכוּ פִּתְגָם וְלָא יְבַטֵל אֲרֵי קֳדָמַי תִּכְרַע כָּל בֶּרֶךְ תְּקַיֵם כָּל לִישָׁן:


I have sworn by my WORD, the word is gone forth in righteousness from my presence, and shall not fail, That before me every knee shall bow, every tongue shall swear.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:24)


בְּרַם בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ עֲלֵי אֲמַר לְאַיְתָאָה זַכְוָן וּתְקוֹף בְּמֵימְרֵהּ יוֹדוּן וְיִבְהַתוּן בְּטַעֲוָתְהוֹן כָּל עַמְמַיָא דַהֲווֹ מִתְגָרִין בְּעַמֵהּ:


Surely, He has promised to bring me righteousness and strength by the WORD of the Lord. In His WORD they shall offer praise, and all the nations that are incensed against His people shall be ashamed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 45:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/45:25)


בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ יִזְכּוּן וְיִשְׁתַּבְּחוּן כָּל זַרְעָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


In the WORD of the Lord all the seed of Israel shall be justified and glorified.



## Chapter 46

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:1)


חֲמִיט בַּל אִתְקְטֵף נְבוֹ הֲווֹ צַלְמָנֵיהוֹן דְמוּת חֵיוָא וּבְעִירָא מַטוּלֵי טַעֲוָתְהוֹן יַקִירִין עַל נָטְלֵיהוֹן וְאִנוּן מְשַׁלְהַיָן:


Bel is bowed down, Nebo is cut down. Their images are in the likeness of serpents and beasts. The burdens of your idols shall be heavy upon those who carry them; because they be exhausted


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:2)


אִתְקְצִיצוּ וְאִתְקְטִפוּ כַּחֲדָא וְלָא יָכִילוּ לְשֵׁיזָבָא נַטְלֵיהוֹן וּפִלְחֵיהוֹן בְּשִׁבְיָא אֲזָלוּ:


They are cut off, yea, they are altogether cut to pieces, and they were not able to deliver them that carried them; and their worshippers are gone into captivity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:3)


קַבִּילוּ לְמֵימְרִי דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב וְכָל שְׁאָרָא דְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל דִרְחִימִין מִכָּל עַמְמַיָא וַחֲבִיבִין מִכָּל מַלְכְּוָתָא:


Hearken unto me, O house of Jacob, and all the remnant of the house of Israel, who are beloved above all nations, yea, beloved above all kingdoms.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:4)


וְעַל עַלְמָא אֲנָא הוּא וְעַד עֲלָם עַלְמַיָא מֵימְרִי קַיָם אֲנָא בָרֵית כָּל אֱנָשָׁא וַאֲנָא בַדְרֵית יַתְהוֹן לְבֵינֵי עַמְמַיָא אַף אֲנָא אֶשְׁבּוֹק לְחוֹבֵיהוֹן וְאֶסְלַח:


Even unto eternity I am He, and my WORD shall endure for ages after ages. It is I who have created all men, and I have scattered them among the nations, yea, I will forgive their sins, and I will pardon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:5)


לְמַן תְּדַמוּן קֳדָמַי וּתְשַׁווּן וּתְכַוְנוּן קֳדָמַי בִּקְשׁוֹט:


To whom will ye liken me, and equal me, and compare me in truth?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:6)


הָא עַמְמַיָא גָבָן דְהַב מִכִּיס וְכַסְפָא בְּמֹאזְנַיָא תַּקְלִין אָגְרִין קִינָאָה וְעָבֵיד לֵיהּ דַחֲלָא סָגְדִין אַף מִשְׁתַּעְבְּדִין:


Behold, the people collect gold out of the bag, and weigh silver in the balance, and hire a goldsmith; and he maketh it a god: they fall down, yea, they do it reverence.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:7)


נַטְלִין לֵיהּ עַל כַּתְפֵּיהוֹן סַבְלִין לֵיהּ מַחֲתִין לֵהּ בְּאַתְרֵהּ וְקָאֵים מֵאַתְרֵהּ לָא אֶפְשַׁר לֵיהּ דִינוּד אַף יִבְעֵי מִנֵהּ לָא יְתִיבִינֵהּ מַעַקְתֵהּ לָא יִפְרְקִנֵהּ:


They bear him upon the shoulder, they carry him and set him in his place, and he abideth; it is not possible for him to move from his place; yea, one supplicates to him; but he does not answer him, nor save him out of his distress.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:8)


אַדְכָּרוּ דָא וְאִתַּקָפוּ וַאֲתִיבוּ מְרוֹדִין עַל לֵב:


Remember this, and be ye strong; and bear it in mind, O rebels!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:9)


אִדְכַּרוּ קַדְמְיָתָא דְמִן עַלְמָא אֲרֵי אֲנָא אֱלָהּ וְלֵית עוֹד אֱלָהּ בַּר מִינִי:


Remember the former things of old; for I am God, and there is no God whatever besides me,


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:10)


מְחַוֵי מִן אַוְלָא לְסוֹפָא וּמִלְקַדְמִין דְלָא אִתְעֲבִידָא אֲמַר מִלְכִי יִתְקַיֵם וְכָל רְעוּתִי אַעְבֵּד:


Declaring the end from the beginning, and from ancient times the things that are not yet done, saying, My counsel shall stand, and I will do all my pleasure:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:11)


דַאֲמַר לְכַנָשָׁא גַלְוָתָא מִמַדִינְחָא לְאַיְתָאָה בִגְלֵי כְעוֹף קַלִיל מַאֲרַע רְחִיקָא בְּנֵי אַבְרָהָם רְחִימִי אַף מַלְלֵית אַף אַיְתִינָהּ אַף אַתְקֵינְתָּא אַף אַעְבְּדִינָהּ:


Who hath promised to gather together the captivity from the East, to bring openly, as a swift bird, the sons of Abraham, my chosen, from a distant land; yea, I have promised it; yea, I will bring it to pass! I have ordained it; yea, I will do it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:12)


קַבִּילוּ לְמֵימְרִי תַּקִיפֵי לִבָּא דִרְחִיקִין מִזְכוּתָא:


Hearken unto my WORD, ye stout-hearted, that are far from righteousness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 46:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/46:13)


קְרִיבָא זַכְוָתִי לָא תִתְרַחֵק וּפוּרְקָנִי לָא יִתְעַכֵּב וְאֶתֵּן בְּצִיוֹן פָּרִיק לְיִשְׂרָאֵל תּוּשְׁבַּחְתִּי:


My righteousness is nigh, it is not far off, and my salvation shall not tarry: and I will place salvation in Zion, and my glory in Israel.



## Chapter 47

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:1)


חוּתִי וְתִיבִי עַל עַפְרָא מַלְכּוּ כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְבָבֶל תִּיבִי לְאַרְעָא לֵית כּוּרְסֵי יְקָר מַלְכוּת כַּשְׂדָאֵי אֲרֵי לָא תוֹסִיפִין דְיִקְרוּן לָךְ רַכִּיבָא וּמְפַנְקָא:


Descend and sit in the dust, o kingdom of the congregation of Babylon; sit on the ground, there is no throne of glory, o kingdom of the Chaldeans; for thou shalt no more be called tender and delicate.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:2)


קַבִּילִי מְרוֹעָא וְעוּלִי בְשִׁעְבּוּד גְלָא יְקַר מַלְכוּתִיךְ אִתְּבַּרוּ שִׁלְטוֹנִיךְ אִתְבַּדְרוּ עַם מַשִׁרְיָתֵיךְ גְלוֹ כְמֵי נַהֲרָא:


Receive this calamity and go into servitude; put away the glory of thy kingdom, thy princes are overthrown, the people of thy armies are scattered, they have vanished away like waters of the river.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:3)


תִּתְגְלֵי בַּהֲתָתִיךְ אַף יִתְחֲזֵי קְלָנִיךְ פּוּרְעֲנוּת גְמִירָא אִתְפְּרַע מִינִיךְ וַאֲשַׁנֵי דִינִיךְ מִבְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא:


Thy nakedness shall be uncovered, thy shame shall be seen; I will take full vengeance, on thee and I will change thy judgment from the children of men.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:4)


פָּרְקָנָא יְיָ צְבָאוֹת שְׁמֵהּ קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


As for our Redeemer, the Lord of hosts is His name, the Holy One of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:5)


תְּבִי שַׁתְקָא וְעוּלֵי בְקִבְלָא יְקַר מַלְכוּת כַּסְדָאֵי אֲרֵי לָא תּוֹסִיפִין דְיִקְרוֹן לִיךְ תְּקֵיפַת מַלְכְּוָן:


Sit thou silent, and get thee into darkness, o glory of the kingdom of Chaldeans; thou shalt no more be called, The mighty one of the kingdoms.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:6)


רְגֵיזִית עַל עַמִי אַפֵיסִית אַחְסַנְתִּי וּמְסַרְתִּינוּן בִידָךְ לָא אִתְמַלֵית עֲלֵיהוֹן רַחֲמִין עַל סָבָא אַתְקֵיפְתָּא מַרְוָתִיךְ לַחְדָא:


I was angry with my people, I have polluted mine inheritance, and given them into thine hand: thou hast not had compassion upon them, thou didst make thy dominion over the ancient very cruel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:7)


וַאֲמַרְתְּ לַעֲלָם אֱהֵי תַקִיפַת מַלְכְּוָן עַד לָא שַׁוִית אִלֵין עַל לִבִּיךְ לָא אִדְכַּרְתְּ לְסוֹפָא:


And thou saidst, I shall be the mighty one of kingdoms, so that thou didst not lay these things to thy heart, neither didst remember the end of them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:8)


וּכְעַן שְׁמַעִי דָא מְפַנֶקְתָּא דְיָתְבָא לְרָחֲצָן דְאָמְרָא בְּלִבָּהּ אֲנָא וְלֵית בַּר מִינִי עוֹד לָא אֵיתֵיב אַרְמְלוּ וְלָא אֵדַע תִּיכְלוּ:


Therefore, hear now this, O voluptuous one, that dwellest in security, that saith in her heart, I am, and there is none else besides me; I shall not sit a widow, neither shall I know the loss of children:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:9)


וְיָתְיָן לִיךְ תַּרְתֵּין אִלֵין זְמַן בְּיוֹמָא חַד תְּכַל וְאַרְמְלוּ כִּדְשָׁלְמָן יֵיתוּן עֲלָךְ בִּסְגִיאוּת חָרָשָׁךְ בִּתְקוֹף קְסָמָךְ לַחֲדָא:


But these two things shall come to thee, in an appointed time, in one day, loss of children, and widowhood: they shall come upon thee in their perfection: notwithstanding the multitude of thy sorceries, notwithstanding the great strength of thine enchantments.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:10)


וְאִתְרְחֵיצְתְּ בְּבִישְׁתִּיךְ אֲמַרְתְּ לֵית דְחָזֵי לִי חוּכְמְתִיךְ וּמַדְעִיךְ הִיא קַלְקַלְתִיךְ וַאֲמַרְתְּ בְּלִבֵּיךְ אֲנָא וְלֵית בַּר מִינִי עוֹד:


For thou hast trusted in thy wickedness; thou hast said, None seeth thee. Thy wisdom and thy knowledge have corrupted thee; thou hast said in thine heart, I am, and there is none else besides me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:11)


וְתֵיתֵי עֲלָךְ בִּישְׁתָּא לָא תֵדְעִין לְמִבְעֵי עֲלָהּ וְתִיפּוֹל עֲלָךְ עָקָא לָא תִיכְּלִין לְאַעְדָיוּתָהּ וְתֵיתֵי עֲלָךְ בִּתְכֵּיף אִתְרְגוּשָׁא לָא תִידְעִין:


Therefore shall evil come upon thee: thou shalt not know how to deprecate it; distress shall fall upon thee, and thou shalt not be able to remove it; a tumult shall come upon thee suddenly, unawares.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:12)


קוּמִי כְעַן בְּקִסוּמִיךְ וּבְסַגְיוּת חָרָשִׁיךְ דַהֲוֵית מִתְעַסְקָא בְּהוֹן מִיַנְקוּתִיךְ מָאִים תִּכְלִין לַהֲנָאָה מָאִים תִּכְלִין לְמִתְקַף:


Persist now in thine enchantments; and in the multitude of thy sorceries, in which thou wast occupied from thy youth; if so be thou shalt be able to prevail.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:13)


לָאֵית בִּסְגִיאוּת מַלְכַּךְ יְקוּמוּן כְּעַן וְיִפְרְקוּנִיךְ דְמַסְכִּין לְמַזָלַת שְׁמַיָא וּדְחָזָן בְּכוֹכְבַיָא מְהוֹדְעִין זִמְנַיָא מַטְעָן לִיךְ לְמֵימָר כֵּן עֲתִיד לְמֵיתֵי עֲלָךְ יְרַח בִּירָח:


Thou art wearied in the multitude of thy counsels. Let them now rise up, and save thee, who are familiar with the Zodiac of the heavens, who look at the stars, who make known appointed seasons: deceiving thee, saying, Thus it shall happen unto thee each month.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:14)


הָא הֲווֹ חֲלָשִׁין כְּקַשָׁא עַמְמַיָא דְתַקִיפִין כְּאֶשְׁתָּא שֵׁיצִיאוּנוּן לָא יְשֵׁיזְבוּן יַת נַפְשְׁהוֹן מִיַד קָטוֹלִין לֵית שְׁאָר וּמְשֵׁיזֵיב אַף לָא אֲתַר לְאִשְׁתֵּיזָבָא בֵיהּ:


Behold, they shall be weak as stubble. The nations which are as strong as fire shall consume them, they shall not deliver themselves from the hand of the slayers: there shall be no remnant nor escaped of them, yea, not a place, in which one might deliver himself.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 47:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/47:15)


כֵּן הֲווֹ בִיךְ עוֹבָדֵי שְׁקָרַיִךְ דַהֲוֵית מִתְעַסְקָא בְּהוֹן מִיַנְקוּתִיךְ שִׁלְטוֹנַי תּוּקְפִיךְ גְבַר לָקֳבֵיל אַפּוֹהִי גְלוּ לֵית דְפָרִיק לִיךְ:


Thus shall the workers of thy lies be in thee, with whom thou wast occupied from thy youth; the princes of thy power, shall go into captivity; each one straight before his face,: none shall save thee.



## Chapter 48

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:1)


שְׁמָעוּ דָא בֵית יַעֲקֹב דָמִתְקְרָן בִּשְׁמָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וּמִזַרְעִית יְהוּדָא נְפַקוּ דִגְזַר לְהוֹן קְיָם בִּשְׁמָא דַייָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל לָא יִפְסֵיק דָכְרָנֵיהוֹן הֲלָא קַיָם מֵימְרֵהּ בִּקְשׁוֹט וּבְזָכוּ:


Hear ye this, O house of Jacob, who are called by the name of Israel, and are come forth from the stock of Judah, with whom he hath made a covenant in the name of the Lord, the God of Israel, that the remembrance of them shall not cease. Shall not His WORD stand in truth and righteousness?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:2)


אֲרֵי בְקַרְתָּא דְקוּדְשָׁא חוּלָקְהוֹן וְעַל אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל רוֹחֲצָנְהוֹן יְיָ צְבָאוֹת שְׁמוֹ:


For their portion is in the holy city, and their confidence is in the God of Israel; The Lord of hosts is His name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:3)


קַדְמֵיתָא מִבְּכֵן חַוֵתִי וּמִמֵימְרִי נְפַקָא וּמְסַרְתִּינוּן בִּתְכֵיף עֲבַדְתִּינוּן וַאֲתָאָה:


I have declared the former things from the beginning; and they have gone forth from my WORD, and I announced them; suddenly I will do them, and they shall come to pass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:4)


גְלֵי קֳדָמַי דְאַתְּ סַרְבָן וְקָשֵׁי כְּבַרְזְלָא קָדְלָךְ וּבֵית עֵינָךְ חֲסִין כִּנְחָשָׁא:


I knew that thou wouldest be a rebel, and thy neck would be as hard iron, and thy brow strong as brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:5)


וְחַוֵיתִי לָךְ מִבְּכַן עַד לָא יֵיתְיָן בְּסַרְתָּךְ דִלְמָא תֵימַר דְחַלְתִּי עֲבַדְתִּינוּן וְצַלְמִי וּמַתָּכִי אַתְכִינוּן:


Therefore I have declared these things to thee from the beginning; before they came to pass I announced them unto thee: lest thou shouldest say, Mine idol hath done them, and my molten image hath prepared them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:6)


שְׁמַעְתָּא הָא אִתְגְלִיאַת לְכָל עַמָא מַה דְאִתְגְלִיאַת לְכוֹן וְאַתּוּן הֲלָא תְחַווּן בְּסַרְתָּךְ חַדְתָּן מִכְּעַן וּנְטִירַן וְלָא יְדַעְתִּינוּן:


Hast thou heard whether that was revealed to any people which was revealed unto thee? and as for you, will ye not declare it? I have shewed thee new things from this time, and hidden things, and thou didst not know them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:7)


כְּעַן אִתְבְּרִיאָה וְלָא מִבְכֵן וּקְדָם יוֹם מֵיתֵיהוֹן וְלָא בְסַרְתִּינוּן דִלְמָא תֵימַר הָא יְדַעְתִּינוּן:


They are created now, and not of old; yea, I have not announced them before the day of their coming to pass, lest thou shouldest say, Behold, I have known them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:8)


אַף לָא שְׁמַעְתְּ לְמִלֵי נְבִיַיָא אַף לָא קַבֵּילְתָּא אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתָא אַף לְפִתְגָמֵי בִּרְכַּן וּלְוָטִין קְיָמִי דַאֲקֵמִית עִמְכוֹן בְּחוֹרֵב לָא אַרִיכֵינְתָּא אוּדְנָךְ לְקַבָּלָא אֲרֵי גְלֵי קֳדָמַי דְשַׁקָרָא תְשַׁקֵר וּמְרוֹד מִמְעַיִן יִתְקְרֵי לָךְ:


Yea, thou didst not listen to the words of the prophets; yea, thou didst not accept the instruction of the law; yea, thou didst not incline thine ear to receive the words of the blessings and curses of my covenant which I made with you in Horeb; for it was manifest to me that thou wouldest altogether deal falsely, and be called a rebel from the womb.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:9)


בְּדִיל שְׁמִי אַרְחֵיק רוּגְזִי וְתוּשְׁבַּחְתִּי אָקִים לָךְ בְּדִיל דְלָא לְשֵׁיצָיוּתָךְ:


For my name's sake, I will defer mine anger, and for my praise I will preserve thee, so as not to destroy thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:10)


הָא צְרַפְתָּךְ וְלָא בִּכְסַף בְּחַרְתָּךְ בִּדְחוֹק מִסְכֵּינוּ:


Behold, I have refined thee, but not in the crucible of silver; I tried thee in the distress of poverty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:11)


בְּדִיל שְׁמִי בְּדִיל מֵימְרִי אַעְבֵּיד דְלָא יִתָּחָל וִיקָרִי דְאִתְגְלֵתִי עֲלֵיכוֹן לְעַם אוֹחֲרָן לָא אֶתֵּן:


For my name's sake, for the sake of my WORD, that it may not be profaned, I will do it, and my glory in which I have revealed myself unto you, I will not give to another people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:12)


קַבִּילוּ לְמֵימְרִי דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב וְיִשְׂרָאֵל מְזַמְנָא אֲנָא הוּא אֲנָא הוּא דְמִלְקָדְמִין אַף עַלְמֵי עַלְמַיָא דִילִי אִינוּן וּבַר מִנִי לֵית אֱלָהָא:


Hearken unto my WORD, ye who are of the house of Jacob and Israel, my called, I am He, who was of old, yea, ages after ages are mine, and besides me there is no God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:13)


אַף בְּמֵימְרִי שַׁכְלֵילִית אַרְעָא וּבִגְבוּרְתִּי תָּלֵית שְׁמַיָא קְרֵית אֲנָא לְהוֹן קָמוּ כַּחֲדָא:


Yea, by my WORD I have laid the foundation of the earth, and by my might I have suspended the heavens: when I call unto them, they stand up together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:14)


אִתְכְּנָשׁוּ כוּלְהוֹן וּשְׁמָעוּ מַן בְּהוֹן חַוִי יַת אִלֵין יְיָ דְרַחֵם לֵיהּ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל יַעְבֵּיד רְעוּתֵהּ בְּבָבֶל וּתְקוֹף דְרַע גְבוּרְתֵּהּ יַגְלֵי בְכַסְדָאֵי:


All ye, assemble yourselves, and hear: who among them hath declared these things ever since the Lord loved Israel? He will do His pleasure on Babylon, and He will reveal the strength of His arm on the Chaldeans.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:15)


אֲנָא בְּמֵימְרִי גְזָרִית קְיָם עִם אַבְרָהָם אֲבוּכוֹן אַף רַבִּיתֵהּ אָעִילְתֵּהּ לְאַרַע בֵּית שְׁכִינְתִּי וְאַצְלַח יַת אוֹרְחֵהּ:


I by my WORD have made a covenant with Abraham your father; yea, I have called him, I have brought him into the land of the place of my Shekinah, and I prospered his way.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:16)


אִתְקְרָבוּ לְמֵימְרִי שְׁמָעוּ דָא לָא מִן אַוְלָא בְּסִתְרָא מַלְלֵית מֵעִדַן דְאִתְפְּרִישׁוּ עַמְמַיָא מִדְחַלְתִּי תַּמָן אַבְרָהָם אֲבוּכוֹן קָרִיבְתֵּהּ לְפוּלְחָנִי אֲמַר נְבִיָא וּכְעַן יְיָ אֱלֹהִים שַׁלְחַנִי וּמֵימְרֵהּ:


Come ye near to my WORD; hear ye this; I have not spoken in secret from the beginning; at the time when the nations separated themselves from fearing me, at that time I brought Abraham your father to my service. The prophet saith: And now the Lord God, and His WORD, hath sent me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:17)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ פְּרִיקָךְ קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מַלֵיף לָךְ לַהֲנָאָה מְחַוֵי לָךְ בְּאוֹרַח דִתְהָךְ:


Thus saith the Lord, thy Redeemer, the Holy One of Israel; I am the Lord thy God, who teacheth thee what will profit thee, who sheweth the way wherein thou shouldest walk.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:18)


אִילוּ אֲצִיתָא לְפִקוּדַי וַהֲוֵי פוֹן כִּשְׁפַע נְהַר פְּרָת שְׁלָמָךְ וּזְכוּתָךְ כְּגַלֵי יַמָא:


If thou hadst hearkened to my commandments, thy peace would certainly have been as the overflowing of the river Euphrates, and thy righteousness as the waves of the sea.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:19)


וַהֲווֹ פּוֹן סַגִין כְּחָלָא דְיַמָא בְּנָךְ וּבְנֵי בְנָךְ כְּפֵּירוּדוֹהִי לָא יִפְסוֹק וְלָא יִשְׁתֵּצֵי שְׁמֵיהּ דְיִשְׂרָאֵל מִן קֳדָמַי לַעֲלַם:


Thy seed had certainly been as numerous as the sand; and thy children's children as the gravel thereof: the name of Israel should not have ceased nor have been destroyed from before me for ever.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:20)


פּוּקוּ מִבָּבֶל עֲרוּקוּ מִמְדִינַת אֲרַע כַּסְדָאֵי בְּקַל תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא חַווּ וּבַסַרוּ דָא אַפְקוּהָא עַד סְיָפֵי אַרְעָא אֱמָרוּ פְּרַק יְיָ עַבְדוֹהִי דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב:


Go ye forth from Babylon, flee ye from the province of the land of the Chaldeans, declare ye with a voice of singing, bring the joyful tidings of this, bring it to the ends of the earth, say ye, The Lord hath redeemed His servants of the house of Jacob.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:21)


וְלָא אַצְחֵינוּן בְּחָרְבָתָא דַבְּרִינוּן מַיָא מִטוּנְרָא אַפִּיק לְהוֹ וּבְזַע טִינְרָא וּנְגוּדוּ מַיָא:


He will not suffer them to thirst in the desert; He will guide them; He will cause water to flow for them from the rock: yea, He will cleave the rock, and the waters shall gush out.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 48:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/48:22)


לֵית שְׁלָם אֲמַר יְיָ לְרַשִׁיעַיָא:


There is no peace, saith the Lord, unto the wicked.



## Chapter 49

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:1)


קַבִּילוּ נַגְוָן לְמֵימְרִי וְאַצִיתוּ מַלְכְּוָן מֵרָחִיק יְיָ עַד לָא הֲוֵיתִי זַמְנַנִי מִמְעֵי אִמִי אִדְכַּר שְׁמִי:


Listen, O isles, unto my WORD, and hearken, O kingdoms, from afar; the Lord called me before I was; He hath made mention of my name from the bowels of my mother.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:2)


וְשַׁוִי פִּתְגָמוֹהִי בְּפוּמִי כַּחֲרַב חֲרִיפָא בִּטְלַל גְבוּרְתֵּהּ אָגִין עָלַי וְשַׁוְיַנִי כְּגִיר בְּחיר דִי בְּבֵית אֲזֵינִין מִסַתָּר:


And he hath put His words in my mouth like a sharp sword; in the shadow of His power hath He protected me, and He hath made me like a choice arrow, which is hidden in the quiver.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:3)


וַאֲמַר לִי עַבְדִי אַתְּ יִשְׂרָאֵל דְבָךְ אֶשְׁתַּבָּח:


And said unto me, Thou art my servant, O Israel, in whom I will be glorified.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:4)


וַאֲנָא אָמָרֵית לְרֵיקָנוּ לָאֵיתִי לִלְמָא וְלָא מִדָעַם חֵילֵי שֵׁיצֵיתִי בְּרַם דִינִי גְלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וַאֲגַר עוֹבָדִי קֳדָם אֱלָהִי:


And as for me, I said, I have laboured in vain, I have spent my strength for naught, and in vain; but my cause is known to the Lord, and the reward of my work is before my God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:5)


וּכְעַן אֲמַר יְיָ דְאַתְקְנַנִי מִמְעַיִן לְמֶהֱוֵי עָבֵיד פַלָח קָדָמוֹהִי לַאֲתָבָא דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב לְפוּלְחָנֵהּ וְיִשְׂרָאֵל לִדְחַלְתֵּהּ יִתְקְרַב וְיַקִירְנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וּמֵימַר אֱלָהִי הֲוָה בְסַעֲדִי:


And now, saith the Lord, that formed me from the womb to be a perfect servant before Him, to make the house of Jacob to return to His service, and Israel shall be brought to fear Him and we shall be glorious before the Lord; and the WORD of my God shall be my support.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:6)


וַאֲמַר הַזְעֵיר לְכוֹן דְאַתּוּן מִתְקְרַן עַבְדַי לַאֲקָמָא יַת שִׁבְטֵי יַעֲקֹב וְגַלְוַת יִשְׂרָאֵל לַאֲתָבָא וְאֶתְּנִינָךְ לְנֵיהוֹר עַמְמִין לְמֶהֱוֵי פּוּרְקָנִי עַד סְיָפֵי אַרְעָא:


And He said, Is it too little for you that ye should be called my servants, to raise up the tribes of Jacob, and to bring back the captivity of Israel? Yea, I will give thee for a light to the nations, to be my salvation unto the ends of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:7)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ פַּרְקֵהּ דְיִשְׂרָאֵל קַדִישֵׁהּ לְדִבְּסִירִין בֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא לְדִמְטַלְטְלִין בֵּינֵי מַלְכְּוָתָא לִדְהֲווֹ עָבְדִין לְשִׁלְטוֹנִין לְהוֹן מַלְכִין יֶחְזוּן וִיקוּמוּן רַבְרְבִין וְיִסְגְדוּן בְּדִיל יְיָ דִמְהֵימָן קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִתְרְעֵי בָךְ:


Thus saith the Lord, the Redeemer of Israel, and His Holy One, to them that are despised among the nations, to them that are scattered among the kingdoms, to them that are the servants to rulers : Kings shall look at them; and princes shall rise and fall prostrate, because of the Lord that is faithful, and the Holy One of Israel, and He shall delight in thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:8)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ בְּעִידַן דְאַתּוּן עָבְדִין רְעוּתִי אֲנָא מְקַבֵּיל צְלוֹתְכוֹן וּבְיוֹם עָקָא אֲנָא מְקַיֵם פּוּרְקַן וְסָעֵיד לְכוֹן וְאֶתְקְנִינָךְ וְאֶתְּנִינָךְ לִקְיַם עַם לַאֲקָמָא צַדִיקַיָא דְשָׁכְבִין בְּעַפְרָא לְאַחְסָנָא יְרוּתָן דְצָדְיָן:


Thus saith the Lord, at the time when ye do my will, I hear your prayer, and in the day of distress I uphold you with salvation and support; for I will maintain thee, and I will give thee for a covenant with the people, to raise up the righteous that lie in the dust, to cause to inherit the desolate heritages;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:9)


לְמֵימָר לִדְאֲסִירִין בֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא פּוּקוּ וְלִדְעַגִינִין בֵּינֵי מַלְכְּוָתָא כַּד בְּקִבְלָא אִתְגְלוּ לְנֵיהוֹר עַל אוֹרְחָן יִשְׁרוּן וּבְכָל נֶגְדֵין בֵּית מֵישְׁרֵיהוֹן:


Saying to them who are prisoners among the nations, Go forth to them who are kept back in darkness among the kingdoms; shew yourselves to the light; they shall dwell in all the paths, and by all the rivers shall be the place of their dwelling.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:10)


לָא יִכְפְנוּן וְלָא יִצְחוּן וְלָא יִלְקִינוּן שְׁרָבָא וְשִׁמְשָׁא אֲרֵי דַעֲתִיד לְרַחֲמָא עֲלֵיהוֹן יְדַבְּרִינוּן וְעַל מַבּוּעֵי מַיִין יַשְׁרִינוּן:


They shall not hunger nor thirst, neither shall the heat nor the sun smite them: for He that shall have mercy on them shall guide them, and He shall cause them to dwell by the springs of water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:11)


וַאֲשַׁוֵי כָּל טוּרַיָא כְּבִישִׁין קֳדָמֵיהוֹן כְּאוֹרַח וְכִבְשַׁיָא יְרוּמוּן:


And I will make all the mountains trodden paths like a way before them, and the foot-paths shall be cast up.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:12)


הָא אִלֵין מֵרָחִיק יֵיתוֹן וְהָא אִלֵין מִצִפּוּנָא וּמִמַעֲרְבָא וְאִלֵין מֵאֲרַע דָרוֹמָא:


Behold, these shall come from far: and, lo, these from the North and from the West, and these from the land of the South.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:13)


שַׁבָּחוּ שְׁמַיָא וּבוּעִי אַרְעָא וְדוּצוּ טוּרַיָא תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא אֲרֵי עֲתִיד יְיָ לְנַחֲמָא עַמֵיהּ וְעַל עִנְוְתָנוֹהִי יְרַחֵם:


Sing, O heavens; and be joyful, O earth; and be glad, O mountains, with praise, for the Lord will comfort His people, and will have mercy upon His afflicted.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:14)


וַאֲמַרֵת צִיוֹן סַלֵיק שְׁכִינְתֵּהּ יְיָ מִנִי וַיָי רַחֲקָנִי:


But Zion said, The Lord hath taken His Shekinah from me, and the Lord hath cast me away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:15)


הַאֶפְשַׁר דְתִתְנְשֵׁי אִתְּתָא בְּרָהּ מִלְרַחָמָא עַל בַּר מְעָהָא מְתִיבָא כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲמַרַת אִם לֵית קֳדָמוֹהִי אִתְנַשָׁאָה דִלְמָא לָא מִתְנַשֵׁי לִי יַת דַעֲבָדִית עֵגֶל דִדְהַב אֲמַר לָהּ נְבִיָא אַף אִלֵין אִתְנַשְׁיָן מְתִיבָא וְאֶמְרָה לֵיהּ אִם אִית קֳדָמוֹהִי אִתְנְשָׁאָה דִלְמָא מִתְנְשֵׁי לִי יַת דַאֲמָרִית בְּסִינַי נַעְבֵּד וּנְקַבֵּל מֵיתִיב וְאָמַר לָהּ מֵימְרִי לָא יְרַחֲקִינָךְ:


Is it possible that a mothers can forget her child, that she should not have compassion on the son of her womb? The congregation of Israel answered and said: If there is no forgetfulness with Him, perhaps He will not forget that I have made a calf of gold? The prophet said to her, Yea, these things shall be forgotten. And she said to him, If there is forgetfulness with Him, perhaps it will be forgotten that I said on Sinai: We will do and obey? He answered and said to her: My Word shall not cast you off.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:16)


הָא כִּדְעַל יְדִין אַתְּ צַיְרָא קֳדָמָי שׁוּרָךְ לְקִבְלִי תְּדִירָא:


Behold, as upon the hands thou art engraven before me; thy walls are continually before me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:17)


יוֹחוּן בְנַיְכִי חָרְבָתַיִךְ מְפַגְרָךְ וּמְחַרְבָּךְ מִנִיךְ יִגְלוֹן:


Those that shall build thy desolate places shall make haste, thy destroyers and they that made thee waste, shall go forth of thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:18)


זְקוּפִי יְרוּשְׁלֵם סְחוֹר סְחוֹר עֵינָךְ וַחֲזָא כָּל בְּנֵי עַם גַלְוָתִיךְ מִתְכַּנְשִׁין וְאָתָן לְגַוִיךְ קְיַם אֲנָא אֲמַר יְיָ אֲרֵי כוּלְהוֹן לִיךְ כִּלְבוּשׁ דִיקָר וְעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן בְּגַוִיךְ כְּקִישׁוּט כַּלָתָא:


Lift up thine eyes round about, O Jerusalem, and see; all the children of the people of thy captivity are gathered together and come unto thee. As I live, saith the Lord, all these shall be unto thee as a vesture of glory, and their works in the midst of thee as the ornament of a bride.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:19)


אֲרֵי חָרְבָתַיִךְ וְצַדְיוּתִיךְ וַאֲרַע מְפַגְרוּתִיךְ אֲרֵי כְעַן תִּדַחֲקוּן מִיָתִיב וְיִתְרַחֲקוּן מְסַלְעֲמָךְ:


For thy waste and desolate places, and the land of thy destruction, surely now shall be too strait by reason of the inhabitants, and they that swallowed thee up shall be far away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:20)


מִכְּעַן יֵימְרוּן חַד חַד בְּגַוִיךְ בְּנֵי עַם גַלְוָתִיךְ עָק לִי אַתְרָא רְוַח לִי וְאֵיתִיב:


Henceforth shall each of the children of thy captivity in the midst of thee say: The place is too strait for me: make room for me that I may dwell.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:21)


וְתֵימְרִין בְּלִבִּיךְ מַן רַבִּי לִי יַת אִלֵין וַאֲנָא תִכְלָא וִיחִידָא גַלְיָא וּמְטַלְטְלָא וְאִלֵין מַן רַבִּי הָא אֲנָא אִשְׁתַּאֲרֵית בִּלְחוֹדִי אִלֵין הֵיכָא הֲווֹ:


Then thou shalt say in thine heart, Who hath nourished up these for me, seeing I have lost my children, and have been solitary; an exile, and an outcast; who then hath brought up these? Lo! I was left alone; these, where were they?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:22)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים הָא אֶגְלֵי בְּעַמְמַיָא גְבוּרְתִי וְעַל מַלְכְּוָתָא אֲרִים נִסִי וְיֵיתוּן בְנָךְ בְּצִיבִין וּבְנָתָךְ עַל פַּרְוָן יִתְנַטְלָן:


Thus saith the Lord God, Behold, I will reveal my power among the Gentiles, and unto the kingdoms I will raise my standard, and thy children shall come in palanquins, and thy daughters shall be carried upon the shoulders.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:23)


וִיהוֹן מַלְכַיָא תּוּרְבְּיָנָךְ וּמַלְכְּוַתְהוֹן יְשַׁמְשׁוּנִיךְ עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן עַל אַרְעָא יִשְׁתַּטְחוּן לְמִבְעֵי מִנִיךְ וַעֲפַר רִגְלָךְ יְלַחֲכוּן וְתֵידְעִין אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ דְלָא יִבְהֲתוּן צַדִיקַיָא דִמְסַבְרִין לְפוּרְקָנִי:


And kings shall be thy nursing fathers, and their queens shall minister unto thee; they shall prostrate themselves with their faces to the earth to supplicate to thee, and lick the dust of thy feet; and thou shalt know that I am the Lord; because the righteous that hope for my salvation shall not be ashamed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:24)


הֲיִתְנְסֵיב מִגָבָּרָא עֲדִי וְאִם דִשְׁבוֹ זַכָּאִין יִשְׁתַּזֵיב: [ת"א] אֲמָרֵת יְרוּשְׁלֵם הַאֶפְשַׁר דְיִתְנְסֵיב מֵעֵשָׂו רַשִׁיעָא דַאֲמִיר עֲלֵהּ דְצַדִיקַיָא הוּא יִשְׁתֵּיזֵיב:


Shall the spoil be taken from the mighty, and shall that which the just have taken captive be delivered? [ANOTHER PARAPHRASE — Jerusalem says, Is it possible that the prey shall be taken from Esau the wicked, which he took from me? concerning whom it was said, By thy sword thou shalt live. Or the captive whom Ishmael led captive, concerning whom it has been said, That which belongeth to the righteous shall he deliver?]


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:25)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אַף דִשְׁבוֹ גִבָּרִין אָתִיב וְדַעֲדוֹ תַקִיפִין אֱשֵׁיזֵיב וְיַת פּוּרְעֲנוּתִיךְ אֲנָא אִתְפְּרַע וְיַת בְּנָךְ אֲנָא אֶפְרוֹק: [ת"א] אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אַף עֲדָאָה דַעֲדָא מִנִיךְ עֵשָׂו גִבָּרָא יִתְנְסֵב מִנֵהּ וְשִׁבְיָא דִשְׁבָא מִנִיךְ יִשְׁמָעֵאל גֵיוְתָנָא דַאֲמִיר עֲלֵהּ עָרוֹד בֶּאֱנָשָׁא יִשְׁתֵּיזֵיב וְיַת פּוּרְעֲנוּתִיךְ אֲנָא אִתְפְּרַע וְיַת בְּנָךְ אֲנָא אֶפְרוֹק:


For thus saith the Lord, I will surely restore the captivity of the mighty, and I will deliver the prey of the terrible: Surely the captivity of the mighty I will restore, and the prey of the terrible I will deliver, and I will take vengeance for thee, and I will save thy children. [ANOTHER PARAPHRASE — For thus saith the Lord: Yea, the prey, which the mighty Esau hath taken of thee, shall be taken from him, and the captivity, which the proud Ishmael led captive (concerning whom it has been said: He shall be a conqueror among men) be delivered and I will save thy children.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 49:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/49:26)


וְאֶתֵּן יַת בְּסַר דַהֲווֹ מוֹנָן לִיךְ מֵיכַל לְכָל עוֹפָא דִשְׁמַיָא וּכְמָא דְמִתְרַוָן מֵחֲמַר מְרֵית כֵּן חַיַת בְּרָא מִדִמְהוֹן תִּתְרַוֵי וְיֵדְעוּן כָּל בִּסְרָא אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ פָּרְקִיךְ וּמְשֵׁיזְבִיךְ תַּקִיפָא דְיַעֲקֹב:


And I will give the flesh of them who oppress thee for food to every fowl of the heavens, and as they are drunken with new wine, so the beasts of the field shall be drunk with their blood, and all flesh shall know, that I, the Lord, am thy Saviour and thy Redeemer, the mighty One of Jacob.



## Chapter 50

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:1)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֵידָא הִיא אִגְרַת פִּטוּרִין דִיהָבֵית לִכְנִשְׁתְּכוֹן אֲרֵי אִתְרַחֲקַת אוֹ מִן גְבַר דְלֵהּ חוֹבָא קֳדָמַי דְזַבְּנֵית יַתְכוֹן לֵהּ הָא בְחוֹבֵיכוֹן אִזְדַבַּנְתּוּן וּבְמָרְדֵיכוֹן אִתְרַחֲקַת כְּנִשַׁתְכוֹן:


Thus saith the Lord, Where is the bill of divorcement, which I gave to your congregation, that she is cast off? Or who is the man who has debt against me, unto whom I sold you? Behold, for your sins ye were sold, and for your congregation was put away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:2)


מָא דֵין שְׁלִיחַת נְבִיֵי וְלָא תָבוּ אִתְנַבִּיאוּ וְלָא קַבִּילוּ הַאִתְקְפָדָא אִתְקְפָדַת גְבוּרְתִּי מִלְמִפְרַק וְאִם לֵית קֳדָמַי חֵיל לְשֵׁיזָבָא הָא בִמְזוֹפִיתִי אַחֲרִיב יַמָא אֱשַׁוֵי נַהֲרִין מַדְבְּרָא יִסְרוֹן נוּנֵיהוֹן מִבְּלִי מַיָא וִימוּתוּן בְּצַחוּתָא:


Wherefore have I sent my prophets, and they have not repented? They prophesied, but they obeyed not. Is my power altogether deficient, that I cannot save ? Behold, at my rebuke I dry up the sea, I make the rivers a wilderness: their fish stinketh, because there is no water, and dieth for thirst.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:3)


אֲכַסֵי שְׁמַיָא כִּדְבְּקַבְלָא וּבְסַקָא אֲשַׁוֵי כְּסוּתְהוֹן:


I cover the heavens as with blackness, and as with sackcloth I make their covering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:4)


יְיָ אֱלֹהִים יְהַב לִי לְשַׁן דְמַלְפִין לְהוֹדָעָא אַלְפָא לְצַדִיקַיָא דִמְשַׁלְהָן לְפִתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא חוּכְמָא בְכֵן בִּצְפַר בִּצְפַר מַקְדִים לְשַׁלָחָא נְבִיאוֹהִי מָאִים יִתְפַּתְחַן אוּדְנֵי חַיָבַיָא וִיקַבְּלוּן אוּלְפַן:


The Lord God hath given me a tongue to teach, to give knowledge, and to instruct the righteous with wisdom, who weary themselves with the words of the law; each morning rising up early to send His prophets, that perhaps the ears of the sinners might be opened, and they might receive instruction.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:5)


יְיָ אֱלֹהִים שָׁלָחַנִי לְאִתְנַבָּאָה וַאֲנָא לָא סְרֵיבֵית לַאֲחוֹרָא לָא אִסְתַּחֲרֵית:


The Lord God hath sent me to prophesy, and I did not refuse, neither turned away back.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:6)


גַבֵּי יְהָבֵית לְמָחָן וְלִיסְתֵּי לְמָרְטָן אַפִּי לָא טַמְרֵית מֵאִתְכְּנָעוּ וָרוֹק:


I gave my back to the smiters, and I my cheeks to them that plucked off the hair : I hid not my face from shame and spitting.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:7)


וַיָי אֱלֹהִים סָעִיד לִי עַל כֵּן לָא אִתְכְּנָעִית עַל כֵּן שַׁוֵיתִי אַפִּי תַקִיפִין כְּטִינָרָא וְיָדַעֲנָא אֲרֵי לָא אִתְבְּהֵית:


The Lord God is my helper, therefore shall I not be confounded : therefore have I set my face strong as a rock, and I know that I shall not be confounded.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:8)


קְרִיבָא זְכוּתִי מַן יְדִין עִמִי נְקּוּם כַּחֲדָא מִן בְּעֵיל דִינִי יִתְקְרֵיב לְוָתִי:


My righteousness is near; who is he that will contend with me? Let us stand forth together: who is my adversary? let him come near me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:9)


הָא יְיָ אֱלֹהִים סָעִיד לִי מַן הוּא דִי יְחַיְבִינַנִי הָא כוּלְהוֹן כִּלְבוּשָׁא דִבְלִי וּכְעָשָׁא אָכִיל לֵהּ:


Behold, the Lord God is my helper; who is he that shall condemn me? lo, they all shall be as a garment that waxeth old, yea, as when the moth eateth it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:10)


(אֲמַר נְבִיָא עֲתִיד קוּדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא לְמֶהֱוֵי אֲמַר לְעַמְמַיָא) מַן בְּכוֹן מִדַחֲלַיָא דַייָ דִי שְׁמַע בְּקַל עַבְדֵהּ נְבִיָא דַעֲבַד אוֹרַיְתָא בְעָקָא כְּגִבַּר דִמְהַלֵךְ בְּקָבְלָא וְלֵית זְהוֹר לֵהּ מִתְרְחִיץ בִּשְׁמָא דַייָ וּמִסְתְּמִיךְ עַל פּוּרְקָנָא דֶאֱלָהֵהּ:


The prophet said: The Holy One, blessed be He, shall say to all the nations: Who is among you that feareth the Lord, that obeyeth the voice of His servant the prophet, who keepeth the law, being in distress, as a man that walketh in darkness and has no light, he trusting in the name of the Lord, will stay upon the salvation of his God?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 50:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/50:11)


(מְתִיבִין עַמְמַיָא וְאָמְרִין קֳדָמוֹהִי רִבּוֹנָנָא לָא אֶפְשַׁר לָנָא לְמֶעְסַק בְּאוֹרַיְתָא אֲרֵי כָּל יוֹמָנָא אִתְגְרֵינָא דֵין עִם דֵין בִּקְרָבָא וְכַד נְצַחְנָא דֵין לְדֵין אוֹקֵידְנָא בָתֵּיהוֹן וּשְׁבִינָא טַפְלְהוֹן וְנִכְסֵיהוֹן וּבַהֲדָא גַוְנָא שְׁלִימוּ יוֹמָנָא וְלָא אֶפְשַׁר לָנָא לְמֶעְסַק בְּאוֹרַיְתָא מְתִיב קוּדְשָׁא בְרִיךְ הוּא וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן) הָא כּוּלְכוֹן מְגָרֵן בְּאֶשְׁתָּא מַתְקְפֵי חֶרֶב אֱזִילוּ פִּילוּ בְּאֶשְׁתָּא דִגְרֵיתוּן וּבְחַרְבָּא דִתְקֵיפְתּוּן מִמֵימְרִי הֲוַת דָא לְכוֹן לְתַקְלוּתְכוֹן תְּחוּבוּן:


The nations answered and said to him: O our Lord! it is not possible for us to occupy ourselves with the law; because we continually wage war against each other, and when we gain the victory one over the other, we burn their houses with fire, and bring their children and their treasures into captivity, and in this manner our days are spent; thus it is impossible for us to occupy ourselves with the law. The Holy One, blessed be He, answered and said unto them: Behold, all of you who stir up a fire, and lay hold on the sword; go ye, fall into the fire which ye have stirred up, and by the sword, which ye have laid hold on. This shall be unto you from my WORD, ye shall turn to your destruction.



## Chapter 51

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:1)


קַבִּילוּ לְמֵימְרִי רָדְפֵי קוּשְׁטָא דְבָעָן אוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אִסְתַּכָּלוּ דִי בַּחֲצִיבָא מִטִינָרָא אִתְחָצַבְתּוּן וְכִפְסוּלָא דְמִגוּב רֵיקָן אִתְפְסִלְתּוּן:


Hearken to my WORD, ye that follow after truth, who seek instruction from the Lord; consider that ye were cut out like a stone' hewn from a rock, that ye were cut out like a mass hewn out of a hollow cistern.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:2)


אִסְתַּכָּלוּ בְּאַבְרָהָם אֲבוּכוֹן וּבְשָׂרָה דְאַעְדִיאַתְכוֹן אֲרֵי חַד הֲוָה אַבְרָהָם יְחִידִי בְּעַלְמָא קָרֵיבְתֵּהּ לְפוּלְחָנִי וּבֵרַכְתֵּהּ וְאַסְגֵיתֵהּ:


Consider Abraham your father, and Sarah who conceived you: for Abraham was one alone in the world, and I brought him to my service, I also blessed him, and multiplied him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:3)


אֲרֵי עֲתִיד יְיָ לְנַחֲמָא צִיוֹן לְנַחֲמָא כָּל חָרְבָתָהָא וִישַׁוֵי מַדְבְּרָהָא כְּעֵדֶן וּמֵישְׁרָאָה כְּגִינְתָא דַייָ בִּיעַ וְחֶדְוָא יִשְׁתְּכַח בָּהּ מַעֲלֵי תוֹדָתָא וְקַל דִמְשַׁבְּחִין:


For the Lord will comfort Zion, He will comfort all her waste places, and He will make her wilderness like Eden, and her desert like the garden of the Lord; joy and gladness shall be found therein, they that offer thanksgiving, and the voice of them that praise.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:4)


קַבִּילוּ לְמֵימְרִי עַמִי וּכְנִשְׁתִּי לְפוּלְחָנִי אֲצִיתָא אֲרֵי אוֹרַיְתָא מִן קֳדָמַי תִּיפּוֹק וְדִינַי כְּנֵיהוֹר לֵהּ עַמְמִין אֲגַלֵי יִשְׁתַּבְּחוּן:


Listen to my WORD, O my people, and give ear, O my congregation, to my service; for the law shall go forth from me, and my judgment like a light; the nations which I led into captivity shall praise it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:5)


קְרִיבָא זְכוּתִי נְפַק פּוּרְקָנִי וּבִתְקוֹף דְרַע גְבוּרְתִּי עַמְמַיָא יִתְדְנוּן לְמֵימְרִי נַגְוָן יְסַבְּרוּן וְלִיתְקוֹף דְרַע גְבוּרְתִּי יִכְתְּרוּן:


My righteousness is near; my salvation is gone forth, and the nations shall be judged by the strength of the arm of my might; the isles shall hope for my WORD, and they shall wait for the strength of the arm of my might.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:6)


זְקִיפוּ לִשְׁמַיָא עֵינֵיכוֹן וְאִסְתַּכָּלוּ בְאַרְעָא מִלְרַע אֲרֵי שְׁמַיָא כִּתְנָנָא דְעָדִי כֵן יֶעְדוּ וְאַרְעָא כִּכְסוּתָא דְבָלְיָא כֵּן תִּבְלֵי וְיָתְבָהָא אַף אִינוּן הָכֵן יְמוּתוּן וּפוּרְקָנִי לַעֲלַם יְהֵי וְזָכוּתִי לָא תִתְעַכָּב:


Lift up your eyes to the heavens, and consider the earth beneath: for the heavens shall vanish away like smoke, and the earth that waxeth old like a garment, so shall it wax old, and its inhabitants, also, they shall die; but my salvation shall be for ever, and my righteousness shall not tarry.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:7)


קַבִּילוּ לְמֵימְרִי יָדְעֵי קוּשְׁטָא עַמָא דְאוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתִי בְּלִבְּהוֹן לָא תִדְחֲלוּן מֵחִיסוּדֵי בְנֵי אֱנָשָׁא וּמֵאִתְרַבְרְבוּתְהוֹן לָא תִתַבְרוּן:


Hearken unto my WORD, ye that know the truth, the people in whose heart is the instruction of my law; be ye not afraid of the reproaches of the sons of men, neither be terrified on account of their grandeur.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:8)


אֲרֵי בִלְבוּשָׁא דְאָכִיל לֵהּ עָשָׁא וּכְעִמְרָא דְאָחֵיד בֵהּ רוּקְבָא וְזָכוּתִי לַעֲלַם תְהֵי וּפוּרְקָנִי לְדָר דָרִין:


For they vanish like a garment, which the moth eateth, and like wool, which rottenness seizeth; but my righteousness shall be for ever, and my salvation from generation to generation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:9)


אִתְגְלָא לִבְשִׁי תְקוֹף גְבוּרָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אִתְגְלָא כְּיוֹמֵי קֳדָם דָרַיָא דְמִלְקָדָמִין הֲלָא בְדִילָךְ כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל תְּבָרֵית גִבָרַיָא שֵׁיצֵיתִי פַּרְעֹה וּמַשִׁרְיָתֵהּ דַהֲווּ תַּקִיפִין כְּתַנִינָא:


Reveal thyself, reveal thyself, put on the strength of might from the Lord; reveal thyself as in the days of old, in the generations which were at the beginning: was it not for thy sake, O congregation of Israel, that I broke the mighty, that I destroyed Pharaoh and his host, who were strong as a dragon?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:10)


הֲלָא בְדִילָךְ כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אַחְרְבֵית יַמָא מֵי תְהוֹם רַבָּה שַׁוֵיתִי עוּמְקֵי יַמָא אוֹרַח לְמֵיגָז מְשֵׁיזְבַיָא:


Was it not for thy sake, O congregation of Israel, that I dried up the sea, the water of the great deep, I made the depth of the sea a way for the ransomed to pass over?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:11)


וּפְרִיקַיָא דַייָ יִתְכַּנְשׁוּן מִבֵּינֵי גַלְוַתְהוֹן וְיַעֲלוּן לְצִיוֹן בְּתוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא וְחֶדְוַת עֲלָם תְּהֵי לְהוֹן דְלָא פָסְקָה וַעֲנַן יְקַר יְהֵי מַטֵיל עַל רֵישֵׁיהוֹן בִּיעַ וְחֶדְוָה יִשְׁכְּחוּן וְיֵסוּף מִנְהוֹן מִבֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל דָווֹנָא וְתֵינַחְתָּא:


Thus the redeemed of the Lord shall be gathered together out of their captivity, and come to Zion with singing; and everlasting joy shall be theirs, which shall not cease: and a cloud of glory shall shadow over their heads; they shall find joy and gladness, and there shall be an end of sorrow and sighing for the house of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:12)


אֲנָא אֲנָא הוּא מְנַחֶמְכוֹן מִמַן אַתּוּן דַחֲלִין מֵאֱנָשָׁא דְמָאֵית וּמִבַּר אֱנָשָׁא דִי כְעִסְבָּא חֲשִׁיב:


I, even I, am He that comforteth you: of whom are ye afraid? of a mortal man or of a son of man, who is counted like grass?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:13)


וְאִתְנְשֵׁיתָא פּוּלְחָנָא דַייָ דְעָבְדָךְ דִתְלָא שְׁמַיָא וְשַׁכְלֵיל אַרְעָא וּדְחַלְתָּא תְדִירָא כָּל יוֹמָא מִן קֳדָם חֲמַת מְעִיקָא כְּמָא דִמְתַקְנִין לְחַבָּלָא וּכְעַן אָן הִיא חֲמַת דִמְעִיקָא:


And that thou shouldest forget the worship of the Lord thy maker, who hath suspended the heavens and founded the earth; and shouldest fear continually all the day because of the fury of the oppressor, as if he were ready to destroy; and where is now the fury of the oppressor?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:14)


מוֹחִי פּוּרְעֲנוּתָא לְאִתְגְלָאָה וְלָא יְמוּתוּן צַדִיקַיָא לְחַבֵּל וְלָא יַחְסְרוּן מְזוֹנֵיהוֹן:


Vengeance hasteneth to be revealed, and the righteous shall not die in the pit, nor be in want of their food.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:15)


וַאֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ דִנְזֵיף בְּיַמָא וְהָמָן גַלוֹהִי יְיָ צְבָאוֹת שְׁמֵהּ:


For I am the Lord thy God, who rebuketh the sea, though its waves roar; the Lord of hosts is His name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:16)


וְשַׁוֵיתִי פִּתְגָמֵי נְבוּאָתִי בְּפוּמָךְ וּבִטְלַל גְבוּרְתִּי אַגְנֵית עֲלָךְ לְקַיָמָא עַמָא דַאֲמִיר עֲלֵיהוֹן דְיִסְגוּן כְּכוֹכְבֵי שְׁמַיָא וּלְשַׁכְלָלָא כְנִשְׁתָּא דַאֲמִיר עֲלֵיהוֹן דְיִסְגוּן כְּעַפְרָא דְאַרְעָא וּלְמֵימַר לִדְדָיְרִין בְּצִיוֹן עַמִי אַתּוּן:


I have put the words of my prophecy in thy mouth, and with the shadow of my power have I protected thee, to raise up the nation, concerning which it hath been promised that they shall be as many as the stars of heaven, and to establish the congregation it has been promised concerning them, that they shall multiply like the dust of the earth, and to say to the inhabitants of Zion, Ye are my people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:17)


אִתְרַבָּא אִתְרַבָּא קוּמִי יְרוּשְׁלֵם דְקַבֵּילְתְּ מִן קֳדָם יְיָ יַת כָּסָא דְחֵימְתֵהּ יַת פַּיְלֵי כָּסָא דִלְוָטָא שְׁתֵית אַעְרֵית:


Magnify thyself, magnify thyself; arise, O Jerusalem ! who hast received from the Lord the cup of His wrath; the vial of the cup of cursing thou hast drunk, and thou hast removed it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:18)


לֵית דִמְנַחֵם לָהּ מִכָּל בְּנִין דִילֵידַת וְלֵית דְמַתְקִיף בִּידָהּ מִכָּל בְּנִין דְרַבִּיאַת:


There is none to comfort thee, of all the sons which she hath brought forth; neither is there one to lay hold on her hand, of all the sons she hath nourished.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:19)


תַּרְתֵּין עָקָן אָתָאָה עֲלָךְ יְרוּשְׁלֵם לָא יְכֵילֵית לְמֵיקָם כַּד אַרְבַּע יֵיתוּן עֲלָךְ בִּיזָא וּתְבָרָא וְכַפְנָא וְחַרְבָּא לֵית דִי מְנַחֲמֵיךְ אֱלָהֵין אֲנָא:


Two tribulations have come upon thee, O Jerusalem, thou art not able to arise; when four shall come upon thee, spoiling, and destruction, and famine, and sword, there shall be none to comfort thee beside me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:20)


בְּנַיִךְ יְהוֹן מִטָרְפִין רָמָן בְּרֵישׁ כָּל שׁוּקַיָא כְּמִזְרָקֵי מְצַדָן מְלַן חֵימְתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ מְזוֹפִיתָא מִן קֳדָם אֱלָהָיִךְ:


Thy children shall be torn in pieces, they shall be cast at the head of all the streets as broken vials: they are full of the fury of the Lord, and of the rebuke of thy God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:21)


בְּכֵן שִׁמְעִי כְעַן דָא מְטַלְטַלְתָּא דְרַוְיָא מִן עָקָא וְלָא מִן חֲמָר:


Therefore hear now this, thou who art cast out, drunk with tribulation and not with wine:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:22)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר רִבּוֹנִיךְ יְיָ וֵאלָהָיִךְ דַעֲתִיד לְמֶעְבַּד פּוּרְעֲנוּת דִין עַמֵהּ הָא קַבֵּלִית מִן יְדִיךְ יַת כָּסָא דִלְוָטָא יַת פַּיְלֵי כָסָא דְחֵימְתִי לָא תוֹסִיפִין לְמִשְׁתָּהּ עוֹד:


Thus saith thy Lord, the Lord, even thy God will take vengeance of judgment for His people, behold, I have taken away the cup of cursing, the vial of the cup of my fury; thou shalt no more drink it again.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 51:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/51:23)


וְאֶמְסְרִינָהּ בְּיַד דַהֲווֹ מוֹנָן לִיךְ דַהֲווֹ אָמְרִין לְנַפְשֵׁיךְ אַמְאִיכִי וְנֶעְדֵי וְאַמְאֵיכְתְּ כְּאַרְעָא יְקָרִיךְ וַהֲוֵית כְּשׁוּק לְעָדָן:


But I will deliver it into the hand of them that afflict thee: which have said to thee, Bow down that we may go over; and thou hast humbled thy glory as the ground, and thou becamest like the street to them that passed by.



## Chapter 52

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:1)


אִתְגְלָא אִתְגְלָא לִבְשִׁי תוּקְפֵךְ צִיוֹן לִבְשִׁי לְבוּשִׁי תּוּשְׁבַּחְתִּיךְ יְרוּשָׁלַםִ קַרְתָּא דְקוּדְשָׁא אֲרֵי לָא יוֹסְפוּן וְיֶעְדוּן בִּיךְ עוֹד עָרְלִין וּמְסַאֲבִין:


Reveal thyself, reveal thyself, put on thy strength, O Zion; put on thy beautiful garments, O Jerusalem, the city of holiness: for the uncircumcised and the polluted shall pass no more through thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:2)


אִתְנַפַּצִי מֵעָפְרָא קוּמִי תְבִי עַל כּוּרְסֵי יְקָרָא יְרוּשְׁלֵם אִתְפְּסָקוּ חִנְקֵי צַוְרֵיכוֹן שְׁבִיָא כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְצִיוֹן:


Shake thyself from the dust; arise, and sit upon the throne of glory, O Jerusalem; the chains of thy neck are broken, O captive congregation of Zion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:3)


אֲרֵי כִדְנָן אֲמַר יְיָ מַגָן אִזְדַבִּנְתּוּן וְלָא בִכְסַף תִּתְפַּרְקוּן:


For thus saith the Lord, Ye were sold for nought; and ye shall be redeemed without money.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:4)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים לְמִצְרַיִם נְחַת עַמִי בְקַדְמֵיתָא לְאִתּוֹתָבָא תַמָן וְאַתּוּרָאָה בִּלְמָא אַנְסֵיהּ:


For thus saith the Lord God, My people went down aforetime into Egypt to sojourn there; and the Assyrian oppressed them without cause.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:5)


וּכְעַן עֲתִידְנָא לְמִפְרַק אֲמַר יְיָ אֲרֵי אִזְדַבַּן עַמִי מַגָן עַמְמַיָא דִשְׁלִיטוּ בְהוֹן מִשְׁתַּבְּחִין אֲמַר יְיָ וּתְדִירָא כָּל יוֹמָא עַל פּוּלְחָן שְׁמִי מַרְגְזִין:


Therefore now I am ready to redeem them, saith the Lord; for my people was sold for nought; the nations that ruled over them boasted, saith the Lord; and they continually, all the day, provoke them to anger, because of the worship of my name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:6)


בְּכֵן יִתְרַבָּא בְעַמְמַיָא שְׁמִי בְּכֵן בְּעִידָנָא הַהִיא תֵּידְעוּן אֲרֵי אֲנָא הוּא דְמַלְלֵית וּמֵימְרִי קַיָם:


Therefore my name shall be magnified among the nations: therefore at that time ye shall know, that I am He that hath spoken: and my WORD shall abide.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:7)


מַה יָאֲוָן עַל טוּרֵי אַרְעָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל רִגְלֵי מְבַסֵר מַשְׁמַע שְׁלָם מְבַסֵר טַב מַשְׁמַע פּוּרְקָן אֲמַר לִכְנִשְׁתָּא דְצִיוֹן אִיתְגְלִיאַת מַלְכוּתָא דֶאֱלָהָיִךְ:


How beautiful upon the mountains of the land of Israel are the feet of him that bringeth good tidings, that publisheth peace, that publisheth salvation, saying to the congregation of Zion, The kingdom of thy God is revealed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:8)


קַל פַּרְנָסַיִךְ דִמְרִימִין קָלְהוֹן כַּחֲדָא מְשַׁבְּחִין אֲרֵי בְעֵינֵיהוֹן יֶחֱזוּן גְבוּרָן דִי עֲבַד יְיָ כַּד יְתִיב שְׁכִנְתֵּהּ לְצִיוֹן:


The voice of thy rulers! They are lifting up their voice, together they offer praise; because with their eyes they see the mighty works which the Lord shall do, when He shall return His Shekinah to Zion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:9)


בּוּעוּ וְשַׁבַּחוּ כַּחֲדָא חָרְבַת יְרוּשְׁלֵם אֲרֵי עֲתִיד יְיָ לְנַחְמָא עַמֵהּ פָּרִיק יְרוּשְׁלֶם:


Rejoice and shout together, ye desolate places of Jerusalem, for the Lord shall comfort His people, Jerusalem shall be redeemed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:10)


גַלֵא יְיָ יַת דְרַע קוּדְשֵׁהּ לְעֵינֵי כָּל עַמְמַיָא וְיֶחֱזוּן כָּל דְבִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא יַת פּוּרְקָנָא דֶאֱלָהָנָא:


The Lord hath revealed His holy arm in the eyes of all the nations; and all that are in the ends of the earth shall see the salvation of our God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:11)


אִתְפְּרַשׁוּ אִתְפְּרַשׁוּ פּוּקוּ מִתַּמָן בִּמְסָאָב לָא תִקְרְבוּן פּוּקוּ מִגַוָהּ אִתְבְּחָרוּ נַטְלֵי מָנֵי בֵית מַקְדְשָׁא דַיָי:


Separate yourselves, separate yourselves, go ye out from thence, do not come near the unclean; come forth from the midst of her: those that carry the vessels of the house of the sanctuary of the Lord have been chosen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:12)


אֲרֵי לָא בִּבְהִילוּ תִּפְּקוּן מִבֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא וּבְעֵירוֹקֵי לָא תִתּוֹבְלוּן לְאַרְעֲכוֹן אֲרֵי מְדַבֵּר קֳדָמֵיכוֹן יְיָ וַעֲתִיד לְכַנָשָׁא גַלְוַתְכוֹן אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


For ye shall not go forth in haste from among the nations, nor be carried in flight to your land: for your guide is the Lord, and He that shall gather your captivity is the God of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:13)


הָא יַצְלַח עַבְדִי מְשִׁיחָא יְרוּם וְיִסְגֵי וְיִתְקוֹף לַחֲדָא:


Behold, my servant the messiah shall prosper, he shall be exalted and extolled, and he shall be very strong.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:14)


כְּמָא דִסְבַרוּ לֵיהּ בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל יוֹמִין סַגִיאִין דַהֲוָה חֲשִׁיךְ בֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא חֶזְוֵהוֹן וְזִיוְהוֹן מִבְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא:


As the house of Israel anxiously hoped for him many days, (which was poor among the nations; their appearance and their brightness being worse than that of the sons of men)


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 52:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/52:15)


כֵּן יְבַדַר עַמְמִין סַגִיאִין עֲלוֹהִי יִשְׁתְּקוּן מַלְכִין יְשַׁווּן יְדֵיהוֹן עַל פּוּמְהוֹן אֲרֵי דְלָא אִשְׁתָּעִיאוּ לְהוֹן חֲזוֹ וּדְלָא שְׁמָעוּ אִסְתַּכֵּלוּ:


Thus shall he scatter many nations; before him kings shall keep silence: they shall put their hands upon their mouths, for that which had not been told them shall they see: and that which they had not heard shall they consider.



## Chapter 53

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:1)


מַן הֵימִין לִבְסוֹרְתָּנָא דָא וּתְקוֹף דְרַע גְבוּרְתָּא דַייָ כְּדוּן עַל מַן אִתְגְלִיאַת:


Who hath believed this our report? and to whom is now the power of the arm of the Lord revealed?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:2)


וְיִתְרְבָא צַדִיקָא קֳדָמוֹהִי הָא כְלַבְלְבִין דְפָרְחִין וּכְאִילָן דִמְשַׁלַח שׁוֹרְשׁוֹהִי עַל נַגְדִין דְמַיִין כֵּן יִסְגוֹן תּוּלְדַת קוּדְשָׁא בְּאַרְעָא דַהֲוַת צְרִיכָא לֵיהּ לָא חִיזוּ חוּלָא חֶזְוֵיהּ וְלָא אֵימְתֵהּ אֵימַת הֶדְיוֹט וִיהֵי זִיו קוּדְשָׁא זִיוֵיהּ דְכָל דְיַחְזִינֵיהּ יִסְתַּכֵּל בֵּיהּ:


The righteous shall be great before him, behold, like branches that bud; and like a tree which sends forth its roots by the streams of water, thus shall the generation of the just multiply in the land, which hath need of him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:3)


בְּכֵן יְהֵי לְבוּסְרָן וְיִפְסִיק יְקָר כָּל מַלְכְּוָתָא יְהוֹן חֲלָשִׁין וְדָוָן הָא כֶּאֱנַשׁ כֵּיבִין וּמְזוּמַן לְמַרְעִין וּכְמָא דַהֲוַת מְסַלְקָא אַפֵּי שְׁכִנְתָּא מִנָנָא בְסִירִין וְלָא חֲשִׁיבִין:


His visage shall not be the visage of a common person, neither his fear the fear of a plebeian; but a holy brightness shall be his brightness, that every one who seeth him shall contemplate him. Although he shall be in contempt; yet he shall cut off the glory of all the wicked, they shall be weak and wretched. Lo, we are in contempt and not esteemed, as a man of pain and appointed to sickness, and as if He had removed the face of His Shekinah from us.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:4)


בְּכֵן עַל חוֹבָנָא הוּא יִבְעֵי וַעֲוָיָתָנָא בְּדִילֵהּ יִשְׁתַּבְקוּן וַאֲנַחְנָא חֲשִׁיבִין כְּתִישִׁין מָחָן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּמְעַנָן:


Therefore he shall pray for our sins, and our iniquities for his sake shall be forgiven us; for we are considered crushed, smitten of the Lord, and afflicted.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:5)


וְהוּא יִבְנֵי בֵּית מַקְדְשָׁא דְאִתְּחַל בְּחוֹבָנָא אִתְמְסַר בַּעֲוָיָתָנָא וּבְאוּלְפְנֵהּ שְׁלָמָא יִסְגֵי עֲלָנָא וְכַדְנָצֵית לְפִתְגָמוֹהִי חוֹבָנָא יִשְׁתַּבְּקוּן לָנָא:


He shall build the house of the sanctuary, which has been profaned on account of our sins; it was delivered over on account of our iniquities, and through His doctrine peace shall be multiplied upon us, and through the teaching of His words our sins shall be forgiven us.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:6)


כּוּלָנָא כְּעָנָא אִתְבַּדַרְנָא גְבַר לָקֳבֵיל אוֹרְחֵהּ גְלֵינָא וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ הֲוָת רַעֲוָא לְמִשְׁבַּק חוֹבֵי כוּלָנָא בְדִילֵיהּ:


All we like sheep have been scattered, every one of us has turned to his own way; it pleased the Lord to forgive the sins of all of us for His sake.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:7)


בְּעֵי וְהוּא מִתְּתָב וְעַד לָא פְּתַח פּוּמֵיהּ מִתְקַבֵּל תַּקִיפֵי עַמְמַיָא כְּאִמְרָא לְנִכְסְתָא יִמְסַר וְכִרְחֵלָא דְקָדָם גָזְזָהָא שָׁתְקָא וְלֵית לְקִיבְלֵהּ דְפָּתַח פּוּמֵהּ מְמַלֵיל מִלָא:


He shall pray and he shall be answered, yea, before he shall be heard; he shall deliver over the mighty of the nations as a lamb to the slaughter, and like a sheep before her shearers is dumb, none shall in His presence open his mouth, or speak a word.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:8)


מִיסוּרִין וּמִפּוּרְעֲנוּת יְקָרֵיב גַלְוָתָנָא וּפְרִישָׁן דְיִתְעַבְדָן לָנָא בְּיוֹמוֹהִי מַן יִכּוּל לְאִשְׁתְּעָאָה אֲרֵי יַעְדֵי שׁוּלְטָן עַמְמַיָא מֵאַרְעָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל חוֹבִין דְחָבוּ עַמִי עַד לְוַתְהוֹן יִמְטוֹ:


He shall gather our captives from affliction and pain, and who shall be able to narrate the wonderful works which shall be done for us in his days? He shall remove the rule of the nations from the land of Israel, the sins which my people have committed have come upon them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:9)


וְיִמְסַר יַת רַשִׁיעַיָא לְגֵהִנָם וְיַת עֲתִּירֵי נִכְסַיָא דַאֲנַסוּ בְּמוֹתָא דְאָבַדְנָא בְּדִיל דְלָא יִתְקַיְמוּן עָבְדֵי חֶטְאָה וְלָא יְמַלְלוּן נִכְלִין בְּפוּמְהוֹן:


And he shall deliver the wicked into hell, and the riches of treasures which they got by violence unto the death of Abaddon, that they who commit sin shall not remain, and that they should not speak folly with their mouth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:10)


וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ הֲוָת רַעֲוָא לְמִצְרַף וּלְדַכָּאָה יַת שְׁאָרָא דְעַמֵהּ בְּדִיל לְנַקָאָה מֵחוֹבִין נַפְשֵׁיהוֹן יֶחֱזוּן בְּמַלְכוּת מְשִׁיחֵיהוֹן יִסְגוּן בְּנִין וּבְנָן יוֹרְכוּן יוֹמִין וְעָבְדֵי אוֹרַיְתָא דַייָ בִּרְעוּתֵהּ יִצְלְחוּן:


And it was the pleasure of the Lord to refine and to purify the remnant of His people, in order to cleanse their souls from sin, that they might see the kingdom of their messiah, that their sons and daughters might multiply, and prolong their days, and those that keep the law of the Lord shall prosper through His pleasure.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:11)


מִשִׁעְבּוּד עַמְמַיָא יְשֵׁיזֵיב נַפְשְׁהוֹן יֶחֱזוּן בְּפּוּרְעֲנוּת סַנְאֵיהוֹן יִסְבְּעוּן מִבִּזַת מַלְכֵיהוֹן בְּחוּכְמְתֵהּ יְזַכֵּי זַכָּאִין בְּדִיל לְשַׁעְבָּדָא סַגִיאִין לְאוֹרַיְתָא וְעַל חוֹבֵיהוֹן הוּא יִבְעֵי:


He shall deliver their souls from the servitude of the nations, they shall see the vengeance upon their enemies; they shall be satisfied with the spoil of their kings. By His wisdom he shall justify the righteous, in order to make many to keep the law, and he shall pray for their sins.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 53:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/53:12)


בְּכֵן אֲפַלֵיג לֵיהּ בִּזַת עַמְמִין סַגִיאִין וְיַת נִכְסֵי כְּרַכִּין תַּקִיפִין יְפַלֵג עֲדָאָה חֲלַף דִמְסַר לְמוֹתָא נַפְשֵׁהּ וְיַת מְרוֹדַיָא שַׁעְבַּד לְאוֹרַיְתָא וְהוּא עַל חוֹבִין סַגִיאִין יִבְעֵי וְלִמְרוֹדַיָא יִשְׁתְּבֵיק בְּדִילֵהּ:


Therefore I will divide to him the spoil of many people, and the treasures of strong fortifications; he shall divide the spoil; because he has delivered his life unto death, and he shall make the rebellious to keep the law; he shall pray for the sins of many, and as for the transgressors, each shall be pardoned for his sake.



## Chapter 54

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:1)


שַׁבַּחִי יְרוּשְׁלֶם דַהֲוַת כְּאִתָּא עֲקָרָה דְלָא יְלֵידַת בּוּעִי תוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא וְדוּצִי דַהֲוָת כְּאִתָּא דְלָא עֲדִיאַת אֲרֵי סַגִיאִין יְהוֹן בְּנֵי יְרוּשְׁלֶם צְדִיתָא מִבְּנֵי כְרַכָּא יְתִיבְתָּא אֲמַר יְיָ:


Sing, O Jerusalem, who was like a barren woman that beareth not; rejoice with praise and be glad, who was like a woman that conceiveth not: for more shall be the children of Jerusalem that was laid desolate, than of the inhabited city, saith the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:2)


אַפְתִּי אֲתַר בֵּית מֵישְׁרָךְ וּמִקִרְוֵי אַרְעִיךְ יָתְבֵי לָא תִמְנְעִין אַסְגָא עַם מַשִׁרְיָתִיךְ וְשִׁלְטוֹנָיִךְ תַּקִיפִי:


Enlarge the place of the house of thy dwelling, and from the cities of the land do not keep back the inhabitants; multiply the people of the camp, and increase the number of thy governors.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:3)


אֲרֵי לְדָרוֹמָא וּלְצִפּוּנָא תִּתְקְפִין וּבְנָךְ עַמְמִין יֵירְתוּן וְקִרְוִין דְצַדְיָן יֵיתְבוּן:
