For thou shalt spread abroad to the south and to the north, and thy children shall inherit the nations, and make the desolate cities to be inhabited.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:4)


לָא תִדְחֲלִין אֲרֵי לָא תִבְהֲתִין וְלָא תִתְכַּנְעִין אֲרֵי לָא תִתְכַּלְמִין אֲרֵי בַהֲתַת עוּלֵמוּתִיךְ תִּתְנְשִׁין וְחִיסוּדֵי אַרְמְלוּתִיךְ לָא תִדְכְּרִין עוֹד:


Fear not, for thou shalt not be ashamed; neither be thou confounded, for thou shalt not be put to shame: for thou shalt forget the shame of thy youth, and shalt not remember the reproaches of thy widowhood any more.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:5)


אֲרֵי מָרִיךְ דְעַבְדִיךְ יְיָ צְבָאוֹת שְׁמֵהּ וּפָרְקִיךְ קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֱלָהּ כָּל אַרְעָא יִתְקְרֵי:


For thy husband, who is thy Maker, the Lord of hosts is His name, and thy Redeemer is the Holy One of Israel; the God of the whole earth shall He be called.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:6)


אֲרֵי כְאִתָּא שְׁבִיקָא וְעָיְקַת רוּחַ עַרְעָךְ שְׁכִנְתָּא דַייָ וּכְאִתַּת עוּלֵמִין דְאִתְרַחֲקַת אֲמַר אֱלָהָיִךְ:


For as a woman forsaken, and afflicted in spirit, the Shekinah, the Lord, met thee, like a woman of youth which was forsaken, saith thy God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:7)


בְּשָׁעָה זְעֵירָא רְחַקְתִּיךְ וּבְרַחֲמִין סַגִיאִין אֲקָרֵב גַלְוָתָךְ:


In a little anger have I forsaken thee; but with great mercies will I bring together thy captivity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:8)


בִּרְגַז זְעֵיר סַלְקִית אַפֵי שְׁכִנְתִּי זְמַן זְעֵירָא מִנִיךְ וּבְטַבְוַת עֲלָם דְלָא פְסָקָן אֲרַחֵם עֲלָךְ אֲמַר פָּרְקִיךְ יְיָ:


For a little moment I removed the presence of my Shekinah, yea, for a short time from thee, but with everlasting kindness, which shall not cease, will I have mercy on thee, saith the Lord thy Redeemer.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:9)


כְּיוֹמֵי נֹחַ דָא קֳדָמַי דְקַיְמֵית בְּמֵימְרִי דְלָא יֶעְדוּן מֵי טוּפָנָא דַהֲווֹ בְּיוֹמֵי נֹחַ עוֹד עַל אַרְעָא כֵּן קַיְמִית בְּמֵימְרִי דְלָא יֵחוֹל רוּגְזִי עֲלָךְ וְלָא אֶזוֹף בִּיךְ:


This shall be before me as the days of Noah, when I swore by my WORD, that the waters of the deluge, which were in the days of Noah, shall no more pass over the earth; thus I swear by my WORD, that my anger shall not be hurled upon thee, neither will I reprove thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:10)


אֲרֵי טוּרַיָא יֶעְדוּן וְרָמָתָא יִתְפַּרְקוּן וְטוּבִי מִנִיךְ יְרוּשְׁלֵם לָא יֶעְדֵי וּקְיַם שְׁלָמִי לָא יִתְרְחַק אֲמַר דְעָתִיד לְרַחֲמָא עֲלָךְ יְיָ:


For the mountains shall depart, and the hills shall be rent; but my kindness shall not depart from thee, O Jerusalem, neither shall the covenant of my peace be removed, saith the Lord, that will have mercy on thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:11)


חֲשִׁיבָתָא מְקַבְּלָא עוּלְבַּן קַרְתָּא דְאָמְרִין עֲלָה עַמְמַיָא לָא תִתְנְחֵם הָא אֲנָא כָּבֵשׁ בִצְדִידָא אַבְנֵי רִצְפָּתִיךְ וַאֲשַׁכְלְלִינִיךְ בְּאַבְנִין טָבִין:


O distressed city that receiveth affliction, concerning her the nations say, She shall not be comforted: behold, I lay the stones of thy pavement with mosaic, and thy foundations with costly stones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:12)


וֶאֱשַׁוֵי בְמַרְגְלִין אָעָךְ וְתַרְעַיִךְ לְאַבְנֵי גְמַר וְכָל תְּחוּמִיךְ לְאַבְנֵי צְרוֹךְ:


And I will place thy timber with gems, and thy gates shall be of carbuncles, and all thy borders with precious stones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:13)


וְכָל בָּנַיִךְ אַלְפִין בְּאוֹרַיְתָא דַייָ וְסַגֵי יְהֵי שְׁלָם בְּנָךְ:


And all thy children shall learn the law of the Lord; and great shall be the peace of thy children.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:14)


בְזָכוּתָא תִּתַּקְנִין אִתְרַחֲקִי מֵעוּשְׁקָא אֲרֵי לָא תִדְחֲלִין וּמִתְּבִירָא אֲרֵי לָא יֵעוּל עֲלָךְ:


In righteousness shalt thou be established: thou shalt be far removed from oppression, for thou shalt not fear it: and from terror; for it shall not come upon thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:15)


הָא אִתְכַּנָשָׁא יִתְכַּנְשׁוּן לִיךְ גַלְוַת עַמִיךְ לְסוֹפָא מַלְכֵי עַמְמַיָא דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין לַאֲעָקָא לִיךְ יְרוּשְׁלֵם בְּגַוִיךְ יִתְרְמוּן:


Behold, the captivity of thy people shall surely be gathered unto thee: in the end, the kings of the nations, who are gathered together to oppress thee, O Jerusalem, shall be cast down in the midst of thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:16)


הָא אֲנָא בָּרֵית נַפָּחָא נָפַח נוּר בִּשְׁחוֹרִין וּמַפִּיק מָנָא לְצוֹרְכֵהּ וַאֲנָא בָּרֵית מְחַבְּלָא לְחַבָּלָא:


Behold, I have created the smith that bloweth the coals in the fire, and that bringeth forth an instrument for his use; and I have created the destroyer to destroy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 54:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/54:17)


כָּל זַיִן דְיִתַּקַן עֲלַךְ יְרוּשְׁלֵם לָא יַצְלַח וְכָל לִישָׁן דִיקוּם עִמָךְ לְדִינָא תְּחַיְבִינֵהּ דָא אַחְסָנַת עֲבָדַיָא דַייָ וְזַכְוָתֵיהוֹן מִן קֳדָמַי אֲמַר יְיָ:


No weapon that is formed against thee, O Jerusalem, shall prosper; and every tongue that shall rise against thee in judgment thou shalt condemn. This is the heritage of the servants of the Lord, and their righteousness is from me, saith the Lord.



## Chapter 55

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:1)


וַי כָּל דְצָבִי לְמֵילָף יֵיתֵי וְיֵלֵף וּדְלֵית לֵיהּ כְּסַף אֱתוֹ שְׁמָעוּ וַאֲלִיפוּ וֶאֱתוֹ שְׁמָעוּ בְלָא בְדָמִין וּבְלָא בְּמָמוֹן אוּלְפַן דְטַב מַחֲמָר וּמֵחָלָב:


Ho, every one that is willing to learn, let him come and learn, and he that hath no silver, come, hear and learn; come, hear and learn, without price and money, doctrine that is better than wine and milk.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:2)


לְמָא תִתְקְלוּן כַּסְפְּכוֹן בְּלָא לְמֵיכַל וְלֵאוּתְכוֹן בְּלָא לְמִשְׂבַּע קַבִּילוּ קַבְּלָא לְמֵימְרִי וֶאֱכוּלוּ דְטַב וְתִתְפַּנֵק בְּדִדְהֵין נַפְשְׁכוֹן:


Wherefore do ye weigh out your silver for that which is not food? and your wealth for that which satisfieth not? Hearken diligently to my WORD, and ye shall eat that which is good, and let your soul delight itself in fatness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:3)


אַרְכִינוּ אוּדְנֵיכוֹן וְקַבִּילוּ לְמֵימְרִי שְׁמָעוּ וְתִתְקַיֵם נַפְשְׁכוֹן וְאֶגְזַר לְכוֹן קְיַם עֲלָם טַבְוַת דָוִד דִמְהֵימְנָן:


Incline your ear and receive instruction of my law, obey my WORD and your soul shall be quickened, and I will make an everlasting covenant with you, even the mercies of David, which are sure.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:4)


הָא רַב לְעַמְמַיָא מַנִיתֵהּ מְלַךְ וְשַׁלִיט עַל כָּל מַלְכְּוָתָא:


Behold, I have appointed him a prince, a king, and a ruler over all the kingdoms.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:5)


הָא עַם דְלָא תֵידַע יִפְלְחִינָךְ וְעַם דְלָא יֵדְעוּךְ יִרְהֲטוּן לְאַסָקָא לָךְ מִסִין בְּדִיל יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּלְקַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי שַׁבְּחָךְ:


Behold, a people which thou knowest not shall serve thee, and a people which hath not known thee shall run to offer tribute unto thee, because of the Lord thy God, and for the Holy One of Israel; for He hath glorified thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:6)


תְּבָעוּ דְחַלְתָּא דַייָ עַד דְאַתּוּן חַיִין בְּעוֹ מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי עַד דְאַתּוּן קַיָמִין:


Seek ye the fear of the Lord whilst ye are alive, supplicate before His presence whilst ye remain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:7)


יַשְׁבִּיק רַשִׁיעָה אוֹרְחֵהּ דְרִשְׁעָא וּגְבַר אָנֵיס עֶשְׁתּוֹנוֹהִי וִיתוּב לְפוּלְחָנָא דַייָ וִירַחֵם עֲלוֹהִי וְלִדְחַלְתָּא דֶאֱלָהָנָא אֲרֵי מַסְגֵי לְסַלָחָא:


Let the wicked forsake his way of wickedness, and the man of violence his thoughts, and let him return to the worship of the Lord, and He will have mercy upon him; and unto the fear of our God, for He will abundantly pardon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:8)


אֲרֵי לָא מַחְשְׁבָתַי מַחְשָׁבָתְכוֹן וְלָא אוֹרְחָתְכוֹן תַּקָנָן כְּאוֹרְחַת טוּבִי אֲמַר יְיָ:


For your thoughts are not as my thoughts, and your ways are not right, as the ways of my goodness, saith the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:9)


אֲרֵי כְמָא דְרָמִין שְׁמַיָא מִן אַרְעָא כֵּן תַּקְנָן אוֹרְחָתַי מֵאוּרְחַתְכוֹן וּמַחֲשְׁבָתִי תַּקְנָן מִמַחְשְׁבָתְכוֹן:


For as the heavens are higher than the earth, thus are my ways better than your ways, and my thoughts better than your thoughts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:10)


אֲרֵי כְּמָא דְנָחֵית מִטְרָא וְתַלְגָא מִן שְׁמַיָא וּלְתַמָן לָא אֶפְשַׁר לֵיהּ דִיתוּב אֱלָהֵין מְרַוֵי יַת אַרְעָא וּמַרְבֵּי לָהּ וּמוֹעִילָהּ וְיָהַב בַּר זַרְעָא כְּמִסַת לְזָרֵעַ וְלַחְמָא כְּמִסַת לְמֵיכָל:


For as the rain cometh down, and the snow from heaven, and it is impossible that it should return thither, but watereth the earth, and maketh it to sprout, and to be fruitful, that it may give sufficiente grain of seed to the sower, and food sufficient to the eater:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:11)


כֵּן הֲוֵי פִּתְגָם טוּבִי דְנָפֵק מִן קֳדָמַי לָא אֶפְשַׁר דְיִתוּב לִקְדָמַי רֵיקָן אֱלָהֵן עָבַד יַת דְרָעֵינָא וּמַצְלַח לִדְשָׁלַחְנָא לֵיהּ:


Thus shall be the word of my kindness, which proceeds from my presence, it is not possible that it shall return to my presence void, but it shall accomplish that which I please, and shall prosper in the thing whereto I sent it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:12)


אֲרֵי בְחֶדְוָא תִפְקוּן מִבֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא וּבִשְׁלָמָא תִּתּוֹבְלוּן לְאַרְעֲכוֹן טוּרַיָא וְרָמָתָא יְבוּעוּן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן תּוּשְׁבְּחָא וְכָל אִילָנֵי חַקְלָא יִשְׁעֲנוּן בְּעַנְפֵיהוֹן:


For ye shall go out with joy from among the nations, and with peace ye shall be carried to your land: the mountains and the hills shall rejoice before you with praise, and all the trees of the field shall clap with their branches.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 55:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/55:13)


חֲלַף רַשִׁיעַיָא וַחֲלַף חַיָבַיָא יִתְקַיְמוּן דַחֲלֵי חֶטְאָה וִיהֵי קֳדָם יְיָ לְשׁוּם לְאָת עֲלַם דְלָא יִפְסוֹק:


Instead of the wicked the righteous shall rise up, and instead of sinners shall rise up those that fear sin: and it shall be before the Lord for a name, for an everlasting sign, that shall not cease.



## Chapter 56

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:1)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ טַרוּ דִינָא עֲבִידוּ צִדְקָתָא אֲרֵי קָרִיב פּוּרְקָנִי לְמֵיתֵי וּזְכוּתִי לְאִתְגְלָאָה:


Thus saith the Lord, keep ye judgment, and do justice: for my salvation is near to come, and my righteousness to be revealed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:2)


טוּבֵי אֱנָשׁ דְיַעְבַּד דָא וּבַר אֱנָשָׁא דְיִתְקֵף בָּהּ יִטַר שַׁבְּתָא מֵאַחֲלוּתֵהּ וְיִטֵר יְדוֹהִי מִלְמֶעְבַּד כָּל דְבִישׁ:


Blessed is the man that doeth this, and the son of man that layeth hold on it; every one that shall keep the Sabbath from polluting it, and shall keep his hand from doing any evil.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:3)


וְלָא יֵימַר בַּר עַמְמִין דְמִתּוֹסַף עַל עַמָא דַייָ לְמֵימָר אַפְרָשָׁא יִפְרְשִׁנַנִי יְיָ מֵעַל עַמֵהּ וְלָא יֵימַר סָרִיסָא הָא אֲנָא חֲשִׁיב כְּאָע יָבֵישׁ:


Neither let the son of the heathen, that is joined to the people of the Lord, say, The Lord hath utterly separated me from His people: neither let the eunuch say, Behold, I am considered a dry tree.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:4)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ לְסָרִיסַיָא דְיִטְרוּן יַת יוֹמֵי שַׁבַּיָא דִילִי וּמִתְרָעָן בִּדְצָבֵנָא וּמַתְקְפִין בִּקְיָמִי:


For thus saith the Lord unto the eunuchs that keep my Sabbath days, and delight in what I desire, and take hold on my covenant:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:5)


וְאֶתֵּן לְהוֹן בְּבֵית מַקְדְשִׁי וּבַאֲרַע בֵּית שְׁכִנְתִּי אֲתַר וְשׁוּם דְטַב מִבְּנִין וּמִבְּנָן שׁוּם עֲלַם אֶתֵּן לְהוֹן דְלָא יִפְסוֹק:


Even unto them will I give in the house of my sanctuary, and in the land of the house of my Shekinah a place, and a name that is better than that of sons and of daughters: I will give them an everlasting name that shall not be cut off.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:6)


וּבְנֵי עַמְמַיָא דְמִתּוֹסְפִין עַל עַמֵהּ דַייָ לְשַׁמָשׁוּתֵהּ וּלְמִרְחַם יַת שְׁמָא דַייָ לְמֶהֱוֵי לֵיהּ לְעָבְדִין כָּל דְיִטַר שַׁבְּתָא לְאַחֲלוּתֵהּ וּמַתְקְפִין בִּקְיָמִי:


And the sons of the Gentiles, that are joined unto the people of the Lord, to serve Him, and to love the name of the Lord, to be His servants, every one that keepeth the Sabbath from polluting it, and taketh hold of my covenant;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:7)


וְאַיְתִינוּן לְטוּרָא דְקוּדְשִׁי וְאַחֲדִינוּן בְּבֵית צְלוֹתִי עַלְוַתְהוֹן וְנִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיהוֹן יִתַּסְקוּן לְרַעֲוָא עַל מַדְבְּחִי אֲרֵי בֵית מַקְדְשִׁי בֵית צְלוֹ יִתְקְרֵי לְכָל עַמְמַיָא:


Even them will I bring to my holy mountain, and make them joyful in my house of prayer: their burnt offerings and their holy sacrifices shall be offered up with acceptance upon mine altar; for the house of my sanctuary shall be called an house of prayer for all people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:8)


אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים דַעֲתִיד לְכַנָשָׁא מְבַדְרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עוֹד אֲקָרֵב גַלְוַתְהוֹן לְכַנָשָׁא יַתְהוֹן:


The Lord God, who will gather together the scattered ones of Israel, saith, I will again gather their captivity, by collecting them together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:9)


כָּל מַלְכֵי עַמְמַיָא דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין לַאֲקָעָא לִיךְ יְרוּשְׁלֵם בְּגַוִיךְ יִתְרְמוּן יְהוֹן לְמֵיכַל לְחַיַת בְּרָא תִּשְׂבַּע מִנְהוֹן חֵיוַת חוּרְשָׁא:


All the kings of the nations, who are assembled together to oppress thee, O Jerusalem, shall be cast down in the midst of thee; they shall be for food to the beasts of the field, the beasts of the forest shall be satisfied with them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:10)


סַכְוָאֵיהוֹן סָמָן כּוּלְהוֹן לָא יָדְעִין מִדַעַם כּוּלְהוֹן כַּלְבִין חַרְשִׁין לָא יָכְלִין לְמִנְבַּח נַיְמִין שָׁכְבִין רַחֲמִין לְמִדְמָךְ:


Their watchmen are blind: they know nothing, all of them are dumb dogs, they are not able to bark, they are slumbering, they lie down, they love to sleep.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:11)


וְכַלְבַיָא תַקִיפֵי נַפְשָׁן לָא יָדְעִין לְמִשְׂבַּע וְאִינוּן מַבְאֲשִׁין לָא יָדְעִין לְאִסְתַּכָּלָא כּוּלְהוֹן גְבַר לְקָבֵל אוֹרְחֵהּ גְלוֹ גְבַר לְמִבַּז מָמוֹנֵהּ דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Yea, they are greedy dogs, they know not how to be satisfied; they are evildoers, they know not how to become wise: all of them go their own way, to rob the treasures of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 56:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/56:12)


אָמְרִין אֱתוֹ וְנִסַב חֲמַר וְנִתְרְוֵי מִן עַתִּיק וּתְהֵי שֵׁירוּתָנָא דִמְחָר טָבָא מִדְיוֹמָא דֵין רַבָּא סַגִיאָה לַחֲדָא:


Come ye, say they, We will fetch wine, and we will be inebriated with old wine, and tomorrow our banquet shall be better than to-day, great, very great.



## Chapter 57

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:1)


צַדִיקַיָא מַיְתִין וְלֵית אֱנַשׁ דִמְשַׁוִי דְחַלְתִּי עַל לֵב וְגַבְרֵי גַמְלֵי חִסְדָא מִתְכַּנְשִׁין וְלָא מִסְתַּכְּלִין אֲרֵי מִן קֳדָם בִּישְׁתָּא דַעֲתִידָא לְמֵיתֵי מִתְכַּנְשִׁין צַדִיקַיָא:


The righteous die, and no man layeth my fear to heart; and the men who shew mercy are taken away, and they consider not that the righteous are taken away on account of the evil which shall come.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:2)


יְהָכוּן שְׁלָם יְנוּחוּן עַל אֲתַר בֵּית מִשְׁכָּבֵיהוֹן עָבְדֵי אוֹרַיְתֵהּ:


They shall enter into peace; they shall rest in the place of their beds, those that do His law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:3)


וְאַתּוּן אִתְקְרָבָא הַלְכָא עַם דָרָא דְעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן בִּישִׁין דְמִנִצְבַת קוּדְשָׁא נִצְבַתְהוֹן וְאִינוּן מְנַאֲפִּין וּמְזַנָן:


But ye, come nigh hither, O people of a generation whose works are evil, whose plant was of a holy plant; but they are adulterers, and fornicators.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:4)


עַל מַן אַתּוּן מִתְפַּנְקִין וְקָדָם מַן תִּפְתְּחוּן פּוּמְכוֹן תִּסְגוּן לְמַלָלָא רַבְרְבָן הֲלָא אַתּוּן יַלְדֵי מְרוֹד זַרְעָא שִׁקְרָא:


Of whom do ye make your sport? and before whom do ye open your mouth? do ye continue speaking great things? are ye not rebellious children, a lying seed?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:5)


דְפַלְחִין לְטַעֲוָתָא תְּחוֹת כָּל אִילַן עַבּוּף נִכְסֵי יִנְקַיָא בְּנַחֲלַיָא תְּחוֹת שְׁקִיפֵי כֵיפַיָא:


Who worship idols beneath every green tree, who sacrifice the children in the valleys under the clifts of the rocks.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:6)


בִּשְׁעִיעוּת כֵּיף נַחֲלָא חוּלָקִיךְ אַף תַּמָן אִינוּן עַדְבָךְ אַף לְהוֹן נְסֵיכְתְּ נַסְכִין אֲסֵיקְתְּ קוּרְבָּנִין הַעַל אִלֵין יְתוּב מֵימְרִי:


Among the smooth stones of the valley is thy portion; yea, there they shall be thy lot, even to them thou hast poured out drink-offerings, thou hast offered sacrifices; ah! on account of these things my WORD shall retribute.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:7)


עַל טוֹר רָם וּמְנַטֵל שַׁוֵית אֲתַר בֵּית מֵישְׁרִיךְ אַף לְתַמָן סְלֵיקְתָּ לְדַבָּחָא דְבָח:


Upon a lofty and high mountain hast thou set the place of the house of thy dwelling, even thither didst thou go up to sacrifice sacrifice.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:8)


וַאֲחוֹרֵי דֶשֶׁא וּמְזוּזָתָא שַׁוֵית דוּכְרָן טַעֲוָתִיךְ הֲוֵית דַמְיָא לְאִתְּתָא דִרְחִימָא עַל בַּעֲלָהּ וּטְעַת בָּתַר נוּכְרָאִין אַפְתֵּית אֲתַר בֵּית מֵישְׁרָךְ וְגָזַרְתְּ לִיךְ מִנְהוֹן קְיַם רְחֵימְתְּ אֲתַר בֵּית מַשְׁכַּבְהוֹן אֲתַר בְּחַרְתְּ:


Behind the doors and the posts hast thou set up the remembrance of thy idols; thou hast been like a woman who is beloved by her husband, but goeth astray after strangers; thou hast enlarged thy bed: thou hast made a covenant with some of them, thou lovedst the place, the place of their beds, the place thou hast chosen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:9)


כַּד עֲבַדְתְּ לִיךְ אוֹרַיְתָא אַצְלַחְתְּ בְּמַלְכוּתָא וְכִדְאַסְגֵית לִיךְ עוֹבָדִין טָבִין סַגִיאָה מַשִׁרְיָתִיךְ וּשְׁלַחְתְּ אִזְגַדָךְ עַד מֵרָחִיק וְאַמְאֵיכְתְּ תַּקִיפֵי עַמְמַיָא עַד לִשְׁאוֹל:


When thou didst keep the law, thou didst prosper in the kingdom; and when thou didst multiply for thyself good works, thy camp was enlarged, and thou didst send thy messengers to a distant land; and thou didst humble mighty nations unto bades.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:10)


בְּסַגִיאוּת אוֹרְחָתִיךְ לָאֵית לָא אֲמַרְתְּ לְמִתַּב נִכְסִין סַגִיאִין אַסְגֵית עַל כֵּן לָא סַבַּרְתְּ לְמֵיתָב:


Thou hast wearied thyself in the greatness of thy way; yet thou didst not thinks to return the many treasures thou hast multiplied, wherefore thou thoughtest not to repent.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:11)


וְיַת מַן יְצֵיפְתְּ וְקָדָם מַן דְחֵילְתְּ אֲרֵי אַסְגֵית לְמַלָלָא כַדְבִין וּפוּלְחָנִי לָא אַדְכַּרְתְּ וְלָא שַׁוֵית דְחַלְתִּי עַל לִבִּיךְ הֲלָא אֲנָא יְהָבִית אַרְכָּא מֵעַלְמָא דְאִם תְּתוּבִין לְאוֹרַיְתָא וְלִקֳדָמַי לָא תַבְתְּ:


And of whom hast thou been afraid? and on account of whom hast thou feared? Thou hast surely multiplied speaking lies, and thou hast not remembered my service, and hast not had my fear in thine heart: I would have given you the ends of the world, if you had returned to my law; but thou hast not returned to me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:12)


אֲנָא חֲוֵיתִי לָךְ עוֹבְדִין טְבִין דְאִינוּן זַכְוָן לִיךְ וְאַתְּ אַסְגֵית לִיךְ עוֹבָדִין בִּישִׁין דְלָא יִחֲנוּן לִיךְ:


I have shewn thee those good works, which would have been thy righteousness; but thou hast multiplied evil works which did not profit thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:13)


אַצְוָחִי כְעַן אִם יִפְרְקוּנִיךְ עוֹבָדֵי שִׁקְרִין דַהֲוֵית מִתְעַסְקָא בְהוֹן מִיַנְקוּתִיךְ וְיַת כּוּלְהוֹן יִטוֹל רוּחָא יְהוֹן כִּלְמָא וּדְרָחֵיץ עַל מֵימְרִי יַחְסִין אַרְעָא וְיֵירַת טוּרָא דְקוּדְשִׁי:


Proclaim aloud, whether thy deceitful works will save thee, in which thou hast been occupied from thy youth? the wind shall carry away all of them, they shall be as nothing: but he that putteth his trust in my WORD shall possess the earth, and shall inherit my holy mountain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:14)


וְיֵימַר אַלִיפוּ וְאַזְהָרוּ אַפְנוֹ לֵב עַמָא לְאוֹרַח תִּקָנָא סְלִיקוּ תַקְלַת רַשִׁיעַיָא מֵאוֹרַח כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְעַמִי:


And he shall say, Teach and admonish, turn the heart of this people to the right way; remove the stumbling-block of the wicked out of the way of the congregation of my people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:15)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר רָמָא וּמְנַטְלָא דְשָׁרֵי בִשְׁמַיָא וְקַדִישׁ שְׁמֵהּ בְּרוּמָא שְׁרֵי וְקַדִישָׁא שְׁכִנְתֵּהּ אֲמַר לְמִפְרַק לִתְבִירֵי לִבָּא וּלְמַכִּיכֵי רוּחָא לְקַיָמָא רוּחַ מַכִּיכִין וּלְמִסְעַד לֵב תְּבִירִין:


For thus saith the high and lofty One, that dwelleth in the heavens, and whose name is Holy, who inhabits the height-yea, His holy Shekinah hath promised to save the contrite of heart, and the humble of spirit, to revive the spirit of the humble, and to support the heart of the contrite.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:16)


אֲרֵי לָא לְעַלְמָא אִתְפְּרַע בְּדִין וְלָא לְאַפְרֵשׁ יְהֵי רוּגְזִי אֲרֵי רוּחִי מֵתַיָא אֲנָא עֲתִיד לַאֲתָבָא וְנִשְׁמָתָא אֲנָא עֲבָדֵית:


For I will not take vengeance of judgment for ever, neither shall my wrath be eternal: I will revive the spirits of the dead, and the souls I have created.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:17)


בְּחוֹבֵי מָמוֹנְהוֹן דַאֲנָסוּ הֲוָה רוּגְזִי עֲלֵיהוֹן וְאַלְקֵיתִינוּן סַלֵיקִית שְׁכִנְתִּי מִנְהוֹן וְטַלְטֵלְתִּינוּן בַּדְרֵית גַלְוַתְהוֹן עַל דְטָעוּ בָתַר הִרְהוּר לִבְּהוֹן:


My wrath is upon them on account of their robbed riches, and I smote them; I removed my Shekinah from them, and cast them out; I scattered their captives, because they went astray after the imagination of their hearts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:18)


אוֹרַח תְּיוּבְתְּהוֹן גַלְיָא קֳדָמַי וְאֶשְׁבּוֹק לְהוֹן וַאֲרַחֵם עֲלֵיהוֹן וַאֲשַׁלֵם תַּנְחוּמִין לְהוֹן וּלְמִתְאַבְּלִין עֲלֵיהוֹן:


The way of their repentance is revealed before me, and I will forgive them, and I will have compassion upon them, and requite consolations to them and to those that mourn with them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:19)


דִבְרָא מַמְלַל סִפְוָן בְּפוּם כָּל אֱנָשָׁא אֲמַר נְבִיָא שְׁלָמָא יִתְעֲבֵד לְצַדִיקַיָא דִנְטָרוּ אוֹרַיְתִי מִלְקָדְמִין וּשְׁלָמָא יִתְעֲבֵד לְתָבַיָא דְתָבוּ לְאוֹרַיְתָא קָרִיב אֲמַר יְיָ וְאֶשְׁבּוֹק לְהוֹן:


The prophet saith: He that creates the speech in the lips of every man, peace shall be wrought for the righteous, who have kept my law of old, and peace shall be wrought out for the penitent, who return to my law. It is at hand that I will pardon them, saith the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:20)


וְרַשִׁיעַיָא כְּיַמָא דִטְרִיד דְבָעֵי לְמֵינַח וְלָא יָכִיל וְעָבְדִין מוֹהִי סִין וְטִין:


But the wicked are like the troubled sea, that seeketh to rest, but cannot, and its waters bring forth mire and dirt.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 57:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/57:21)


לֵית שְׁלָמָא אֲמַר אֱלָהּ לְרַשִׁיעַיָא:


There is no peace, saith my God, to the wicked.



## Chapter 58

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:1)


נְבִיָא אַכְלֵי בִגְרוֹנָךְ לָא תִמְנַע כְּקַל שׁוֹפָרָא אֲרֵים קָלָךְ וְחַוֵי לְעַמִי מִרְדֵיהוֹן וּלְבֵית יַעֲקֹב חוֹבֵיהוֹן:


Cry aloud, O prophet, spare not, lift up thy voice like the voice of a trumpet, and shew unto my people their rebellion, and unto the house of Jacob their sins.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:2)


וְקָדָמַי יוֹם יוֹם אוּלְפַן תָּבְעִין כְּאִלוּ לְמֵידַע אוֹרְחָן דְתַקְנָן קֳדָמַי צָבָן כְּעַמָא דְזָכוּ עֲבַד וּמִדִין אֱלָהֵהּ לָא אִתְרַחֵק שַׁאֲלִין מִן קֳדָמַי דִין וּקְשׁוֹט כְּאִלוּ לְאִתְקְרָבָא לִדְחַלְתָּא דַייָ צָבָן:


And they seek daily instruction from me, as if to know the ways which are right before me, finding delight in my presence as a people that doeth righteousness, and have not forsaken the judgment of their God: they ask of me judgment and truth, as if they delighted to draw nigh to the fear of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:3)


אָמְרִין לְמָא אֲנַחְנָא צַיְמִין וּקְדָמָךְ גְלֵי מְסַגְפִין נַפְשָׁתָנָא וּקְדָמָךְ יְדִיעַ נְבִיָא אֲמַר לְהוֹן הָא בְיוֹם תַּעֲנִיתְכוֹן אַתּוּן תָּבְעִין צָרְכֵיכוֹן וְכָן תַּקְלַתְכוֹן אַתּוּן מְקָרְבִין:


They say, Wherefore do we fast, as it is revealed before Thee? wherefore have we afflicted ourselves, as known unto Thee? The prophet said unto them, Behold, in the day of your fast ye seek your extortions, and ye bring near all your stumbling-blocks.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:4)


הֵן לְתַחֲרוּ וּלְמַצוּ אַתּוּן צַיְמִין וּלְמִמְחֵי בְּכוּרְמֵיזָא דְרִשְׁעָא לָא תִתְעֲנוּן תַּעֲנִין כְּאִלֵין לְאַשְׁמָעָא בִמְרוֹמָא קָלְכוֹן:


Behold, ye fast for provocation and strife, and to smite with the fist of wickedness; ye shall not fast a fast as if to make your voice to be heard on high.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:5)


הַהָדָא הוּא תַּעֲנִיתָא דְרָעִינָא בָהּ יוֹמָא דִמְסַגֵף אֱנָשָׁא נַפְשֵׁהּ כָּפֵיף רֵישֵׁה כְאַגְמוֹן כָּפֵיף וְעַל סַקָא וְקִיטְמָא בָּאֵית הַלְדָא אַתּוּן קְרַן תַּעֲנִית וְיוֹמָא דְרַעֲוָא בֵיהּ מִן קֳדָם יְיָ:


Is this the fast which I delight in? a day to afflict oneself? Is it to have his head bent down as a bulrush, and passing the night in sackcloth and ashes? Will ye call this a fast before the presence of the Lord, and a day in which I delight?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:6)


הֲלָא דָא הִיא תַעֲנֵיתָא דְרָעֵינָא בָהּ פְּרִדוּ כְּנִישַׁת רִשְׁעָא שְׁרוֹ קִטְרֵי כִתְבֵי דִין מַסְטֵי וּפַטָרוּ דַהֲווֹ אֲנוּסִין בְּנֵי חוֹרִין וְכָל דִין מַסְטֵי תְּסַלְקוּן:


Is not this the fast which I have chosen? to scatter the assembly of wickedness, to loose the fetters of a decree of perverted judgment, to set at liberty the sons of the freeborn, who have suffered violence, and to remove all perverted judgment?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:7)


הֲלָא תְפַרְנֵס לְכַפְנָא מִלַחְמֵךְ וַחֲשִׁיבִין מְטַלְטְלִין תָּעֵיל לְגוֹ בֵיתָא אֲרֵי תֱחֶזֵי עַרְטִילָאָה וּתְכַסִינֵהּ וּמִקָרִיב בִּשְׂרָךְ לָא תִכְבּוֹשׁ עֵינָךְ:


Shouldest not thou sustain the hungry with thy bread, and bring the poor that are cast out into the midst of thy house? When thou seest the naked, that thou cover him, and shouldest not hide thine eyes from the kindred who is thy flesh ?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:8)


בְּכֵן יִתְגְלֵי כִּשְׁפַּרְפָּרָא נְהוֹרָךְ וְאַסוּת מַחֲתָךְ בִּפְרִיעַ תִּיסָק וִיהָכוּן קֳדָמָךְ זַכְוָתָךְ בִּיקָר מִן קֳדָם יְיָ תִתְכְּנִישׁ:


Then shall thy light be revealed like the early dawn, and the healing of thy wound shall appear quickly; thy great righteousness shall go before thee, and thou shalt be gathered to the glory, which is in the presence of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:9)


בְּכֵן תְּצַלִי וַיָי יְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתָךְ תִּבְעֵי מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי וְיַעְבֵּד בָעוּתָךְ אִם תַּעְדֵי מִבֵּינָךְ אַסְטָיוּת דִין מִרְמַז בְּאֶצְבַּע וּמַלֵילָא מִלִין דְאוֹנֵס:


Then thou shalt pray, and the Lord shall hear thy prayer; thou shalt supplicate before Him, and He shall grant thy supplication, if thou put away from the midst of thee perverted judgment, the pointing of the finger, and the speaking of violent words;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:10)


וְתִתְפַּח קֳדָם כַּפְנָא נַפְשָׁךְ וּנְפַשׁ מְסַגְפָא תִּשְׂבַּע וְיִדְנַח בַּחֲשׁוּכָא נְהוֹרָךְ וְקִבְלָךְ יְהֵי כְטִהֲרָא:


And if thou breathest out thy soul before the hungry and satisfiest the afflicted soul, then shall thy light rise in obscurity, and thy darkness be as the noonday:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:11)


וִידַבְּרִינָךְ יְיָ תְּדִירָא וְיִשְׂבַּע בִּשְׁנֵי בְּצוֹרְתָא נַפְשָׁךְ וְגוּפָךְ וְחַיֵי בְחַיֵי עַלְמָא וּתְהֵי נַפְשָׁךְ מַלְיָא תַפְנוּקִין כְּגִנַת שַׁקְיָא דִמְרַוְיָא וּכְמַבּוּעַ דְמַיָין דְלָא פַּסְקִין מוֹהִי:


And the Lord shall guide thee continually, and satisfy thy soul in the years of drought, and He shall quicken thy body with eternal life, and thy soul shall be full with delicacies like a watered garden, well irrigated, and like a spring of water, whose waters fail not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:12)


וְיִבְנוֹן מִנָךְ חָרְבַת עֲלַם יְסוֹדֵי דַר וְדַר תְּקוֹמֵם וְיִקְרוּן לָךְ מְקַיֵים אוֹרְחָא דְתַקְנָא מֵתִיב רַשִׁיעַיָא לְאוֹרַיְתָא:


Thy offspring shall build the old waste places: thou shalt raise up the foundations of many generations, and they shall call thee, The restorer of the right way; The converter of the wicked to the law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:13)


אִם תְּתִיב מִשַׁבַּתָא רִגְלָךְ לְמֶעְבַּד צוֹרְכָךְ בְיוֹמָא דְקוּדְשִׁי וּתְעָרַע לְשַׁבְּתָא בְּתַפְנוּקִין לְקַדִישָׁא דַייָ תְּיַקַר וּתְיַקַר קֳדָמוֹהִי מִלְמֶעְבַּד אוֹרְחָךְ מִלְסַפְקָא צוֹרְכָךְ וּמַלָלָא מִלִין דְאוֹנָס:


If thou turn away thy foot from the Sabbath from following thine employment on my holy day, and dost meet the Sabbath with great delight to sanctify the Lord, and honour Him, not doing thine own ways, nor making thy wants ample, nor speaking violent words:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 58:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/58:14)


בְכֵן תִּתְפַּנַק קֳדָם יְיָ וְיַשְׁרִינָךְ עַל תּוּקְפֵי אַרְעָא וְיוֹכְלִינָךְ פֵּרֵי אַחְסָנַת יַעֲקֹב אֲבוּךְ אֲרֵי בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ גְזִיר כֵּן:


Then shalt thou delight thyself in the Lord; and He shall cause thee to dwell in the strong places of the earth, and feed thee with the heritage of Jacob thy father; for thus it is decreed by the WORD of the Lord.



## Chapter 59

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:1)


הָא לָא מִקְפֵּידוּת יָדָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לֵית אַתּוּן מִתְפָּרְקִין וְלָא מִדְיַקַר קֳדָמוֹהִי מִלְמִשְׁמַע לָא מִתְקַבְּלָא צְלוֹתְכוֹן:


Behold, it is not on account of any deficiency in the power of the Lord that ye are not saved; nor because it is too difficult for Him to hear, that your prayer is not received:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:2)


אֱלָהֵן חוֹבֵיכוֹן הֲווֹ מַפְרְשִׁין בֵּינֵיכוֹן לְבֵין אֱלָהָכוֹן וַחֲטָאֵיכוֹן גְרָמוּ לְסַלָקָא אַפֵּי שְׁכִנְתָּא מִנְכוֹן מִלְקַבָּלָא צְלוֹתְכוֹן:


But your iniquities separate between you and your God, and your sins have caused Him to take away the favour of His Shekinah from you, not to receive your prayer.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:3)


אֲרֵי יְדֵיכוֹן שׁוּטְפִין בְּדַם זַכַּאי וְאֶצְבְּעַתְכוֹן בְּחוֹבִין סִפְוַתְכוֹן מְמַלְלִין שְׁקַר לִישַׁנְכוֹן נִכְלִין מְחַשֵׁב:


For your hands are associated with innocent blood, and your fingers with iniquity; your lips speak lies, your tongue muttereth deceit.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:4)


לֵית דִמְצַלֵי בִקְשׁוֹט וְלֵית דְמִתְדַן בְּהֵימְנוּתָא מִתְרַחֲצִין עַל לְמָא וּמְמַלְלִין שְׁקַר מוֹחָן וּמַפְקִין מִלִבְּהוֹן מִלִין דְאוֹנָס:


There is none that truly prayeth, nor any that pleadeth for faithfulness: they trust in vanity, and speak lies; they hasten, and bring forth words of violence out of their heart.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:5)


הָא כְבֵיעֵי חֵיוִי חוּרְמְנִין מְפַרְתִין וּכְקַוִין דְעַכּוֹבִיתָא מָחַיָא דְמַן דְאָכִיל מִבֵּיעֵיהוֹן מָאֵית וּמְשַׁחְנָן וּמַפְקָן חֵיוָן חוּרְמְנִין מְפַרְתִין:


Behold, they hatch as it were eggs of a basilisk; they are like the threads which the spiders weave: he that eateth of their eggs and when they are hatched, they bring forth the flying serpents.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:6)


הָא כְקַוִין דְעַכּוֹבִיתָא דְלָא כַשְׁרִין לְאִתְכַּסָאָה כֵּן לֵית הֲנָאָה בְּעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן דְרַשִׁיעַיָא עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן עוֹבָדֵי אוֹנֶס וְלֵיאוּת שְׁקַר בִּידֵהוֹן:


Behold, like a spider's web, with which no one can be covered, thus there is no profit from the works of the wicked; their works are the works of violence, and the work of a lie is in their hands.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:7)


רַגְלֵיהוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד דְבִישׁ רַהֲטִין וּמוֹחִין לְמִשְׁפַּךְ דַם זַכַּאי עֶשְׁתּוֹנֵיהוֹן עֶשְׁתּוֹנֵי אוֹנָס בִּזָא וּתְבָרָא בְּכִבְשֵׁיהוֹן:


Their feet run to do that which is evil, and they make haste to shed innocent blood: their thoughts are thoughts of violence, plunder and destruction are in their paths.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:8)


אוֹרַח שְׁלָמָא לָא יָדְעִין וְלֵית דִינָא בִּמְהָכֵיהוֹן שְׁבִילֵיהוֹן עֲקִימוּ לְהוֹן כָּל דְדָרִיךְ בְּהוֹן לָא יְדַע שְׁלָמָא:


The way of peace they know not; and there is no justice in their ways, they have perverted their ways: whoever goeth therein shall not know peace.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:9)


עַל כֵּן אִתְרָחַק דִינָא מִנָנָא וְלָא מְעַרְעָן לָנָא זַכְוָן סַבַּרְנָא לְנֵיהוֹר וְהָא חֲשׁוֹךְ לְזֵיהוֹר וְהָא כִדְבְּקַבְלָא אֲנַחְנָא מְהַלְכִין:


Therefore is judgment removed far from us, and the least righteousness does not meet us: we wait for light, but behold, darkness; for brightness, but behold, we are walking in obscurity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:10)


נְמַשְׁמֵשׁ כְּסָמָן כּוּתְלִין וְכִדְלֵית לְהוֹן עַיְנִין נְמַשְׁמֵשׁ אִתְקֵילָנָא בְטִיהֲרָא כְּמָא דְמִתַּקְלִין בְּקִבְלָא אִתְאֲחַד בְּאַפָּנָא כְּמָא דַאֲחִידִין קִבְרַיָא בְּאַפֵּי מֵתַיָא:


We grope for the wall like the blind, and we grope as if we had no eyes : we stumble at noonday as those that stumble in the dark; it is shut up before graves are shut up before the dead.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:11)


עַסֵינָא מִן קֳדָם בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָנָא דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין עֲלָנָא כְּדוּבִין כּוּלָנָא וּכְיוֹנִין נָהָמָא נָהֵימְנָא סַבַּרְנָא לְדִינָא וְלֵית לְפוּרְקָנָא אִתְרְחֵק מִנָנָא:


We all roar like bears by reason of our enemies, who are gathered against us, and mourn like doves: we hoped for judgment, but there is none; for salvation, but it is far removed from us.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:12)


אֲרֵי סַגִיאִין חוֹבָנָא קֳדָמָךְ וְחַטָאנָא אַסְהִידוּ בָנָא אֲרֵי חוֹבָנָא גְלַן לָנָא זַעֲוָיָתָנָא יְדַעְנָא לְהוֹן:


Because our iniquities are multiplied before Thee, and our sins testify against us: for our iniquities are revealed unto us, and our crimes we know them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:13)


מְרֵידְנָא וְכַדִיבְנָא בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ וְאִסְתְּחַרְנָא לְאַחוֹרָא מִבָּתַר פּוּלְחָנָא דֶאֱלָהָנָא וַהֲוֵינָא מְמַלְלִין שְׁקַר וְסַטְיָא מוֹחַן וּמַפְקִין מִלִבְּהוֹן פִּתְגָמֵי שְׁקָר:


We have rebelled, and acted falsely against the WORD of the Lord; we have turned backward from following after the worship of our God; we have spoken falsehood and apostasy, hastening and bringing forth from their hearts lying words.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:14)


וְאִסְתְּחַר לַאֲחוֹרָא עָבְדֵי דִינָא וְעָבְדֵי זְכוּתָא מֵרָחִיק קָמִין אֲרֵי אִתְקִילוּ עַבְדֵי קוּשְׁטָא וְעָבְדֵי הֵימְנוּתָא לָא יָכְלִין לְאִתְגַלָאָה:


And justice is turned away backward, and righteousness standeth afar off: for they that follow truth stumble in the street; and they that follow faithfulness cannot shew themselves.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:15)


וַהֲווֹ עָבְדֵי קוּשְׁטָא מְטַמְרִין וּדְסָטָן מִבִּישׁ מִתְבַּזְזִין וּגְלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וּבְאֵישׁ קֳדָמוֹהִי אֲרֵי לֵית דִינָא:


And they that follow truth are hidden, and those that depart from evil become a prey. It is revealed before the Lord, that there is no justice, which is evil in His sight.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:16)


וּגְלֵי קֳדָמוֹהִי דְלֵית גְבַר דְלֵיהּ עוֹבָדִין טָבִין וִידִיעַ קֳדָמוֹהִי וְלֵית אֱנַשׁ דִיקוּם וְיִבְעֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן וּפַרְקִינוּן בִּדְרַע תּוּקְפֵהּ וּבְמֵימָר רְעוּתִי סַעְדִינוּן:


And it is revealed in His sight that there is no man who hath good works, and it is known to Him that there is no man, who would arise and seek after them: therefore He will redeem them by the arm of His strength, and by the WORD of His delight He will help them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:17)


אִתְגְלֵי לְמֶעְבַּד זַכְוָן לְעַמֵהּ תְּקוֹף וּפוּרְקָן אַיְתֵי בְמֵימְרֵהּ לְדַחְלוֹהִי וְיִתְגְלֵי לְמֶעְבַּד לְאִתְפְּרָעָא בִתְקוֹף מְסַנְאֵי עַמֵהּ וִיתַב נִקְמָא לְבַעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹהִי:


It is revealed, that He will work a great salvation for His people, yea, He will render vengeance to His enemies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:18)


מָרֵי גַמְלַיָא הוּא גִמְלָא יְשַׁלֵם פּוּרְעֲנוּתָא לְסָנְאוֹהִי גִמְלָא לְבַעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹהִי לְנַנְוָתָא גִמְלָא יְשַׁלֵם:


He is the Lord of retributions, He shall render recompense vengeance to His enemies, retribution to His adversaries; He shall render recompense to the islands.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:19)


וְיִדְחֲלוּן מִמַעֲרְבָא יַת שְׁמָא דַייָ וּמִמַדְנַח שִׁמְשָׁא יַת יְקָרֵהּ אֲרֵי יֵיתוּן כִּשְׁפַע נְהַר פְּרָת מְעִיקָן בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ יִתַּבְּרוּן:


They shall fear the name of the Lord from the west, and His glory from the rising of the sun. When the oppressors shall come in like an inundation of the river Euphrates, they shall be broken by the WORD of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:20)


וְיֵיתֵי לְצִיוֹן פְרִיק וְלַאֲתָבָא מְרוֹדַיָא דְבֵית יַעֲקֹב לְאוֹרַיְתָא אֲמַר יְיָ:


And the redeemer shall come to Zion, and turn the transgressors of the house of Jacob to the law, saith the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 59:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/59:21)


וַאֲנָא דֵין קְיָמִי עִמְהוֹן אֲמַר יְיָ רוּחַ קוּדְשִׁי דַעֲלָךְ וּפִתְגָמֵי נְבוּאָתִי דְשַׁוֵתִי בְּפוּמָךְ לָא יֶעְדוּן מִפּוּמָךְ וּמִפּוּם בְּנָךְ וּמִפּוּם בְּנֵי בְנָךְ אֲמַר יְיָ מִכְּעַן וְעַד עַלְמָא:


And as for me, this shall be my covenant with them, saith the Lord; My Holy Spirit that is upon thee, and the words of my prophecy which I have put in thy mouth, shall not depart out of thy mouth, nor out of the mouth of thy children, nor out of the mouth of thy children's children, saith the Lord, from henceforth and for ever.



## Chapter 60

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:1)


קוּמִי אַנְהָרִי יְרוּשְׁלֵם אֲרֵי מְטָא זְמַן פּוּרְקָנִיךְ וִיקָרָא דַייָ עֲלָךְ יִתְגְלִי:


Arise, shine, O Jerusalem, for the time of thy redemption is come, and the glory of the Lord is revealed upon thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:2)


אֲרֵי הָא חֲשׁוֹכָא יַחְפֵּי אַרְעָא וְקִבְלָא לְמַלְכְּוָתָא וּבִיךְ יִשְׁרֵי שְׁכִנְתָּא דַייָ וִיקָרֵהּ עֲלָךְ יִתְגְלֵי:


For, behold, the darkness shall cover the earth, and gross darkness the kingdoms: but in thee the Shekinah of the Lord shall dwell, and His glory shall be revealed upon thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:3)


וִיהָכוֹן עַמְמִין לִנְהוֹרִיךְ וּמַלְכַיָא לִקְבֵל זְהוֹרִיךְ:


And the Gentiles shall come to thy light, and kings to thy brightness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:4)


זְקוּפִי יְרוּשְׁלֵם סְחוֹר סְחוֹר עֵינָךְ וְחַזֵי כָּל בְּנֵי עַם גַלְוָתָךְ דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין וְאָתָן לְגַוִיךְ בְּנָךְ מֵרָחִיק יֵיתוּן וּבְנָתָךְ עַל גִסְסִין יִתְנַסְבָן:


Lift up thine eyes, O Jerusalem, round about, and see all the children of the people of thy captivity, who are gathered together: thy sons shall come from far into thy midst, and thy daughters shall be carried upon the arms.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:5)


בְּכֵן תֶּחֱזִין וּתְנַהֲרִין וְתִדְחֵלִין וְיִפְתִּי לִבִּיךְ בְּדַחֲלַת חֶטְאִין אֲרֵי יִתְחֲלֵיף לִיךְ עוֹתַר מַעַרְבָא נִכְסֵי פַּלְחֵי כּוֹכְבַיָא יִתְעֲלוּן לְגַוִיךְ:


Then thou shalt see and be enlightened, and thou shalt be struck with awe, and thine heart shall be enlarged on account of the terror of sinners, for the riches of the west shall be transferred unto thee; the treasures of the nations shall be brought unto thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:6)


שְׁיָרֵת עַרְבָאֵי תַּחֲפֵי סַחֲרָנִיךְ הוֹגְנֵי מִדְיָן וְהוֹלַד כּוּלְהוֹן מִשְׁבָא יֵיתוּן דְהַב וּלְבוֹנָה יְהוֹן טְעִינִין וּדְאָתָן עִמְהוֹן בְּתוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא דַייָ יְהוֹן מִשְׁתְּעָן:


A multitude of Arabians shall cover thee round about, the dromedaries of Midian and Holad, all of them from Sheba shall come: they shall be laden with gold and frankincense, and those that are coming with them, shall shew forth the praises of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:7)


כָּל עָאן עֲרָבָאֵי יִתְכַּנְשִׁין לְגַוִיךְ דִכְרֵי נְבָיוֹת יְשַׁמְשׁוּנִיךְ יִתַּסְקוּן לְרַעֲוָא עַל מַדְבְּחִי וּבֵית תּוּשְׁבַּחְתִּי אֲשַׁבֵּח:


All the sheep of the Arabians shall be gathered unto thee, the rams of Nebaioth shall minister unto thee: they shall be offered up with acceptance upon mine altar, and I will glorify the house of my glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:8)


מַן אִלֵין דְאָתָן בִּגְלֵי כַּעֲנָנִין קַלִילִין וְלָא אִתְעַכָּבָא גַלְוָתָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְמִתְכַּנְשִׁין וְאַתְיָן לְאַרְעֲהוֹן הָא כְיוֹנִין דְתַיְבָן לְגוֹ שׁוֹבְכֵיהוֹן:


Who are these that are coming openly like swift clouds, and tarry not? the captives of Israel, who are gathered together, come to their land, lo, as doves which return to their dove-houses.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:9)


אֲרֵי לְמֵימְרִי נַגְוָן יְסַבְּרוּן וְנַחֲתֵי סְפִינֵי יַמָא אַיְדָא פַרְסָא קַלְעָהָא בְקַדְמֵיתָא לְאַיְתָאָה בְּנָךְ מֵרָחִיק כַּסְפֵּהוֹן וּדְהַבְהוֹן עִמְהוֹן לִשְׁמָא דַייָ אֱלָהָךְ וּלְקַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי שַׁבְּחָךְ:


Surely, the isles shall wait for my WORD, and those that embark in the ships of the sea, the hand that stretcheth out their sails shall be first, to bring thy sons from far, their silver and their gold with them, unto the name of the Lord thy God, and to the Holy One of Israel, because He hath glorified thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:10)


וְיִבְנוֹן בְּנֵי עַמְמַיָא שׁוּרָךְ וּמַלְכֵיהוֹן יְשַׁמְשׁוּנִיךְ אֲרֵי בְרוּגְזִי אַלְקֵיתִיךְ וּבִרְעוּתִי אֲרַחֵם עֲלָךְ:


And the sons of the nations shall build up thy walls, and their kings shall minister unto thee: for in my wrath I smote thee, but in my favour have I had mercy on thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:11)


וְיִתְפַּתְּחוּן תַּרְעָךְ תְּדִירָא יֵימָם וְלֵילֵי לָא יִתְאַחֲדוּן לַאֲעָלָא לְגַוִיךְ נִכְסֵי עַמְמַיָא וּמַלְכֵיהוֹן זְקִיקִין:


Thy gates shall be open continually; they shall not be shut day nor night; that men may bring unto thee the treasures of the nations, and their kings in chains.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:12)


אֲרֵי עַם וּמַלְכוּ דְלָא יִפְלְחוּנִיךְ יְרוּשְׁלֵם יֵיבְדוּן וְעַמְמַיָא אִשְׁתֵּיצָאָה יִשְׁתֵּיצוּן:


For the nation and kingdom that will not serve thee, 0 Jerusalem, shall perish; yea, those nations shall be utterly destroyed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:13)


יְקַר לִבְנָן לְגַוִיךְ יִתְעַל בִּירְוַן מוֹרְנְיָן וְאֶשְׁכַּרְעִין כַּחֲדָא לְשַׁבְּחָא אֲתַר בֵּית מַקְדְשִׁי וַאֲתַר בֵּית אַשְׁרָיוּת שְׁכִנְתִּי אֲיַקֵר:


The glory of Lebanon shall be brought unto thee, the fir-tree, the elm, and the box together, to beautify the place of the house of my sanctuary; and I will make the place of the dwelling of my Shekinah glorious.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:14)


וִיהָכוּן לְגַוִיךְ כְּפִיפִין בְּנֵי מְשַׁעְבְּדַיִךְ וְיִשְׁטַתְּחוּן לְמִבְעֵי מִנִיךְ עַל פַּרְסַת רִגְלִיךְ כָּל דַהֲווֹ מַרְגְזִין לִיךְ וְיִקְרוֹן לִיךְ קַרְתָּא דַייָ צִיוֹן דְאִתְרְעֵי בָהּ קַדִישָׁא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


The sons of them that enslaved thee shall come and bow down to thee, and all that provoked thee to anger shall prostrate themselves at the soles of thy feet to make supplication unto thee; and they shall call thee, The city of the Lord, The Zion in which the Holy One of Israel delights.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:15)


חֲלַף דַהֲוֵית שְׁבִיקָא וּמְטַלְטְלָא וְלֵית דְעָדֵי וַאֲשַׁוִינָךְ לִיקַר עֲלַם בֵית דִיץ דָר וְדָר:


Whereas thou hast been forsaken, and an outcast, so that none went through thee, I will make thee an eternal glory, the house of joy for generation and generation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:16)


וְתִסְבְּעִין נִכְסֵי עַמְמַיָא וּבְבִזַת מַלְכִין תִּתְפַּנְקִין וְתֵידְעִין אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ פָּרְקִיךְ וּמְשֵׁיזְבִיךְ תַּקִיפָא דְיַעֲקֹב:


And thou shalt be satisfied with the riches of the nations, and in the spoil of their kings thou shalt delight thyself; and thou shalt know that I am the Lord, thy Saviour and thy Redeemer, the mighty One of Jacob.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:17)


חֲלַף נְחָשָׁא דִבְזוֹ מִנִיךְ יְרוּשְׁלֵם אַיְתֵי דַהֲבָא וַחֲלַף בַּרְזְלָא אַיְתֵי כַסְפָּא וַחֲלַף אָעַיָא נְחָשָׁא וַחֲלַף אַבְנַיָא בַּרְזְלָא וַאֲשַׁוֵי פַּרְנָסָךְ שְׁלַם וְשִׁלְטוּנַיִךְ בְּזָכוּ:


For the brass which they spoiled thee of, o Jerusalem, I will bring gold, and for the iron I will bring silver, and for the wood brass, and for the stones iron: I will also make thy pastors peace, and thy rulers righteousness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:18)


לָא יִשְׁתְּמַע עוֹד חִיטְפָא בְּאַרְעִיךְ בִּזָא וּתְבִירָא בִּתְחוּמִיךְ וִיעַרְעוּן פּוּרְקַן עַל שׁוּרָךְ וְעַל תַּרְעָךְ יְהוֹן מְשַׁבְּחִין:


Violence shall no more be heard in thy land, spoil nor destruction within thy borders; but they shall proclaim salvation on thy walls, and on thy gates there shall be those that praise.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:19)


לָא תִצְטַרְכִין לְנֵיהוּר שִׁמְשָׁא בִּימָמָא וְאַף לָא לְזֵיהוּר סִיהֲרָא בְּלֵילְיָא וִיהֵי לִיךְ יְיָ לְנֵיהוֹר עֲלָם וֶאֱלְהָךְ לְתוּשְׁבַּחְתִּיךְ:


Thou shalt need no more the sun for a light by day, neither for brightness the moon by night: but the Lord shall be unto thee an everlasting light, and thy God thy glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:20)


לָא תְבַטֵל עוֹד מַלְכוּתִיךְ וִיקָרִיךְ לָא יֶעְדִי אֲרֵי יְיָ יְהֵי לִיךְ לְנֵיהוֹר עֲלָם וְיִשְׁלְמוּן יוֹמֵי אֶבְלַיִךְ:


Thy kingdom shall cease no more, and thy glory shall not be removed: for the Lord shall be thine everlasting light, and the days of thy mourning shall be ended.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:21)


וְעַמִיךְ כּוּלְהוֹן זַכָּאִין לַעֲלַם יַחְסְנוּן אַרְעָא נִיצְבָא דְחֶדְוָתִי עוֹבַד גְבוּרְתִּי לְאִשְׁתַּבָּחָא:


Thy people shall be all righteous: for ever shall they inherit the land, the plant of my delight, the work of my might, that I may assuredly be glorified.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 60:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/60:22)


דִזְעֵיר בְּהוֹן יְהֵי לְאַלָפָא וּדְחַלָשׁ לְעַם תַּקִיף אֲנָא יְיָ בְּזִמְנָהּ אַיְתִינָהּ:


He that is little amongst them shall become a thousand, and he that is weak shall become a strong nation: I the Lord will bring it to pass in its time.



## Chapter 61

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:1)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא רוּחַ נְבוּאָה מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלֹהִים עֲלַי חֲלַף דְרַבֵּי יָתִי יְיָ לְבַשָׂרָא עִנְוְתָנַיָא שַׁלְחַנִי לְתַקָפָא לִתְבִירֵי לִבָּא לְמִקְרֵי לְדִשְׁבַן חֵירוּ וְלִדְאֲסִירִין אִתְגְלוּ לְנֵיהוֹר:


The prophet said, the spirit of prophecy from before the presence of the Lord God is upon me; because that the Lord hath anointed me to preach good tidings to the meek; He hath sent me to strengthen the brokenhearted, to proclaim liberty to the captives, and to the prisoners! Appear in light!


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:2)


לְמִקְרֵי שְׁנַת רַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיוֹם פּוּרְעֲנוּתָא קֳדָם אֱלָהָנָא לְנַחָמָא כָּל אֲבֵלַיָא:


To proclaim the acceptable year of the Lord, and the day of vengeance of our God; to comfort all that mourn.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:3)


לְשַׁוָאָה לַאֲבֵילֵי צִיוֹן לְמִתַּן לְהוֹן כְּלִיל חֲלַף קְטַם מְשַׁח דְחֶדְוָה חֲלַף אֶבְלָא רוּחַ מְשַׁבָּחָא חֲלַף רוּחֲהוֹן דַהֲוָת עַמְיָא וְיִקְרוֹן לְהוֹן רַבְרְבֵי קוּשְׁטָא עַמֵהּ דַייָ לְאִשְׁתַּבָּחָא:


To appoint unto them that mourn in Zion, that unto them be given a crown for ashes, the oil of joy for mourning, the spirit of praise for their spirit which is faint; they shall be called princes of truth, the people of the Lord, that He may assuredly be glorified.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:4)


וְיִבְנוֹן חָרְבַת עֲלָם צַדְיָת קַדְמָאֵי יְקוֹמְמוּן וִיחַדְתּוּן קִירְוִין דַהֲוָאָה חָרְבָּן צַדְיָא דָר וְדָר:


And they shall build the old wastes, they shall raise up the former desolations, and they shall repair the cities which were wastes, the desolations of many generations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:5)


וִיקוּמוּן נוּכְרָאִין וְיִרְעוּן עָנְכוֹן וּבְנֵי עַמְמַיָא אִכָּרֵיכוֹן וּמְפַלְחֵי כַּרְמֵיכוֹן:


And strangers shall stand and feed your flocks, and the sons of the Gentiles shall be your plowmen, and they shall dress your vineyards.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:6)


וְאַתּוּן כַּהֲנַיָא דַייָ תִּתְקְרוּן דִמְשַׁמְשִׁין קֳדָם אֱלָהָנָא יִתְאֲמַר לְכוֹן נִכְסֵי פַּלְחֵי כּוֹכְבַיָא תֵּיכְלוּן וּבִיקָרְהוֹן תִּתְפַּנְקוּן:


But ye shall be called the Priests of the Lord: it shall be said of you that ye are those who minister before our God: ye shall eat the riches of the Gentiles, and in their glory ye shall delight yourselves.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:7)


חֲלַף דִבְהַתְהוּן וְאִתְכְּנַעְתּוּן עַל חַד תְרֵין בְּטַבְוָן דַאֲמָרֵית לְכוֹן אַיְתֵי לְכוֹן וְיִתְבָּהֲתוּן עַמְמַיָא דַהֲווֹ מִשְׁתַּבְּחִין בְּחוּלַקְהוֹן בְּכֵן בְּאַרְעֲהוֹן עַל חַד תְּרֵין יַחְסְנוּן חֶדְוַת עֲלָם תְּהִי לְהוֹן:


Instead of your having suffered shame, and confusion, I will bring unto you double mercies, from which I have promised you; and the Gentiles who glory in their portion shall be confounded: therefore in their own land they shall possess double: everlasting joy shall be unto them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:8)


אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ רָחֵימְנָא דִינָא מְרַחֵק קֳדָמַי שִׁקְרָא וְאוֹנָסָא וְאֶתֵּן אֲגַר עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן בִּקְשׁוֹט וּקְיַם עֲלַם אֶגְזַר לְהוֹן:


For I, the Lord, love justice; lying and violence are an abomination in my presence; and I will render the reward of their work in truth, and I will make an everlasting covenant with them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:9)


וְיִתְרַבּוּן בְּעַמְמַיָא בְנֵיהוֹן וּבְּנֵי בְנֵיהוֹן בְּגוֹ מַלְכְּוָתָא כָּל חֶזְאֵיהוֹן יִשְׁתְּמוֹדְעִינוּן אֲרֵי אִינוּן זַרְעָא דְבָרְכֵהּ יְיָ:


And their sons shall be magnified amongst the nations, and their sons' sons amongst the kingdoms: all that see them shall acknowledge them, that they are the seed which the Lord has blessed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:10)


אֲמָרַת יְרוּשְׁלֵם מֶחְדָא אֶחְדֵי בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ תְּבוּעַ נַפְשִׁי בְּפוּרְקָנָא דֶאֱלָהִי אֲרֵי אַלְבְּשַׁנִי לְבוּשִׁין דְפוּרְקַן מְעִין דִזְכוּ עִטְפַנִי כַּחֲתָנָא דְמִצְלַח בִּגְנוּנֵהּ וּכְכַהֲנָא רַבָּא דְמַתְקַן בִּלְבוּשׁוֹהִי וּכְכַלְתָא דְמִתְקַשְׁטָא בְתִיקוּנָהָא:


Jerusalem shall say, I will greatly rejoice in the Word of the Lord, my soul shall be joyful in the salvation of my God; for He hath clothed me with the garments of salvation, He hath covered me with the upper garments of righteousness, like a bridegroom who is happy in his bridechamber, and like the high priest who decketh himself with his robes, and like a bride who is adorned with her jewels.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 61:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/61:11)


אֲרֵי כְאַרְעָא דְמַפְקָא צִמְחָהּ וּכְגִנַת שַׁקְיָא דְזֵירוּעָהָא מְרַבְיָא כֵּן יְיָ אֱלֹהִים יִגְלֵי זְכוּתָא וְתוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא דִירוּשְׁלֵם לָקֳבֵל כָּל עַמְמַיָא:


For as the earth bringeth forth her shoots, and as a watered garden causeth the things that are sown in it to grow, thus the Lord God shall reveal the righteousness and the praise of Jerusalem before all the nations.



## Chapter 62

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:1)


עַד דְאַעְבֵּד פּוּרְקַן לְצִיוֹן לָא אָנִיחַ לְעַמְמַיָא וְעַד דְאַיְתֵי נְחָמָא לִירוּשְׁלֵם לָא אַשְׁקֵט לְמַלְכְּוָתָא עַד דְיִתְגְלֵי כִּשְׁפַרְפָרָא נְהוֹרָא וּפוּרְקָנָא כְּבָעִיר יִבְעָר:


Till I work salvation for Zion, I will give no rest to the nations, until I bring consolation to Jerusalem, I will give no rest to the kingdoms, till her light be revealed as the early dawn, and her salvation shall burn as a lamp.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:2)


וְיֶחְזוּן עַמְמַיָא זְכוּתִיךְ וְכָל מַלְכַיָא יְקָרִיךְ וְיִקְרוֹן לִיךְ שְׁמָא חַדְתָּא דִי בְמֵימְרֵהּ דַייָ יִפְרְשִׁינָהּ:


And the Gentiles shall see thy righteousness, and all kings thy glory: and they shall call thee by a new name, which the Word of the Lord shall declare.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:3)


וּתְהוֹן כְּלִיל דְחֶדְוָא קֳדָם יְיָ וּכְתַר דְתוּשְׁבְּחָא קֳדָם אֱלָהָיִךְ:


Ye shall be a crown of joy before the Lord, and a diadem of glory before thy God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:4)


לָא יִתְאֲמַר לִיךְ עוֹד שְׁבִיקָא וּלְאַרְעִיךְ לָא יִתְאֲמַר עוֹד צַדְיָא אֲרֵי לִיךְ יִתְקְרֵי עָבְדֵי רְעוּתִי בָה וּלְאַרְעִיךְ יָתְבָא אֲרֵי תְהֵי רַעֲוָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ בִיךְ וְאַרְעֵיךְ תִּתְיַתָב:


Thou shalt no more be termed Forsaken; neither shall thy land any more be termed Desolate: but thou shalt be called Aběday Rěoothee Bahh, and thy land Inhabited: for the Lord shall delight in thee, and thy land shall be inhabited.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:5)


אֲרֵי כְמָא דְמִתְיַתַּב עוּלֵים עִם בְּתוּלְתָּא כֵּן יִתְיַתְבוּן בְּגַוַיְכִי בְּנַיְכִי וּכְמָא דְחָדֵי חֲתָנָא עִם כַּלְתָא יֶחְדֵי עֲלָךְ אֱלָהָיִךְ:


For as a young man dwelleth with a virgin, thus thy sons shall dwell in thee; and as the bridegroom rejoiceth over the bride, so shall thy God rejoice over thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:6)


הָא עַל עוֹבָדֵי אֲבָהָתָךְ צַדִיקַיָא קַרְתָּא דִירוּשְׁלֵם מְתַקְנִין נַטְרִין קֳדָמַי כָּל יְמָמָא וְכָל לֵילְיָא תְּדִירָא לָא פָּסְקִין מִתְאֲמַר דָכְרָן טַבְוָתִיךְ קֳדָם יְיָ לָא פָסֵיק לְכוֹן:


Behold, the works of thy righteous fathers, O city of Jerusalem, are right, and kept by me all the day and all the night, continually; the remembrance of thine excellencies is declared before the Lord, it shall not fail you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:7)


וְלָא יִפְסוֹק דָכְרַנְהוֹן מִן קָדָמוֹהִי עַד דִיתַקֵן וְעַד דִישַׁוֵי יַת יְרוּשְׁלֵם תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא בְּאַרְעָא:


Their remembrance shall not cease from before Him, till He establish and make Jerusalem a praise in the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:8)


קַיָם יְיָ בִּימִינֵהּ וּבִדְרַע תּוּקְפֵהּ אִם אֶתֵּן יַת עִבּוּרִיךְ עוֹד מֵיכַל לְבַעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיךְ וְאִם יִשְׁתּוּן בְּנֵי עַמְמַיָא חַמְרִיךְ דִלְאֵית בֵּיהּ:


The Lord hath sworn by His right hand, and by the arm of His strength, I will no more give thy corn to be food for thy enemies; and the sons of the Gentiles shall not drink thy wine, for the which thou hast laboured.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:9)


אֲרֵי דְכַנְשׁוֹהִי לְעִבּוּרָא יֵיכְלוּנֵהּ וִישַׁבְּחוּן קֳדָם יְיָ וּדְעַצְרוֹהִי לְחַמְרָא יִשְׂתּוּנֵהּ בְּדָרַת קוּדְשִׁי:


But they that gather the corn, they shall eat it, and offer praise before the Lord; and they that tread out the wine, they shall drink it in the courts of my holiness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:10)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא עֲבָרוּ וְתוּבוּ בְּתַרְעַיָא אַפְנוֹ לֵב עַמָא לְאוֹרַח תַּקְנָא בַּסְרוּ בְּשׂוּרָן טָבָן וְנֶחָמָן לְצַדִיקַיָא דִסְלִיקוּ הִירְהוּר יִצְרָא דְהוּא כְּאֶבֶן תַּקְלָא אֲרִימוּ אָתָא עַל עַמְמַיָא:


O prophets, pass through and return through the gates; turn the heart of the people into the right way; bring good tidings of good things, and consolations to the righteous, who remove the thoughts of an evil imagination, which are like a stone of stumbling; lift up a standard “ for the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:11)


הָא יְיָ אַשְׁמַע לִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא אֱמָרוּ לִכְנִשְׁתָּא דְצִיוֹן דָא פָרְקִיךְ מִתְגְלֵי הָא אֲגַר עָבְדֵי מֵימְרֵהּ עִמֵהּ וְכָל עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן גְלַן קֳדָמוֹהִי:


Behold, the Lord hath proclaimed unto the ends of the world, Say ye to the congregation of Zion, Behold, thy Redeemer shall be revealed; Behold, the reward for them that do His word is with Him: and all their works are manifest before Him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 62:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/62:12)


וְיִקְרוֹן לְהוֹן עַמָא דְקוּדְשָׁא פְּרִיקַיָא דַייָ וְלִיךְ יִתְקְרֵי תְבִיעֲתָא קַרְתָּא דְלָא אִתְרְחָקַת:


And they shall call them, The holy people, The redeemed of the Lord: and thou shalt be called Teviātha, (i. e. "Sought out"), A city not forsaken.”



## Chapter 63

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:1)


מַן אֲמַר אִלֵין עָתִיד לְאַיְתָאָה מָחָא עַל אֱדוֹם פּוּרְעָנָא תַקִיפָא עַל בָּצְרָה לְמֶעְבַּד פּוּרְעֲנוּת דִין עַמֵהּ כְּמָא דְקַיֵים לְהוֹן בְּמֵימְרֵהּ אֲמַר הָא אֲנָא מִתְגְלֵי כְּמָא דְמַלֵלִית בְּזָכוּ סַגְיָא קֳדָמַי חֵיל לְמִפְרַק:


Who hath spoken these things? He who shall bring the blow upon Persia, the mighty vengeance upon Bozrah, to execute vengeance of judgment of His people, as He hath sworn unto them by His Word. He hath said, Behold, I shall reveal myself as I have spoken in righteousness, great is my power to save.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:2)


מָא דֵין יִסְמְקוּן טוּרִין מִדַם קְטִילִין וּמֵישְׁרִין יִפְקוּן כַּחְמַר בְּמַעְצְרָא:


Wherefore are the mountains red from the blood of the slain? yea, the valleys shall flow as the wine from the winepress.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:3)


הָא כְּבִיעוּט דְמִתְבָּעֵיט בְּמַעְצְרָא כֵּן יִסְגֵי קְטוֹל בְּמַשִׁרְיַת עַמְמַיָא וְלָא יְהֵי לְהוֹן תְּקוֹף קֳדָמַי וְאֶקְטְלִינוּן בְּרוּגְזִי וַאֲדוּשִׁינוּן בְּחַמְתִי וְאֶתְבַּר תַּקִיפֵיהוֹן קֳדָמַי וְכָן חַכִּימֵיהוֹן אֱסַלְעִים:


Behold, as the grapes are trodden in the vat, thus He shall increase the slaughter in the camps of the nations; they shall have no strength before me: yea, I will slay them in my anger, and tread them down in my fury; and I will break the strength of their mighty ones before me, and all their wise men will I consume.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:4)


אֲרֵי יוֹם פּוּרְעֲנוּתָא קֳדָמַי וּשְׁנַת פּוּרְקַן עַמִי מְטָת:


For the day of vengeance is before me, and the year of the salvation of my people hath come.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:5)


וּגְלֵי קֳדָמַי וְלֵית גְבַר דְלֵיהּ עוֹבָדִין טָבִין וִידִיַע קֳדָמַי וְלֵית אֱנַשׁ דִיקוּם וְיִבְעֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן וּפְרַקְתִּינוּן בִּדְרַע תּוּקְפִי וּבְמֵימַר רְעוּתִי סְעַדְתִּינוּן:


And it is revealed before me, that there is no man whose works are good, and it is known to me, that there is no man who will stand up and pray in behalf of them; yea, I will redeem them by the arm of my strength, and by the WORD of my delight I will help them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:6)


וַאֲקַטֵל עַמְמַיָא בְּרוּגְזִי וַאֲדוֹשְׁשִׁינוֹן בְּחֵימְתִי וְאֶרְמֵי לַאֲרַע אַרְעִיתָא קְטִילֵי גִבָּרֵיהוֹן:


And I will slay the nations in my anger, and I will tread them down in my fury, and I will cast the slain of their mighty ones into the lowest parts of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:7)


אֲמַר נְבִיָא טִיבוּתָא דַייָ אֲנָא מַדְכַּר תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא דַייָ כְּעַל כָּל דְגַמְלָנָא יְיָ וְסַגִי טוּבֵהּ לְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל דְגַמְלִינוּן כְּרַחֲמוֹהִי וּכְסַגְיוּת טוּבֵהּ:


The prophet said, I will mention the lovingkindness of the Lord; the praise of the Lord according to all that the Lord hath dealt bountifully with us, and His great goodness toward the house of Israel which He hath bestowed upon them according to His mercies, and according to the multitude of His loving-kindnesses.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:8)
