וַאֲמַר בְּרַם עַמִי אִינוּן בְּנַיָא דְלָא יְשַׁקְרוּן וַהֲוָה מֵימְרֵהּ לְהוֹן פָּרִיק:


And He said, Surely, they are my people, children that will not lie: and His WORD became their salvation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:9)


בְּכָל עִדַן דְחָבוּ קֳדָמוֹהִי לְאַיְתָאָה עֲלֵיהוֹן עָקָא לָא אָעִיק לְהוֹן וּמַלְאַךְ שְׁלִיחַ מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי פְרֵיקִינוּן בְּרַחֲמָתֵהּ וּבִמְחָסֵהּ עֲלֵיהוֹן הָא שֵׁיזְבִינוּן וְנַטְלִינוּן וְסוֹבְרִינוּן כָּל יוֹמֵי עַלְמָא:


Whenever they sinned against Him, that He might have brought upon them distress, He did not distress them; but an angel was sent from Him, who in His mercy redeemed them; and in His compassion, behold, He delivered them; and He bare them, and carried them all the days of old.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:10)


וְאִינוּן סָרִיבוּ וְאַרְגִיזוּ עַל מֵימַר נְבִיֵי קוּדְשֵׁהּ וְאִתְהַפֵּךְ מֵימְרֵהּ לְהוֹן לִבְעֵיל דְבַב הוּא אַגִיחַ קְרָבָא בְּהוֹן:


But they rebelled against the word of His holy prophets, and blasphemed, and His WORD became their enemy, and He waged war against them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:11)


וְחָס עַל יְקַר שְׁמֵהּ בְּדִיל דוּכְרָן טַבְוָתֵהּ דְמִן עַלְמָא גְבוּרָן דַעֲבַד עַל יְדֵי מֹשֶׁה לְעַמֵהּ דִלְמָא יֵימַר אָן דְאַסְקִינוּן מִן יַמָא אָן דְדַבְּרִינוּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא כְּרַעֲיָא לְעָנֵהּ אָן דְאַשְׁרֵי בֵינֵיהוֹן יַת מֵימַר נְבִיֵי קוּדְשֵׁהּ:


And He had compassion for the glory of His name, because of the remembrance of His goodness of old, the mighty works which He did by the hands of Moses for His people; lest the Gentiles should say, Where is He that brought them up out of the sea? Where is He that led them through the wilderness, as a shepherd his flock? Where is He that made the word of His holy prophets to dwell amongst them?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:12)


דַבֵּר לְיַמִינָא דְמֹשֶׁה דְרַע תּוּשְׁבַּחְתֵּהּ בְּזַע מֵי יַמָא דְסוּף מִן קֳדָמֵיהוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד לֵיהּ שׁוּם עֲלָם:


That led them by the right hand of Moses, the arm of His glory, dividing the waters of the Red Sea before them, to make Himself an everlasting name?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:13)


דַבְּרִינוּן בֵּין תְּחוֹמַיָא כְּסוּסְיָא דִי בְמַדְבְּרָא לָא מַתְקַל כֵּן אַף אִינוּן לָא אִתְקִילוּ:


That led them through the depths, as a horse that stumbleth not in the plain? thus also they stumbled not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:14)


כִּבְעִירָא דִי בְּמֵישְׁרָא מַדְבְּרָא מֵימְרָא דַייָ דַבְּרִינוּן כֵּן דִבַּרְתֵּהּ לְעַמָךְ לְמֶעְבַּד לָךְ שׁוּם דְתוּשְׁבְּחָא:


As a beast is led in a plain, the WORD of the Lord led them; so didst Thou lead Thy people to make Thyself a glorious name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:15)


אִסְתְּכֵי מִן שְׁמַיָא וְאִתְגְלֵי מִמְדוֹר קוּדְשָׁךְ וְתוּשְׁבַּחְתָּךְ אָן פּוּרְעֲנוּתָךְ וְגִבַּרְוָתָךְ הֲמוֹן טַבְוָתָךְ וְסַגְיוּת רַחֲמָךְ עֲלַי יֶחְסְנוּן:


Look down from heaven, and reveal Thyself from the habitation of Thy holiness and of Thy glory: where is Thy vengeance, and Thy great might? the multitude of Thy mercies and Thy compassion towards me are restrained.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:16)


אֲרֵי אַתְּ הוּא דְרַחֲמָךְ עֲלָנָא סַגִיאִין כְּאָב עַל בְּנִין אֲרֵי אַבְרָהָם לָא אַסְקָנָא מִמִצְרַיִם וְיִשְׂרָאֵל לָא עָבַד לָנָא פְּרִישָׁן בְּמַדְבְּרָא אַתְּ הוּא יְיָ וְרַחְמָךְ עֲלָנָא סַגִיאִין כְּאָב עַל בְּנִין פִּרְקְנָא מַעֲלַם שְׁמָךְ:


For Thou art He, whose mercies towards us are as many as a father's towards his children: for Abraham hath not brought us up out of Egypt, and Israel hath not wrought for us wonderful works in the wilderness; Thou art the Lord; Thy mercies towards us are many, like a father's towards his children, O, our Redeemer; Thy name is from everlasting.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:17)


לְמָה תְרַחֲקִינָנָא יְיָ לְמִטְעֵי מֵאוֹרְחָן דְתַקְנָן קֳדָמָךְ כְּעַמְמַיָא דְלֵית לְהוֹן חוּלַק בְּאוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתָךְ לָא יִתְפְּנֵי לִבָּנָא מִדְחַלְתָּךְ אֲתֵיב שְׁכִנְתָּךְ לְעַמָךְ בְּדִיל עַבְדָךְ צַדִיקַיָא דְקַיֵמְתָּא לְהוֹן בְּמֵימְרָךְ לְמֶעְבַּד בֵּינֵיהוֹן שִׁבְטֵי אַחְסַנְתָּךְ:


Why hast Thou cast us off, O Lord, that we should go astray from the paths which are right in Thy sight, as the nations which have no part in the instruction of Thy law? Our heart is not turned away from Thy fear: return Thy Shekinah unto Thy people, for the sake of Thy righteous servants, unto whom Thou hast sworn by Thy WORD to make the tribes amongst them Thine inheritance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:18)


בִּזְעֵיר דִירִיתוּ עַמָא דְקוּדְשָׁךְ בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָנָא דוֹשִׁישׁוּ מַקְדָשָׁךְ:


The people of Thy holiness have possessed Thy sanctuary but a little time, our adversaries have trodden it down.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 63:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/63:19)


הָא אֲנַחְנָא עַמָךְ דְמִן עַלְמָא לָא לְעַמְמַיָא יְהַבְתְּ אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתָךְ לָא אִתְקְרֵי שְׁמָךְ עֲלֵיהוֹן לָא לְהוֹן אַרְכֵינְתָּא שְׁמַיָא אִתְגְלֵיתָא מִן קֳדָמָךְ טוּרַיָא זָעוּ:


We are Thy people that were of old: not unto the Gentiles hast Thou given the doctrine of Thy law, neither is Thy name invoked upon them; not unto them hast Thou inclined the heavens and revealed Thyself; the mountains quaked before Thee.



## Chapter 64

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:1)


כַּד שְׁלַחְתָּא רוּגְזָךְ כְּאֶשְׁתָּא בְּיוֹמֵי אֵלִיָהוּ אִתְמַסְיָא יַמָא מַיָא מְלָחֲכָא אֶשְׁתָּא לְהוֹדִיעַ שְׁמָךְ לְסַנְאֵי עַמָךְ מִן קֳדָמָךְ עַמְמַיָא זָעוּ:


When Thou didst send forth Thine anger like fire in the days of Elijah, the sea was melted, the waters were flames of fire, to make Thy name known to the enemies of Thy people, that the nations may tremble at Thy presence.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:2)


בְּמַעְבְּדָךְ פְּרִישָׁן לָא סַבַּרְנָא לְהוֹן אִתְגְלִיתָא מִן קֳדָמָךְ טוּרַיָא זָעוּ:


When Thou didst wonderful things, which we expected not, Thou didst reveal Thyself; the mountains trembled at Thy presence.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:3)


וּמֵעַלְמָא לָא שְׁמַעְתְּ אוֹדֶן קַל גְבוּרָן לָא אֲצֵיתַת לְמִלוּל זִיעַ עַיִן לָא חָזַת מָא דַחֲזוֹ עַמָךְ שְׁכִינַת יְקָרָךְ יְיָ אֲרֵי לֵית בַּר מִנָךְ דְאַתְּ עֲתִיד לְמֶעְבַּד לְעַמָךְ צַדִיקַיָא דְמִן עַלְמָא דִמְסַבְּרִין לְפוּרְקָנָךְ:


And since the world was, ear hath not heard the report of such mighty deeds, nor hearkened to the speech of rapture, nor hath eye seen, what Thy people saw, the Shekinah of Thy glory, O Lord; for there is none besides Thee, who will do such things for Thy righteous people, who were of old; who hope for Thy salvation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:4)


מִתְעַרְעִין קֳדָמָךְ עוֹבָדֵי אַבְהָתָנָא צַדִיקַיָא דַחֲדִיא לְמֶעְבַּד רְעוּתָךְ בִּקְשׁוֹט וּבְזָכוּ בְּאוֹרַח טוּבָךְ וְרַחֲמָךְ הֲווֹ דְכִירִין לִדְחַלְתָּךְ הָא בְּכָל עִדַן דַהֲוָה רְגַז מִן קֳדָמָךְ עֲלָנָא עַל דְחַבְנָא בְהוֹן בְּעוֹבָדֵי אֲבָהָתָנָא צַדִיקַיָא דְמִן עַלְמָא אֲנַחְנָא מִתְפָּרְקִין:


The works of our righteous fathers were acceptable in Thy sight, who rejoiced to do Thy will in truth and in righteousness: in the way of Thy goodness and mercy they remembered Thy fear whenever Thy wrath from Thy presence was upon us, because we had sinned: on account of the works of our righteous fathers, we have been saved.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:5)


וַהֲוֵינָא כִמְסָאָב כּוּלָנָא וְכִלְבוּשׁ מְרָחֵק כָּל זַכְוָתָנָא וּנְתַרְנָא כְּמִתַּר טְרַף כּוּלָנָא וּבְחוֹבָנָא כְּרוּחָא אִתְנְטֵילְנָא:


But we are all as an unclean thing; all our righteousnesses are as an abominable garment, and we all do fade as the fading of a leaf; and because of our sins, we are taken away as by the wind.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:6)


וְלֵית דְמָצְלֵי בִשְׁמָךְ מִתְרְעֵי לְאַתְקָפָא בִדְחַלְתָּךְ אֲרֵי סְלֵיקְתָּא אַפֵּי שְׁכִנְתָּךְ מִנָנָא וּמְסַרְתָּנָא בְּיַד חוֹבָנָא:


And there is none that prayeth in Thy name willing to lay hold on Thy fear; because Thou hast taken away the fear of Thy Shekinah from us, and Thou hast delivered us to the power of our sins.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:7)


וְאַתְּ יְיָ רַחֲמָךְ עֲלָנָא סַגִיאִין כְּאַב עַל בְּנִין אֲנַחְנָא טִינָא וְאַתְּ בְּרֵיתָנָא וְעוֹבַד גְבוּרְתָּךְ כּוּלָנָא:


And Thy mercies, O Lord, have been as many upon us as a father's upon his children; we are the clay, and Thou hast formed us, and we all are the work of Thy hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:8)


לָא יְהֵי רְגַז מִן קֳדָמָךְ יְיָ בָּנָא עַד לַחֲדָא לָא לְעַלְמָא תִדְכַּר חוֹבִי הָא גְלֵי קֳדָמָךְ דְעַמָךְ אֲנַחְנָא כּוּלָנָא:


Let there not be, O Lord, very sore wrath from Thee against us, neither remember iniquities for ever: behold, it is revealed before Thee, that we are all Thy people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:9)


קִרְוֵי קוּדְשָׁךְ הֲוָאָה מַדְבְּרָא צִיוֹן מַדְבְּרָא הֲוָת וִירוּשְׁלֵם צַדְיָא:


Thy holy cities have become a wilderness, Zion is a wilderness, Jerusalem has become a desolation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:10)


בֵּית קוּדְשָׁנָא וְתוּשְׁבַּחְתָּנָא אֲתַר דִפְלָחוּ קֳדָמָךְ אֲבְהָתָנָא הֲוָה לִיקֵידַת נוּר וְכָל בֵּית רַנְגָנָא הֲוָה לְחָרְבָּא:


The house of our holiness and of our glory, where our fathers worshipped Thee, is burned up with fire: and every place of our desire hath become a waste.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 64:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/64:11)


הַעַל אִלֵין תִּתְחַסָן יְיָ וְאַתְּ יָהֵיב אַרְכָּא לְרַשִׁיעַיָא דִמְשַׁעְבְּדִין בָּנָא עַד עַלְמָא:


Wilt Thou refrain Thyself for these things, O Lord? and wilt Thou give prosperity for ever to the wicked, who make us servile?



## Chapter 65

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:1)


אִשְׁתְּאֵילִית בְּמֵימְרִי לִדְלָא שְׁאִילוּ מִן קֳדָמַי תְּבָעֵית אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְתִי לִדְלָא תְּבָעוּ דְחַלְתִּי אֲמָרֵית הָא אֲנָא מִשְׁתָּאֵיל תְּדִירָא כָּל יוֹמָא לְעַם דְלָא מְצַלֵי בִשְׁמִי:


I am asked concerning my WORD of them that have not asked after me; I am inquired of for instruction in my law, of them who have not sought my fear: I will say, Behold, I am inquired after continually, all the day, of a people that hath not prayed in my name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:2)


שְׁלָחִית נְבִיאֵי כָּל יוֹמָא לְעַמָא מְסָרְבָנָא דְאָזְלִין בְּאוֹרַח דְלָא תַקְנָא בָּתַר עֶשְׁתּוֹנֵיהוֹן:


I have sent my prophets every day unto a rebellious people, which walketh in a way which was not good, after their own thoughts;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:3)


עַמָא דְמַרְגְזִין עַל מֵימְרִי קֳדָמַי תְּדִירָא דָבְחִין בְּגִנָן טַעֲוָתָא וּמַסְקִין בּוּסְמַיָא עַל לִבְנַיָא:


A people that provoketh my WORD to anger continually to my face; that sacrificeth in gardens to idols, and offereth aromatic spices upon bricks;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:4)


דְיָתְבִין בְּבָתַּיָא דְבָנָן מֵעֲפַר קִבְרַיָא וְעִם פִּגְרֵי בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא דַיְרִין דְאָכְלִין בְּשַׂר חֲזִירָא וּרְטַב פִּגוּל בְּמַנֵיהוֹן:


Who dwell in houses, which are built of the dust of graves, with the dead bodies of the sons of men they dwell, which eat swine's flesh, and broth of abominable things is in their vessels;


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:5)


דְאָמְרִין רָחֵיק לְהָלָא לָא תִקְרַב בִּי אֲרֵי דָכֵינָא מִנָךְ אִלֵין רוּגְזֵיהוֹן כִּתְנָנָא קֳדָמַי פּוּרְעֲנוּתְהוֹן בְּגֵיהִנָם דְאֶשְׁתָּא דְלָקָא בָהּ כָּל יוֹמָא:


Who say, Stand by thyself, come not near to me; because I am purer than thou. These their provocations are as smoke before me: their recompense shall be in hell, where the fire burneth all the day.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:6)


הָא כְתִיבָא קֳדָמַי לָא אֶתֵּן לְהוֹן אַרְכָּא בְּחַיַיָא אֱלָהֵן אֲשַׁלֵם לְהוֹן פּוּרְעֲנוּת חוֹבֵיהוֹן וְאֶמְסוֹר לְמוֹתָא תִּנְיָנָא יַת גְוִיַתְהוֹן:


Behold, it is written before me: I will not give unto them prolongation in this life; but I will recompense unto them the wages for their sins, and deliver their bodies to the second death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:7)


חוֹבֵיכוֹן וְחוֹבֵי אֲבָהַתְכוֹן גְלָן קֳדָמַי כַּחֲדָא אֲמַר יְיָ דַאֲסִיקוּ בוּסְמִין עַל טוּרַיָא וְעַל רְמָתָא חֲסִידוּ קֳדָמַי וְאֶתֵּן אֲגַר עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן בְּקַדְמֵיתָא עַל חֵיקְהוֹן:


Your sins and the sins of your fathers together are revealed before me, saith the Lord, which have offered incense upon the mountains, and blasphemed me upon the hills: therefore will I give the reward of their former works into their bosom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:8)


כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ כְּמָא דְאִשְׁתְּכַח נֹחַ זַכַּאי בְּדָרָא דְטוֹפָנָא וַאֲמָרֵית דְלָא לְחַבְּלוּתֵהּ בְּדִיל לְקַיָמָא עַלְמָא מִנֵיהּ כֵּן אַעְבֵּיד בְּדִיל עַבְדֵי צַדִיקַיָא בְּדִיל דְלָא לְחַבָּלָא כֻּלָא:


Thus saith the Lord, As Noah was found righteous in the generation of the deluge, and I said that I would not destroy him, for the purpose that I might raise up the world by him; so will I do for my righteous servants' sakes, in order that I may not destroy them all.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:9)


וְאַפֵיק מִיַעֲקֹב זַרְעָא וּמִיהוּדָה יָרֵית טוּרֵי וְיַחְסְנוּנָהּ בְּחִירִי וְעַבְדֵי צַדִיקַיָא יֵשְׁרוּן תַּמָן:


And I will bring forth a seed out of Jacob, and out of Judah an inheritor of my mountains: and mine elect shall inherit it, and my righteous servants shall dwell there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:10)


וִיהֵי שָׁרוֹנָא לְבֵית מֵישְׁרֵי עֶדְרִין דְעָן וּמֵישַׁר עָכוּר לְבֵיתָא רַבָּעָה בַּקְרָן דְתוֹרִין לְעַמִי דִתְבָעוּ דְחַלְתִּי:


And Sharon shall be a fold of flocks, and the valley of Achor a place for the crouching down of the herds of oxen, for my people which have sought my fear.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:11)


וְאַתּוּן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל שְׁבַקְתּוּן פּוּלְחָנָא דַייָ אִתְנְשֵׁיתוּן יַת פּוּלְחַן טוּרָא דְקוּדְשִׁי דִמְסַדְרִין לְטַעֲוָן פְּתוֹרִין וּמְמַזְגִין לִדְחַלְתְּהוֹן אֲגָנִין:


But ye, O house of Israel, ye have forsaken the worship of the Lord, ye have forgotten the worship of my holy mountain, that prepare tables for the idols, and mix goblets for their gods.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:12)


וְאֶמְסַר יַתְכוֹן לְחַרְבָּא וְכוּלְכוֹן לְקַטָלָא תִתְמַסְרוּן חֲלַף דִשְׁלָחֵית נְבִיֵי וְלָא תַבְתּוּן אִתְנַבִּיאוּ וְלָא קַבֵּילְתּוּן וַעֲבַדְתּוּן דְבִישׁ קֳדָמַי וּבִדְלָא צְבִינָא אִתְרְעֵיתוּן:


Therefore will I deliver them to the sword, yea, all of you shall be delivered to the slaughter; because I sent my prophets, and ye repented' not; they prophesied, and ye did not receive them; but did evil before me, and delighted yourselves in that I would not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:13)


בְכֵן כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ אֱלֹהִים הָא עַבְדֵי צַדִיקַיָא יֵכְלוּן וְאַתּוּן רַשִׁיעַיָא תִּכְפְּנוּן הָא עַבְדֵי צַדִיקַיָא יִשְׁתּוּן וְאַתּוּן רַשִׁיעַיָא תִּצְחוֹן הָא עַבְדֵי צַדִיקַיָא יֶחְדוֹן וְאַתּוּן רַשִׁיעַיָא תִּבְהֲתוּן:


Therefore thus saith the Lord God, Behold, my righteous servants shall eat, but ye, O wicked, shall be hungry: behold, my righteous servants shall drink, but ye, O wicked, shall be thirsty: behold, my righteous servants shall rejoice, but ye, O wicked, shall be ashamed:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:14)


הָא עַבְדֵי צַדִיקַיָא יְשַׁבְּחוּן מִטוּב לֵב וְאַתּוּן תִּצַוְחוּן מִכְּאֵב לֵב וּמִתְּבַר רוּחַ תְּיַלְלוּן:


Behold, my righteous servants shall sing for joy of heart, but ye shall cry for sorrow of heart, and ye shall howl for vexation of spirit.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:15)


וְתִשְׁבְּקוּן שׁוּמְכוֹן לְקַיָמָא לִבְחִירִי וִימִיתְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלֹהִים מוֹתָא תִנְיָנָא וּלְעַבְדוֹהִי צַדִיקַיָא יִקְרֵי שְׁמָא אוֹחֲרָנָא:


And ye shall leave your name for a curse to my chosen: for the Lord God shall slay you with the second death, 10 and call His righteous servants by another name:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:16)


דִמְבָרֵךְ בְּאַרְעָא יְבָרֵךְ בֶּאֱלָהָא קַיָמָא וְדִמְקַיֵם בְּאַרְעָא יְקַיֵם בֶּאֱלָהָא קַיָמָא אֲרֵי יִתְנַשְׁיָן עָקָתָא קַדְמְיָתָא וַאֲרֵי מְסַתְּרָן מִן קֳדָמַי:


That he who blesseth in the earth shall bless by the God of the covenant, and he that sweareth in the earth shall swear by the God of the covenant; because, the former troubles shall be forgotten, and because they shall be hidden from before me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:17)


אֲרֵי הָא אֲנָא בְרָא שְׁמַיָא חַדְתִּין וְאַרְעָא חַדְתָּא וְלָא יְדַכְּרוּן קַדְמְיָתָא וְלָא יִתַּסְקוּן עַל לֵב:


For, behold, I create new heavens and a new earth: and the former shall not be remembered, neither shall they come into mind.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:18)


אֱלָהֵין יֶחְדוּן וִיבוּעוּן בַּעֲלַם עָלְמַיָא דַאֲנָא בָרֵי אֲרֵי הָא אֲנָא בָרֵי יַת יְרוּשְׁלֵם בִּיעָא וְעַמָהּ חָדֵי:


But be ye glad and rejoice in the world of worlds, which I create; for behold, I create Jerusalem a rejoicing, and her people a joy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:19)


וַאֲבוּעַ בִּירוּשְׁלֵם וְיֶחְדוּן בָהּ עַמִי וְלָא יִשְׁתְּמַע בָּהּ עוֹד קַל דְבָכָן וְקַל דִמְצַוְחִין:


And I will rejoice in Jerusalem, and my people shall be glad in her: and the voice of weeping shall be no more heard in her, nor the voice of those that cry.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:20)


וְלָא יְהֵי מִתַּמָן עוֹד יָנִיק יוֹמִין וְסָבָא דְלָא יַשְׁלַם יַת יוֹמוֹהִי אֲרֵי דְחַיֵיב עוֹלֵים בַּר מְאָה שְׁנִין יְהֵי מָאֵית וּדְחָטֵי בַּר מְאָה שְׁנִין יִתְּרָךְ:


There shall be no more thence a suckling of days, or an old man that shall not accomplish his days, for even the child that sinneth shall die an hundred years old; but he that is a sinner being an hundred years old shall be thrust out.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:21)


וְיִבְנוֹן בָּתִּין וְיִתְבוּן וְיִצְבוּן כַּרְמִין וְיֵכְלוּן אִבְּהוֹן:


And they shall build houses, and inhabit them; and they shall plant vineyards, and eat the fruit of them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:22)


לָא יִבְנוֹן וְאָחְרִין יָתְבוּן לָא יִצְבוּן וְאוֹחֲרָנִין יֵכְלוּן אֲרֵי כְיוֹמֵי אִילָן חַיַיָא יוֹמֵי עַמִי וְעוֹבֵד יְדֵיהוֹן יְבַלוּן בְּחִירָי:


They shall not build, and others inhabit; they shall not plant, and others eat: for as the days of the tree of life are the days of my people, and mine elect shall wear out the work of their hands.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:23)


לָא יֶחְלוּן לְרֵיקָן וְלָא יִרְבּוּן לְמוֹתָא אֲרֵי זַרְעָא דְבָרְכֵיהּ יְיָ אִינוּן וּבְנֵי בְנֵיהוֹן עִמְהוֹן:


They shall not labour in vain, neither shall they rear up children for death, for they shall be the seed which the Lord has blessed, and the children of their children with them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:24)


וִיהֵי עַד לָא יִצְלוּן קֳדָמַי וַאֲנָא אֲקַבֵּל צְלוֹתְהוֹן עַד לָא יִבְעוֹן מִן קֳדָמַי אַעְבֵּיד בָּעוּתְהוֹן:


And it shall come to pass, before they shall pray before me, that I will hear their prayer; and before they supplicate before me, I will grant their request.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 65:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/65:25)


דֵיבָא וְאִמְרָא יִרְעוּן כַּחֲדָא וְאַרְיָא כְּתוֹרָא יֵיכוּל תִבְנָא וְחִוְיָא עַפְרָא מְזוֹנֵיהּ לָא יִבְאֲשׁוּן וְלָא יְחַבְּלוּן בְּכָל טוּרָא דְקוּדְשִׁי אֲמַר יְיָ:


The wolf and the lamb shall feed together, and the lion shall eat straw like the bullock: and as for the serpent, dust shall be its food. They shall not hurt nor destroy in all my holy mountain, saith the Lord.



## Chapter 66

###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:1)


כִּדִנַן אֲמַר יְיָ שְׁמַיָא כוּרְסֵי יְקָרֵי וְאַרְעָא כְּבַשׁ קֳדָמַי אֵידֵין בֵּיתָא דְתִבְנוֹן קֳדָמַי וְאֵידֵין אֲתַר בֵּית אַשְׁרָיוּת שְׁכִנְתִּי:


Thus saith the Lord, the heavens are the throne of my glory, and the earth is a footstool before me: where is the house that ye build unto me? and where is the place of the dwelling of my Shekinah?


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:2)


וְיַת כָּל אִלֵין גְבוּרְתִי עֲבָדַת וַהֲלָא הֲוָאָה כָל אִלֵין אֲמַר יְיָ וּבְדֵין רַעֲוָא קֳדָמַי לְאִסְתַּכָּלָא בֵּיהּ בִּדְעַנְוְתָן וּמַכִּיךְ רוּחַ וּמִשְׁתְּוֵי לִקֳבֵל פִתְגָמִי:


For all these things my power hath made, and have not all these things been? saith the Lord: but it shall be my delight to consider the man, eren him, who is of an humble and a contrite spirit, and trembleth on account of my word.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:3)


נָכֵיס תּוֹרָא כְּקָטִיל גְבַר דָבַח אִימַר כְּנָקֵיף כְּלֵב מַסְקֵי קוּרְבַּן דַם חֲזִירָא קוּרְבַּן מַתְּנַתְהוֹן מַתְּנַת אוֹנָס אַף אִינוּן אִתְרְעִיאוּ בְּאוֹרְחַתְהוֹן וּבְשִׁיקוּצֵיהוֹן נַפְשֵׁהוֹן אִתְרְעִיאַת:


He that killeth an ox is as if he slew a man; he that sacrificeth a lamb, as if he cut off a dog's neck; he that offereth an oblation,”as if he offered swine's blood ; the offering of their gifts is the gift of violence. Yea, they delight in their paths, and their soul findeth pleasure in their abominations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:4)


אַף אֲנָא אֶצְבֵּי בְתַבְרֵהוֹן וּמִמַה דְיָצְפָן לָא יִשְׁתֵּיזְבוּן חֲלַף דִשְׁלָחֵית נְבִיֵי וְלָא תָבוּ אִתְנַבִיאוּ וְלָא קַבִּילוּ וַעֲבָדוּ דְבִישׁ קֳדָמַי וּבִדְלָא צְבִינָא אִתְרְעִיאוּ:


I also will desire their destruction, and they shall not be delivered from that which they dread; because, when I sent my prophets, yea, they did not repent; they prophesied, but they obeyed not: but they did evil before me, and they delighted in that I desired not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:5)


קַבִּילוּ פִּתְגָמָא דַייָ צַדִיקַיָא דְמִשְׁתְּוָן לִקֳבֵיל פִּתְגָמֵי רְעוּתֵיהּ אָמְרִין אֲחֵיכוֹן סַנְאֵיכוֹן מֵרַחֲקֵיכוֹן בְּדִיל שְׁמִי יִסְגֵי יְקָרָא דַייָ וְנֶחֱזֵי בְחֶדְוַתְכוֹן וְאִינוּן יִבְהֲתוּן:


Hear the word of the Lord, O ye righteous! who tremble on account of the words of His will; your brethren that hate you, that cast you out for my name's sake, say “Let the glory of the Lord be magnified, that we may see your joy; but they shall be confounded.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:6)


קַל אִתְרְגוֹשָׁא מֵקַרְתָּא דִירוּשְׁלֵם קָלָא מַהֵיכְלָא קַל מֵימְרָא דַייָ דִמְשַׁלֵים גַמְלָא לְבַעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹהִי:


A voice of a tumult from the city of Jerusalem, a voice from the temple, a voice of the WORD of the Lord, who rendereth recompense to His enemies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:7)


עַד לָא מֵיתֵי עָקָא לָהּ תִּתְפְּרִיק עַד לָא יֵיתֵי לָהּ זִיעַ בְּחֶבְלִין עַל יְלִידָה יִתְגְלֵי מַלְכָּהּ:


Before distress cometh upon her, she shall be redeemed; and before trembling cometh upon her, like the pains upon a woman in child-bearing, her King shall be revealed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:8)


מַן שְׁמַע כַּחֲדָא מַן חֲזָא כְּאִלֵין הָאֶפְשַׁר דְתִתְעֲבֵיד אַרְעָא בְּיוֹמָא חָד אִם יִתְבְּרֵי עַמָא זְמַן חֲדָא אֲרֵי עֲתִידָא דְתִתְנְחֵים צִיוֹן וְתִתְמְלֵי מֵעַם שְׁבִי גַלְוָתָהָא:


Who hath heard such a thing? who hath seen such things? Is it possible that a country be made in one day, and a nation be created at once? but Zion shall be comforted, and shall be filled with the people of the captivity of her captivity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:9)


אֲנָא אֱלָהָא בְּרִית עַלְמָא מִבְּרֵאשִׁית אֲמַר יְיָ אֲנָא בְרֵית כָל אֱנָשָׁא אֲנָא בַדְרֵית יַתְהוֹן לְבֵינֵי עַמְמַיָא אַף אֲנָא עֲתִיד לְכַנָשָׁא גַלְוָתִיךְ אֲמַר אֱלָהָיִךְ:


I am God; I have created the world from the beginning, saith the Lord; I, even I have created all men; I, even I have scattered them among the nations, also I will gather thy captivity, saith thy God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:10)


חֲדוֹ בִירוּשְׁלֵם וּבוּעוּ בָהּ כָּל רָחֲמָהָא דוּצוּ עִמָהּ דַיִץ כָּל דַהֲווֹ מִתְאַבְּלִין עֲלָה:


Rejoice ye on account of Jerusalem, and be ye glad on account of her, all ye that love her: rejoice greatly with her, all ye that mourn for her:


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:11)


בְּדִיל דְתִתְפַּנְקוּן וְתִשְׂבְּעוּן מִבִּזַת תַּנְחוּמָתָהָא בְּדִיל דְתִשְׁתּוּן וְתִתְרַווֹן מַחֲמַד יְקָרָהּ:


In order that ye may delight yourselves and be satisfied with the food of her consolations, in order that ye may drink and overflow with the wine of her glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:12)


אֲרֵי כִדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ הָא אֲנָא מַיְתִי לָהּ כִּשְׁפַע נְהַר פְּרָת שְׁלַם וְכִנְחַל מַגְבַּר יְקַר עַמְמַיָא וְתִתְפַּנְקוּן עַל גִסְסִין תִּתְנַטְלוּן וְעַל רְכוּבִין תִּתְרַבּוּן:


For thus saith the Lord, behold, I bring unto her peace, as the floods of the river Euphrates, and the glory of the nations, as an overwhelming stream, and ye shall delight yourselves: ye shall be borne upon the sides, and ye shall be nourished upon the knees.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:13)


כִּגְבַר דְאִמֵיהּ מְנַחֲמָא לֵיהּ כֵּן מֵימְרִי יְנַחֵים יַתְכוֹן וּבִירוּשְׁלֵם תִּתְנַחֲמוּן:


As a man whom his mother comforts, so my WORD shall comfort you; and ye shall be comforted in Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:14)


וְתֶחֱזוּן וְיֶחְדֵי לִבְּכוֹן וּגְוִיַתְכוֹן כְּדִתְאִין יִזְהֲרוּן וְתִתְגְלֵי גְבוּרְתָּא דַייָ לְאוֹטָבָא לְעַבְדוֹהִי צַדִיקַיָא וְיֵתֵי לְוָט לְבַעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹהִי:


And when ye see this, your heart shall rejoice, and your bodies shall flourish like grass; and the power of the Lord shall be revealed to do good to His righteous servants; but a curse shall come upon His enemies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:15)


אֲרֵי הָא יְיָ בְאֶשְׁתָּא מִתְגְלֵי וּכְעַלְעוּלִין רְתִיכוֹהִי לַאֲתָבָא בִּתְקוֹף רוּגְזֵיהּ וּמְזוֹפִיתֵיהּ בְּשַׁלְהוֹבִית אֶשְׁתָּא:


For, behold, the Lord shall be revealed with fire, and His chariots as a whirlwind, to render His anger with fury, and His rebuke with flames of fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:16)


אֲרֵי בְּאֶשְׁתָּא עֲתִיד יְיָ לְמִידַן וּבְחַרְבֵּיהּ יַת כָּל בִּשְׂרָא וְסַגִיאִין קְטִילַיָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


For by fire and by His sword will the Lord judge all flesh, and the slain shall be many before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:17)


דְמִזְדַמְנִין וְדִמְדַכָּן לְגִנָן דְטַעֲוָתָא סִיעָא בָּתַר סִיעָא אָכְלֵי בְּשַׂר חֲזִירָא וְשִׁקְצָא וְעַכְבְּרָא כַּחֲדָא יְסוּפוּן אֲמַר יְיָ:


They who prepare and purify themselves in the gardens of idols, multitude after multitude, eating swines' flesh, and the abomination, and the mouse, shall be consumed together, saith the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:18)


וּקֳדָמַי גְלַן עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן וְעֶשְׁתּוֹנֵיהוֹן עֲתִידָנָא לְכַנָשָׁא יַת כָּל עַמְמַיָא אוּמַיָא וְלִישָׁנַיָא וְיֵתוּן וְיֶחְזוּן יַת יְקָרִי:


Their works and their thoughts are revealed before me: I will gather all people, nations and tongues; and they shall come and see my glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:19)


וַאֲשַׁוֵי בְהוֹן אָתָא וַאֲשַׁלַח מִנְהוֹן מְשֵׁיזְבִין לְבֵינֵי עַמְמַיָא לִמְדִינַת יַמָא פּוּלָאֵי וְלוּדָאֵי דְנַגְדִין וּמָחָן בְּקַשְׁתָּא לִמְדִינַת תּוּבַל וְיָוָן נַגְוָתָא רְחִיקָן דְלָא שְׁמָעוּ יַת שְׁמַע גְבוּרְתִי וְלָא חֲזוֹ יַת יְקָרִי וְיַחְווּן יַת יְקָרִי בְּעַמְמֵיָא:


And I will set a sign among them, and I will send those that escape of them among the nations to the province of the sea, Pul and Lud, that draw the bow, and smite with it, to the province of Tubal, and Javan, the isles that are afar off, that have not heard the fame of my might, neither have seen my glory; but they shall declare my glory among the nations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:20)


וְיֵיתוּן יַת כָּל אֲחֵיכוֹן מִכָּל עַמְמַיָא קוּרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ בְסוּסְוָן וּבִרְתִיכִין וּבִרְחֵילִין וּבְכִדְנְוָן וּבְכִרְכְּרָן וּבְתוּשְׁבְּחָן עַל טוּרָא דְקוּדְשָׁא אֲמַר יְיָ כְּמָא דְיֵיתוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יַת קוּרְבָּנָא בְּמָנָא דְכֵי לְבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא דַיָי:


And they shall bring all your brethren out of all nations an offering before the Lord upon horses, and in chariots, and litters, and upon mules, yea, with songs unto my holy mountain in Jerusalem, saith the Lord, as the children of Israel bring an offering in a clean vessel into the house of the sanctuary of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:21)


וְאַף מִנְהוֹן אֲקָרֵב לְמֶהֱוֵי כַהֲנַיָא וְלֵוָאֵי אֲמַר יְיָ:


And I will also take of them to be priests and Levites, saith the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:22)


אֲרֵי כְּמָא דִשְׁמַיָא חַדְתִּין וְאַרְעָא חַדְתָּא דַאֲנָא עָבִיד קַיְמִין קֳדָמַי אֲמַר יְיָ כֵּן יִתְקַיֵם זַרְעֲכוֹן וְשׁוּמְכוֹן:


For as the new heavens and the new earth, which I will make, shall remain before me, saith the Lord, thus shall your seed and your name be made to remain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:23)


וִיהֵי בִּזְמַן רֵישׁ יְרַח בִּירַח וּבִזְמַן שַׁבָּא בְּשַׁבָּא יֵיתוּן כָּל בִּשְׂרָא לְמִסְגַד קֳדָמַי אֲמַר יְיָ:


And it shall come to pass at the time of the beginning of each month, and at the time of each Sabbath, that all flesh shall come to worship before me, saith the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Isaiah 66:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Isaiah/r/66:24)


וְיִפְקוּן וְיֶחְזוּן בְּפִגְרֵי גַבְרַיָא חַיָבַיָא דִמְרָדוּ בְמֵימְרִי אֲרֵי נִשְׁמַתְהוֹן לָא יְמוּתוּן וְאֶשְׁתְּהוֹן לָא תִטְפֵי וִיהוֹן מִתְדָנִין רַשִׁיעַיָא בְּגֵיהִנָם עַד דְיֵימְרוּן עֲלֵיהוֹן צַדִיקַיָא מִיסַת חֲזֵינָא:


And they shall go forth, and look upon the carcases of the men, the sinners, who have rebelled against my WORD: for their souls shall not die, and their fire shall not be quenched; and the wicked shall be judged in hell, till the righteous shall say concerning them, we have seen enough.
